Explanatory Remarks:

1. Structural elements are enclosed in <>.

Text:

<1>
śrīḥ
lakṣmītantram
prathamo'dhyāyaḥ
namo nityānavadyāya jagataḥ sarvahetave|
jñānāya nistaraṅgāya lakṣmīnārāyaṇātmane || 1 ||
nama iti viśiṣṭopāyasya, lakṣmīnārāyaṇātmana iti viśiṣṭopeyasya ca nirdeśaḥ| nityānavadyāyeti nārāyaṇasya jagadupādānatvaśaṅkitasavikāratvanirāsaḥ baddhamuktajīvavailakṣaṇyaṃ coktam| jagataḥ sarvahetave ityaupaniṣadamabhinnanimittopādānatvaṃ nityajīvavailakṣaṇyaṃ coktam| jñānāyeti jagatkāraṇatvaupayikaguṇagaṇapūrtiḥ svarūpanirūpakadharmaścoktaḥ| nistaraṅgāyeti ṣaḍūrmirāhityaṃ satatapariṇāmyacidvailakṣaṇyaṃ coktam|
khagāsanaṃ ghṛṇādhāramīdṛśaṃ somabhūṣitam|
akalaṅkendusūryāgniṃ lakṣmīrūpamupāsmahe || 2 ||
īdṛśamityādi| īkārarūpamityarthaḥ| akalaṅketyādi| svarasparśavyāpakākṣararūpamiti devyā vāksvarūpatvamuktaṃ bhavati| yathoktaṃ prapañcasāre "svarākhyāḥ ṣoḍaśa proktāḥ sparśākhyāḥ pañcaviṃśatiḥ| vyāpakāśca daśaite syuḥ somenāgnyātmakāḥ kramāt||" iti|
vedavedāntatattvajñaṃ sarvaśāsraviśāradam|
sarvasiddhāntatattvajñaṃ dharmāṇāmāgatāgamam || 3 ||
jitendriyaṃ jitādhāraṃ rāgadveṣāvaśīkṛtam|
caturdaśāṅgayogasthaṃ prasaṃkhyānaparāyaṇam || 4 ||
caturdaśeti| bhaktinyāsayogetyarthaḥ|
ṭippaṇī
namasyāmaḥ śriyaṃ devīṃ devo dīvyati yatsakhaḥ|
traiguṇyaphalake citre kṣiptairbrahmādipāśakaiḥ||
lakṣmītantramahāmbhodhau tattvaratnāni cinvatām|
tattvaprakāśanī ṭīkā sāhāyyaṃ kartumīhate||
viddhe svarbhānunā bhānau purā tapanatāṃ gatam|
nidānaṃ tapasāmādyaṃ tejorāśimanāmayam || 5 ||
viddha iti| atra mahābhāratānuśāsanike 260 adhyāyoktamanusaṃdheyam|
atrimatriguṇopetamatrivargasthamavyayam|
prātaḥ saṃdhyāmupāsīnamṛṣiṃ hutahutāśanam || 6 ||
atriguṇetyādinā maharṣeratrināmno'nvarthatvaṃ vyajyate|
pativratānāṃ paramā dharmapatnī yaśasvinī|
brahmaviṣṇumaheśānāṃ jananī kāraṇāntare || 7 ||
brahmetyādi| purā trimūrtayo'nasūyāyāḥ pātivratyaṃ parīkṣitumayatanta| tena kruddhayā tayā te dvihāyanāḥ śiśavo'kriyanta| tatastanmahiṣībhiḥ prasāditā sā tān yathāpuramakaroditi paurāṇikī kathātrānusaṃdheyā|
devairabhiṣṭutā śaśvacchāntinityā tapasvinī|
viduṣī sarvadharmajñā nityaṃ patimanuvratā || 8 ||
patyuḥ śrutavatī tāstā vividhā dharmasaṃhitāḥ|
praṇipātapuraskāramanasūyā vaco'bravīt || 9 ||
anasūyā
bhagavan sarvadharmajña mama nātha jagatpate|
tvatta eva śrutā dharmāste te bahuvidhātmakāḥ || 10 ||
jñānāni ca vicitrāṇi phalarūpādibhedataḥ|
etebhyo bhagavaddharmo viśiṣṭo vidhṛto mayā || 11 ||
bhagavaddharmaḥ| bhagavatprāptiphalo nivṛttidharmaḥ|
tvayā kathayatā tāstā bhagavaddharmasaṃhitāḥ|
sūcitaṃ tatra tatraiva lakṣmīmāhātmyamuttamam || 12 ||
rahasyatvādapṛṣṭatvānna tvayā prakaṭīkṛtam|
tadahaṃ śrotumicchāmi lakṣmīmāhātmyamuttamam || 13 ||
apṭaṣṭatvāditi| "nāpṭaṣṭaḥ kasyacid brūyāt" iti vidhiratrābhipretaḥ|
yatsvabhāvā hi sā devī yatsvarūpā yadudbhavā|
yatpramāṇā yadādhārā yadupāyātha yatphalā || 14 ||
tadahaṃ śrotumicchāmi tvatto brahmavidāṃ vara|
bhaveyaṃ kṛtakṛtyāhaṃ yasya vijñānayogataḥ || 15 ||
taṃ me darśaya panthānamupasannāsmyadhīhi bho|
iti tasyā vacaḥ śrutvā bhagavānatrirabravīt || 16 ||
upasanneti| "tasmai sa vidvānupasannāya samyak provāca tāṃ tattvato brahmavidyām" iti śrutyartho'bhipretaḥ| panthānamiti| sadupāyamityarthaḥ| "mahājano yena gataḥ sa panthāḥ" itivat|
atriḥ
sādhu saṃbodhito'smyadya dharmajñe dharmacāriṇi|
mayā pṭaṣṭena vaktavyamiti noddhāṭitaṃ purā || 17 ||
arhā tvamasi kalyāṇi lakṣmīmāhātmyamuttamam|
śrotuṃ śrutiśiraḥśreṇihṛdayasthaṃ sanātanam || 18 ||
purā malayaśailasthā munayo dharmatatparāḥ|
śrutasāttvatavijñānā nāradāddevadarśanāt || 19 ||
apṭacchannetamevārthaṃ bhagavantaṃ sanātanam|
nāradaṃ brahmasaṃkāśaṃ bhagavaddharmavedinam || 20 ||
ṛṣayaḥ
bhagavaṃstvacchruto'smābhiḥ sāttvataḥ sattvasaṃśrayaḥ|
śuddho bhāgavato dharmo mokṣaikaphalalakṣaṇaḥ || 21 ||
sāttvataḥ sattvasaṃśrayaḥ| sattvaguṇaikapradhāno dharma ityarthaḥ| anena sāttvataśabdanirvacanamapi sūcitam| yathā sattvaṃ sattvaguṇaḥ asyāstīti sattvataḥ| parvatādivat matvarthe tappratyayaḥ| sattvata eva sāttvata iti| tasyaiva vivaraṇaṃ śuddho bhāgavato dharma iti|
tatra tattvārthakathane lakṣmīmāhātmyamuttamam|
sūcitaṃ tatra tatraiva nāpṭaṣṭatvātprakāśitam || 22 ||
icchāmastadidaṃ śrotuṃ bhavasāgaratārakam|
padminīvaibhavaṃ sarvaṃ prajñāpayuta no bhavān || 23 ||
natāḥ sma śirasā pādau tava saṃsāratārakau|
adhīhi bho mune divyaṃ prapannāstvāṃ ciraṃ vayam || 24 ||
ciramiti| "nāsaṃvatsaravāsine brūyāt" iti vidhirabhipretaḥ|
nāradaḥ
sādhu saṃbodhito'smyadya munayaḥ saṃśitavratāḥ|
prasannaḥ kathayāmyadya lakṣmītantraṃ sanātanam || 25 ||
lakṣmītantramiti granthanāma| idaṃ ca tantraṃ śatakoṭigranthaparimitāt mūlabhūtalakṣmītantrāt sāramuddhṛtya kathitamiti vakṣyate'traiva |
yatra sā dṛśyate devī svarūpaguṇavaibhavaiḥ|
padminī padmanābhasya mahiṣī padmasaṃbhavā || 26 ||
purā durvāsasaḥ śāpādabhibhūte puraṃdare|
niḥsvādhyāyavaṣaṭkāre bhraṣṭaśrīke jagattraye || 27 ||
puretyādi| iyamākhyāyikā viṣṇupurāṇe prathamāṃśe draṣṭavyā| niḥsvādhyāyavaṣaṭkāratva bhraṣṭaśrīkatve hetuḥ|
daridre devavarge ca kṛśe dharme nisaṃtate|
pitāmahe suraiḥ sārdhaṃ kṣīrodārṇavameyuṣi || 28 ||
nisaṃtate| vicchinna ityarthaḥ|
bahūna varṣagaṇān divyāṃstaptvā tīvraṃ mahattapaḥ|
saṃbodhite jagannāthe devadeve janārdhane || 29 ||
pitāmahena devāya kārye ca vinivedite|
kṣīrode mathite devaistadādiṣṭena vartmanā || 30 ||
pārijāte hayaśreṣṭhe gajendre'psarasāṃ gaṇe|
kālakūṭe samudbhūte vāruṇyāmamṛte tathā || 31 ||
saha candramasā devyāmutthitāyāṃ mahārṇavāt|
padminyāṃ padmanābhasya vakṣaḥsthāyāmanantaram || 32 ||
tayāvalokite devavarge śriyamupeyuṣi|
tayānavekṣite daityavarge caiva parājite || 33 ||
anenānvayavyatirekābhyāṃ lakṣmīkaṭākṣapātasya sarvasaṃpannidānatvaṃ nirūpyate| yatsattve yatsattvamityanvaye yathā deveṣu iti dṛṣṭāntaḥ| yadabhāve yadabhāva iti vyatireke yathā daityeṣu iti dṛṣṭāntaḥ| imameva viṣayamanantarameva 35 tamaśloke vakṣyati|
svārājyamakhilaṃ prāpya modamāne puraṃdare|
bṛhaspatirupāgamya rahasīdaṃ vaco'bravīt || 34 ||
bṛhaspatiḥ
kāle saṃbodhayāmyetacchṛṇu vākyaṃ puraṃdara|
anvayavyatirekābhyāṃ lakṣmyāste kathitā purā || 35 ||
mahattā mahatāṃ nātha tasyāmāyatate sthitiḥ|
na bhraśyeta yathaivaiṣā tava rājyasthiteḥ parā || 36 ||
tathā yatasva deveśa śaraṇaṃ gaccha padminīm|
eṣā hi śreyaso mūlameṣā hi paramā gatiḥ || 37 ||
śrutīnāmabhisaṃdhiśca saiva devī sanātanī|
eṣaiva jagatāṃ prāṇā eṣaiva jagatāṃ kriyā || 38 ||
śrutīnāmiti| "vedaiśca sarvairahameva vedyaḥ" ityuktarītyā sarvavedatātparyaparyavasānabhūmirityarthaḥ| ubhayorapṛthagbhāvānna vacanavirodhaḥ|
eṣaiva jagatāmicchā jñānameṣā parāvarā|
eṣaiva sṛjate kāle saiṣā pāti jagattrayam || 39 ||
jagatkāraṇatvasya brahmāsādhāraṇatvasiddhānte'pi tacchaktirūpatvena tadapṛthaksiddhatvāt śaktikṛtasyāpi śaktimatkṛtatvavyapadeśo yujyata eva| vakṣyate caitat suspaṣṭaṃ devyaiva.
jagatsaṃharate cānte tattatkāraṇasaṃsthitā|
mātaraṃ jagatāmenāmanārādhya mahat kutaḥ || 40 ||
etattu vaiṣṇavaṃ dhāma yate nāvartate yatiḥ |
eṣā sā paramā niṣṭhā sāṃkhyānāṃ viditātmanām || 41 ||
eṣā sā yogināṃ niṣṭhā yatra gatvā na śocati|
eṣā pāśupatī niṣṭhā saiṣā vedavidāṃ gatiḥ || 42 ||
pañcarātrasya kṛtsnasya saiṣā niṣṭhā sanātanī|
saiṣā nārāyaṇī devī sthitā nārāyaṇātmanā || 43 ||
pṛthagbhūtāpṛthagbhūtā jyotsneva himadīdhiteḥ|
taistairjñānaiḥ pṛthagbhūtairāgamaiśca pṛthagvidhaiḥ || 44 ||
nārāyaṇyā nārāyaṇātmanāvasthānaṃ pūrvaślokoktamevopapādayati pṛthagbhūteti apṛthagbhūteti ca| dharmadharmiṇorapṛthaksiddhayoḥ niṣkarṣavivakṣāyāṃ dharmasya pṛthagvyapadeśaḥ| aniṣkarṣe tu dharmitayā| yathā `śuklaṃ rūpam, śuklaḥ paṭaḥ' iti|
ekaivaiṣā parā devī bahudhā samupāsyate|
tāmupehi mahābhāgāṃ śaraṇaṃ padmasaṃbhavām || 45 ||
tapoviśeṣairvividhaistaistaiśca niyamaiḥ śubhaiḥ|
ārādhya mahiṣīṃ viṣṇoḥ sthirīkuru nijaśriyam || 46 ||
eṣā prasādasumukhī svaṃ padaṃ prāpayiṣyati|
abhīpsitārthadā devī kāmināmapi kāmadā || 47 ||
devīprasāde paramapadarūpamokṣaprāptiḥ, ānuṣaṅgikatrivargaphalaprāptiścānenocyate|
nāradaḥ
iti saṃbodhitaḥ śakro guruṇā guruṇā svayam|
ārādhayitukāmastāṃ kṣīrodasyottaraṃ yayau || 48 ||
tatra divyaṃ tapastepe bilvamūlaniketanaḥ|
ekapādasthito maunī kāṣṭhabhūto'nilāśanaḥ || 49 ||
ūrdhvadṛgbāhuvaktraśca niyato niyatātmavān|
divyaṃ varṣasahasraṃ vai tapastepe suduścaram || 50 ||
tapaso'vabhṛthe tasya sā devī padmasaṃbhavā|
prasannavadanā viṣṇormahiṣī darśanaṃ yayau || 51 ||
agrataḥ saṃsthitāṃ devīṃ jagatāṃ mātaraṃ parām|
tāṃ śakraścakṣuṣā vīkṣya vismayaṃ paramaṃ yayau || 52 ||
vihvalaḥ praṇipatyātha pāñjalirbalasūdanaḥ|
śriyaṃ sūktena tuṣṭāva padminīṃ pākaśāsanaḥ || 53 ||
ekāntabhāvamāpannamavyājāṃ bhaktimāsthitam|
taṃ vīkṣya jagatāṃ mātā vākyametaduvāca ha || 54 ||
śrīḥ
vatsa śakra parasannāsmi tapasā tava suvrata|
varaṃ vṛṇu mahābhāga kimiṣṭaṃ karavāṇite || 55 ||
śakraḥ
adya me tapaso devi yamasya niyamasya ca|
sadyaḥ phalamavāptaṃ yad dṛṣṭā bhagavatī mayā || 56 ||
yadi vāpi varo deyastvayā me parameśvari|
tattvaṃ kathaya deveśi yāsi tvaṃ yatprakārikā || 57 ||
yatpramāṇā yadādhārā yadupāyā sanātanī|
yasya tvaṃ tena vā devi saṃbandhastava yadvidhaḥ || 58 ||
yaccānyadveditavyaṃ te nānāśāsropabṛṃhitam|
kathayeśvari tatsarvamupasanno'smyadhīhi bho || 59 ||
iti prasāditā tena vatseneva payasvinī|
snihyatā manasā padmā pākaśāsanamabravīt || 60 ||
śrīḥ
śṛṇu śakra mahābhāga yā hyahaṃ yatprakārikā|
yasyāhaṃ tena vā yādṛk saṃbandho mama vṛtrahan || 61 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre śāsrāvatāro nāma prathamo'dhyāyaḥ
iti prathamo'dhyāyaḥ

<2>
dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīruvāca
asti nirduḥkhaniḥsīmasukhānubhavalakṣaṇaḥ|
paramātmā paraṃ yasya padaṃ paśyanti sūrayaḥ || 1 ||
nirduḥkhetyādinā nikhilaheyapratyanīkatvānantakalyāṇaguṇākaratvarūpobhayaliṅgatvaṃ paramātmana uktaṃ bhavati|
kaścitkeṣāṃcidātmā syāttasyānyeṣāṃ ca kaścana|
tasyāpyanya itītthaṃ tu yatraiṣā vyavatiṣṭhate || 2 ||
ātmā| niyantetyarthaḥ| eṣeti| vyavastheti śeṣaḥ| vyavatiṣṭhate samāpnoti, sa paramātmeti pūrveṇānvayaḥ|
adhvanāmadhvanaḥ pāraṃ paramātmānamūcire|
ahaṃ nāma smṛto yo'rthaḥ sa ātmā samudīryate || 3 ||
adhvanāmiti| traivargikādhvāpekṣayā paramo yo'dhvā mokṣādhvā arcirādiḥ, tasya pāraṃ prāpyamityarthaḥ; "so'dhvanaḥ pāramāpnoti tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam" iti vacanāt| "adhvanāmadhvapate" iti śrutyuddharaṇamatra| ahamiti| yaḥ ahaṃpratyayagocaraḥ sa ātmetyarthaḥ|
anavacchinnarūpo'haṃ paramātmeti śabdyate |
kroḍīkṛtamidaṃ sarvaṃ cetanācetanātmakam || 4 ||
anavacchinneti| vastvādyaparicchedena yo'haṃśabdāparyavasānavṛttyā pratīyate sa paramātmetyarthaḥ|
yena so'haṃsmṛto bhāvaḥ paramātmā sanātanaḥ|
sa vāsudevo bhagavān kṣetrajñaḥ paramo mataḥ || 5 ||
viṣṇurnārāyaṇo viśvo viśvarūpa itīryate|
ahaṃtayā samākrāntaṃ yasya viśvamidaṃ jagat || 6 ||
āha ca śakro'nyatra "tvayaitadviṣṇunā cāmba jagadvyāptaṃ carācaram" iti|
vastvavastu ca tannāsti yannākrāntamahaṃtayā|
idaṃtayā yadālīḍhamākrāntaṃ tadahaṃtayā || 7 ||
idaṃtayeti| idaṃśabdārthatayā pratītaṃ sarvaṃ jagadityarthaḥ|
sarvataḥ śānta evāsau nirvikāraḥ sanātanaḥ|
ananto deśakālādiparicchedavivarjitaḥ || 8 ||
mahāvibhūtirityukto vyāptiḥ sā mahatī yataḥ|
tad brahma paramaṃ dhāma nirālambanabhāvanam || 9 ||
nistaraṅgāmṛtāmbhodhikalpaṃ ṣāḍguṇyamujjvalam|
ekaṃ taccidghanaṃ śāntamudayāstamayojjhitam || 10 ||
nistaraṅgetyādinā bhagavataḥ śāntoditāvasthocyate| tadevāha śāntamiti|
apṛthagbhūtaśaktitvād brahmādvaitaṃ taducyate|
tasya yā paramā śaktirjyotsneva himadīdhiteḥ || 11 ||
brahmādvaitamiti| svāpṛthaksiddhaśaktyahaṃtāviśiṣṭatvāt tadviśiṣṭaṃ brahmaikameva tattvamityarthaḥ| svasvarūpasya tadapṛthaksiddhatvameva dṛṣṭāntamukhenāha jyosneti|
sarvāvasthāgatā devī svātmabhūtānapāyinī|
ahaṃtā brahmaṇastasya sāhamasmi sanātanī || 12 ||
sarvāvasthāgatānapāyinītyanena "rāghavatve'bhavatsītā rukmiṇī kṛṣṇajanmani| anyeṣu cāvatāreṣu viṣṇoreṣānapāyinī" iti maharṣivacanaṃ smāryate|
ātmā sa sarvabhūtānāmahaṃbhūto hariḥ smṛtaḥ|
ahaṃtā sarvabhūtānāmahamasmi sanātanī || 13 ||
yena bhāvena bhavati vāsudevaḥ sanātanaḥ|
bhavatastasya devasya sa bhāvo'hamitīritā || 14 ||
bhavadbhāvātmakaṃ brahma tatastacchāśvataṃ padam|
bhavannārāyaṇo devo bhāvo lakṣmīrahaṃ parā || 15 ||
lakṣmīnārāyaṇākhyātamato brahma sanātanam|
ahaṃ tayā samākrānto hyahamarthaḥ prasidhyati || 16 ||
śrīviśiṣṭameva paraṃ brahma| na tu niḥśrīkamityarthaḥ| lakṣmīnārāyaṇayorubhayorātmahaviḥśeṣitve'pi āgnāvaiṣṇavādiṣviva viśiṣṭasyaive ddeśyatvāt tasya caikatvāt na śeṣidvitvaprasaktiriti bhāvaḥ|
ahamarthasamutthā ca sāhaṃtā parikīrtitā|
anyonyenāvinābhāvādanyonyena samanvayāt || 17 ||
tādātmyaṃ viddhi saṃbandhaṃ mama nāthasya cobhayoḥ|
ahaṃtayā vināhaṃ hi nirupākhyo na sidhyati || 18 ||
nirupākhyo nirviśeṣaḥ| loke sarvamapi vastu idamitthamiti prakāraviśeṣapuraskāreṇaiva sidhyati; na tu nirviśeṣaṇiti bhāvaḥ|
ahamarthaṃ vināhaṃtā nirādhārā na sidhyati|
bhavadbhāvātmakaṃ rūpaṃ samastavyastagocaram || 19 ||
parokṣamaparokṣaṃ ca jagati pravicintyate|
nirunmeṣe nirunmeṣā sāhaṃtā parameśvarī || 20 ||
kroḍīkṛtyākhilaṃ sarvaṃ brahmaṇi vyavatiṣṭhate|
unmeṣastasya yo nāma yathā candrodaye'mbudheḥ || 21 ||
ahaṃ nārāyaṇī śaktiḥ sisṛkṣālakṣaṇā tadā|
nimeṣastasya yo nāma saṃhṛtau paramātmanaḥ || 22 ||
devyāḥ śaktirūpatvaṃ pūrvamuktam| adhunā tadbheda ucyate sisṛkṣāśaktiḥ suṣupsāśaktiśceti| prathamayā sṛṣṭiḥ, dvitīyayā saṃhāraśca jāyate| tatra nityavibhūtiviṣaye prathamāyā eva pravṛttiḥ| dvitīyāyāstu līlāvibhūtāvevopayogaḥ|
ahaṃ nārāyaṇī śaktiḥ suṣupsālakṣaṇā hi sā|
sisṛkṣāyā mamodyantyā devāllakṣmīpateḥ svayam || 23 ||
avyāhatamasaṃkocamaiśvaryaṃ pravijṛmbhate|
jñānaṃ tatparamaṃ brahma sarvadarśi nirāmayam || 24 ||
jñānādiṣu ṣaṭsu guṇeṣu jñānaṃ tatsvarūpanirūpakadharmaḥ| anye guṇāstu nirūpitasvarūpaviśeṣaṇabhūtā iti vivekaḥ| tadeva vakṣyatyuttaratra `śeṣamaiśvaryavīryādi jñānadharmaḥ' iti| jñānākhyasya svarūpasya dharma ityarthaḥ|
jñānātmikā tathāhaṃtā sarvajñā sarvadarśinī|
jñānātmakaṃ paraṃ rūpaṃ brahmaṇo mama cobhayoḥ || 25 ||
śeṣamaiśvaryavīryādi jñānadharmaḥ sanātanaḥ|
ahamityāntaraṃ rūpaṃ jñānarūpamudīryate || 26 ||
prakāśakādikaṃ rūpaṃ sphaṭikādisalakṣaṇam|
atastu jñānarūpatvaṃ mama nārāyaṇasya ca || 27 ||
avyāhatiryadudyatyāstadaiśvaryaṃ paraṃ mama|
iccheti socyate tattattattvaśāsreṣu paṇḍitaiḥ || 28 ||
iccheti| sisṛkṣāśakteraiśvaryarūpatvaṃ pūrvaṃ caturviṃśe śloke varṇitamatra smartavyam| sisṛkṣā hi sraṣṭumicchā|
jagatprakṛtibhāvo me yaḥ sā śaktiritīryate|
sṛjantyā yacchramābhāvo mama tadbalamiṣyate || 29 ||
devyā jagatprakṛtibhāvo na svarūpataḥ, tathātve vikāritvaprasaṅgāt| kiṃtu svaprakārabhūtacidacidātmaneti draṣṭavyam| eṣaiva hi brahmaṇo jagatprakṛtitve gatiḥ|
bharaṇaṃ yacca kāryasya balaṃ tacca pracakṣate|
śaktyaṃśakena tatprāhurbharaṇaṃ tattvakovidāḥ || 30 ||
vikāraviraho vīryaṃ prakṛtitve'pi me sadā|
svabhāvaṃ hi jahātyāśu payo dadhisamudbhave || 31 ||
vikāravirahitvaṃ, pūrvoktarītyā prakārāṃśe piraṇāmaḥ na tu prakāryaṃśa iti svīkārāt| prakāribhūte svarūpa eva pariṇāmamaṅgīkṛtya aghaṭitaghaṭanāsāmarthyāt vikārābhāvanirvāhastu na yuktisahaḥ| atha eva "māyāṃ tu prakṛtiṃ vidyāt" ityatreśvaropādhibhūtamāyāyāṃ prakṛtitvamartha iti keṣāṃcidabhyupagamaḥ|
jagadbhāve'pi sā nāsti vikṛtirmama nityadā|
vikāraviraho vīryamatastattvavidāṃ matam || 32 ||
vikramaḥ kathito vīryamaiśvaryāṃśaḥ sa tu smṛtaḥ|
sahakāryanapekṣā me sarvakāryavidhau hi yā || 33 ||
tejaḥ ṣaṣṭhaṃ guṇaṃ prāhustamimaṃ tattvavedinaḥ|
parābhibhavasāmarthyaṃ tejaḥ kecitpracakṣate || 34 ||
aiśvarye yojayantyeke tattejastattvakovidāḥ|
iti pañca guṇā ete jñānasya srutayo'mahāḥ || 35 ||
jñānādyāḥ ṣaḍguṇā ete ṣāḍguṇyaṃ mama tadvapuḥ|
udyatītthaṃ sisṛkṣāyā mamāyutatamī kalā || 36 ||
udyatīti| udayaṃ prāptetyarthaḥ|
śuddhāśuddhātmako vargastayā kroḍīkṛto'khilaḥ|
tatra śuddhamayaṃ mārgaṃ vyākhyāsyāmi sureśvara || 37 ||
śuddha sṛṣṭiḥ, aśuddhasṛṣṭiśceti dvividhā sṛṣṭiḥ| tatra śuddhā tnaiguṇyarahitā śuddhasattvamayī| tasyāḥ svayaṃprakāśatvādajaḍatvamiti kecit| jaḍatvameveti pare| asyā eva nityavibhūtiriti vyavahāraḥ| aśuddhasṛṣṭistu traiguṇyamayī jaḍarūpā līlāvibhūtiriti vyavahṛtā|
abhivyaktānabhivyaktaṣāḍguṇyakramamujjvalam|
ālambitacatūrūpaṃ rūpaṃ tatpārameśvaram || 38 ||
na kevalaṃ nityavibhūtireva śuddhasṛṣṭiḥ| kiṃtu tatra vidyotamānāḥ vāsudevasaṃkarṣaṇapradyumnāniruddhākhyā vyūhā api tatheti mantavyam| tatra prathame vāsudeve ṣāḍguṇyakramasya na sphuṭāvabhāsaḥ| tadanantaravyūheṣu tu kiṃcitkiṃcit krameṇeti jñeyam|
guṇakalpanayādhyasto guṇonmeṣakṛtakramaḥ|
mūrtīkṛtaguṇaśceti tridhā mārgo'yamadbhutaḥ || 39 ||
teṣu vkacit vyūhe guṇānāṃ samyaganunmeṣāt na guṇātmatayā jñānam| vkacittu krameṇonmeṣāt kramādabhivyaktiḥ| vkacittu guṇānāmatiprakāśāt guṇātmanā parijñānamiti kramaḥ|
yugāni trīṇi ṣaṇṇaṃ yānyāhurjñānādikāni vai
samāsavyāsatasteṣāṃ cāturātmyaṃ vivicyate || 40 ||
samastavyastabhedena guṇānāṃ tadyugatrayam|
vivakṣyate yadā sā me śāntāyāścāturātmyatā || 41 ||
ākṛtīranavekṣyāpi guṇānāṃ kalpanākṛtam|
cāturātmyamidaṃ prāhuḥ śāntāyāstattvacintakāḥ || 42 ||
śāntātiśāntādunmeṣo mama rūpādyugatraye|
kramavyaktaṃ tadādyaṃ me cāturātmyamamūrtimat || 43 ||
ataraṅgamanirdeśyaṃ niḥsattaṃ sattvamavyayam|
saccinmātrākhya unmeṣaḥ sādyā me śāntatācyutiḥ || 44 ||
vyaktajñānabalākyāyāṃ pūrvaṃ saṃkarṣaṇātmani|
tilakālakavatsarvo vikāro mayi tiṣṭhati || 45 ||
tilakālakavaditi| yathā prāṇināṃ dehe tilavat sūkṣmaḥ kālavarṇaśca binduviśeṣo'tyalpaparimite vkacitkoṇe dṛśyate, tadvat sarvo'pi prapañcaḥ mamaikadeśe tiṣṭhatītyarthaḥ| saṃkarṣatīti saṃkarṣaṇa ityanvarthaṃ nāma| atra jñānabalayoḥ samunmeṣaḥ|
tanmāṃ saṃkarṣaṇātmānaṃ vidurjñānabale budhāḥ|
svayaṃ gṛhṇāmi kartṛtvamunmiṣantī tataḥ param || 46 ||
pradyumna iti māmāhuḥ sarvārthadyotanīṃ tadā|
yugaṃ prasphuritaṃ rūpaṃ tasminnaiśvaryavīryayoḥ || 47 ||
prakṛṣṭaṃ dyumnaṃ tejo vā yasyetyanvarthaṃ nāma| sṛṣṭikartṛtvāt atra vīryaiśvaryayoḥ samunmeṣaḥ|
tatastayā kriyāśaktyā labdhāveśā cikīrṣayā|
yujyamānāniruddhākhyāṃ lambhitā tattvakovidaiḥ || 48 ||
aniruddheti| pālanakartaiṣaḥ| atra śaktitejasoḥ samunmeṣaḥ| tathā ca saṃjagṛhuḥ "ṣāḍguṇyādvāsudeḥ para iti sa bhavānmuktabhogyo balāḍhyādbodhātsaṃkarṣaṇastvaṃ harasi vitanuṣe śāstramaisvaryavīryāt| pradyumnaḥ sargadharmau nayasi ca bhagavan śaktitejo'niruddho bibhrāṇaḥ pāsi sattvaṃ samayasi ca tathā vyūhya raṅgadhirāja|| " iti|
avasthāḥ kramaśo me tāḥ suṣuptisvapnajāgarāḥ|
tisro mama svabhāvākhyā vijñānaiśvaryaśaktayaḥ || 49 ||
suṣuptītyādi| saṃkarṣaṇapradyumnāniruddhānāṃ suṣuptyādyadhiṣṭhātṛtvam| vāsudevastu turīyādhiṣṭhāteti vevekaḥ| ayameva vibhavagaḥ māṇḍūkyopaniṣadi viśvataijasaprājñaturyanāmabhirnirdiṣṭaḥ|
unmiṣantyaḥ pṛthaktattvatrayeṇa parikīrtitāḥ|
balaṃ vīryaṃ tathā teja ityetattu guṇatrayam || 50 ||
śramādyavadyābhāvākhyaṃ jñānāderupasarjanam|
itthaṃ śāntoditāvasthādvayabhedajuṣo mama || 51 ||
śāntoditeti| śāntāvasthā paravāsudevāvasthā| uditāvasthā vyūhāvasthā| tatrāpi śāntoditāvasthaḥ prathamavyūhaḥ| nityoditāvasthā anye traya iti viśeṣaḥ|
svadharmormisamullāso na bhedāyāmbudheriva|
prāyo yadguṇakartavye varte kṛtyā yayā hyaham || 52 ||
tatra tadguṇayugmaṃ tu mama rūpatayocyate|
ato jñānabale devaḥ saṃkarṣaṇa udīryate || 53 ||
aiśvaryavīrye pradyumno'niruddhaḥ śaktitejasī|
ādyastvabhinnaṣāḍguṇyo brahmatattvāpṛthaksthitau || 54 ||
eko'pyanunayaudāryakrauryaśauryādibhirguṇaiḥ|
naṭaḥ pravartate yadvadveṣaceṣṭādibhedavān || 55 ||
tadvadekāpi saivāhaṃ jñānaśaktyādibhirguṇaiḥ|
saṃkarṣaṇādisadbhāvaṃ bhaje lokahitepsayā || 56 ||
kramaśaḥ pralayotpattisthitibhiḥ prāṇyanugrahaḥ|
prayojanamathānyacca śāstraśāstrārthatatphalaiḥ || 57 ||
saṃkarṣaṇaḥ prakalayakartā śāstropadeṣṭā ca| pradyumnaḥ sṛṣṭikartā śāstrārthapravartayitā ca| aniruddhaḥ pālanakartā śāstrārthaphalanirvāhakaśceti pratyekaṃ vyāpāravibhāgaḥ|
daśāsturyasuṣuptyādyāścaturvyūhe'pi lakṣayet|
vibhavo'nantarūpastu padmanābhamukho vibhoḥ || 58 ||
paravyūhāvupavarṇya vibhavaḥ kathyate'tra| vibhavāvatārāḥ padmanābhādayo bahavaḥ| matsyakūrmādayo'pyatraiva parigaṇitā avatārāḥ|
aniruddhasya vistāro darśitastasya sāttvate|
arcāpi laukikī yā sā bhagavadbhāvitātmanām || 59 ||
arcā nāma devālayeṣu pratiṣṭhitā bimbaviśeṣāḥ|
mantramantreśvaranyāsātsāpi ṣāḍguṇyavigrahā|
parādyarcāvasāne'sminmama rūpacatuṣṭaye || 60 ||
turyādyavasthā vijñeyā itīyaṃ śuddhapaddhatiḥ|
īṣadbhedena vijñeyaṃ tadvayūhavibhavāntaram||
śuddhetaraṃ tvatho mārgaṃ mama śakra niśāmayā || 61 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre śuddhamārgaprakāśo nāma dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ
iti dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ

<3>
tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīruvāca
nityanirdoṣaniḥsīmakalyāṇaguṇaśālinī|
ahaṃ nārāyaṇī nāma sā sattā vaiṣṇavī parā || 1 ||
deśātkālāttathā rūpātparicchedo na me smṛtaḥ|
saṃvittireva me rūpaṃ sarvaiśvaryādiko guṇaḥ || 2 ||
svarūpanirūpako dharmaḥ jñānam| anye pañcāpi guṇāḥ nirūpitasvarūpaguṇabhūtā ityarthaḥ|
svasvātantryavaśenaiva vibhāgastatra vartate|
vijñānaiśvaryaśaktyātmā vibhāgo yaḥ sa īritaḥ || 3 ||
itthaṃ vibhāgo'pi madicchākṛta evetyāha svasvātantryeti|
vijñānaiśvaryaśaktīnāmunmeṣastvaparo'dhunā|
atarkyāyā mamodyatyā niyogānarhayā sadā || 4 ||
apara iti| pūrvaṃ śuddhasṛṣṭau eka unmeṣa uktaḥ| adhunā aśuddhātmakatraiguṇyasṛṣṭāvanya unmeṣa ityarthaḥ|
icchayānyatkṛtaṃ rūpamāsījjñānādike trike|
yathaivekṣurasaḥ svaccho guḍatvaṃ pratipadyate || 5 ||
jñānāditrikarūpaṃ sattvāditrikātmanā anyathā kṛtamāsīdityarthaḥ|
tadvatsvacchamayaṃ jñānaṃ sattvatāṃ pratipadyate|
rajastvaṃ ca mamaiśvaryaṃ tamastvaṃ śaktirapyuta || 6 ||
tadevāha tadvaditi| jñānaṃ sattvatayā, aiśvaryaṃ rajastayā, śaktiśca tamastayā jātamiti bhāvaḥ|
ete trayo guṇāḥ śakra traiguṇyamiti śabdyate|
rajaḥpradhānaṃ tatsṛṣṭau traiguṇyaṃ parivartate || 7 ||
traiguṇyamiti cāturvarṇyamitivat svārthe ṣyañ|
sthitau sattvapradhānaṃ tat saṃhṛtau tu tamomukham|
ahaṃ saṃvinmayī pūrvā vyāpinyapi puraṃdara || 8 ||
adhiṣṭhāya guṇān sṛṣṭisthitisaṃhṛtikāriṇī|
nirguṇāpi guṇānetānadhiṣṭhāyātmavāñchayā || 9 ||
nirguṇāpīti| pūrvaṃ ṣāḍuguṇyasyoktatvāt atra nirguṇapadasya sattvarajastamorūpamiśraguṇarahitetyarthaḥ| ātmavāñchayetyanena jagatsṛṣṭyādau līlaiva prayojanamityuktaṃ bhavati| yathāha bhagavān bādarāyaṇaḥ "lokavattu līlākaivalyam" iti|
cakraṃ pravartayāmyekā sṛṣṭisthityantarūpakam|
śakraḥ
vidhādvayaṃ samāsthāya jñānādye tu yugatraye || 10 ||
śuddhetaravibhāgena kimarthaṃ tvaṃ pravartase|
vidhayoranayoḥ padme saṃbandhaḥ kaḥ parasparam || 11 ||
etatpṛṣṭā mayā brūhi namaste padmasaṃbhave|
śrīḥ
aniyojyaṃ mamaiśvaryamicchaiva mama kāraṇam || 12 ||
muhyantyatra mahānto'pi tattvaṃ śrṛṇu tathāpi me|
īśeśitavyabhāvena parivarte sadā hyaham || 13 ||
śuddhetarasṛṣṭyoḥ saṃbandha ucyate īśeśitavyeti| rakṣyarakṣakabhāva ityarthaḥ|
īśo nārāyaṇo jñeya īśatā tasya cāpyaham|
īśitavyaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ cidacicca puraṃdara || 14 ||
cicchaktistu parā tatra bhoktṛtāṃ pratipadyate|
bhogyopakaraṇasthānarūpaṃ tasyā acitpadam || 15 ||
anādyayā samāviddhā sā cicchaktiravidyayā|
matpravartitayā nityaṃ cicchaktirbhoktṛtāṃ gatā || 16 ||
ahaṃmamatvasaṃbandhāddhyacitsvenābhimanyate|
avidyā sā tirobhāvaṃ vidyayā yāti vai yadā || 17 ||
cicchaktirnirabhīmānā tadā madbhāvameṣyati|
tāṃ vidyāṃ śuddhamārgasthāṃ paravyūhādirūpiṇī || 18 ||
pravartayāmi kāruṇyājjñānasadbhāvadarśinī|
rakṣyarakṣakabhāvo'yaṃ saṃbandho vidhayordvayoḥ || 19 ||
vidhā rakṣati śuddhādyā rakṣyate ca vidhāparā|
etatte kathitaṃ śakra kiṃ bhūyaḥ śrotumicchasi || 20 ||
śakraḥ
īśeśitavyabhāvena kimarthaṃ tvaṃ pravartase|
īśitavyaṃ kiyadbhedaṃ kiṃrūpaṃ tatra me vada || 21 ||
śrīḥ
svabhāvo nānuyojyo'yaṃ mama nārāyaṇasya ca|
īśo'hamīśitavyo na sa ca devaḥ sanātanaḥ || 22 ||
īśitavyaṃ dvidhā proktaṃ cidacivdyatirekataḥ|
cicchaktirbhoktṛrūpātra sā ca cidrūpadhāriṇī || 23 ||
bhogyopakaraṇasthānairacicchaktistridhā sthitā |
prasarantyāstṛtīyaṃ me sā ca parva smṛtaṃ budhaiḥ || 24 ||
vibhakte api te ete śaktī cidacidātmike|
matsvācchandyavaśenaiva mama rūpe sanātane || 25 ||
cicchaktirvimalā śuddhā cinmayyānandarūpiṇī|
anādyavidyāviddheyamitthaṃ saṃsarati dhruvam || 26 ||
acicchaktirjaḍāpyevamaśuddhā pariṇāminī|
triguṇaāpi mamaivedaṃ svācchandyāt pravijṛmbhitam || 27 ||
dhūmaketuryathā dhūmaṃ dīpyamāno bhajet svayam|
śuddhasaṃvitsvarūpāpi bhaje sāhamacidgatim || 28 ||
jvalanasvabhāvo'pi dhūmaketuryathā malinadhūmarūpatāṃ pratipadyate, tathā jñānasvarūpāpyahamacidbhāvamāpadya ityarthaḥ|
anākrāntā vikalpena śabdairapyakadarthitā|
ādyānopadhināpyevaṃ varte'hamacidātmanā || 29 ||
ādhyānopadhineti| madicchārūpopādinetyarthaḥ| dhyānālambanārthamiti vārthaḥ| yathā vakṣyati ‘dhyānaviśrāmabhūmayaḥ’  iti|
bahirantaḥpadārthe hi citsvarūpamakhaṇḍitam|
viśinaṣṭi tathāpyetaccitrayopādhisaṃpadā || 30 ||
svātantryameva me heturnānuyojyāsmi kiṃcana|
itthaṃprabhāvāmevaṃ māṃ vidan bhuddho bhaviṣyasi || 31 ||
śakraḥ
kathaṃ sṛjasi vai lokān sukhaduḥkhasamanvitān|
asṛṣṭirhi varaṃ yadvā sṛṣṭirastu sukhātmikā || 32 ||
asṛṣṭiriti| atra "sṛjecca sukhamevaikamanukampāpracoditaḥ" iti ślokavārttikavacanaṃ smartavyam|
śrīḥ
anādyavidyāviddhānāṃ jīvānāṃ sadasanmayam|
saṃcitaṃ karma saṃprekṣya miśrāṃ sṛṣṭiṃ karomyaham || 33 ||
śakraḥ
kṣīrodasaṃbhave devi svācchandyaṃ te kathaṃ bhavet|
karma cetsamavekṣya tvaṃ vidadhāsi sukhāsukhe || 34 ||
śrīḥ
kurvatyā mama kāryāṇi karma tatkaraṇaṃ smṛtam|
kartuśca karaṇāpekṣā na svātantryavighātinī || 35 ||
niravadyā svatantrāhaṃ nānuyogapade sthitā|
vibhaje bahudhātmānaṃ kartṛkarmakriyādinā || 36 ||
śakraḥ
yadvā tadvāstu taddevi svātantryaṃ te yadīdṛśam|
sṛṣṭiprakāramākhyāhi namaste padmasaṃbhave || 37 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre traiguṇyaprakāśo nāma tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ
iti tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ

<4>
caturtho'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ
nirmalākāśakalpāhaṃ niḥsamānandacinmayī|
ahaṃ nārāyaṇī nāma bhāvo'haṃ tādṛśo hareḥ || 1 ||
na śāntā noditā nāpi madhyamāhaṃ cidātmikā|
tādṛśasya harerviṣṇoḥ svarūpamaśilātmanaḥ || 2 ||
tasyācitraikarūpasya vikalpapadavījuṣaḥ|
acitrāhaṃ tadākārā sarvataḥ samatāṃ gatā || 3 ||
tayornau saṃvidātmaiva vkacidunmeṣa utthitaḥ|
koṭikoṭisahasraughakoṭikoṭitamī kalā || 4 ||
sisṛkṣā nāma tadrūpā sṛṣṭimiṣṭāṃ karomyaham|
ekāṃśena viśuddhādhvarūpā varte'hamañjasā || 5 ||
vajraratnaprabhā yadvatparisphurati sarvataḥ|
evaṃ śuddhamayo mārgo mama sphurati sarvataḥ || 6 ||
ameghākāśasaṃkāśānniṣpandodadhirūpataḥ|
mama jñānaghanādrūpācchuddhā sṛṣṭiḥ pravartate || 7 ||
nirvyāpāraṃ sadānandaṃ śuddhaṃ sarvātmakaṃ param|
vyajyate prathamaṃ jñānaṃ sa saṃkarṣaṇa ucyate || 8 ||
hetvantarānapekṣaṃ yat svātantryaṃ viśvanirmitau|
tadaiśvaryaṃ tadāsīnme pradyumraḥ puruṣottamaḥ || 9 ||
nilīnacitrarūpā yā sarvatra samavasthitā|
avyāhatāsīcchaktirme so'niruddhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 10 ||
sṛṣṭisthityantakartāro vijñānaiśvaryaśaktayaḥ|
mama rūpamamī devāḥ puruṣāḥ puṣkarekṣaṇāḥ || 11 ||
atarahgārṇavābhāsamastāmbhodāmbaropamam |
rūpaṃ sisṛkṣamāṇāyā vāsudevo mamādimam || 12 ||
jñānaśaktibalaiśvaryavīryatejāṃsyaśeṣataḥ|
unmiṣanti yadā tulyaṃ vāsudevastadocyate || 13 ||
teṣāṃ jñānabalonmeṣe saṃkarṣaṇa udīryate|
bibharti sakalaṃ viśvaṃ tilakālakavatsvataḥ || 14 ||
balamityeva tannāma tato vedāntaśabditam|
vīryaiśvaryasamunmeṣe pradyumnaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 15 ||
vikāraviraho vīryamavikārī tataśca saḥ|
śaktitejaḥsamunmeṣe hyaniruddhaḥ sa īritaḥ || 16 ||
tejastvanyānapekṣatvamaniruddhatvamapyuta |
śāstraṃ saṃkarṣaṇādeva bhāti nirghātaśabdavat || 17 ||
tatkriyā sakalā devātpradyumnāt saṃbhavedyataḥ|
kriyāphalamaśeṣaṃ tadaniruddhātpracakṣate || 18 ||
sṛjate hyaniruddho'tra pradyumnaḥ pāti tatkṛtam|
sṛṣṭaṃ tadrakṣitaṃ cātti sa ca saṃkarṣaṇaḥ prabhuḥ || 19 ||
atrāniruddhasya sṛṣṭikartṛtvaṃ pradyumnasya pālanakartṛtvaṃ cocyate| pūrvaṃ tu dvitīyādhyāye aniruddhasya pālanakartṛtvaṃ pradyumnasya sṛṣṭikartṛtvaṃ coktam| tattu kalpāntareṇeti dhyeyam|
sṛṣṭisthityantakāryeṇa śāstradharmaphalena ca|
anugrahamime devāḥ sadā vidadhate svayam || 20 ||
yadyapyekaguṇonmeṣastathāpyete hi ṣaḍguṇāḥ|
anyūnānadhikāḥ sarve vāsudevātsanātanāt || 21 ||
aṅgapratyaṅgabuddhyādirnaiṣāṃ bhūtamayaḥ smṛtaḥ|
ṣāḍguṇyamaya evaiṣāṃ divyo dehaḥ sanātanaḥ || 22 ||
buddhiratrāntaḥ karaṇam| `naiṣāṃ bhūtamayaḥ' iti vacanena "na bhūtasaṅghasaṃsthāno deho'sya paramātmanaḥ" iti śāntiparvavacanaṃ smāritam|
naivaiṣāṃ vāstavo bhedaścintanīyo divaspate|
tattatkāryaprasiddhyarthaṃ kṛto'sau kalpanāvaśāt || 23 ||
jñānānnānyattathaiśvaryaṃ tasmānnānyā ca śaktikā|
mayaitāḥ kalpitāḥ śakra dhyānaviśrāmabhūmayaḥ || 24 ||
dhyāneti| upāsakānāṃ yathārucyālambanadānāya tatra bhedaparikalpanetyarthaḥ|
vastu pūrvaṃ tato bhāvaḥ paścādarthastataḥ kriyā|
cātūrūpyamidaṃ jñeyaṃ sarvabhāveṣu sarvadā || 25 ||
vāsudevādirūpeṇa caturdhātmānamātmanā|
saṃvibhajyāvatiṣṭhe'haṃ sarvamāvṛtya saṃvidā || 26 ||
vāsudevādayo devāḥ pratyekaṃ tu tridhā tridhā|
keśavādisvarūpeṇa vibhajanti svakaṃ vapuḥ || 27 ||
evaṃ ca keśavādidāmodarāntā dvādaśāpi vyūhāntarabhūtā dhyeyāḥ|
etadvyūhāntaraṃ nāma pañcarātrābhiśabditam|
kāryasya nayane devā dvādaśaite vyavasthitāḥ || 28 ||
vibhorapyaniruddhasya hitāya jagatāṃ hareḥ|
prasaro vibhavo nāma padmanābhādayaḥ smṛtāḥ || 29 ||
āviśyāviśya kurute yatra devanarādikam|
jagaddhitaṃ jagannāthastajjñeyaṃ vibhavāntaram || 30 ||
`devanarādikam' ityanena dattātreyabādarāyaṇādīnāmapi vibhavāntaratvaṃ dhyeyam|
devarṣipitṛsiddhādyaiḥ svayaṃ vā jagatāṃ hite|
nirmitaṃ bhagavadrūpamarcā sā śuddhacinmayī || 31 ||
svayamityanena svayaṃvyaktakṣetrasthārcā gṛhyante|
ityeṣa leśato mārgaḥ śuddhaste saṃpradarśitaḥ|
traiguṇyamaparaṃ mārgaṃ gadantyā me niśāmaya || 32 ||
yatte jñānaṃ purā proktaṃ tatsattvena vivartate|
rajastayā tadaiśvaryaṃ śaktiścāpi tamastayā || 33 ||
prādhānyena rajastatra sṛṣṭau saṃparivartate|
abhitaḥ sattvatamasī guṇau dvau tasya tiṣṭhataḥ || 34 ||
yā sā pūrvaṃ mayā proktā koṭikoṭitamī kalā|
tasyāḥ koṭitamenāhamaṃśena visṛje jagat || 35 ||
sarvasyādyā mahālakṣmīsriguṇāhaṃ maheśvarī|
rajorūpamadhiṣṭhāya sṛṣṭimiṣṭāṃ karomyaham || 36 ||
anena triguṇajagatsṛṣṭikartṛtvadaśāyāṃ mahālakṣmīsamākhyeti jñāyate|
agnīṣomamayau bhāvau divyau srīpuṃsalakṣaṇau|
bibhratī cārusarvāṅgī lokānāṃ hitakāmyayā || 37 ||
caturbhujā viśālākṣī taptakāñcanasaṃnibhā|
mātulaṅgaṃ gadāṃ kheṭaṃ sudhāpātraṃ ca bibhratī || 38 ||
mahālakṣmīḥ samākhyātā sāhaṃ sarvāṅgasundarī|
mahāśrīḥ sā mahālakṣmīścaṇḍā caṇḍī ca caṇḍikā || 39 ||
bhadrakālī tathā bhadrā kālī durgā maheśvarī|
triguṇā bhagavatpatnī tathā bhagavatī parā || 40 ||
etāḥ saṃjñāstathā cānyāstatra me bahudhā smṛtāḥ|
vikārayogādanyāśca tāstā vakṣyāmyaśeṣataḥ || 41 ||
lakṣayāmi jagatsarvaṃ puṇyāpuṇye kṛtākṛte|
mahanīyā ca sarvatra mahālakṣmīḥ prakīrtitā || 42 ||
mahadbhiḥ śrayaṇīyatvānmahāśrīriti gadyate|
caṇḍasya dayitā caṇḍī caṇḍatvāccaṇḍikā matā || 43 ||
kalyāṇarūpā bhadrāsmi kālī ca kalanātsatām|
dviṣatāṃ kālarūpatvādapi kālī prakīrtitā || 44 ||
suhṛdāṃ dviṣatāṃ caiva yugapatsadasadvidheḥ|
bhadrakālī samākhyātā māyāscaryaguṇātmikā || 45 ||
māyāścaryeti| anenāścaryāvahatvaṃ māyāśabdārtha ityuktaṃ bhavati| anena māyāśabdasya mithyārthakalvakalpanaṃ vāryate|
mahattvācca mahāmāyā mohanānmohinī matā|
durgā ca durgamatvena bhaktarakṣāvidherapi || 46 ||
yojanāccaiva yogāhaṃ yogamāyā ca kīrtitā|
māyāyogeti vijñeyā jñānayojanato nṛṇām || 47 ||
jñānayojanata iti| anena "māyā vayunaṃ jñānam" iti yāskavacanāt māyāśabdasya jñānārthakatvamuktaṃ bhavati| "saṃbhavāmyātmamāyayā" ityatra ātmasaṃkalpenetyarthavarṇanamapyetanmūlakameva|
pūrṇaṣāḍguṇyarūpatvātsāhaṃ bhagavatī smṛtā|
bhagavadyajñasaṃyogātpatnī bhagavato'smyaham || 48 ||
yajñasaṃyogāditi| "patyurno yajñasaṃyoge" ityanusāsanasiddhaṃ rūpam| bhagavadyajñasca āśritajanarakṣaṇarūpo jñeyaḥ| tatrāsyāḥ saṃyogaḥ puruṣakāratvādinā|
viśālatvātsmṛtā vyoma pūraṇācca purī smṛtā|
parāvarasvarūpatvāt smṛtā cāhaṃ parāvarā || 49 ||
śakanācchaktiruktāhaṃ rājñyahaṃ rañjanāt sadā|
sadā śāntavikāratvācchāntāhaṃ parikīrtitā || 50 ||
mattaḥ prakriyate viśvaṃ prakṛtiḥ sāsmi kīrtitā|
śrayantī śrayaṇīyāsmi śrṛṇāmi duritaṃ satām || 51 ||
"śri śrayaṇe" "śṝ hiṃsāyām" iti ca dhātuḥ|
śṛṇomi karuṇāṃ vācaṃ śṛṇāmi ca guṇairjagat|
 sarvabhūtānāṃ rame'haṃ puṇyakarmaṇām || 52 ||
"śru śravaṇe" "śṝ prīṇane" "śī svapne" "ram krīḍāyām" iti dhātavo vivakṣitāḥ| śrīnāmni śakāravivaraṇam śaye'ntariti| rephavivaraṇam rame'hamiti|
īḍitā ca sadā devaiḥ śarīraṃ cāsmi vaiṣṇavam|
etānmayi guṇān dṛṣṭvā vedavedāntapāragāḥ || 53 ||
īkāravivaraṇam īḍiteti| "īḍa stutau" iti dhātuḥ| viṣṇoḥ śarīraṃ rūpamityarthaḥ|
guṇayogavidhānajñāḥ śriyaṃ māṃ saṃpracakṣate|
sāhamevaṃvidhā nityā sarvākārā sanātanī || 54 ||
guṇatrayamadhiṣṭhātrī triguṇā parikīrtitā|
guṇavaiṣamyasargāya pravṛttāhaṃ sisṛkṣayā || 55 ||
taptakāñcanavarṇābhā taptakāñcanabhūṣaṇā|
nirālokamimaṃ lokaṃ pūrayāmi svatejasā || 56 ||
śūnyaṃ tadakhilaṃ lokaṃ svena pūrayituṃ purā|
bharāmi tvaparaṃ rūpaṃ tamasā kevalena tu || 57 ||
sā bhinnāñjanasaṃkāśā daṃṣṭrāñcitavarānanā|
viśālalocanā nārī babhūva tanumadhyamā || 58 ||
khaḍgapātraśiraḥkheṭairalaṃkṛtamahābhujā|
kabandhahārā śirasi bibhrāṇāhiśiraḥsrajam || 59 ||
sā māṃ provāca saṃbhūtā tāmasī pramadottamā|
nāma karma ca me mātardehi tubhyaṃ namo namaḥ || 60 ||
śrīḥ
tāmabravaṃ varārohāṃ tāmasīṃ pramadottamām|
dadāmi tava nāmāni yāni karmāṇi tāni te || 61 ||
mahākālī mahāmāyā mahāmārī kṣudhā tṛṣā|
nidrā kṛṣṇā caikavīrā kālarātrirduratyayā || 62 ||
etāni tava nāmāni pratipādyāni nāmabhiḥ|
ebhiḥ karmāṇi te jñātvā yo'dhīte so'śnute sukham || 63 ||
aparyāptamimaṃ sargaṃ manyamānāhamādimam|
sattvonmeṣamayaṃ rūpaṃ bharāmi smendusaṃnibham || 64 ||
akṣamālāṅkuśadharā vīṇāpustakadhāriṇī|
sā babhūva varā nārī nāma karma tadā hyadām || 65 ||
mahāvidyā mahāvāṇī bhāratī vāk sarasvatī|
āryā brāhnī mahādhenurvedagarbhā ca dhīśca gīḥ || 66 ||
nāmānurūpaṃ karma syāt sāttvikyāḥ kāryamadbhutam|
vayaṃ tisro jagaddhātryo mātaraśca prakīrtitāḥ || 67 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre mahālakṣmīsamudbhūtirnāma caturtho'dhyāyaḥ
iti caturtho'dhyāyaḥ

<5>
pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ
yā sāhaṃtā harerādyā sarvākārā sanātanī|
śuddhānandacidākārā sarvataḥ samatāṃ gatā || 1 ||
atra śāntāvasthā| dvitīyaśloke uditāvasthā| tṛtīyaśloke traiguṇyāvasthā mahālakṣmīsamākhyā| caturthe rajaḥpradhānāvasthā mahāśrīḥ mahālakṣmayaparaparyāyā| tamaḥ pradhānatayā pūrvādhyāyoktā mahāmāyā| pañcame pūrvādhyāyoktasattvapradhānā mahāvidyā|
sāhaṃ sisṛkṣayā yuktā svalpālpenātmabindunā|
sṛṣṭiṃ kṛtavatī śuddhāṃ pūrṇaṣāḍguṇyavigrahām || 2 ||
anujjhitasvarūpāhaṃ madīyenālpabindunā|
mahālakṣmīḥ samākyātā traiguṇyaparivartinī || 3 ||
rajaḥpradānā tatrāhaṃ mahāśrīḥ parameśvarī|
madīyaṃ yattamorūpaṃ mahāmāyeti sā smṛtā || 4 ||
madīyaṃ sattvarūpaṃ yanmahāvidyeti sā smṛtā|
ahaṃ ca te ca kāminyau tā vayaṃ tisra ūrjitāḥ || 5 ||
sṛṣṭavatyastu mithunānyanurūpāṇi ca tridhā|
madīyaṃ mithunaṃ yattanmānasaṃ rucirākṛti || 6 ||
hiraṇaygarbhaṃ padmākṣaṃ sundaraṃ kamalāsanam|
pradyumnāṃśādidaṃ viddhi saṃbhūtaṃ mayi mānasam || 7 ||
dhātā vidhirviriñciśca brahmā ca puruṣaḥ smṛtaḥ|
śrīḥ padmā kamalā lakṣmīstatra nārī prakītitā || 8 ||
saṃkarṣaṇāṃśato dvandvaṃ mahāmāyāsamudbhavam|
trinetraṃ cārusarvāṅgaṃ mānasaṃ tatra yaḥ pumān || 9 ||
sa rudraḥ śaṃkaraḥ sthāṇuḥ kapardī ca trilocanaḥ|
tatra trayīśvarā bhāṣā vidyā caivākṣarā tathā || 10 ||
kāmadhenuśca vijñeyā sā strī gauśca sarasvatī|
aniruddhāṃśasaṃbhūtaṃ mahāvidyāsamudbhavam || 11 ||
mithunaṃ mānasaṃ yattatpuruṣastatra keśavaḥ|
viṣṇuḥ kṛṣṇo hṛṣīkeśo vāsudevo janārdanaḥ || 12 ||
umā gaurī satī caṇḍā tatra strī subhagā satī|
brahmaṇastu trayī patnī sā babhūva mamājñayā || 13 ||
rudrasya dīyitā gaurī vāsudevasya cāmbujā|
rajasastamasaścaiva sattvasya ca vivartanam || 14 ||
ādyaṃ parva tadetatte kathitaṃ mithunatrayam|
madyamaṃ parva vakṣyāmi guṇānāṃ tadidaṃ śṛṇu || 15 ||
ādyaṃ parveti| rājasyā lakṣmyāḥ pradyumnāṃśāt mānasī sṛṣṭiḥ viriñciḥ, śrīśca| tāmasyā mahāmāyāyāḥ saṃkarṣaṇāṃśāt mānasī sṛṣṭiḥ rudraḥ, trayī ca| sāttvikyā mahāvidyāyā aniruddhāṃśāt mānasī sṛṣṭiḥ viṣṇuḥ, gaurī ca| tataḥ rājasasya viriñceḥ tāmasyā trayyā, tāmasasya rudrasya sāttvikyā gauryā, sāttvikasya viṣṇoḥ rājasyā śriyā ceti dāṃpatyaparikalpanarūpamityarthaḥ| madhyamaṃ parveti| dhātrā aṇḍasṛṣṭiḥ| rudreṇa tadbhedanam| tatra punaśca dhātrā aṇḍamadhye pradhānasṛṣṭiḥ| viṣṇunā tatparipālanarūpamityarthaḥ|
bhāṣayā saha saṃbhūya viriñco'ṇḍamajījanat|
madājñayā bibhedaitatsa gauryā saha śaṃkaraḥ || 16 ||
aṇḍamadhye pradānaṃ yatkāryamāsīttu vedhasaḥ|
tadetatpālayāmāsa padmayā saha keśavaḥ || 17 ||
tadetanmadhyamaṃ parva guṇānāṃ parikīrtitam|
tṛtīyaṃ parva vakṣyāmi tadihaikamanāḥ śṛṇu || 18 ||
tṛtīyaṃ parveti| viṣṇornābhisaroruhāt hiraṇyagarbhasya trayyā saha prādurbhāvaḥ| nābhipadmaṃ tadudbhūtaṃ dvandvaṃ ceti trayaṃ samuditaṃ mahaditi kathyate| tasmādahaṃkārādikrameṇa prapañcasṛṣṭirūpa mityarthaḥ|
aṇḍamadhye pradhānaṃ hi yattatsadasadātmakam|
traiguṇyaṃ prakṛtirvyoma svabhāvo yonirakṣaram || 19 ||
tadetatsalilīkṛtya tattvamavyaktasaṃjñakam|
hṛṣīkeśaḥ sa bhagavān padmayā saha vidyayā || 20 ||
apsu saṃśayanaṃ cakre nidrāyogamupāgataḥ|
yā sā proktā mahākālī sā nidrā tāmasī hyabhūt || 21 ||
śayānasya tadā padmamabhūnnābhyāṃ puraṃdara|
tatkālamayamākhyātaṃ paṅkajaṃ yadapaṅkajam || 22 ||
jalādhikaraṇaṃ padmamādhāraḥ puṣkaraṃ tathā||
cakraṃ ca puṇḍarīkaṃ cetyevaṃ nāmāni tasya tu || 23 ||
śakraḥ
cidacittattvamākhyātaṃ cetanaścitprakītitaḥ|
acit traiguṇyamityuktaṃ kīdṛk kālo'paraḥ smṛtaḥ || 24 ||
śrīḥ
acidaṃśo'paraḥ kālasraiguṇyamaparaṃ smṛtam|
balādikaṃ tu yatpūrvaṃ ṣāḍguṇye trikamīritam || 25 ||
trikamiti| balaiśvaryavīryarūpamityarthaḥ|
tadetatkālarūpeṇa sṛṣṭau saṃparivartate|
svataścāpariṇāmīdaṃ traiguṇyaṃ pariṇāmi tat || 26 ||
kālakālyātmakaṃ dvandvamacidetatprakīrtitam|
sṛjantyā vividhān bhāvānmama devyā mahāśriyaḥ || 27 ||
kālaḥ kṣaṇādiḥ| kālyaṃ triguṇaṃ tadvikārajātaṃ ca|
kālo'yaṃ karaṇatvena vartate manmayaḥ sadā|
tasmātkālamayātpadmādviṣṇunābhisamudbhavāt || 28 ||
brahmā vedamayo jajñe sa trayyā saha vīryavān|
hiraṇyagarbha ukto yaḥ pūrvaṃ lakṣmīsamudbhavaḥ || 29 ||
mahākālīsamudbhatā yā sā nārī trayī smṛtā|
tadetanmithunaṃ jajñe viṣṇornābhisaroruhāt || 30 ||
padmaṃ padmodbhavadvandvaṃ tadetat tritayaṃ saha|
mahāṃstāmasa ākyāto vikāraḥ pūrvakairbudhaiḥ || 31 ||
padmodbhavadvandvaṃ hiraṇyagarbhastrayī ca|
prāṇo hiraṇyagarbhaśca buddhiśceti tridhā bhidā|
padmapuṃsrīsamālambānmahattvaṃ tasya śabdyate || 32 ||
prāṇaḥ hiraṇyagarbhaḥ buddhiriti mahato bhedāḥ|
guṇaḥ prāṇasya tu spando buddheradyavasāyatā|
dharmādikamadharmādyaṃ dvayaṃ puṃso guṇo mataḥ || 33 ||
puṃsa iti| hiraṇyagarbhasyetyarthaḥ|
dharmo jñānaṃ ca vairāgyamaiśvaryaṃ ceti varṇitaḥ|
dharmādiko guṇo yasmādadharmādyāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 34 ||
yasmāditi| dharmādīnāṃ viparyāsā adharmaḥ, ajñānam, avairāgyam, anaiśvaryaṃ ceti jñeyā ityartaḥ|
mahāntamāviśantyenaṃ prerayāmi svasṛṣṭaye|
preryamāṇāttatastasmādahaṃkāraśca jajñivān || 35 ||
pūrvaṃ yaḥ śaṃkaraḥ prokto mahāmāyāsamudbhavaḥ|
yā patnī tasya gaurī sā jajñe'bhimatiratra tu || 36 ||
ahaṃkārasyaivābhimatiriti guṇaḥ ākhyāntaraṃ ca|
āviśyāmumahaṃkāraṃ sṛṣṭaye prerayāmyaham|
sa babhūva tridhā pūrvaṃ guṇavyatikarāttadā || 37 ||
tāmasastatra bhūtādistasya sarvamidaṃ śrṛṇu|
bhūtādeḥ śabdatanmātraṃ tanmātrācchabdasaṃbhavaḥ || 38 ||
tāmasāhaṃkārasya bhūtādiriti, sāttvikāhaṃkārasya vaikārika iti, rājasāhaṃkārasya taijasa iti ca nāmāntaraṃ bodhyam|
matpreritācchabdamātrātsparśamātraṃ babhūva ha|
sparśastu sparśatanmātrāttanmātrātpreritānmayā || 39 ||
tadāsīdrūpatanmātraṃ tasmācca preritānmayā|
rūpamāvirbabhūvādyaṃ rasamātraṃ tataḥ param || 40 ||
rasamātrānmayā kṣiptāttasmājjajñe rasastataḥ|
gandhatanmātramapyāsīttasmācca preritānmayā || 41 ||
śuddho gandhaḥ samudbhūta itīyaṃ bhautikī bhidā|
mātrāṇi sūkṣmabhūtāni sthūlabhūtāni cāpare || 42 ||
śabdādayaḥ samākhyātā guṇāḥ śabdādayastu ye|
sthūlabhūtavisargāste nānye śabdādayo guṇāḥ || 43 ||
śāntatvaṃ caiva ghoratvaṃ mūḍhatvaṃ ceti tat tridhā|
sattvādyunmeṣarūpāṇi tāni sūkṣmeṣu santi na || 44 ||
tena tanmātratā teṣāṃ sūkṣmāṇāṃ parikīrtitā|
sukhaduḥkhādidāyitvāt sthūlatvamitaratra tu || 45 ||
sthūlānāmeva bhūtānāṃ tridhāvasthā prakīrtitā|
sūkṣmāsca pitṛjāścaiva prabhūtā iti bhedataḥ || 46 ||
ghaṭādyā vividhā bāhyāḥ prabhūtā iti śabdyate|
śuklaśoṇitasaṃbhūtā viśeṣāḥ pitṛjāḥ smṛtāḥ || 47 ||
sūkṣmāstu pañcabhūtāḥ syuḥ sūkṣmadehavyapāśrayāḥ|
sargo bhūtādijo hyevaṃ kramaśaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 48 ||
śabdatanmātrāt śabdaḥ sparśatanmātraṃ ca jātam| sparśatanmātrāt sparśaḥ rūpatanmātraṃ ca jātam| rupatanmātrāt rūpaṃ rasatanmātraṃ ca jātam| rasatanmātrāt rasaḥ gandhatanmātraṃ ca jātam| gandhatanmātrāt gandho jāta ityatratyā prakriyā| sāṃkhyāstu bhūtādereva sarveṣāṃ tanmātrāṇāmutpattiṃ vadanti| aupaniṣadaprakriyā tu bhūtādeḥ śabdatanmātram, tasmādākāśaḥ, tasmāt sparśatanmātram, tasmādvāyuḥ; tasmāt rūpatanmātram, tasmādagniḥ, tasmāt rasatanmātram, tasmāt jalam, tasmāt gandhatanmātram, tasmāt pṛthivī jāteti|
ahaṃkārasya yāvaṃśau rajaḥsattvasamāśrayau|
vaikārika iti proktaḥ sāttvikoṃ'śastayoḥ paraḥ || 49 ||
taijasaḥ kathitaḥ sadbhistayoḥ sṛṣṭimimāṃ śrṛṇu|
vaikārikādahaṃkārādāsīcchrotrādidhīndriyam || 50 ||
karmendriyaṃ ca vāgādi taijasātsaṃpravartate|
ubhayasmāttataścāsīd buddhikarmendriyaṃ manaḥ || 51 ||
vaikārikāt jñānendriyāṇāṃ, taijasāt karmendriyāṇāṃ cotpattiriti bhedaparikalpanamatra| aupaniṣadāstu sarveṣāmapīndriyāṇāṃ vaikārikādutpattimācakṣate| tathā manasa ubhayendriyatvaṃ sāṃkhyamatarītyā| aupaniṣadamate tu jñānendriyatvameva.
śrotraṃ tvak caiva cakṣuśca jihvā ghrāṇaṃ ca pañcamam|
buddhīndriyāṇi pañcāhuḥ śaktireṣā madātmikā || 52 ||
vāk ca hastau ca pādau ca tathopasthaṃ ca pāyu ca|
karmendriyāṇi pañcāhuḥ śaktireṣā madātmikā || 53 ||
yā sā vijñānaśaktirme pāraṃparyakramāgatā|
buddhīndriyāṇyadhiṣṭhāya viṣayeṣu pravartate || 54 ||
kriyāśaktisca yā sā me pāraṃparyakramāgatā|
karmendriyāṇyadhiṣṭhāya kartavyeṣu pravartate || 55 ||
śrotrasya viṣayaḥ śabdaḥ śravaṇaṃ ca kriyā matā|
tvacaśca viṣyaḥ sparśaḥ sparśanaṃ ca kriyā matā || 56 ||
cakṣuṣo viṣayo rūpaṃ darśanaṃ ca kriyā matā|
jihvāyā viṣayo rasyo rasanaṃ ca kriyā matā || 57 ||
ghrāṇasya viṣayo gandha āghrāṇaṃ ca kriyā matā|
vṛttayo viṣayeṣvasya śrotrādeḥ śravaṇādayaḥ || 58 ||
ālocanāni kathyante dharmimātragrahaśca saḥ|
dik ca vidyuttathā sūryaḥ somo vasumatī tathā || 59 ||
ālocanaṃ nāma śabdādidharmāṇāṃ sphuṭagrahaṇamantarā vastumātragrahaṇam| tadapyasphuṭameva| āhuśca "asti hyālocanaṃ jñānaṃ prathamaṃ nirvikalpakam| bālamūkādivijñānasadṛśaṃ mugdhavastukam||" iti|
adidaivamiti proktaṃ kramācchrotrādipañcake|
adhibhūtamiti proktaḥ śabdādyo viṣayaḥ kramāt || 60 ||
śrotrādipañcakaṃ tvetadadhyātmaṃ parikīrtitam|
śrotrādeḥ sāttvikātsṛṣṭirviyadādivyapekṣayā || 61 ||
tena bhautikamityuktaṃ kramācchrotrādipañcakam|
vācastu viṣayaḥ śabdo vacanaṃ ca kriyā matā || 62 ||
hastendriyasya cādeyamādānaṃ ca kriyā matā|
pādendriyasya gantavyaṃ gamanaṃ ca kriyā matā || 63 ||
upasthasya tadānandyamānandaśca kriyā matā|
visṛjyaṃ viṣayaḥ pāyorvisargaśca kriyā matā || 64 ||
hastādikaṃ catuṣkaṃ yattatpañcaviṣayātmakam|
agnirindraśca viṣṇuśca tathaivādyaḥ prajāpatiḥ || 65 ||
mitraśceti kramājjñeyā adidevā vicakṣaṇaiḥ|
śabdaḥ pañcātmakaṃ caiva vāgāderviṣayo hi yaḥ || 66 ||
so'dhibhūta iti prokto vāgādyadhyātmamucyate|
manastu sahakāryasminnubhayatrāpi pañcake || 67 ||
jñānendriyagaṇaiścaitadvikalpaṃ tanute manaḥ|
vikalpo vividhā klṛptistacca proktaṃ viśeṣaṇam || 68 ||
dharmeṇa saha saṃbandho dharmiṇaśca sa ucyate|
vikalpaḥ pañcadhā jñeyo dravyakarmaguṇādibhiḥ || 69 ||
daṇḍīti dravyasaṃyogācchuklo guṇasamanvayāt|
gacchatīti kriyāyogātpumān sāmānyasaṃsthiteḥ || 70 ||
ḍitthaḥ śabdasamāyogāditīyaṃ pañcadhā sthitiḥ|
karmendriyagaṇaiścaitatsaṃkalpaṃ tanute manaḥ || 71 ||
audāsīnyacyutiryā sā saṃkalpodyoganāmikā|
ahaṃkāreṇa caitasminrubhayatra gaṇe sthitiḥ || 72 ||
jñānendriyagaṇe so'yamabhimānena vartate|
deśakālānvayo jñāturabhimānaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 73 ||
mamādya purato bhātītyevaṃ vastu pratīyate|
karmendriyagaṇe tveṣa saṃrambheṇa pravartate || 74 ||
saṃkalpapūrvarūpastu saṃrambhaḥ parikīrtitaḥ|
buddhiradhyavasāyena jñānendriyagaṇe sthitā || 75 ||
buddhiradhyavasāyārthāvadhāraṇamudīryate|
avadhāraṇamarthānāṃ niścayaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 76 ||
karmendriyagaṇe buddhiḥ prayatnena pravartate|
trayodaśavidhaṃ jñeyaṃ tadetatkaraṇaṃ budhaiḥ || 77 ||
bāhyaṃ daśavidhaṃ jñeyaṃ tridhāntaḥkaraṇaṃ smṛtam|
trayoviṃśatirete tu vikārāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 78 ||
karaṇāni daśa trīṇi sūkṣmāṃśāḥ sthūlasaṃbhavāḥ|
etatsṛkṣmaśarīraṃ tu virājaḥ parikīrtitam || 79 ||
vyaṣṭayaḥ sūkṣmadehāśca pratijīvaṃ vyavasthitāḥ||
apavarge nivartante jīvebhyaste svayonijāḥ || 80 ||
anyonyānugraheṇaite trayoviṃśatirutthitāḥ|
mahadādyā viśeṣāntā hyaṇḍamutpādayanti te || 81 ||
tadaṇḍamabhavaddhaimaṃ sahasrāṃśusamaprabham|
tasmin prajāpatirjajñe virāḍ devaścaturmukhaḥ || 82 ||
atra pūrvokta eva sṛṣṭikramo manvādisṛṣṭyā saha saṃkalayyocyate|
virājaśca manurjajñe manoste mānavāḥ smṛtāḥ|
marīcipramukhāstebhyo jagadetaccarācaram || 83 ||
prakāro'yaṃ mamodyatyā leśataste pradarśitaḥ|
svataḥ śuddhāpi cicchaktiḥ saṃviddhānādyavidyayā || 84 ||
duḥkhaṃ janmajarādyutthaṃ tatrasthā pratipadyate|
śuddhavijñānasaṃbandhācchuddhakarmasamanvayāt|
yadā dhunotyavidyāṃ tāṃ tadā sānandamaśnute || 85 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre prākṛtasṛṣṭiprakāśo nāma pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ
iti pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ

<6>
ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ
pūrṇastimitaṣāḍguṇyacidānandamahodadheḥ|
ahaṃtāhaṃ harerādyā nistaraṅgārṇavākṛteḥ || 1 ||
sāhamevaṃvidhā śuddhā vkaciducchūnatāṃ gatā|
sisṛkṣālakṣaṇā devī svatantrā saccidātmikā || 2 ||
ṣaṭkośatāṃ samāpadye sattāhaṃ vaiṣṇavī parā|
śaktirmāyā prasūtiśca prakṛtisriguṇātmikā || 3 ||
śaktimāyāprasūtiprakṛtibrahmāṇḍajīvadehākhyeṣu ṣaṭsu kośeṣu śaktikośaḥ suddhamārgapravartakaḥ| tatraiva saṃkarṣaṇādīnāṃ trayāṇāmāvirbhāvaḥ|
brahmāṇḍaṃ jīvaladehaścetyete ṣaṭkośasaṃjñitāḥ|
sisṛkṣā yā parā viṣṇorahaṃtāyāḥ samudgatā || 4 ||
śaktiḥ sā prathamaḥ kośaḥ śuddhamārgapravartanī|
kośaḥ kulāyaparyāyaḥ śarīrāparanāmavān || 5 ||
śuddhe'smin prathame kośe prathamonmeṣalakṣaṇe|
ahaṃmānī paro hyāsīddevaḥ saṃkarṣaṇaḥ prabhuḥ || 6 ||
tilakālakavattatra vikāro masṛṇaḥ sthitaḥ|
tasyāhaṃtā tu yā devī sāhaṃ sāṃkarṣaṇī parā || 7 ||
śrīrityeva samākhyātā vijñānabalaśālinī|
yastasyā me samunmeṣaḥ pradyumnaḥ sa tu kīrtyate || 8 ||
saṃkarṣaṇasya mahiṣī śrīḥ| pradyumnasya sarasvatī| aniruddhasya ratiḥ| vāsudevasya śāntiḥ|
saṃkarṣaṇasya devasya śaktikośābhimāninaḥ|
buddhitve vartate devaḥ pradyumnaḥ puruṣottamaḥ || 9 ||
bhoktṛbhogyasamaṣṭistu nilīnā tatra tiṣṭhati|
manobhūtasya devasya tasyāhaṃtā tu yā smṛtā || 10 ||
sāhaṃ sarasvatī nāma vīryaiśvaryavivartinī|
yo me tasyāḥ samunmeṣaḥ so'niruddhaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 11 ||
tasya saṃkarṣaṇasyāhamahaṃkāravidhau sthitā|
saṃkarṣaṇādayo devāstraya ete purātanāḥ || 12 ||
jīvo buddhirahaṃkāra iti nāmnā prakīrtitāḥ|
naivaite prākṛtā devāḥ kiṃtu śuddhacidātmakāḥ || 13 ||
ādivyūhasya devasya vāsudevasya dīvyataḥ|
tattatkāryakaratvena tattannāmnā nirūpitāḥ || 14 ||
sarve te ṣaḍguṇāḥ proktāḥ sarve te puruṣottamāḥ|
pūrṇastimitaṣāḍguṇyasadānandamahodadheḥ || 15 ||
ṣaṇṇāṃ yugapadunmeṣo guṇānāṃ kāryavattayā|
yo'bhūtsa vāsudevastu vyūhaḥ prathamakalpitaḥ || 16 ||
tasya śāntirahaṃtā tu sāhaṃ śaktiḥ prakīrtitā|
śaktikośasthitā devāḥ sūyante yatra cintitāḥ || 17 ||
aniruddhasya yāhaṃtā ratirityeva saṃjñitā|
saiva devī mahālakṣmīrmāyākośaḥ sa ucyate || 18 ||
māyākośamārabhyāśuddhasṛṣṭiḥ| mahālakṣmyā rājasatvaṃ prapañcasṛṣṭinidānatvaṃ ca pūrvamuktam|
mahālakṣmyā ya unmeṣo māyāyā guṇasaṃjñitaḥ|
mahākālīmahāvidyādvayaṃ saṃparikīrtyate || 19 ||
mahālakṣmīmahāmāyāmahāvidyāmayo mahān|
prasūtirnāma kośo me tṛtīyaḥ paripaṭhyate || 20 ||
prasūtikośaḥ rājasyā mahālakṣmyāḥ, tāmasyā mahāmāyāyāḥ, sāttvikyā mahāvidyāyāśca samavāyarūpaḥ|
trīṇyatra mithunānyāsan yāni pūrvoditāni te|
pradhānaṃ salilīkṛtya yacchete puruṣottamaḥ || 21 ||
trīṇi mithunāni pañcamādhyāye saptamādibhiḥ saptabhiḥ ślokairvarṇitāni| pradhānameva prakṛtikośa ityucyate| etāsāṃ māyāprasūtiprakṛtīnāṃ tamo'vyaktaprakṛtināmabhirupaniṣatsu vyavahāraḥ|
sā proktā prakṛtiryonirguṇasāmyasvarūpiṇī|
viriñco'janayadyadvai pūrvamaṇḍaṃ svamātmani || 22 ||
tadeke prakṛtiṃ prāhustattvaśāsraviśāradāḥ|
mahadādyaiḥ pṛthivyantairaṇḍaṃ yannirmitaṃ saha || 23 ||
tadbrahmāṇḍamiti proktaṃ yatra brahmā virāḍabhūt|
aṅgapratyaṅgayuktaṃ yaccharīraṃ jīvināmiha || 24 ||
eṣā kośavidhā ṣaṣṭhī kramaśastanutāṃ gatā|
avarohāḥ ṣaḍete me pūrṇāyāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 25 ||
ādye kośe svayaṃ devasridhaivāhaṃtayā sthitaḥ|
pañcasvanyeṣu kośeṣu jīvā nānāvidhāḥ sthitāḥ || 26 ||
śubhāśubhavibhāgotthāṃ bhajante vividhāṃ daśām|
divyāstisrasrayastāsāṃ mithunāni ca yāni tu || 27 ||
aṇḍamadhye'vatārāśca tāsāṃ teṣāṃ ca ye smṛtāḥ|
svātantryanirmitāstvete naiva karmavaśānugāḥ || 28 ||
aprākṛtāśca te dehā ubhayeṣāṃ prakīrtitāḥ|
anye pañcasu kośeṣu devādyāḥ sthāvarāntimāḥ || 29 ||
nānāsthānajuṣo jīvāḥ karmabhiḥ saṃsaranti ye|
adhikārakṣayaṃ nītvā śubhapākavaśādime || 30 ||
adhikāreti "yāvadadhikāramavasthitirādhikārikāṇām" iti bādarāyaṇasūtram|
saṃprāpya jñānabhūyastvaṃ yogakṣapitakalmaṣāḥ|
ārohanti śanaiḥ kośānārūḍhā na patanti te || 31 ||
satyalokātprabhṛtyete yāṃ bhūmimadhirohitāḥ|
punaste na nivartante tiṣṭhantyūrdhvaṃ vrajanti te || 32 ||
śakraḥ
kṣīrodasaṃbhave devi padmanābhakuṭumbini|
jīvaḥ ko nāma tad brūhi namaste padmasaṃbhave || 33 ||
śrīḥ
pūrṇāhaṃtā harerādyā sāhaṃ sarveśvarī parā|
tasyāḥ smṛtāścatasro me daśāsridaśapuṃgava || 34 ||
pramāteti vidhā tvekā tadantaḥkaraṇaṃ parā|
bahiḥ karaṇamanyā ca caturthī bhāvabhūmikā || 35 ||
pramātā jīvaḥ| antaḥkaraṇaṃ manobuddhyahaṃkārarūpeṇa trividham| bahiḥ karaṇaṃ jñānendriyakarmendriyarūpeṇa daśavidham| bhāvabhūmikā prameyaprapañcajātam|
pramātā cetanaḥ prokto matsaṃkocaḥ sa ucyate|
ahaṃ hi deśakālādyairaparicchedamīyuṣī || 36 ||
svātantryādeva saṃkocaṃ bhajāmyajahatī svatām|
prathamastatra saṃkocaḥ pramāteti prakīrtyate || 37 ||
cidātmani yathā viśvaṃ mayi līnamavasthitam|
pramātari tathaivaitaddarpaṇodaraśailavat || 38 ||
aikarūpyaṃ dvirūpatvaṃ trirūpatvaṃ caturbhidām|
saptapañcakarūpatvaṃ pramātā yatprapadyate || 39 ||
catvāro bhedāḥ jāgradādyavasthāḥ|
prakāśenātmano hyeko grāhyagrāhakatāvaśāt|
dvairūpyaṃ tattrirūpatvaṃ jñānākārakriyātmanā || 40 ||
saptapañcakarūpatvaṃ tattattattvasthitau sthitam|
śakraḥ
kāni tattvāni padmākṣi kati kīdṛgvidhāni ca || 41 ||
etatpṛṣṭā mayā brūhi namaste sindhusaṃbhave|
śrīḥ
sthūlasūkṣmavibhedena bhūtāni daśa khāni ca || 42 ||
jñānakarmavibhedena trīṇyantaḥkaraṇāni ca|
prakṛtiśca prasūtiśca māyā sattvaṃ rajastamaḥ || 43 ||
kālaśca niyatiḥ śaktiḥ puruṣaḥ paramaṃ nabhaḥ|
bhagavāniti tattvāni sāttvatāḥ samadhīyate || 44 ||
śakraḥ
mayā śrutāni tattvāni tvadvaktrasarasīruhāt|
vyācakṣvaitāni me devi namaste sarasīruhe || 45 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre ṣaṭkośaprakāśo nāma ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ
iti ṣaṣṭho'dhyāyaḥ

<7>
saptamo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ
vyācakṣe'haṃ tataḥ śakra kramaśastattvapaddhatim|
śuddhāśuddhavimiśreyaṃ tattvapaddhatirucyate || 1 ||
nirambhodāmbarābhāso niṣpandodadhisaṃnibhaḥ|
svacchasvacchandacaitanyasadānandamahodadhiḥ || 2 ||
pūrvādhyāyoktāni pañcatriṃśat tattvāni vyutkrameṇa nirūpyante| tatrādāvatra bhagavattattvanirūpaṇam| bhagavattattvaṃ ca śāntāvasthaḥ paravāsudevaḥ, uditāvasthā vyūhāḥ, teṣāmapṛthaksiddhā devī ceti|
ākāradeśakālādiparicchedavivarjitaḥ|
bhagavāniti vijñeyaḥ paramātmā sanātanaḥ || 3 ||
tasyāhaṃtā parā tādṛgbhagavattā sanātanī|
nārāyaṇī parā sūkṣmā nirvikalpā nirañjana || 4 ||
jñānaśaktibalaiśvaryavīryatejomahodadhiḥ|
ṣaṇṇāṃ yugapadunmeṣo guṇānāṃ prathamo hi yaḥ || 5 ||
bhavadbhāvātmakatvena dvidhā sa vyapadiśyate|
bhavaṃstu vāsudevo'tra bhāvo'smin vāsudevatā || 6 ||
śāntirnāmnā samākhyātā sāhaṃ devī sanātanī|
saṃkarṣaṇādayo vyūhāḥ sāhaṃtāḥ prāṅ nirūpitāḥ || 7 ||
trayaśca cāturātmyaṃ taccatvāro'mī sureśvara|
etāvadbhagavadvācyaṃ nistattvaṃ tattvamuttamam || 8 ||
nistattvam; tattvāntarebhyo niṣkrāntam, atiśayitamityarthaḥ| tadevāha tattvamuttamamiti|
nabhastu paramaṃ vyoma paramākāśaśabditam|
yatra devo mayā sārdhaṃ vibhajyātmānamātmanā || 9 ||
krīḍate ramayā viṣṇuḥ paramātmā sanātanaḥ|
ṣāḍguṇyasya samunmeṣaḥ sa deśaḥ paramāmbaram || 10 ||
paravyomnaḥ ṣāḍguṇyasamunmeṣarūpatvakathanenājaḍatvamabhipretam; jaḍatvapakṣo'pyasti pareṣām|
puruṣo bhoktṛkūṭasthaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvatomukhaḥ|
aṃśataḥ prasarantyasmātsarve jīvāḥ sanātanāḥ || 11 ||
bhoktṛkūṭastho jīvasamaṣṭirūpaḥ puruṣo hitaṇyagarbhaḥ|
pralaye tvapiyantyenaṃ karmātmāno naraṃ param|
iyaṃ mātṛdaśā sā me yā te pūrvaṃ mayoditā || 12 ||
mahālakṣmīḥ samākhyātā śaktitattvaṃ manīṣibhiḥ|
niyatistu mahāvidyā kālaḥ kālī prakīrtitā || 13 ||
sattvaṃ rajastamaśceti guṇatrayamudāhṛtam|
sukharūpaṃ smṛtaṃ sattvaṃ svacchaṃ jñānakaraṃ laghu || 14 ||
duḥkharūpaṃ rajo jñeyaṃ calaṃ raktaṃ pravartakam|
moharūpaṃ tamo jñeyaṃ guru kṛṣṇaṃ niyāmakam || 15 ||
māyā caiva prasūtiśca prakṛtiśceti vāsava|
purastādvyākṛtaṃ tubhyaṃ tadetatprakṛtitrikam || 16 ||
bhūtāni daśasaṃkhyāni tathā khāni trayodaśa|
trayoviṃśatirapyete suspaṣṭaṃ vyākṛtā purā || 17 ||
tadayaṃ mama saṃkocaḥ pramātā śuddhacinmayaḥ|
svāntaḥsphuritatattvaughaḥ sthito darpaṇavatsadā || 18 ||
evaṃ ṣaṣṭhādhyāye 42, 43, 44 tamaiḥ ślokaiḥ prastutāni pañcatriṃśataṃ tattvāni nirūpya, tatraiva 35 tame śloke prastutā ātmanaścatasro daśā nirūpayati tadayamityārabhya yāvadadhyāyaparisamāpti|
cātūrūpyaṃ tu yattasya tadihaikamanāḥ śṛṇu|
ādyaṃ śūnyamayo mātā mūrchādau parikītitaḥ || 19 ||
tataḥ prāṇamayo mātā suṣuptau parikīrtitaḥ|
prāṇā eva pratāyante suṣuptau puruṣasya tu || 20 ||
mūrchāviṣopaghātādau prāṇo'pi vinivartate|
kevalaṃ svātmasattaiva tataḥ śūnyastadā pumān || 21 ||
tṛtīyo'ṣṭapurīmātraḥ svapne mātā prakīrtitaḥ|
prāṇā bhūtāni karmāṇi karaṇāni trayo guṇāḥ || 22 ||
aṣṭapurīmātraḥ| mātraśabdaḥ pramāṇārthakaḥ; kevalārthako vā| bāhyaceṣṭārahita ityarthaḥ| aṣṭapuryaścānantaraśloke vakṣyante|
prāgvāsanā avidyā ca liṅgaṃ puryaṣṭakaṃ smṛtam|
svapne'ntaḥkaraṇenaiva svairaṃ hi parivartate || 23 ||
liṅgaṃ sūkṣmaśarīram|
ceṣṭamānaḥ svadehena dehī jāgraddaśāṃ gataḥ|
cātūrūpyamidaṃ puṃsasrairūpyamapi me śṛṇu || 24 ||
jñānakriyāsvarūpāṇāṃ saṃkocasrividhastu yaḥ|
tasya taddhi trirūpatvaṃ tasya vyākhyāmimāṃ śṛṇu || 25 ||
māyayā jñānasaṃkoca ānaiśvaryātkriyāvyayaḥ|
aśakteraṇutā rūpe tridhaiva vyapadiśyate || 26 ||
kriyāvyayaḥ abhīpsitakriyākaraṇāsāmarthyam| ānaiśvaryamityatra anīśvarasya bhāva ityarthe ṣyañi ubhayapadavṛddhiḥ| svarūpasaṃkocenāṇurūpa ityuktyā jīvasya svābhāvikaṃ vibhurūpatvamiti na mantavyam, tasyāṇusvarūpatvasyānekapramāṇasiddhatvāt| tasmādatra viśiṣyāṇurūpatvavarṇanamasarvraśaktatvāsarvajñatvādiparaṃ veditavyam| tadevāśakterityanena vivriyate|
aṇuḥ kiṃcitkaraścaiva kiṃcijjñaścāyamityuta|
dvairūpyamaikarūpyaṃ ca pūrvameva nirūpitam || 27 ||
evaṃ mātṛdaśā me'dya saviśeṣā prakīrtitā|
āntaḥ karaṇikīṃ caiva daśāṃ śakrādya me śṛṇu || 28 ||
pūrvādyāye antaḥkaraṇadaśā dvitīyā nirdiṣṭā| sātra vivriyate|
svacchandā saṃvidevāhaṃ svataścetanatāṃ gatā|
hitvā cetanatāṃ tāṃ cāpyavarūḍhā tataḥ kramāt || 29 ||
caityasaṃkocanī cittamantaḥ karaṇamīritam|
manobuddhirahaṃkāra ityetat tritayaṃ ca tat || 30 ||
caityam; cito bhāvaḥ, caitanyamityarthaḥ|
vikalpo'dhyavasāyaścāpyabhimānaśca vṛttayaḥ|
mano vikalpayatyarthamahaṃkāro'bhimanyate || 31 ||
vikalpaḥ; vividhaḥ kalpaḥ, anirṇayātmakaṃ jñānam| abhimanyate; svakīyatvena budhyate|
adhyavasyati buddhiśca cetanādhiṣṭhitā sadā|
buddhiradhyātmamityuktā nirṇayo'pyadhibhūtakam || 32 ||
adhyavasyati; niśvinoti|
buddhidarpaṇasaṃlīnaḥ kṣetrajñaścādhidaivatam|
ahaṃkṛtistathādhyātmamabhimāno'dhibhūtakam || 33 ||
adhidaivamatho rudro mano'dhyātmaṃ prakīrtitam|
vikalpo'pyadhibhūtastu candramā adhidaivatam || 34 ||
prāṇasaṃrambhasaṃkalpā guṇā eṣāṃ kriyāvidhau|
prāṇaḥ prayatna ityuktaḥ saṃrambho garva ucyate || 35 ||
phalasvāmyasvarūpaśca garvaḥ saṃrambha ucyate|
audāsīnyacyutiḥ proktaḥ saṃkalpo mānaso budhaiḥ || 36 ||
vyākhyāteyaṃ dvitīyā me hyāntaḥkaraṇikī daśā|
pracyavantī tato rūpādāntaḥ karaṇikādaham || 37 ||
styānatāṃ kramaśaḥ prāptā bahiṣkaraṇasaṃjñitā|
karaṇāni tu bāhyāni vyākhyātāni mayā purā || 38 ||
jñānendriyapravṛttau tu manaādi pravartate|
cakṣurālokayatyarthaṃ vikalpayati tanmanaḥ || 39 ||
ālokanavikalpasthamahaṃkāro'bhimanyate|
adhyavasya tato buddhiḥ kṣetrajñāya prayacchati || 40 ||
adhyavasya; adhyavasāya, adhyavasāyaṃ kṛtvetyarthaḥ|
karmendriyapravṛttau tu viparyastaḥ kramaḥ smṛtaḥ|
saṃkalpādeḥ parācīnā vacanādikriyā yataḥ || 41 ||
parācīnā; pascādbhavā|
adhyātmādiviśeṣo'tra sarvaḥ pūrvamudīritaḥ|
tṛtīyeyaṃ vidhākhyātā bahiṣkaraṇavartinī || 42 ||
caturthīṃ tvamimāṃ koṭiṃ meyarūpāṃ tu me śṛṇu|
meyaṃ tu dvividhaṃ tāvadbahirantarvyavasthayā || 43 ||
meyeti| idameva bhāvabhūmiketi pūrvaṃ nirdiṣṭam|
bāhyaṃ tu nīlapītādi sukhaduḥkhādyathāntaram|
ābhiścatasṛbhiścāhaṃ vidhābhiḥ styānatāṃ gatā || 44 ||
svacittotthavikalpārthaiḥ pratyakṣāpyasmi vismṛtā|
sadācāryopadeśena sattarkamanurundhatā || 45 ||
nirūpye nipūṇairyatra meye'pyasmi tadā sphuṭam|
vilāpya sakalaṃ bhāvaṃ cetyarūpamimaṃ tathā || 46 ||
cetyam; meyam|
svacchandā pūrṇacidrūpā prakāśe'haṃ tadā svayam|
svacchā svacchatarā sāhaṃ tataḥ karaṇasaṃjñitā || 47 ||
tato mātaryatisvacchā sphurāmi svacchacinmayī|
ārohamavarohaṃ ca bhāvayanmāmakāvubhau|
maccitto madgataprāṇo madbhāvāyopapadyate || 48 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre pramātṛkaraṇaprakāśo nāma saptamo'dhyāyaḥ
iti saptamo'dhyāyaḥ

<8>
aṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ
śakraḥ
namaste sindhusaṃbhūte namaste padmasaṃbhave|
namaḥ saroruhāvāse nārāyaṇakuṭumbini || 1 ||
avatārāstu ye proktāstvadīyāḥ kośapañcake|
 tānme vistarataḥ padme pṛcchate vaktumarhasi || 2 ||
prathamaḥ śaktikośo bhagavataḥ pararūpasya caturvyūhānāṃ ca saṃbaddha iti suviditamiti kṛtvā tamupekṣya māyādipañcakośaviṣayaḥ praśnaḥ kriyate'tra kośapañcaka iti|
kimarthāḥ kiṃprakārāste kiyantaḥ kiṃsvarūpakāḥ|
tattvaṃ kathaya me devi sarvajñā hyasi śāśvatī || 3 ||
śrīḥ
ataraṅgamanirdeśyamaprakampyamanūpamam|
 aprakāramasaṃbhedyamavikalpamanākulam || 4 ||
prasaṅgādbhagavataḥ pararūpasya śaktikośāvasthitimāha ataraṅgamityādi| atra ṣaṣṭhādhyāyasaptadaśaslokoktamanusaṃdheyam|
ekaṃ nārāyaṇaṃ brahma śūnyaṃ śuddhaṃ nirāmayam|
 yadidaṃ dṛśyate kiṃcicchrūyate vānumīyate || 5 ||
nārāyaṇa ityākhyātaṃ nārāyaṇam|
pramāṇatrayasaṃbodhyaṃ bhāvābāvasvalakṣaṇam|
carācaraṇamu sthūlaṃ cetanācetanaṃ jagat || 6 ||
pramāṇatrayaṃ pratyakṣānumānaśabdāḥ|
tadidaṃ sakalaṃ brahma nārāyaṇamanuttaram|
 avidyāvidhurānandasvacchasvacchandicaddhanam || 7 ||
bhavadbhāvātmakaṃ divyamadhvanaḥ pāramuttamam|
śaktimacchaktibhāvena tad dvidhā vyavatiṣṭhate || 8 ||
śaktimattat paraṃ brhana nārāyaṇamahaṃ bhavat|
śaktirnārāyaṇī sāhamahaṃtā bhāvarūpiṇī || 9 ||
aham; ahaṃpratītiviṣayaḥ|
sa pradeśo na tasyāsti yena bhūtaṃ mayā vinā|
sa pradeśo na me kaścidvinā tadyena bhūyate || 10 ||
vinā tat; tat brahma vinetyarthaḥ|
ekadhā ca dvidhā caiva taistaistattvābdhipāragaiḥ|
vyapadiśyāvahe śāsraistāvāvāṃ sarvakāraṇam || 11 ||
bhāvottarā vkacitsṛṣṭiḥ vkacitsā bhavaduttarā|
bhavadbhāvottarā vkāpi vidvāstatra na muhyati || 12 ||
eka evāvatīrṇo hi devānāṃ kāryavattayā|
nārāyaṇo yadā sāhaṃ tatra tadbhāvabhāvinī || 13 ||
ekaiva cāvatīrṇāhaṃ yadā devahitepsayā|
ahaṃtāyāṃ mayi vyaktaḥ sa devo'haṃpadārthavān || 14 ||
avatīrṇau yadā tulyaṃ devakāryacikīrṣayā|
anyonyayoḥ sthitāvāvāṃ bhavadbhāvātmakau dvayoḥ || 15 ||
itthaṃ vyavasthite tattve hyavatāragatiṃ śṛṇu|
aniruddho vibhurdevo devadevaḥ sanātanaḥ || 16 ||
mahāvidyāsamudbhūtastadāhamapi vāsava|
matta eva mahālakṣmyā abhūvaṃ kamalākhyayā || 17 ||
māyākośāvatāramāha matta eva mahālakṣmyā iti| atra ṣaṣṭhādhyāyāṣṭādaśaślokoktamanusaṃdheyam|
tāvimau dampatī divyau pitarau jagatāṃ matau|
padmanābhāvatāre tu tāvetau dvāvayonijau || 18 ||
prasūtikośāvatāramāha padmanābhāvatāra ityādinā| mahālakṣmīmahāvidyāmahāmāyānāmavatāro'tra ṣaṣṭhādhyāye viṃśaśloke pūrvamuktaḥ
nārāyaṇāvatāro yaḥ śaktīśo nāma nāmataḥ|
prakārā bahavastasya sarvatrāhamanuvratā || 19 ||
ekadhā dvicaturdhā ca ṣoḍhā caiva tathāṣṭadhā|
punardvādaśadhā caiva tatra nāmāni me śṛṇu || 20 ||
śrīrnāma dvibhujasyāhamaṅkasthā varavarṇinī|
tasyaivobhayatorūpe śrīśca puṣṭiśca vāsava || 21 ||
caturdiśaṃ tu tasyaiva śrīḥ kīrtiśca jayā tathā|
māyeti kṛtvā rūpāṇi bhujye'haṃ tena viṣṇunā || 22 ||
tasyaiva koṇaṣaṭkasthā ṣoḍhāhaṃ śṛṇu nāma ca|
śuddhirnirañjanā nityā jñānaśaktiśca vāsava || 23 ||
tathāparājitā caiva ṣaṣṭhī tu prakṛtiḥ parā|
tasyaiva cāṣṭadhā dikṣu sāhaṃ rūpairvyavasthitā || 24 ||
lakṣmīḥ sarasvatī sarvakāmadā prītivardhanī|
yaśaskarī śāntidā ca tuṣṭidā puṣṭiraṣṭamī || 25 ||
koṇadviṣaṭke tasyaiva sthitā dvādaśadhāramyaham|
śrīśca kāmeśvarī kāntiḥ kriyāśaktivibhūtayaḥ || 26 ||
icchā prītī ratiścaiva māyā dhīrmahimeti ca|
evaṃ caturbhujasyāpi ṣoḍhāhaṃ kramaśaḥ sthitā || 27 ||
tasyaiva ṣaḍbhujasyāhamaṣṭabāhośca vāsava|
dviṣaḍbāhostathā sāhaṃ dvisaptakabhujasya ca || 28 ||
tathā ṣoḍaśahastasya bhujadvinavakasya ca|
vibhajya bahudhātmānamiyadbhedā vyavasthitā || 29 ||
avatāro hi yo viṣṇoḥ sindhuśāyīti saṃjñitaḥ|
sthitāhaṃ paritastasya caturdhā rūpameyuṣī || 30 ||
prakṛtikośāvatāramāha sindhuśāyīti| atra pañcamādhyāyaikaviṃśaślokoktamanusaṃdheyam|
lakṣmīrnidrā tathā prītirvidyā ceti vibhedinī|
avatāro hi yo viṣṇoḥ śrīpatirnāma nāmataḥ || 31 ||
śrīrityevākhyayā tatra tasyāhaṃ vāmataḥ sthitā|
avatāro hi yo viṣṇornāmataḥ pārijātajit || 32 ||
tadaṃsasthakarā tasya vāmotsaṅge hareḥ sthitā|
avatāro hi yo viṣṇornāmnā mīnadharaḥ śubhaḥ || 33 ||
brahmāṇḍakośāvatāramāha nāmnā mīnadhara ityādi|
anubhramāmi taṃ tatra sāhaṃ naurūpadhāriṇī|
traivikramodayo viṣṇoravatāraḥ paraḥ smṛtaḥ || 34 ||
āhlādajananī gaṅgā tatpādātprabhavāmyaham|
anantaśayano nāma yo'vatāro hareraham | 35 ||
sthitā caturdiśaṃ tasya cāturātmyamupeyuṣī|
lakṣmīścintā tathā nidrā puṣṭiścetyākhyayā yutā || 36 ||
ityeṣu saha siddhāhamavatīrṇāṇḍamadhyataḥ|
avatārāḥ pṛthagbhūtā yadā brahmāṇḍamadhyataḥ || 37 ||
anuvratā tathaivāhamavatīrṇā pṛthak pṛthak|
avatāro hi yo nāma varāho vedaviśrutaḥ || 38 ||
tadāhamapi bhūrnāma pṛthagbhūtā bhajāmyaham|
avatāro hi yo nāma dharmo viṣṇuḥ purātanaḥ || 39 ||
tadāhaṃ bhārgavī nāma khyātijā śrīḥ prakīrtitā|
avatāro hi yo nāma dattātreyo'trinandanaḥ || 40 ||
tadā hi tasya bhogāya saraso'haṃ samutthitā|
avatāro hi yo nāma vāmano vaiṣṇavaḥ śubhaḥ || 41 ||
padmādahaṃ samutpannā tadā padmeti viśrutā|
avatāro yadā viṣṇorbhārgavo rāmasaṃjñitaḥ || 42 ||
tadāhaṃ dharaṇī nāma śaktirāsamayonijā|
avatāro hi yo nāma rāmo dāśarathiḥ śubhaḥ || 43 ||
jātā janakayajñe'haṃ kṣetrāddhalamukhakṣatāt|
nāmnā sīteti vikhyātā daśānanavināśanī || 44 ||
avatāro hi yo viṣṇoścaturdhā saṃbhaviṣyati|
madhurāyāmahaṃ vyaktiṃ caturdhaiṣyāmi vai tathā || 45 ||
revatī rukmiṇī caiva ratirnāmnā tathā hyuṣā|
avatārāntaraṃ yattu mohanaṃ buddhasaṃjñakam || 46 ||
tārāhaṃ tatra nāmnā vai dhārā caiva prakītitā|
dhruvādayo'vatārā ye kevalā vaiṣṇavāḥ smṛtāḥ || 47 ||
jīvadehakośāvatāramāha dhruvādaya iti|
tattaccharīrabhūtāhaṃ teṣāṃ bhogyā vyavasthitā|
yattu me mohanaṃ rūpaṃ śrūyate'mṛtadhārakam || 48 ||
bhavadbhāvau tadā tatra rūpe tulyopalakṣitau|
devaiḥ puruṣarūpeṇa srīrūpeṇa tathetaraiḥ || 49 ||
saha siddhaṃ pṛthaksiddhamityetajjanma me'dbhutam|
kīrtitaṃ tava deveśa kevalaṃ janma me śrṛṇu || 50 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre lakṣmyavatāraprakāśo nāmāṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ
ityaṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ

<9>
navamo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ
ahaṃ nārāyaṇī devī nārāyaṇamanuvratā|
jñānānandakriyātmānaṃ jñānānandakriyāmayī || 1 ||
tasyā me na vinābhāvastena vā tasya vā mayā|
prakartuṃ śakyate kāle kasmiṃściddeśa eva vā || 2 ||
tattatkāryavaśāccaivānyadbhūtādbhutarūpakau|
ātmayogabalāttau svaḥ sahaiva ca vinaiva ca || 3 ||
brahmādirdattavān yādṛktapoyogabalātkṛtaḥ|
daityādibhyo jagaddhvaṃsakarebyo varamuttamam || 4 ||
tādṛśaṃ tādṛśaṃ rūpamāsthāyāvāṃ sanātanau|
tattatprīticikīrṣāyai carāvau devakāryataḥ || 5 ||
māyayā bhāvamācchādya paramārthaṃ svatejasā|
ahamevāvatīrṇā hi tattaddhvaṃsijighāṃsayā || 6 ||
ādau devī mahālakṣmīḥ smṛtāhaṃ parameśvarī|
abhūvaṃ ca punardvedhā kṛṣṇā brāhnīti rūpataḥ || 7 ||
guṇatrayavibhāgena rūpametatparaṃ mama|
mahālakṣmīrahaṃ śakra punaḥ svāyaṃbhuve'ntare || 8 ||
hitāya sarvalokānāṃ jātā mahiṣamardanī|
madīyā śaktileśā ye tattaddevaśarīragāḥ || 9 ||
saṃbhūya te mamābhūvan rūpaṃ paramaśobhanam|
āyudhāni ca devānāṃ yāni yāni sureśvara || 10 ||
tacchaktayastadākārā āyudhāni mamābhavan|
abhiṣṭutā suraiḥ sāhaṃ mahiṣaṃ jaghnuṣī kṣaṇāt || 11 ||
mahiṣāntakarīsūktaṃ sarvasiddhipradaṃ tadā|
‘devyā yayā’ dikaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ sendrairdevaiḥ saharṣibhiḥ || 12 ||
idaṃ ca sūktaṃ mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe devīmāhātmye caturthādhyāye paṭhitaṃ draṣṭavyam|
utpattiṃ yuddhavikrāntiṃ stotraṃ ceti sureśvara|
kathayanti suvistīrṇaṃ brāhnaṇā vedapāragāḥ || 13 ||
evaṃprabhāvāṃ devīṃ tāṃ stuvan dhyāyannamannapi|
labhate ca phalaṃ śaśvadādhipatyamanaśvaram || 14 ||
yoganidrā hareruktā yā sā devī duratyayā|
mahākālītanuṃ viddhi tāṃ māṃ devīṃ sanātanīm || 15 ||
madhukaiṭabhanāśe hi mohitau tau tayā tadā|
jaghnāte varalābhena devadevena viṣṇunā || 16 ||
viśvesvaryādikaṃ sūktaṃ dṛṣṭaṃ tad brahmaṇā sadā|
stutaye yoganidrāyā mama devyāḥ puraṃdara || 17 ||
idaṃ copākhyānaṃ mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe devīmāhātmye prathamādhyāye paṭhitam|
eṣā sā vaiṣṇavī māyā mahākālī duratyayā|
stutyā vaśīkṛtā kuryādvaśe stotuścarācaram || 18 ||
asyā devyāḥ samutpattiścaritaṃ stotramityapi|
hitāya sarvabhūtānāṃ dhāryante brahmavādibhiḥ || 19 ||
tāmase tvantare śakra mahāvidyā hi yā parā|
gaurīdehātsamudbhūtā kauśikīti tadā hyaham || 20 ||
vadhāya duṣṭadaityānāṃ tathā sumbhanisumbhayoḥ|
rakṣaṇāya ca lokānāṃ devānāmupakāriṇī || 21 ||
madīyāḥ śaktayo yāstā devaśreṣṭhaśarīragāḥ|
tāstāstadrūpadhāriṇyaḥ sāhāyyaṃ vidadhurhi me || 22 ||
tābhirnihatya daityendrān hantavyā ye tathā tathā|
saṃhṛtyātmani tāḥ sarvā madīyā vipruṣo'khilāḥ || 23 ||
ahaṃ nijaghnuṣī paścāttayoḥ sumbhanisumbhayoḥ|
`devi prapannādihare prasīde' tyādikaṃ tathā || 24 ||
nārāyaṇīstutiḥ mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe devīmāhātmye ekādaśādyāye paṭhītā|
nārāyaṇīstutirnāma sūktaṃ paramaśobhanam|
stutayo me tadā dṛṣṭā devairvahnipurogamaiḥ || 25 ||
eṣā saṃpūjitā bhaktyā sarvajñatvaṃ prayacchati|
kauśikī sarvadeveśa bahukāmapradā hyaham || 26 ||
utpattiryuddhavikrāntiḥ stutiśceti purātanaiḥ|
paṭhyate tritayaṃ viprairvedavedāṅgapāragaiḥ || 27 ||
vaivasvate'ntare caitau punaḥ sumbhanisumbhakau|
utpatsyete varonmattau devopadravakāriṇau || 28 ||
nandagopakule jātā yaśodāgarbhasaṃbhavā|
tāvahaṃ nāśayiṣyāmi sunandā vindhyavāsinī || 29 ||
punaścāpyatiraudreṇa rūpeṇa pṛthivītale|
avatīrya haniṣyāmi vai pracittān mahāsurān || 30 ||
bhakṣayantyāśca tānugrān vaipracittān mahāsurān|
raktā dantā bhaviṣyanti dāḍimīkusumopamāḥ || 31 ||
tato māṃ devatāḥ sarve martyaloke ca mānavāḥ|
stuvanto vyāhariṣyanti satataṃ raktadantikām || 32 ||
tasminnevāntare śakra catvāriṃśattame yuge|
sarvataḥ śatavārṣikyāmanāvṛṣṭyāmanambhasi || 33 ||
munibhiḥ saṃsmṛtā bhūmau saṃbhaviṣyāmyayonijā|
tataḥ śatena netrāṇāṃ nirīkṣiṣyāmyahaṃ munīn || 34 ||
kīrtayiṣyanti māṃ śakra śatākṣīmiti mānavāḥ|
tadāhamakhilaṃ lokamātmadehasamudbhavaiḥ || 35 ||
bhariṣyāmi śubhaiḥ śākairāviṣṭaiḥ prāṇadhārakaiḥ|
śākaṃbharīti māṃ devāstadā stoṣyanti vāsava || 36 ||
tatraiva ca haniṣyāmi durgamākhyaṃ mahāsuram|
durgādevīti vikhyātiṃ tato yāsyāmyahaṃ bhuvi || 37 ||
śākaṃbharīṃ stuvan dhyāyan śakra saṃpūjayannaman|
akṣayyāmaśnute śīghramannapānodbhavāṃ ratim || 38 ||
caturyuge ca pañcāśattame munibhirarthitā|
sundaraṃ cātibhīmaṃ ca rūpaṃ kṛtvā himācare || 39 ||
rakṣāṃsi bhakṣayiṣyāmi munīnāṃ trāṇakāraṇāt|
tato māṃ munayaḥ sarve stoṣyantyānamramūrtayaḥ || 40 ||
`bhīme devi prasīde' ti bhīmāmabhayadāyinīm|
yuge ṣaṣṭitame kaścidaruṇo nāma dānavaḥ || 41 ||
manujānāṃ munīnāṃ ca mahābādhāṃ kariṣyati|
tatrāhaṃ bhrāmaraṃ rūpaṃ kṛtvāsaṃkhyeyaṣaṭpadā || 42 ||
trailokyasya hitārthāya vadhiṣyāmi mahāsuram|
bhrāmarīti ca māṃ lokāstadā stoṣyanti sarvadā || 43 ||
sunandāraktadantikāśatākṣīśākaṃbharīdurgābhīmābhrāmarīṇāmupākhyānāni mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe devī māhātmye ekādaśādhyāye draṣṭavyāni|
itthaṃ yadā yadā bādhā dānavotthā bhaviṣyati|
tadā tadāvatīryāhaṃ haniṣyāmi mahāsurān || 44 ||
amī te leśataḥ śakra darśitāḥ paramādbhutāḥ|
avatārā nirātaṅkā madīyāḥ kevalāhvayāḥ || 45 ||
eteṣāṃ paramā proktā kūṭasthā sā mahīyasī|
mahālakṣmīrmahābhāgā prakṛtiḥ parameśvarī || 46 ||
amuṣyāḥ stutaye dṛṣṭaṃ brahmādyaiḥ sakalaiḥ suraiḥ|
`namo devyā'dikaṃ sūktaṃ sarvakāmapradaṃ varam || 47 ||
idaṃ ca mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe devīmāhātmye pañcamādhyāye paṭhitam|
imāṃ devīṃ stuvannityaṃ stotreṇānena māmiha|
kleśānatītya sakalānaiśvaryaṃ mahadaśnute || 48 ||
amuṣyāḥ sāvatārāyā mahālakṣmyā mamānagha|
janmāni caritaiḥ sārdhaṃ stotrairvaibhavavādibhiḥ || 49 ||
kathitāni purā śakra vasiṣṭhena mahātmanā|
svārociṣe'ntare rājñe surathāya mahātmane || 50 ||
idaṃ copākhyānaṃ mārkaṇḍeyapurāṇe devīmāhātmye prathamādhyāye dṛsyate|
samādhaye ca vaiśyāya praṇatāyāvasīdate|
bhaktiśraddhāvatā nityaṃ vasiṣṭhena kṛteti me || 51 ||
hṛdi sthitā sadā seyaṃ janmakarmāvalistutiḥ|
etāṃ dvijamukhācchrutvā hyadhīyāno naraḥ sadā || 52 ||
vidhūya nikhilāṃ māyāṃ samyagjñānaṃ samaśnute|
sarvāṃ saṃpadamāproti dhunoti sakalāpadaḥ || 53 ||
mama prabhāvāt saubhāgyaṃ kīrti caiva samaśnute|
kevalā api yadyete madīyā viṣṇunā vinā || 54 ||
na me'sti saṃbhavaḥ so'yamahaṃbhūtaḥ sthito'tra tu|
anyonyenāvinābhāvādanyonyena samanvayāt || 55 ||
mayyayaṃ devadeveśastatrāhaṃ ca sanātanī|
ityete leśataḥ śakra darśitāḥ saprakārakāḥ || 56 ||
avatārā madīyāste saṃbhūtāḥ kośapañcake|
śuddhe kośe samudbhūtā bhavadbhāvātmakāḥ pare || 57 ||
tatrāpyeṣā sthitirjñeyā viṣṇormama saha sthitiḥ|
evaṃprakārāṃ māṃ jñātvā pratyakṣāṃ sarvasaṃmatām || 58 ||
upāyairvividhaiḥ śaśvadupāsya bahudhātmikām|
kleśakarmāśayātīto madbhāvaṃ pratipadyate || 59 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre kevalāvatāraprakāśo nāma navamo'dhyāyaḥ|
iti navamo'dhyāyaḥ

<10>
daśamo'dhyāyaḥ
śakraḥ
kṣīrodamathanāyāsaphalarūpe madhudviṣaḥ|
namaścandrasahodaryai namaste'mṛtayonaye || 1 ||
bhāvottarāḥ prakārāste śrutāstvadvaktrapaṅkajāt|
idānīṃ śrotumicchāmi prakārān bhavaduttarān || 2 ||
vaiṣṇavā avatārāste kiṃrūpāḥ kati vāmbuje|
etatpṛṣṭā mayā brūhi namaste paṅkajāsane || 3 ||
śrīḥ
hanta te śakra vakṣyāmi prakārān bhavaduttarān|
vaiṣṇavā avatārāste yāvanto yadvidhāśca te || 4 ||
ṣāḍguṇyamamalaṃ brahma nirdoṣamajaraṃ dhruvam|
sarvaśakti nirātaṅkaṃ nirālambanabhāvanam || 5 ||
tadunmiṣati vaipūrvaṃ śaktimacchaktibhāvataḥ|
nārāyaṇaḥ paro devaḥ saṃsthitaḥ śaktimattayā || 6 ||
sthirā śaktirahaṃ tasya sarvakāryakarī vibhoḥ|
tāvāvāṃ jagato'rthāya bahudhā vikriyāvahe || 7 ||
yathāhamāsthitā bhedaistathā te kathitaṃ purā|
vikārānavikārasya viṣṇoḥ śrṛṇu mayoditān || 8 ||
aprākṛtānanaupamyānacintayamahimojjvalān|
svāṃ śaktiṃ māmadhiṣṭhāya prakṛtiṃ paramādbhutām || 9 ||
trairūpyeṇa jagannāthaḥ samudeti jagaddhite|
ādyena pararūpeṇa vyūharūpeṇa cāpyatha || 10 ||
tathā vibhavarūpeṇa nānābhāvamupeyuṣā|
vyāpako bhagavān devo bhaktānugrahakāmyayā || 11 ||
anaupamyamanirdaśyaṃ vapuḥ sa bhajate param|
viśvāpyāyanakaṃ kāntyā pūrṇendvayutatulyayā || 12 ||
varadābhayahastaṃ ca dvibhujaṃ padmalocanam|
rekhāmayena cakreṇa śaṅkhena ca karadvaye || 13 ||
aṅkitaṃ nirvikārāṅghristhitaṃ paramaśobhanam|
anyūnānatiriktaiḥ svairguṇaiḥ ṣaḍbhiralaṃkṛtam || 14 ||
samaṃ samavibhaktāṅnaṃ sarvāvayavasundaram|
pūrṇamābharaṇaiḥ śubhraiḥ sudhākallolasaṃkulaiḥ || 15 ||
raśmibhūtairamūrtaiḥ svairacyutādyairavicyutam|
ekā mūrtiriyaṃ divyā parākhyā vaiṣṇavī parā || 16 ||
yogasiddhā bhajantyenāṃ hṛdi turyapadāśritām|
atha vyūhasbarūpaṃ te dvitīyaṃ varṇayāmyaham || 17 ||
vyūhyātmānaṃ caturdhā svaṃ devaḥ prāgādibhedataḥ|
vāsudevādibhedena sauṣuptādhvani tiṣṭhati || 18 ||
saṃsthānamādimūrtervai sarveṣāṃ tu samaṃ smṛtam|
ṣaḍguṇaṃ prathamaṃ rūpaṃ dvandvairjñānādisaṃbhavaiḥ || 19 ||
itarāṇi svarūpāṇi kathitāni mayā purā|
vahnyarkendusahasrābhamānandāspandalakṣaṇam || 20 ||
bījaṃ sarvakriyāṇāṃ tadvikalpānāṃ tadāspadam|
sauṣuptaṃ cāturātmyaṃ tatprathamaṃ viddhi vāsava || 21 ||
atha svāpre pade'pyevaṃ vibhajyātmānamātmanā|
devaḥ prāgādibhedena vāsudevādirūpataḥ || 22 ||
samāsavyāsabhedena guṇaānāṃ puruṣottamaḥ|
sitaraktasuvarṇābhrasadṛśaiḥ paramādbhutaiḥ || 23 ||
ādimūtisamai rūpaiścaturdhā vyavatiṣṭhate|
kaivalyabhogaphaladaṃ bhavabījakṣayaṃkaram || 24 ||
cāturātmyaṃ dvitīyaṃ tat sudhāsaṃdohasundaram|
atha jāgratpade devaḥ sitaraktādibhedataḥ || 25 ||
caturbhujairudārāṅgaiḥ śaṅkhacakrādicihnitaiḥ|
nānādhvajavicitrāṅgairvāsudevādisaṃjñitaiḥ || 26 ||
vyūhaiḥ svaṃ pravibhajyāste vibhurnāma svalīlayā|
tatrādyaṃ bhagavadrūpaṃ himakundendukāntimat || 27 ||
caturbhujaṃ saumyavaktraṃ puṇḍarīkanibhekṣaṇam|
pītakauśeyavasanaṃ suparṇadhvajabhūṣitam || 28 ||
mukyadakṣiṇahastena bhītānāmabhayapradam|
tathāvidhena vāmena dadhānaṃ śaṅkhamuttamam || 29 ||
apareṇa dadhānaṃ ca dakṣiṇena sudarśanam|
vāmena ca gadāṃ gurvīṃ niṣaṇṇāṃ vasudhātale || 30 ||
saṃcintayet puro bhāge vāsudevamitīdṛśam|
sindūraśikharākāraṃ saumyavaktraṃ caturbhujam || 31 ||
atasīpuṣpasaṃkāśavasanaṃ tālalāñchitam|
mukhyena pāṇiyugmena tulyamādyasya vai vibhoḥ || 32 ||
sīraṃ taccakrahaste'sya musalaṃ tu gadākare|
dakṣiṇe cindayedbhāge saṃkarṣaṇamitīdṛśam || 33 ||
prāvṛṇniśāsamuditakhadyotanicayaprabham|
raktakauśeyavasanaṃ makaradhvajaśobhitam || 34 ||
saumyavaktraṃ caturbāhuṃ tṛtīyaṃ parameśvaram|
mukhyahastadvayaṃ cāsya prāgvattulyaṃ mahāmate || 35 ||
vāme'parasmin śārṅgaṃ ca dakṣiṇe bāṇapañcakam|
apare cintayedbhāge pradyumnamiti kīrtitam || 36 ||
añjanādripratīkāśaṃ supītāmbaraveṣṭitam|
caturbhujaṃ viśālākṣaṃ mṛgalāñchanabhūṣitam || 37 ||
ādivat pāṇiyugalamādyamasya vicintayet|
dakṣiṇādikrameṇātha dvābhyāṃ vai khaḍgakheṭakau || 38 ||
dadhānamaniruddhaṃ tu saumyabhāge vicintayet|
vanamālādharāḥ sarve śrīvatsakṛtalakṣaṇāḥ || 39 ||
śobhitāḥ kaustubhenaiva ratnarājena vakṣasi|
jāgratpade sthitaṃ devaṃ cāturātmyamanuttamam || 40 ||
sthityutpattipralayakṛtsarvopakaraṇānvitam|
divyaṃ taccintayedyasya viśvaṃ tiṣṭhati śāsane || 41 ||
trividhaṃ cāturātmyaṃ tu suṣuptyādipadatrike|
suvyaktaṃ tatpade turye guṇalakṣyaṃ paraṃ sthitam || 42 ||
jñānakriyādibhirviṣṇorlokānanusisṛkṣataḥ|
vyūhasaṃjñamidaṃ rūpaṃ dvitīyaṃ kathitaṃ mayā || 43 ||
tṛtīyaṃ vibhavākhyaṃ tu viśvamandiramadhyagam|
nānākārakriyākartṛ rūpaṃ viṣṇorniśāmaya || 44 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre paravyūhaprakāśo nāma daśamo'dhyāyaḥ
iti daśamo'dhyāyaḥ

<11>
ekādaśo'dhyāḥ
śrīḥ
nirdoṣo niradhiṣṭheyo niravadyaḥ sanātanaḥ|
viṣṇurnārāyaṇaḥ śrīmān paramātmā sanātanaḥ || 1 ||
ṣāḍguṇyavigraho nityaṃ paraṃ brahmākṣaraṃ param|
tasya māṃ paramāṃ śaktiṃ nityaṃ taddharmadharmiṇīm || 2 ||
brahmākṣaraśabdayoḥ prakṛtijīvayorapi prayogāt tadvyudāsāya paramiti viśeṣaṇam|
sarvabhāvānugāṃ viddhi nirdoṣāmanapāyinīm|
sarvakāryakarī sāhaṃ viṣṇoravyayarūpiṇaḥ || 3 ||
śuddhāśuddhamayairbhāvairvitatyātmānamātmanā|
paravyūhādisaṃbhedaṃ vyūhayantī hareḥ sadā || 4 ||
śuddhaṣāḍguṇyamādāya kalapayantī tathā tathā|
tena nānāvidhaṃ rūpaṃvyūhādyucitamañjasā || 5 ||
unmeṣayāmi devasya prakāraṃ bhavaduttaram|
vyāpārastasya devasya sāhamasmi na saṃśayaḥ || 6 ||
mayā kṛtaṃ hi yat karma tena tat kṛtamucyate|
ahaṃ hi tasya devasya smṛtā vyāpriyamāṇatā || 7 ||
iti śakra paraṃ rūpaṃ vyūharūpaṃ ca darśitam|
tṛtīyaṃ vibhavākhyaṃ tu rūpamadya niśāmaya || 8 ||
turyādijāgradantaṃ yat proktaṃ padacatuṣṭayam|
vāsudevādinā vyāptamaniruddhāntimena tu || 9 ||
tatra tatra pade caiva cāturātmyaṃ tathā tathā|
avyaktavyaktarūpaiḥ svairuditaṃ te yathoditam || 10 ||
vyūhād vyūhasamutpattau padādyāvatpadāntaram|
antaraṃ sakalaṃ deśaṃ saṃpūrayati tejasā || 11 ||
pūjitastejasāṃ rāśiravyakto mūrtivarjitaḥ|
viśākhayūpa ityuktastattajjñānādibṛṃhitaḥ || 12 ||
viśākhayūpo nāmāprākṛte divyaloke bhrājamāno jyotirmayaḥ stambhākāro bhagavadrūpaviśeṣaḥ| tatrādhobhāgamārabhya ūrdhvabhāgaparyantaṃ catvāri parvāṇi krameṇāniruddhapradyumnasaṃkarṣaṇavāsudevādhiṣṭhitāni spaṣṭataraspaṣṭakiṃcitspaṣṭāspaṣṭaśaṅkhacakrādilakṣmāṇi| pratiparva prāgādikrameṇa caturṣvapi pārśveṣu krameṇa vāsudevādayo vyūhā bhrājante| evaṃ vibhāgaśca yoginā dhyānālambanārthaṃ bhagavataiva kalpitaḥ| asya ca vistaraḥ sāttvatasaṃhitāyāṃ draṣṭavyaḥ|
tasmiṃstasmin pade tasmānmūrtiśākhācatuṣṭayam|
vāsudevādikaṃ śakra prādurbhavati vai kramāt || 13 ||
evaṃ svapnapadājjāgratpadavyūhavibhāvane|
svaprātpadājjāgradante taijasaḥ pūjyate mahān || 14 ||
viśākhayūpo bhagavān sa devastejasāṃ nidhiḥ|
turyādye svapnaparyante cāturātmyatrike hi yat || 15 ||
tattadaiśvaryasaṃpanne ṣāḍguṇyaṃ suvyavasthitam|
tadādāyākhilaṃ divyaṃ śuddhasaṃvitpuraḥsaram || 16 ||
vibhajannātmanātmānaṃ vāsudevādirūpataḥ|
punavibhavavelāyāṃ vinā mūrticatuṣṭayam || 17 ||
viśākhayūpa evaiṣa vibhavān bhāvayatyuta|
te devā vibhavātmānaḥ padmanābhādayo matāḥ || 18 ||
padmanābho dhruvo'nantaḥ śakrīśo madhusūdanaḥ|
vidyādhidevaḥ kapilo viśvarūpo vihaṃgamaḥ || 19 ||
eta eva devā vyañjanādhiṣṭhātṛtayā tattanmahiṣībhiḥ sahātraiva viṃśādhyāye vakṣyante|
kroḍātmā baḍavāvaktro dharmo vāgīśvarastathā|
ekārṇavāntaḥśāyī ca tathaiva kamaṭhākṛtiḥ || 20 ||
varāho narasiṃhaścāpyamṛtāharaṇastathā|
śrīpatirdivyadeho'tha kāntātmāmṛtadhārakaḥ || 21 ||
rāhujit kālanemighnaḥ pārijātaharastathā|
lokanāthastu śāntātmā dattātreyo mahāprabhuḥ || 22 ||
nyagrodhaśāyī bhagavānekaśṛṅgatanustathā|
devo vāmanadehastu sarvavyāpī trivikramaḥ || 23 ||
naro nārāyaṇaścaiva hariḥ kṛṣṇastathaiva ca|
jvalatparaśubhṛdrāmo rāmaścānyo dhanurdharaḥ || 24 ||
vedavidbhagavān kalkī pātālaśayanaḥ prabhuḥ|
triṃśaccāṣṭāvime devāḥ padmanābhādayo matāḥ || 25 ||
vibhorviśākhayūpasya tattatkāryavaśādime|
sphūrtayo vibhavāḥ khyātāḥ kāryaṃ caiṣāmasaṃkaram || 26 ||
śuddhāśuddhādhvanormadhye padmanābho vyavasthitaḥ|
dhruvādayo'pare devā vivṛtā viśvamandire || 27 ||
rūpāṇyastrāṇi caiteṣāṃ śaktayaścāparā vidhāḥ|
sarvaṃ tat sāttvate siddhaṃ saṃjñāmātraṃ pradarśitam || 28 ||
śākhāstu vāsudevādyā vibhordevasya kīrtitāḥ|
viśākhayūpo bhagavān vitatābhiḥ karoti tat || 29 ||
catasṛbhyo'tha śākhābhyaḥ keśavādyaṃ trayaṃ trayam|
dāmodarāntamudbhūtaṃ tad vyūhāntaramucyate || 30 ||
tābhya eva hi śākhābhyaḥ śriyādīnāṃ trayaṃ trayam|
pūrvatrayānurūpeṇa śaktīnāṃ ca samudgatam || 31 ||
parādivibhavāntānāṃ sarveṣāṃ devatātmanām|
śuddhaṣāḍguṇyarūpāṇi vapūṃṣi tridaśeśvara || 32 ||
yāvantyastrāṇi devānāṃ cakraśaṅkhādikāni vai|
bhūṣaṇāni vicitrāṇi vāsāṃsi vividhāni ca || 33 ||
dhvajāśca vividhākārāḥ kāntayaśca sitādikāḥ|
vāhanāni vicitrāṇi satyādyāni sureśvara || 34 ||
śaktayo bhogadāścaiva vividhākārasaṃsthitāḥ|
āntaḥkaraṇiko vargastadīyā vṛttayo'khailāḥ || 35 ||
yacca yaccopakaraṇaṃ sāmānyaṃ puruṣāntaraiḥ|
ṣāḍguṇyanirmitaṃ viddhi tatsarvaṃ balasūdana || 36 ||
śuddhasaṃvinmayī sāhaṃ ṣāḍguṇyaparipūritā|
tathā tathā bhavāmyeṣāmiṣṭaṃ yaddhi yathā yathā || 37 ||
na vinā devedevena sthitirmama hi vidyate|
mayā vinā na devasya sthitirviṣṇorhi vidyate || 38 ||
tāvāvāmekatāṃ prāptau dvidhā bhūtau ca saṃsthitau|
vidhāṃ bhajāvahe tāṃ tāṃ yaddadyatra hyapekṣitam || 39 ||
śakraḥ
sindhukanye namastubhyaṃ namaste sarasīruhe|
paravyūhādibhedena kiṃ prayojanamīśituḥ || 40 ||
śrīḥ
anugrahāya jīvānāṃ bhaktānāmanukampayā|
paravyūhādibhedena devadevapravṛttayaḥ || 41 ||
śakraḥ
devadevapriye devi namaste kamalodbhave|
anugrahāya bhaktānāmekaivāstu vidhā hareḥ || 42 ||
śrīḥ
jīvānāṃ vividhāḥ śakra saṃcitāḥ puṇyasaṃcayāḥ|
saṃcinvanti na te jīvāstulyakālaṃ kathaṃcana || 43 ||
kaściddhi sukṛtonmeṣāt kadācit puruṣo nṛṣu|
śrīmatā kamalākṣeṇa jāyamāno nirīkṣyate || 44 ||
anyadā puruṣo'nyaścetyevaṃ bhinnāḥ śubhāśayāḥ|
bhedo'dhikāriṇāṃ puṇyatāratamyena jāyate || 45 ||
vivekaḥ kasyacinmando bhagavattatvavedane|
madyamastu parasyātha divyo'nyasya tu jāyate || 46 ||
īśānugrahavaiṣamyādevaṃ bhede vyavasthite|
tattatkāryānurodhena paravyūhādibhāvanā || 47 ||
kriyate devadevena śaktiṃ māmadhitiṣṭhatā|
saṃsiddhayogatattvānāmadhikāraḥ parātmani || 48 ||
vyāmiśrayogayuktānāṃ madhyānāṃ vyūhabhāvane|
vaibhavīyādirūpeṣu vivekavidhurātmanām || 49 ||
ahaṃtāmamātārtānāṃ bhaktānāṃ parameśvare|
adhikārasya vaiṣamyaṃ bhaktānāmanudṛśya saḥ || 50 ||
bhajate vividhaṃ bhāvaṃ paravyūhādiśabditam|
iti te leśataḥ śakra darśitā ubhayātmakāḥ || 51 ||
bhavadbhāvottarā vyūhā mama nārāyaṇasya ca|
śuddhe śuddhetarasmiṃśca kośavarge madudbhave || 52 ||
sthitirnau darśitā te'dya pṛthak saha ca kevalā|
evaṃprakārāṃ māṃ jñātvā pratyakṣāṃ sarvasaṃmatām || 53 ||
upāyairvividhaiḥ śaśvadupāsya vividhātmikām|
kleśakarmāśayātīto madbhāvaṃ pratipadyate || 54 ||
kleśakarmāśayā anantarādhyāye vakṣyante|
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre vibhavaprakāśo nāma ekādaśo'dhyāyaḥ
ityekādaśo'dhyāyaḥ

<12>
dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ
śakraḥ
cicchaktireva te śuddhā yadi jīvaḥ sanātanaḥ|
kleśakarmāśayasparśaḥ kathamasya saroruhe || 1 ||
kleśāḥ ke kati te proktāḥ karma kīdṛk ca kiṃvidham|
