āśayo nāma ko devi tadetat kiṃphalaṃ smṛtam || 2 ||
sindhukanye tadetanme brūhi tubhyaṃ namo namaḥ|
sarvajñe na tvadanyena vaktumetaddhi śakyate || 3 ||
śrīḥ
ahaṃ nārāyaṇī devī svacchasvacchandacinmayī|
svatantrā niravadyāhaṃ viṣṇoḥ śrīranapāyinī || 4 ||
īśeśitavyabhedena dvidhā rūpaṃ mayā kṛtam|
īśitavyaṃ ca tadbhinnaṃ svācchandyādeva me dvidhā || 5 ||
cicchaktirekā bhoktrākhyā parā bhogyādirūpiṇī|
kālakālyavibhedena sā dvidhā bheditā mayā || 6 ||
tatra kālyātmikā śaktirmohinī bandhanī tathā|
prakṛtiḥ savikāraiṣā cicchaktirbadhyate'nayā || 7 ||
kliśyate yena rūpeṇa cicchaktirbhoktṛtāṃ gatā|
sa kleśaḥ pañcadhā jñeyo nāmānyasya ca me śṛṇu || 8 ||
tamo moho mahāmohastāmisro hyandhasaṃjñitaḥ|
avidyā pañcaparvaiṣā tamaso gatiruttamā || 9 ||
asaṅginyapi cicchaktiḥ śuddhāpyapariṇāminī|
āviddhamātmano rūpaṃ nairmalyena vibharti sā || 10 ||
śakraḥ
vyāhatāmiva paśyāmi cicchakteḥ kleśasaṅgitām|
muhyatīva mano me'dya taṃ mohaṃ chindhi padmaje || 11 ||
śrīḥ
svatantrā sarvasiddhīnāṃ hetuścātra mahādbhutā|
śaktirnārāyaṇasyāhaṃ nityā devī sadoditā || 12 ||
tasyā me pañca karmāṇi nityāni tridaśeśvara|
tirobhāvastathā sṛṣṭiḥ sthitiḥ saṃhṛtireva ca || 13 ||
anugraha iti proktaṃ madīyaṃ karmapañcakam|
eteṣāṃ kramaśo vyākhyāṃ karmaṇāṃ śakra me śrṛṇu || 14 ||
tatra nāma tirobhāvo'nyadbhāvaḥ parikīrtyate|
svacchāpi sā madīyā hi cicchaktirbhoktṛsaṃjñitā || 15 ||
madīyayā yayā śaktyā vartate prakṛtervaśe|
tirobhāvābhidhānā me sāvidyāśaktirucyate || 16 ||
madīyaṃ bheditaṃ rūpaṃ satyasaṃkalpayā mayā|
yo'varoho madīyaste varṇitaḥ prathamaḥ purā || 17 ||
cicchaktirjīva ityevaṃ vibudhaiḥ parikīrtyate|
matsvācchandyavaśādeva tasya bhedaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 18 ||
madīyaṃ caityarūpaṃ yat satyasaṃkalpayā kṛtam|
mayā tadekīkaraṇaṃ cicchakteḥ kriyate hi yat || 19 ||
avidyā sā parā śaktistirobhāva iti smṛtaḥ|
pañca parvāṇi tasyāstu santi tāni nibodha me || 20 ||
tamastu prathamaṃ parva nāmāvidyeti tasya tu|
anātmanyasvabhūte ca caitye jīvasya yā matiḥ || 21 ||
svatayāhaṃtayā caiva tamo'vidyā ca sā smṛtā|
svīkṛte'haṃtayā caitye māno yastatra jāyate || 22 ||
asmitākhyo mahāmoho dvitīyaṃ kleśaparva tat|
caityacetanayorekabhāvāpattiravidyayā || 23 ||
moho'smitā mahāmoha iti śabdairnigadyate|
sukhānustṛtiheturyā vāsanāsmitayāhitā || 24 ||
sa rāgo rañjyaviṣayastṛtīyaṃ kleśaparva tat|
duḥkhānusmṛtiheturyā vāsanāsmitayāhitā || 25 ||
sa dveṣo dveṣyaviṣayaścaturthaṃ kleśaparva tat|
duḥkhaṃ jihāsato yogaiḥ prepsataśca sukhaṃ tathā || 26 ||
tadantarāyairvitrāso madhye yo nāma jāyate|
andhākhyo'bhiniveśaḥ sa pañcamaṃ kleśaparva tat || 27 ||
dehamātmatayā buddhvā tatastādātmyamāgataḥ|
rañjanīyamabhiprepsurjihāsuśca tathetarat || 28 ||
tadantarāyavitrastastatpratīkāramācaran|
iṣṭasya prāptaye'niṣṭavighātāya ca cetanaḥ || 29 ||
yadayaṃ kurute karma trividhaṃ trividhātmakam|
tatkarma gaditaṃ sadbhiḥ sāṃkhyayogavicakṣaṇaiḥ || 30 ||
tatprasūtaṃ sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ tathā duḥkhasukhātmakam|
vipākastrividhaḥ proktastattvaśāsraviśāradaiḥ || 31 ||
vāsanā āśayāḥ proktāḥ kleśakarmavipākajāḥ|
antaḥkaraṇavartinyaḥ samantāccherate hi tāḥ || 32 ||
janyante vāsanā nityaṃ pañcabhiḥ kleśaparvabhiḥ|
sadṛśārambhahetuśca vāsanā karmaṇāṃ tathā || 33 ||
sukhādivāsanā caiva vipākairjanyate tridhā|
caturbhirlakṣaṇairitthaṃbhūtā kleśādināmakaiḥ || 34 ||
bandhanī jīvakośasya tirobhāvābhidhā vidhā|
śaktyānayaiva baddhānāṃ jīvānāṃ mama nityadā || 35 ||
sāṃtatyena pravartante mama sṛṣṭyādiśaktayaḥ|
sṛṣṭiśaktirdvidhā sā me śuddhyaśuddhivaśānmayā || 36 ||
vivicya darśitā sā te sā punaḥ saptadhā sthitā|
aniśaṃ kriyate tvekā prājāpatyena karmaṇaā || 37 ||
ṣaṭkośasaṃbhavāstvanyāstattatkālasamudbhavāḥ|
sargakrame prakṛtyutthe sṛṣṭirjñeyā tridhā punaḥ || 38 ||
bhāvikī laiṅgikī caiva bhautikī ceti bhedataḥ|
yathā nyagrodhadhānāyāṃ traiguṇye prakṛtau tathā || 39 ||
yā sthitirmahadādeḥ sā bhāvasṛṣṭirnigadyate|
samaṣṭivyaṣṭibhedena liṅgaṃ yatsṛjyate mayā || 40 ||
virājaśca tathānyeṣāṃ bhūtānāṃ liṅgajā tu sā|
mahadādyā viśeṣāntā viṃśatiśca trayaśca ye || 41 ||
padārthā liṅgadehasthā virājaḥ parikīrtitāḥ|
khānāṃ samaṣṭibhūtānāṃ tathāntaḥkaraṇasya ca || 42 ||
tridhā sthitasya ye yeṃ'śāḥ pratijīvaṃ vyavasthitāḥ|
sthūlānāṃ caiva bhūtānāṃ ye sūkṣmāḥ kīrtitāḥ purā || 43 ||
vyaṣṭayo'ṣṭādaśemāśca kleśāḥ karmāṇi vāsanāḥ|
prāṇāśceti taduddiṣṭaṃ liṅgaṃ jīvagaṇāśrayam || 44 ||
cicchaktayo hi liṅgasthāḥ saṃsaranti yathā tathā|
śuddhe hi bhagavajjñāne jāte satkarmajīvināpt || 45 ||
jīvānāṃ vinivartante liṅgānyetāni nānyadā|
virājaḥ sthūladeho yo brahmāṇḍāparanāmavān || 46 ||
caturvidhāni cānyāni śarīrāṇi śarīriṇām|
eṣā me bhautikī sṛṣṭiritīdaṃ sṛṣṭicintanam || 47 ||
sthitirnāma tṛtīyā me śaktiryā te puroditā|
tasyāḥ svarūpaṃ vakṣyāmi tanme śakra niśāmaya || 48 |
ādyasṛṣṭikṣaṇo yastu saṃjihīrṣākṣaṇaśca yaḥ|
yatsthairyakaraṇaṃ nāma tayorantaravartinām || 49 ||
nānārūpairmadīyaiḥ sā sthitiśaktiḥ parā mama|
viṣṇunā devadevena mayā caiva tathā tathā || 50 ||
yā sthitiḥ kathitā sā tu prathamā tattvacintakaiḥ|
manvantarādhipaiścaiva dvitīyā parikīrtitā || 51 ||
manuputraistṛtīyānyā kṣudrairiti caturvidhā|
caturthī saṃhṛtīśaktistasyā bhedamimaṃ śṛṇu || 52 ||
nāśo jarāyujādīnāṃ bhūtānāṃ nityadā tu yā|
sā nityā saṃhṛtistvanyā śakra naimittikī smṛtā || 53 ||
trailokyaviṣayā sā tu brahmaprasvāpahetukā|
tṛtīyā prākṛtī proktā mahadādivyapāśrayā || 54 ||
prāsūtī tu caturthī syādavyaktaviṣayā tu sā|
māyī yā pañcamī proktā prasūtiviṣayā tu yā || 55 ||
śāktī ṣaṣṭhī tu vijñeyā māyāsaviṣayā tu sā|
saptamyātyantikī proktā vilayo yoginā mayi || 56 ||
sūkṣmāṇi vinivartante śarīrāṇi tadā satām|
eṣā saptavidhā śakra saṃhṛtiste mayoditā|
pañcamyanugrahākhyā me śaktirvyākhyāmimāṃ śṛṇu || 57 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre tirobhāvādiśaktiprakāśo nāma dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ
iti dvādaśo'dhyāyaḥ

<13>
trayodaśo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ
anugrahātmikā śakra śaktirme pañcamī smṛtā|
tāmimāṃ tattvato vatsa vadāmi tava sāṃpratam || 1 ||
avidyayā samāviddhā asmitādivaśīkṛtāḥ|
macchaktyaiva tirobhūtāstirodhānābhidhānayā || 2 ||
uccānnīce patantaste nīcādutpatayālavaḥ|
nibaddhāstrividhairbandhaiḥ sthānatrayavivartinaḥ || 3 ||
saṃsārāṅgāramadhyasthāḥ pacyamānāḥ svakarmaṇā|
sukhābhimānino duḥkhe nityamajñānadharṣitāḥ || 4 ||
tā yonīranudhāvantaścarācaravibhedinīḥ|
apūrvāpūrvabhūtābhiścitritābhiḥ svahetubhiḥ || 5 ||
dehendriyamanobuddhivedanābhiraharniśam|
jananāni prabadhnanto maraṇāni tathā tathā || 6 ||
kliśyamānā iti kleśaistaistairyogaviyogajaiḥ|
udyatkāruṇyasaṃtānanirvāpitatadāgasā || 7 ||
mayā jīvāḥ samīkṣyante śriyā duḥkhavivarjitāḥ|
so'nugraha iti proktaḥ śaktipātāparāhvayaḥ || 8 ||
karmasāmyaṃ bhajantyete prekṣyamāṇā mayā tadā|
apaścimā tanuḥ sā syājjīvānāṃ prekṣitā mayā || 9 ||
ahameva hi jānāmi śaktipātakṣaṇaṃ ca tam|
nāsau puruṣakāreṇa na cāpyanyena hetunā || 10 ||
kevalaṃ svecchayaivāhaṃ prekṣe kaṃcit kadāpyaham|
tataḥ prabhṛti sa svacchasvacchāntaḥkaraṇaḥ pumān || 11 ||
karmasāmyaṃ samāsādya śuklakarmavyapāśrayaḥ|
vedāntajñānasaṃpannaḥ sāṃkhyayogaparāyaṇaḥ || 12 ||
samyaksāttvatavijñānādviṣṇau sadbhaktimudvahan|
kālena mahatā yogī nirdhūtakleśasaṃcayaḥ | 13 ||
vidhūya vividhaṃ bandhaṃ dyotamānastatastataḥ|
prāpnoti paramaṃ brahma lakṣmīnārāyaṇātmakam || 14 ||
eṣā tu pañcamī śaktirmadīyānugrahātmikā|
svācchandyameva me hetustirobhāvādikarmaṇi || 15 ||
itthaṃ śakra vijānīhi nānuyojyamataḥ param|
śakraḥ
namaḥ saroruhāvāse namo nārāyaṇāśraye || 16 ||
namo nityānavadyāyai kalyāṇaguṇasindhave|
tvadvāgamṛtasaṃdohakṣālitaṃ me mahattamaḥ || 17 ||
bhūyo'haṃ śrotumicchāmi cicchkate rūpamuttamam|
śrīḥ
eko nārāyaṇo devaḥ paramātmā sanātanaḥ || 18 ||
sadā jñānabalaiśvaryavīryaśaktyojasāṃ nidhiḥ||
anādiraparicchedyo deśakālasvarūpataḥ || 19 ||
tasyāhaṃ paramā devī ṣāḍguṇyamahimojjvalā|
sarvakāryakarī śaktirahaṃtā nāma śāśvatī || 20 ||
saṃvidekā svarūpaṃ me svacchasvacchandanirbharā|
siddhayo viśvajīvānāmāyatante'khilā mayi || 21 ||
ātmabhittau jagatsarvaṃ svecchayonmīlayāmyaham|
mayi lokāḥ sphurantyete jale śakunayo yathā || 22 ||
svācchandyādavarohāmi pañcakṛtyavidhāyinī|
sāhaṃ yadavarohāmi sā hi cicchaktirucyate || 23 ||
saṃkoco māmakaḥ so'yaṃ svacchasvacchandaciddhanaḥ|
asminnapi jagadbhāti darpaṇodaraśailavat || 24 ||
vajraratnavadevaiṣa svacchaḥ sphurati sarvadā|
caitanyamasya dharmo yaḥ prabhā bhānorivāmalā || 25 ||
tayā sphurati jīvo'sau svata evānurūpayā|
vidhatte pañca kṛtyāni jīvo'yamapi nityadā || 26 ||
yā vṛttirnīlapītādau sṛṣṭiḥ sā kathitā budhaiḥ|
saktiryā viṣaye tatra sā sthitiḥ parikīrtyate || 27 ||
gṛhītādviṣayādyo'sya virāmo'nyajighṛkṣayā|
sā saṃhṛtiḥ samākhyātā tattvaśāsraviśāradaiḥ || 28 ||
tadvāsanā tirobhāvo'nugrahastadvilāpanam|
grāhyagrasanaśīlo'yaṃ vahnivadgrasanaḥ sadā || 29 ||
puṣyatyeṣa sadā jīvo mātrayā me samindhanam|
āvidyaṃ matsvarūpaṃ tu vyākyātaṃ te purā mayā || 30 ||
śuddhavidyāsamāyogāt saṃkocaṃ yajjahātyasau|
tadā pradyotamāno'yaṃ sarvato muktabandhanaḥ || 31 ||
jñānakriyāsamāyogāt sarvavitsarvakṛtsadā|
anaṇuścāpyasaṃkocānmadbhāvāyopapadyate || 32 ||
yāvannirīkṣyate nāyaṃ mayā kāruṇyavattayā|
tāvatsaṃkucitajñānaḥ karaṇairviśvamīkṣate || 33 ||
cakṣuṣālokya vastūni vikalpya manasā tathā|
abhimatyāpyahaṃkārād buddhayaiva hyadhyavasyati || 34 ||
jāgarājāmatha svapne karaṇairāntaraiścaran|
vihāya tat suṣuptau tu svarūpeṇāvatiṣṭhate || 35 ||
avasthāstā imāstisraḥ prākṛtyo naiva jīvagā|
turyāpi yā daśā jīve samādhisthe prajāyate || 36 ||
sāpi naivāsya kiṃ tveṣā śuddhasattvavyavasthitiḥ|
anavasthamanāghrātamakhilaiḥ prākṛtairguṇaiḥ || 37 ||
anaupādhikamacchedyaṃ jīvarūpaṃ tu cinmayapt|
evaṃrūpamapi tvetacchādyate'nādyavidyayā || 38 ||
sudṛśyāmātmabhūtāṃ māṃ naiva paśyatyasau tataḥ|
śakraḥ
sudṛśyāsi kathaṃ devi tvaṃ pramāṇātigā satī || 39 ||
vedāntā api naivatvāṃ viduritthaṃtayāmbuje|
śrīḥ
māṃ tu śakra vijānīhipratyakṣāṃ sarvadehinām|
samāhitamanā bhūtvā śrṛṇuṣvedaṃ mataṃ mama || 40 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre jīvasvarūpaprakāśo nāma trayodaśo'dhyāyaḥ
iti trayodaśo'dhyāyaḥ

<14>
caturdaśo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ
jñānasvarūpo bhagavān deśakālādyabheditaḥ|
vāsudevaḥ paraṃ brahma guṇaśūnyaṃ nirañjanam || 1 ||
sukhaṃ sadaikarūpaṃ tu ṣāḍguṇyamajarāmaram|
tasyāhaṃ paramā śaktirahaṃtā śāśvatī dhruvā || 2 ||
vyāpāraśaktireṣā me sisṛkṣālakṣaṇā bhavet|
ayutāyutakoṭyoghakoṭikoṭyayutāṃśataḥ || 3 ||
sāhaṃ sṛjāmi svācchandyād dvidhā bhedamupeyuṣī|
cetyacetanabhāvena cicchaktiścetano'nayoḥ || 4 ||
cetyacetanatāṃ prāptā saṃvideva madātmikā|
saṃvideva hi me rūpaṃsvacchasvacchandanirbharā || 5 ||
sā tvikṣurasavadyogāt styānatāṃ pratipadyate|
ato nirūpyamāṇaṃ taccetyaṃ cittvamupeṣyati || 6 ||
yathā hi vahninā līḍhamindhanaṃ tanmayaṃ bhavet|
evaṃ citā samālīḍhaṃ cetyaṃ cinmayatāṃ vrajet || 7 ||
nīle pīte sukhe duḥkhe citsvarūpamakhaṇḍitam|
viśinaṣṭi vikalpastaccitrayopādhisaṃpadā || 8 ||
vikalpo'pi hi madrūpaṃ svācchandyādeva nirmitam|
cetyaṃ vikalpyate yena bahirantarvyavasthayā || 9 ||
na bahirnaiva cāntastaccidrūpaṃ mama tat param|
vedyavedakarūpeṇa bhedyate me svayaṃtayā || 10 ||
yadvikalpairanākrāntaṃ yacchabdairakadarthitam|
yadupādhibhiramlānaṃ rūpaṃ taccetyatāṃ gatam || 11 ||
dūrāpāstavikalpena cetasā yatra bhūyate|
madhyamāṃ vṛttimāsthāya cetyaṃ saṃvittayā tadā || 12 ||
yathā cakṣusthitaṃ rūpaṃ bāhye svaṃ rūpamīkṣyate|
tathā jñānasthitaṃ rūpaṃ jñeye svaṃ rūpamīkṣyate || 13 ||
yathā vahnisamāviṣṭaṃ kāṣṭhaṃ tadrūpamīkṣyate|
tathā saṃvitsamāviṣṭaṃ cetyaṃ saṃvittayekṣyate || 14 ||
vedyaṃ vedanatāṃ nītvā yadā vettrā nirūpyate|
tadāvittimayī sāhaṃ pratyakṣā sphuṭabhāsinī || 15 ||
ahaṃtaiva hi cittatvaṃ vedyādbhinnaṃ svalakṣaṇam|
sā cāhameva tenāhaṃ sarvataḥ śuddhacinmayī || 16 ||
saṃplutedaṃpadadvīpe prāptaikadhye cidambudhau|
majjatāṃ caiva cetyānāmasmi hastāvalambanam || 17 ||
maddhyānāmṛtaniṣyandakṣālitāśeṣavāsanāḥ|
māmevātmani pasyanti cetyaughagrasanīṃ citam || 18 ||
mama cittaikarūpāyā vedyavedakatāṃ janāḥ|
avidyayaiva manyante matsaṃkalpitayā purā || 19 ||
na śāntā noditā nāpi madyamāhaṃ svarūpataḥ|
madvivekajuṣāmevaṃ prakāśe jāgarāsvapi || 20 ||
parityaktavibhāgena nistaraṅgeṇa cetasā|
jñāye vikalpyamānā tu pratyakṣāpyasmi vismṛtā || 21 ||
puraḥ sthito yathā bhāvaścetaso'nyābhilāṣiṇaḥ|
na bhāsate tathaivāhaṃ na bhāse vāsanājuṣām || 22 ||
bubhutsāvān yathā vṛttīrnirudhyānyatra cetasā|
pratyakṣamīkṣate vastu tathā māṃ śuddhasaṃvidam || 23 ||
sadaivāpratibaddhāyā bhāntyā eva vapurmama|
pratyakṣaṃ cetyasaṃcārakāle'pi vimalātmanām || 24 ||
yathā jātyā sitaṃ vasraṃ raktaṃ rāgeṇa kenacit|
punaḥ svavarṇamaprāpya naiva rāgāntaraṃ śrayet || 25 ||
nīlādyevaṃ vidan pītaṃ madhye śuddhacidātmani|
mayi cennaiva viśrāntaḥ pītaṃ vidyātkathaṃ nvayam || 26 ||
tathaivoccārayan vākyaṃ varṇādvarṇaṃ kathaṃ vrajet|
yadi madhye na viśrānto mayi śuddhacidātmani || 27 ||
evaṃ śuddhā svatantrāpi yadākāroparāgiṇī|
tattyāgāparasaṃcārā madhye śuddhaiva bhāmyaham || 28 ||
dakṣiṇetarasaṃcāranirodhānmadhyamāśritaḥ|
agnīṣomendhano bhāvaḥ prakāśayati me padam || 29 ||
dhiyā dhyeyamanālambya viṣayaṃ cāspṛśan bahiḥ|
yadantarā vedayate tanme rūpamanākulam || 30 ||
anuvṛttā tu yā samyak tejasyapi tamasyapi|
bhāti bhāve'pyabhāve'pi sā me tanurakarburā || 31 ||
nivṛttaviṣayecchasya madbhaktyullasitātmanaḥ|
āntaraṃ yadanālambamahaṃtvaṃ tadvapurmama || 32 ||
tadevābhyasyamānānāṃ dehaprāṇādyagocaram|
vivekināmahaṃrūpaṃ madbhāvenāvatiṣṭhate || 33 ||
tatastejo yathaivārkaṃ vyajyate na tu janyate|
bhāvaiścidrūpamapyevaṃ vyaktaṃ naiva ca janyate || 34 ||
bhāvairvinā yathā bhānuḥ samudeti nabhaḥsthale|
vedyairvinaiva me rūpamevaṃ pradyotate svayam || 35 ||
atyantācchasvabhāvatvāt sphaṭikādiryathā maṇiḥ|
uparakto japādyaistu svena rūpeṇa nekṣyate || 36 ||
matsaṃkalpasamudriktaiścetyaiḥ svacchāhamapyatha|
pṛthagjanairna lakṣyāsmi naivāhaṃ nāsmi tāvatā || 37 ||
kuṇḍalāderyathā bhinnā na lakṣyā kanakasthitiḥ|
na ca śakyā vinirdeṣṭuṃ tathāpyastyeva sā dhruvam || 38 ||
evaṃ nityā viśuddhā ca sukhaduḥkhādyabheditā|
svasaṃvedanasaṃvedyā mama saṃvinmayī sthitiḥ || 39 ||
vijñātari tathā jñāne jñeye jānātinānvayaḥ|
yo'yaṃ madanvayaḥ so'yaṃ pratyayārthāviśeṣitaḥ || 40 ||
deśakālakriyākārāḥ prasiddhā bhedahetavaḥ|
tān bhedayati yā saṃvittasyā bhedaḥ kuto bhavet || 41 ||
cetyabhedo hi yaḥ kālo bhūtāditritayātmakaḥ|
saṃvinmahodadhau so'pi vilīnastanmayo bhavet || 42 ||
yadā hi vartamānāyāṃ mayi bhūtabhaviṣyatī|
pratikṣipte tadā ceyaṃ naiva syādvartamānatā || 43 ||
ādhāro'hamaśeṣāṇāṃ naivādheyāsmi kenacit|
deśo'pyādhārataḥ klaptastato me naiva vidyate || 44 ||
kāpyavasthā na me sāsti yasyāṃ saṃvinna vartate|
tena māṃ ciddhanāmekāṃ sarvākārāmupāsate || 45 ||
kālo deśastathākāraḥ kriyā kartā ca karma ca|
karaṇaṃ saṃpradānaṃ ca bhavedyacca tataḥ phalam || 46 ||
bhogo bhoktā ca tatsarvaṃ vilīnaṃ saṃvidātmani|
devā daityāstathā nāgā gandhravā rakṣasāṃ gaṇāḥ || 47 ||
vidyādharāḥ piśācāśca bhūtāśceti gaṇāṣṭakam|
manujā bahudhātmāno varṇakarmādibheditāḥ || 48 ||
paśavo'tha mṛgāścaiba pakṣiṇaśca sarīsṛpāḥ|
sthāvarāśca tathaivānye kapūyacaraṇātmakāḥ || 49 ||
svargasthā narakasthāśca lokāścaiva caturdaśa|
sariddvīpasamudrāśca vividhā hyaṇḍapaddhatiḥ || 50 ||
uccāvacāni tattvāni vividhāḥ śabdarāśayaḥ|
bhogyaṃ bhogopakaraṇaṃ bhogasthānaṃ ca yat smṛtam || 51 ||
kośāḥ ṣaṭ kośajāścaiva cetanācetanātmakāḥ|
śuddhāśuddhamayau bhāvau puruṣārthaścaturvidhaḥ || 52 ||
sarvaṃ prakṛtibhirnaddhaṃ kālena kalitaṃ tathā|
ityetatsakalaṃ vastu bhāvābhāvasvarūpakam || 53 ||
amanmayaṃ manmayaṃ ca mayi līnamavasthitam|
sarvātmanā sadaivāhaṃ svacchasvacchandacinmayī || 54 ||
lakṣyā sukhamayī śāntā bhāve bhāve vipaścitā|
evaṃ vyavasthitāyā me tirobhāvābhidhānayā || 55 ||
baddhā śaktyā tu cicchaktiḥ svato māṃ naiva vindati|
yadā nirvidyate sāsau madanugrahabindunā || 56 ||
upāyairmāṃ tadārādhya jīvaścicchaktisaṃjñakaḥ|
saṃkṣiṇvan nikhilānkleśānvidhūnvanvāsanārajaḥ || 57 ||
saṃprāpya jñānasadbhāvaṃ yogakṣapitabandhanaḥ|
māmeva paramānandamayīṃ lakṣmīṃ sa vindati || 58 ||
iti caturdaśo'dhyāyaḥ

<15>
pañcadaśo'dhyāyaḥ
śakraḥ
namaste padmasaṃbhūte namaḥ kamalamālini|
namaḥ kamalavāsinyai govindagṛhamedhini || 1 ||
namaste kaṃjakiñjalkakalpitālakavibhrame|
sarvajñe sarvabhūtānāmantaḥsthe sarvasākṣiṇi || 2 ||
tvadvaktrakamalodbhūtaṃ sarvaṃ tadavadhāritam|
tattvatsṛṣṭaṃ tvayā trātaṃ tvayyeva layameṣyati || 3 ||
mātā mānaṃ mitimeyaṃ vidhā etāstvadātmikāḥ|
tvāmevārādhya jīvāste taranti bhavasāgaram || 4 ||
evamādi mayā devi tattvatastvavadhāritam|
kautūhalamidaṃ me'dya vartate padmasaṃbhave || 5 ||
toṣaṇīyāsi kena tvamupāyenāmbujāsane|
paramaḥ puruṣārtho yastvatprītistasya sādhanam || 6 ||
paramaḥ puruṣārtho mokṣaḥ| tasya tvatprītiḥ sādhanam| yathā cāhuḥ "śreyo na hyaravindalocanamanaḥkāntāprasādādṛte saṃsṛtyakṣaravaiṣṇavādhvasu nṛṇāṃ saṃbhāvyate karhicit" iti|
tvatprītau ka upāyaḥ syātkīdṛśaḥ kiṃvidhaḥ smṛtaḥ|
etanme sakalaṃ brūhi namaste padmasaṃbhave || 7 ||
śrīḥ
cāturātmyaṃ paraṃ brahma saccidānandalakṣaṇam|
sarvaṃ sarvottaraṃ sarvabhūtāntaḥsthamanāmayam || 8 ||
"satyaṃ jñānam" ityādiśrutipratipādyaṃ svarūpalakṣaṇamucyate sadityādinā| sarvopādānatvāt sarvātmatvācca sarvamityucyate| tathāpi svetarasamastavastuvilakṣaṇatvamāha sarvottaramiti|
vāsudevaḥ paraṃ brahma nārāyaṇamayaṃ mahat|
tasyāhaṃ paramā śaktirahaṃtānandacinmayī || 9 ||
nārāyaṇamayamiti mayaṭ svārthe|
bhinnā'bhinnā ca varte'haṃ jyotsneva himadīdhiteḥ|
tāvāvāṃ tattvamekaṃ tu dvidhā bhūtau vyavasthitau || 10 ||
prapañca iva brahmaṇa evāvasthābhedaḥ śrīriti keṣāṃcinmatamanabhimatyāha bhinneti|
brahma nārāyaṇaṃ māṃ yajjñānenaivāpruyādyatiḥ|
panthā nānyo'sti vijñānādayanāya vipaścitām || 11 ||
yatiḥ niyatātmavān| nātra caturthāśramī vivakṣitaḥ|
jñānaṃ tacca vivekotthaṃ sarvataḥ śuddhamavraṇam|
vāsudevaikaviṣayamapunarbhavakāraṇam || 12 ||
vivekaḥ brahmaṇaḥ sarvavilakṣaṇatvajñānam|
jñāne tasmin samutpanne viśate māmanantaram|
taistairupāyaiḥ prītāhaṃ jīvānāmamalātmanām || 13 ||
udbhāvayāmi tajjñānamātmajyotiḥ pradarśakam|
upāyāste ca catvāro mama prītivivardhanāḥ || 14 ||
karmajñānabhaktinyāsākhyāḥ catvāro yogā atra vivakṣitāḥ|
śakraḥ
bhagavatyaravindasthe paṅkajekṣaṇakāmini|
upāyāḥ ke ca catvārastānme darśaya paṅkaje || 15 ||
śrīḥ
upāyāṃścaturaḥ śakra śṛṇu matprītivardhanān|
yairahaṃ paramāṃ prītiṃ yāsyāmyanapagāminīm || 16 ||
svajātivihitaṃ karma sāṃkhyaṃ yogastathaiva ca|
sarvatyāgaśca vidvadbhirupāyāḥ kathitā ime || 17 ||
caturbhirlakṣaṇairyuktaṃ trividhaṃ karma vaidikam|
svavarṇāśramasaṃbandhi nityanaimittikātmakam || 18 ||
lakṣaṇacatuṣkaṃ viṃśe śloke vakṣyate| nityanaimittikakāmyabhedena traividhyam|
akāmahatasaṃsiddhaṃ karma tat pūrvasādhanam|
caturvidhastu saṃnyāsastatra kāryo vipaścitā || 19 ||
kāmyakarmasu viśeṣamāha akāmeti| phalaviśeṣakāmanayā kṛtāni karmāṇi kāmyāni jyotiṣṭomādayaḥ| tāni ca kāmahatānītyucyante| kāmanāṃ vinā kevalaṃ bhagavatprītyarthaṃ kṛtāni tānyakāmahatāni| anena kāmyānāmapi karmaṇāṃ kāmanāṃ vinānuṣṭhānamanumanyate| paraṃ tvetadupāyāntaraniṣṭhaviṣayam| nyāsayoganiṣṭhānāṃ tu kāmyakarmaṇāṃ sarvathā svarūpato'pi tyāga eva, na tu kāmanāṃ vināpyanuṣṭhānamiti siddhānto'vagantavyaḥ|
mantroktadevatāyāṃ vā prakṛtāvindriyeṣu vā|
parasmin devadeve vā vāsudeve janārdane || 20 ||
bhagavati vāsudeve karmaṇāṃ saṃnyasanameva mumukṣubhiḥ kartavyam| mantradevatādau vinyasanaṃ tu bubhukṣuviṣayamiti dhyeyam|
pūrvaṃ kartṛtvasaṃnyāsaḥ phalasaṃnyāsa eva ca|
karmaṇāmapi saṃnyāso devadeve janārdane || 21 ||
`bhagavāneva svaśeṣataikarasena mayā svakīyaiścopakaraṇaiḥ svārādhanaikaprayojanāya svaśeṣabhūtamidaṃ karma svayameva kārayati' ityanusaṃdhānaprakāro vivakṣitaḥ|
śāstrīyamācarannevaṃ nityanaimittikātmakam|
madārādhanakāmaḥ sañśaśvat prīṇāti māṃ naraḥ || 22 ||
iti te leśataḥ proktaṃ śrutismṛtinidarśitam|
dvitīyaṃ sāṃkhyavijñānamupāyaṃ śrṛṇu sāṃpratam || 23 ||
saṃkhyāstisro hi mantavyāḥ sāṃkhyaśāsranidarśitāḥ|
prathamā laukikī saṃkhyā dvitīyā carcanātmikā || 24 ||
trayāṇāmapyeṣāṃ jñānānāṃ vivaraṇamatraiva kariṣyate|
samīcīnā tu yā dhīḥ sā tṛtīyā paripaṭhyate|
saṃkhyātrayasamūho yaḥ sāṃkhyaṃ tatparipaṭhyate || 25 ||
saṃkhyāḥ jñānāni pūrvoktāni| teṣāṃ samūhaḥ sāṃkhyamityucyate| etadeva jñānayoga ityucyate|
pṛthivyāpastathā tejo vāyurākāśameva ca|
ahaṃkāro mahāṃścaiva prakṛtiḥ paramā tathā || 26 ||
laukikī lokaviṣayā saṃkhyocyate'nena ślokena| uparitanaiḥ ślokairasyā vivaraṇaṃ kriyate|
etāḥ prakṛtayasttvaṣṭau tāsāṃ vyākhyāmimāṃ śṛṇu|
prakṛtisrividhā proktā māyā sūtirguṇātmikā || 27 ||
māyā prasūtiḥ traiguṇyamiti prakṛtibhedāḥ| yadyapi prakṛtiḥ sūkṣmaiva; tathāpi tatraiva sūkṣmasūkṣmatarasūkṣmatamarūpeṇa traividhyādevaṃ vyavahāraḥ|
niḥsaktāsaktamadvaitamataraṅgamanaśvaram|
acetanānāṃ paramaṃ saukṣmyaṃ māyeti gīyate || 28 ||
īṣaducchūnatā tasyāḥ prasūtiriti gīyate|
guṇatrayasamunmeṣaḥ sāmyena prakṛtiḥ parā || 29 ||
avyaktamakṣaraṃ yoniravidyā triguṇā sthitiḥ|
māyā svabhāva ityādyāḥ śabdāḥ paryāyavācakāḥ || 30 ||
avāntarabhedaiḥ saha saṃkalayya prakṛtiparyāyanāmāni avyaktādīni| upaniṣadi "avyaktamakṣare līyate| akṣaraṃ tamasi līyate| tamaḥ paramātmanyekīyabhavati" iti trayāṇāmeṣāṃ vyavahāraḥ kriyate|
sattvaṃ rajastamaśceti guṇā ete trayo matāḥ|
tatra sattvaṃ laghu jñeyaṃ sukharūpamacañcalam || 31 ||
prakāśo nāma tadvṛttiścaitanyodgrahaṇātmakaḥ|
rajo'pi ca laghu jñeyaṃ duḥkharūpaṃ ca cañcalam || 32 ||
caitanyasya jñānasya udgrahaṇam unmeṣa ityarthaḥ; vastuyāthātmyagrahaṇaśīlateti yāvat|
pravṛttirnāma tadvṛttiḥ spandaheturanaśvaraḥ|
tamo nāma guru jñeyaṃ moharūpamacañcalam || 33 ||
niyamo nāma tadvṛtiḥ vkacit svāpanalakṣaṇam|
na tadasti pṛthivyāṃ vā divi vyomni ca vāsava || 34 ||
niyamo bandhaḥ|
bhūtaṃ prakṛtijairmuktaṃ yadebhiḥ syāt tribhirguṇaiḥ|
ete cittamadhiṣṭhāya guṇā indriyagāstathā || 35 ||
sukhaṃ duḥkhaṃ tathā mohaṃ viṣayasthāśca kurvate|
śarīrendriyatāṃ yātā guṇāḥ karmāṇi kurvate|
iti yasya matirnityā sa guṇātyayamaśnute || 36 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre upāyaprakāraprakāśo nāma pañcadaśo'dhyāyaḥ
iti pañcadaśo'dhyāyaḥ

<16>
ṣoḍaśo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ
vyākhyānaṃ mahataḥ śakra śṛṇuṣvāvahito mama|
vaiṣamyasya samunmeṣo guṇānāṃ prathamo hi yaḥ || 1 ||
anena guṇatrayasya sāmyāvasthāyāṃ prakṛtiriti, vaiṣamyāvasthāyāṃ mahāniti ca vyavahāra ityuktaṃ bhavati|
sa mahānnāma tasyāpi vidhāstisraḥ prakīrtitāḥ|
sāttviko buddhirityukto rājasaḥ prāṇa eva hi || 2 ||
tāmasaḥ kāla ityuktasteṣāṃ vyākhyāmimāṃ śṛṇu|
buddhiradhyavasāyasya prāṇaḥ prayatanasya ca || 3 ||
kālasya tāmasatvamatrocyate| aupaniṣadaprakriyā kālasyāprākṛtatvameveti vadanti|
kālaḥ kalanarūpasya pariṇāmasya kāraṇam|
mahato'pi vikurvāṇādahaṃkāro vyajāyata || 4 ||
kalanaṃ vipariṇatiḥ| āmrādiśalāṭoḥ tatphalātmanā, yuvadehasya valibhavṛddhadehātmanā ca yā pariṇatiḥ setyarthaḥ|
sa cāpi trividho jñeyo guṇavaiṣamyasaṃbhavāt|
tāmasādviyadādistu tanmātragaṇa ujjvalaḥ || 5 ||
jātaḥ sattvasamudriktādbuddhīndriyagaṇo mahān|
karmendriyagaṇaścāpi rājasādubhayātmakam || 6 ||
karmendriyāṇāṃ rājasatvaṃ, manasaḥ sāttvikarājasatvamiti vibhāgastantrikaikadeśināṃ matena| vastutastu ekādaśāpīndriyāṇi sāttvikānītyeva bahūnāṃ pāñcarātrikāṇāṃ matam| "devā vaikārikā daśa| ekādaśaṃ manasca" iti viṣṇupurāṇe coktam|
ubhayasmāt samudbhūtamitīyaṃ tattvapaddhatiḥ|
atra prakṛtirekaiva mūlabhūtā sanātanī || 7 ||
tattvapaddhatiḥ| acittattvapaddhatirityarthaḥ| itthamatra vibhāgaḥ tattvaṃ dvividham, acittattvaṃ cittattvaṃ ceti| tatrācittattvaṃ caturviṃśatidhā prakṛtimahadahaṃkārāsrayaḥ, tanmātrāṇi pañca, mahābhūtāni pañca, ekādaśendriyāṇīti| cittattvaṃ dvedhā jīva īśvaraśceti| āhatya ṣaḍviṃśatistattvāni|
mahadādyāstu saptānye kāryakāraṇarūpiṇaḥ|
tanmātrebhyaḥ samudbhūtā viśeṣā viyadādayaḥ || 8 ||
buddhikarmendriyagaṇau pañcakau mana eva ca|
vikārā eva vijñeyā ete ṣoḍaśa cintakaiḥ || 9 ||
caturviṃśatiretāni tattvāni kathitāni te|
yāvānyaścātra vaktavyo viśeṣo yādṛśastviha || 10 ||
sa sarvaḥ kathitaḥ pūrvaṃ tava vṛtraniṣūdana|
viṃśatyā ca tribhiścaiva vikāraiḥ svaiḥ samanvitā || 11 ||
iyaṃ prakṛtiravyaktā kathitā te surādhipa|
vyaktāvyaktamayī saiṣā nityaṃ prasavadharmiṇī || 12 ||
prakṛtyavasthāyāmavyaktā, pariṇatyavasthāyāṃ vyaktā| kāryakāraṇayorabhedādevamuktiḥ|
vilakṣaṇā sā vijñeyā cicchaktiravinaśvarā|
sa jīvaḥ kathitaḥ sadbhistattvaśāstraviśāradaiḥ || 13 ||
ayaṃ svarasataḥ śuddhapariṇāmavivarjitaḥ|
kūṭasthaściddhano nityo hyananto'pratisaṃkramaḥ || 14 ||
svarasataḥ svabhāvataḥ| anantaḥ; saṃkhyayā jñānādiguṇaiścāparicchinna ityarthaḥ|
imau svarasato'saktau saktātmānāviva sthitau|
prakṛtiḥ puruṣaścaiva mahadbhyaśca mahattarau || 15 ||
liṅgagrāhyāvubhau nityāvaliṅgau cāpyubhāvapi|
sādharmyamevamādyevamanayorunnayedbudhaḥ || 16 ||
vaidharmyamanayoḥ śakra kathyamānaṃ nibodha me|
prakṛtistriguṇā nityaṃ satataṃ pariṇāminī || 17 ||
avivekāpyaśuddhā ca sarvajīvasamā sadā|
viṣayo'cetanā caiva sukhaduḥkhavimohinī || 18 ||
madhyasthaḥ puruṣo nityaḥ kriyāvānapyavihvalaḥ|
sākṣī dṛśistathā draṣṭā śuddho'nanto guṇātmakaḥ || 19 ||
vaidharmyamanayoretat prakṛtiṃ cānayoḥ śṛṇu|
yā sā sadasadākhyādivikalpavikalā dhruvā || 20 ||
vikalpavikaleti| vividhapariṇatyabhāginītyarthaḥ|
nityoditā sadānandā pūrṇaṣāḍguṇyavigrahā|
ahaṃ nārāyaṇī śaktirviṣṇoḥ śrīrapāyinī || 21 ||
mattaḥ prabhavato hyetau mayyeva layameṣyataḥ|
sāhametāvatī bhāvairvividhairvistṛtiṃ gatā || 22 ||
nārāyaṇe pratiṣṭhāya punastasmādudemyaham|
eko nārāyaṇo viṣṇurvāsudevaḥ sanātanaḥ || 23 ||
apṛthagbhūtaśaktitvādadvaitaṃ brahma niṣkalam|
jñānaśaktibalaiśvaryavīryatejomahodadhiḥ || 24 ||
śkatiśaktimatorapṛthaksiddhasaṃbandhāt ekatvam| ato nādvitīyatvavirodha iti bhāvaḥ|
nistaraṅgaḥ sadaivāsau jagadetaccarācaram|
iti te sāṃkhyavijñānaṃ leśataḥ śakra darśitam || 25 ||
asau nārāyaṇa eva carācarātmakajagadrūpatayāvatiṣṭhata iti yāvat|
yā tattvagaṇanā saṃkhyā tāṃ purā śīlayedbudhaḥ|
tataḥ sādharmyavaidharmyasvarūpaprabhavādikām || 26 ||
kuryāccarcātmikāṃ saṃkhyāṃ śāsratattvopadeśajām|
carcāyāmiha saṃkhyāyāṃ siddhāyāmamalātmani || 27 ||
carcā nāma punaḥ punaḥ pariśīlanam|
udeti yā samīcīnā saṃkhyā sattattvagocarā|
eṣā sā paramā saṃkhyā matprasādasamudbhavā || 28 ||
sāṃkhyadarśanametatte parisaṃkhyānamīritam|
evaṃ hi parisaṃkhyāya sāṃkhyā madbhāvamāgatāḥ || 29 ||
upāyo yastṛtīyaste vakṣyate yogasaṃjñakaḥ|
yogastu dvividho jñeyaḥ samādhiḥ saṃyamastathā || 30 ||
yamādyaṅgasamudbhūtā samādhiḥ saṃsthitiḥ pare|
brahmaṇi śrīnivāsākhye hyutthānaparivarjitā || 31 ||
sākṣātkāramayī sā hi sthitiḥ sadbrahmavedinām|
dhyātṛdhyeyāvibhāgasthā matprasādasamudbhavā || 32 ||
avibhāgastheti| "ātmeti tūpagacchanti grāhayanti ca" ityuktarītyā parasya brahmaṇo'vibhāgenopāsanamatrābhipretam|
saṃyamo nāma satkarma paramātmaikagocaram|
tatpunardvividhaṃ proktaṃ śārīraṃ mānasaṃ tathā || 33 ||
vistareṇābhidhāsyete samādhiḥ saṃyamastathā|
prathamo ya upāyaste karmātmā kathitaḥ purā || 34 ||
saṃjñānaṃ janayecchuddhamantaḥkaraṇaśodhanāt|
tena hi prīṇitā sāhaṃ sadācāraniṣevaṇāt || 35 ||
dadāmi buddhiyogaṃ tamantaḥkaraṇaśodhanam|
sāṃkhyaṃ nāma dvitīyo ya upāyaḥ kathitastava || 36 ||
parokṣaḥ śāsrajanyo'sau nirṇayo dṛḍhatāṃ gataḥ|
pratyakṣatāmivāpanno matprītiṃ janayetparām || 37 ||
ahaṃ saṃkhyāyamānā hi svarūpaguṇavaibhavaiḥ |
udbhāvayāmi tajjñānaṃ pratyakṣaṃ yadvivekajam || 38 ||
tṛtīyastu samādhyātmā pratyakṣo'viplavo dṛḍhaḥ|
prakṛṣṭasattvasaṃbhūtaḥ prasādātiśayo hi saḥ || 39 ||
tṛtīyasya vidhā yo'sau saṃyamo nāma varṇitaḥ|
bhogaiḥ śuddhaisridhodbhūtairatyantaprītaye mama || 40 ||
ahaṃ hi tatra viśvātmā viṣṇuśaktiḥ parāvarā|
sākṣādeva samārādhyā devo vā puruṣottamaḥ || 41 ||
iti te kathitāḥ samyagupāyāsraya ūrjitāḥ|
śṛṇūpāyaṃ caturthaṃ me sarvatyāgasamāhvayam || 42 ||
tatra dharmān parityajya sarvānuccāvacāṅgakān|
saṃsārānalasaṃtapto māmekāṃ śaraṇaṃ vrajet || 43 ||
ahaṃ hi śaraṇaṃ prāptā nareṇaānanyacetasā|
prāpayāmyātmanātmānaṃ nirdhūtākhilakalmaṣam || 44 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre upāyaprakāravivaraṇaṃ nāma ṣoḍaśo'dhyāyaḥ
iti ṣoḍaśo'dhyāyaḥ

<17>
saptadaśo'dhyāyaḥ
śakraḥ
namaste kamalāvāse jananyai sarvadehinām|
gṛhiṇyai padmanābhasya namaste sarasīruhe || 1 ||
upāyāste trayaḥ pūrve kathitā avadhāritāḥ|
vyācakṣvāmba caturthaṃ tamupāyaṃ paramambuje || 2 ||
śrīḥ
eko nārāyaṇo devo vāsudevaḥ sanātanaḥ|
cāturātmyaṃ paraṃ brahma saccidānandamavraṇam || 3 ||
ekāhaṃ paramā śaktistasya devī sanātanī|
karomi sakalaṃ kṛtyaṃ sarvabhāvānugāminī || 4 ||
śāntānantacidānandaṃ yad brahma paramaṃ dhruvam|
mahāvibhūtisaṃsthānaṃ sarvataḥ samatāṃ gatam || 5 ||
mahāvibhūtiḥ nityavibhūtiḥ| "tripādasyāmṛtaṃ divi" ityuktarītyā līlāvibhūtyapekṣayā tasyā mahattvāt mahāvibhūtitvam|
tasya śaktirahaṃ brāhnī śāntānandacidātmikā|
mahāvibhūtiranaghā sarvataḥ samatāṃ gatā || 6 ||
āśvāsanāya jīvānāṃ yattanmūrtīkṛtaṃ mahaḥ|
nārāyaṇaḥ paraṃ brahma divyaṃ nayananandanam || 7 ||
tadā mūrtimatī sāhaṃ śaktirnārāyaṇī parā|
samā samavibhaktāṅgā sarvāvayavasundarī || 8 ||
tayornau paramaṃ vyoma nirduḥkhaṃ padamuttamam|
ṣāḍguṇyaprasaro diviyaḥ svācchandyāddeśatāṃ gataḥ || 9 ||
madīyecchayā ṣāḍguṇyaprasara evāprākṛtadivyadeśatāṃ prāpta ityarthaḥ|
svakarmanirataiḥ siddhairvedavedāntapāragaiḥ|
anekajanmasaṃtānaniḥśeṣitakaṣāyakaiḥ || 10 ||
kleśena mahatā siddhairantarāyātigaiḥ kramāt|
saṃkhyāvidhividhānajñaiḥ sāṃkhyaiḥ saṃkhyānapāragaiḥ || 11 ||
pratyāhṛtendriyagrāmairdhāraṇādhyānaśālibhiḥ|
yaugaiḥ samāhitaiḥ śaśvatkleśena yadavāpyate || 12 ||
acchidrāḥ pañcakālajñāḥ pañcayajñavicakṣaṇāḥ|
pūrṇe varṣaśate dhīrāḥ prāpnuvanti yadañjasā || 13 ||
acchidrāḥ; bhagavadananubhavarūpacchidrarahitāḥ| yathoktam "yanmuhūrtaṃ kṣaṇaṃ vāpi vāsudevo na cintyate| sā hānistanmahacchidraṃ sā bhrāntiḥ sā ca vikriyā||" iti| abhigamanopādānejyāsvādhyāyayogākhyāḥ pañca kālāḥ|
yattatpurāṇamākāśaṃ sarvasmāt paramaṃ dhruvam|
yatpadaṃ prāpya tattvajñā mucante sarvabandhanaiḥ || 14 ||
sūryakoṭipratīkāśāḥ pūrṇendvayutasaṃnibhāḥ|
yasmin pade virājante muktāḥ saṃsārabandhanaiḥ || 15 ||
indriyacchidravidhurā dyotamānāśca sarvataḥ|
aniṣyandā anāhārāḥ ṣāḍguṇyatanavo'malāḥ || 16 ||
ekāntino mahābhāgā yatra paśyanti nau sadā|
kṣapayitvādhikārān svān śaśvatkālena bhūyasā || 17 ||
kṣapayitveti| atra "yāvadadhikāramavasthitirādhikārikāṇām" iti śārīrakasūtraṃ tadupabṛṃhaṇāni vacanāni cānusaṃdheyāni|
vedhaso yatra modante śaṃkarāḥ sapuraṃdarāḥ|
sūrayo nityasaṃsiddhāḥ sarvadā sarvadarśinaḥ || 18 ||
vaiṣṇavaṃ paramaṃ rūpaṃ sākṣātkurvanti yatra te|
aṣṭākṣaraikasaktānāṃ dviṣaṭkārṇaratātmanām || 19 ||
aṣṭākṣaraḥ nārāyaṇāṣṭākṣaramantraḥ| dviṣaṭkārṇaḥ vāsudevadvādaśākṣaramantraḥ|
ṣaḍakṣaraprasaktānāṃ praṇavāsaktacetasām|
jitaṃtāsaktacittānāṃ tārikāniratātmanām || 20 ||
ṣaḍhakṣaraḥ śrīviṣṇuṣaḍakṣaramantraḥ| jitaṃtā "jitate puṇḍarīkākṣa" ityādiślokarūpamantraḥ| asya mahi mādikamahirbudhnyasaṃhitāyāmuktaṃ veditavyam| tārikā hrīṃmantraḥ|
anutārāprasaktānāṃ yat padaṃ vimalātmanām|
anantavihageśānaviṣvaksenādayo'malāḥ || 21 ||
anutārā śrīṃmantraḥ|
madājñākāriṇo yatra modante sakaleśvarāḥ|
tatra divyavapuḥ śrīmān devadevo janārdanaḥ || 22 ||
anantabhogaparyaṅke niṣaṇṇaḥ susukhojjvale|
vijñānaiśvaryavīryasthaiḥ śaktitejobalolbaṇaiḥ || 23 ||
āyudhairbhūṣaṇairdivyairadbhutaiḥ samalaṃkṛtaḥ|
pañcātmanā suparṇena pakṣirājena sevitaḥ || 24 ||
pañcātmanā pañcopaniṣanmantrasvarūpeṇa|
sārūpyameyuṣā sākṣācchrīvatsakṛtalakṣmaṇā|
senānyā sevitaḥ samyagviṣvaksenena dīpyatā || 25 ||
sārūpyaṃ śaṅkhacakrādinā bhagavatsārūpyam| tadevāha śrīvatseti| idaṃ ca viśeṣaṇadvayaṃ senānyetyanenānveti|
kṣemāya sarvalokānāmādhyānāya manīṣiṇām|
muktaye'khilabandhānāṃ rūpadānāya yoginām || 26 ||
āste nārāyaṇaḥ śrīmān vāsudevaḥ sanātanaḥ|
sukumāro yuvā devaḥ śrīvatsakṛtalakṣaṇaḥ || 27 ||
caturbhujo viśālākṣaḥ kirīṭī kaustubhaṃ vahan|
hāranūpurakeyūrakāñcīpītāmbarojjvalaḥ || 28 ||
vanamālāṃ dadhaddivyāṃ pañcaśaktimayīṃ parām|
sarvāvayavasaṃpannaḥ sarvāvayavasundaraḥ || 29 ||
sṛṣṭisthitisaṃhāranigrāhānugrahāḥ pañca śakyaḥ|
rājarājo'khilasyāsya viśvasya parameśvaraḥ|
kāntasya tasya devasya viṣṇoḥ sadguṇaśālinaḥ || 30 ||
dayitāhaṃ sadā devī jñānānandamayī parā|
anavadyānavadyāṅgī nityaṃ taddharmadharmiṇī || 31 ||
īśvarī sarvabhūtānāṃ padmākṣī padmamālinī|
śaktibhiḥ sevitā nityaṃ sṛṣṭisthityādibhiḥ parā || 32 ||
īśvarīti| īṣṭe ityarthe kartari auṇādiko varaṭ pratyayaḥ|
dvātriṃśatā sahasreṇa sṛṣṭiśaktibhirāvṛtā|
vṛtā taddviguṇābhiśca divyābhiḥ sthitiśaktibhiḥ || 33 ||
tataśca dviguṇābhiśca pūrṇā saṃhṛtiśaktibhiḥ|
nāyikā sarvaśaktīnāṃ sarvalokamaheśvarī || 34 ||
mahiṣī devadevasya sarvakāmadughā vibhoḥ|
tulyā guṇavayorūpairmanaḥpramathanī hareḥ || 35 ||
taistairanuguṇairbhāvairahaṃ devasya śārṅgiṇaḥ|
karomi sakalaṃ kṛtyaṃ nityaṃ taddharmadharmiṇī || 36 ||
sāhamaṅke sthitā viṣṇordevadevasya śārṅgiṇaḥ|
lālitā tena cātyantaṃ sāmarasyamupeyuṣī || 37 ||
kadācit sarvadarśinyāḥ kṛpā me svayamudgatā|
kliśyataḥ prāṇino dṛṣṭvā saṃsārajvalanodare || 38 ||
kathaṃ nvime bhaviṣyanti duḥkhottīrṇāḥ sukhottarāḥ|
saṃsāraparasīmānamāpruyurmāṃ kathaṃ nviti || 39 ||
sāhamantaḥ kṛpāviṣṭā devadevamacūcudam|
bhagavan devadeveśa lokanātha mama priya || 40 ||
sarvāde sarvamadhyānta sarva sarvottarācyuta|
govinda puṇḍarīkākṣa purāṇa puruṣottama || 41 ||
dustarāpārasaṃsārasāgarottārakāraṇa|
vyaktāvyaktajñakālākhyaklṛptabhāvacatuṣṭaya || 42 ||
vāsudeva jagannātha saṃkarṣaṇa jagatprabho|
pradyumna subhaga śrīmannaniruddhāparājita || 43 ||
nānāvibhavasaṃsthāna nānāvibhavabhājana|
divyaśāntoditānandaṣāḍguṇyodayavigraha || 44 ||
sphuratkirīṭakeyūrahāranūpurakaustubha|
pītāmbara mahodāra puṇḍarīkanibhekṣaṇa || 45 ||
caturmūrte caturvyūha śaradindīvaradyute|
abhirāmaśarīreśa nārāyaṇa jaganmaya || 46 ||
amī hi prāṇinaḥ sarve nimagnāḥ kleśasāgare|
uttāraṃ prāṇināmasmātkathaṃ cintayasi prabho || 47 ||
ityukto devadeveśaḥ smayamāno'bravīdidam|
aravindāsane devi padmagarbhe saroruhe || 48 ||
uttārahetavo'mīṣāmupāyā vihitā mayā|
karma sāṃkhyaṃ tathā yoga iti śāstravyapāśrayāḥ || 49 ||
pratyavocamahaṃ devamityuktā puruṣottamam|
devadeva na te śakyāḥ kartuṃ kālena gacchatā || 50 ||
kālo hi kalayanneva svatantro bhavadātmakaḥ|
jñānaṃ sattvaṃ balaṃ caiṣāmāyuśca vinikṛntati || 51 ||
antaḥkaraṇasaṃsthā hi vāsanā vividhātmikāḥ|
tattatkālavaśaṃ prāpya yātayanti śarīriṇaḥ || 52 ||
udāsīno bhavānevaṃ prāṇināṃ karma kurvatām|
tattatkālānukūlāni tatphalāni prayacchati || 53 ||
yena tvaṃ bata saṃrabdhaḥ prāṇinaḥ pālayiṣyasi|
prabrūhi tamupāyaṃ me praṇatāyai janārdana || 54 ||
ityuktaḥ pratyuvācedaṃ bhagavānutsmayanniva|
saroruhe vijānīṣe sarvamevātmano gatam || 55 ||
māṃ tu jijñāsase devi tathāpi śṛṇu bhāmini|
upāyāścāpyapāyāśca śāsrīyā nirmitā mayā || 56 ||
upāyāḥ puṇyasaṃpādakāḥ jyotiṣṭomādayaḥ| apāyāḥ pāpasaṃpādakāḥ parahiṃsādayaḥ|
vihitā ya upāyāste niṣiddhāścetare matāḥ|
adho nayantyapāyāstaṃ ya enānanuvartate || 57 ||
ūrdhvaṃ nayantyupāyāstaṃ ya enānanuvartate|
upāyāpāyasaṃtyāgī madhyamāṃ vṛttimāśritaḥ || 58 ||
saṃtyāgīti| puṇyapāpasaṃpādakāni kāmyaniṣiddhāni parityajya nityanaimittikakriyāpara ityartaḥ|
māmekaṃ śaraṇaṃ prāpya māmevānte samaśnute|
ṣaḍaṅgaṃ tamupāyaṃ ca śṛṇu me padmasaṃbhave || 59 ||
māmekam; māmevetyarthaḥ| ekaśabdo'vadhāraṇārthaḥ san anyayogavyavacchede vartate| atredaṃ bodhyam śaraṇavaraṇāt pūrvaṃ madhyamavṛttyāśrayaṇaṃ bhavatu vā, mā vā| asti cet, śaraṇavaraṇe tvarāmupajanayati| na cet, vilambaḥ| śaraṇavaraṇānantaraṃ tu yāvajjīvaṃ madhyamavṛttyāśrayaṇamavaśyaṃ kartavyam| na cet vilambena phalaṃ syāt| pūrvakṛtāni tu karmāṇi nyāsabalādeva naśyanti| paścāt kṛtāni tu prāmādikāni na śliṣyanti| buddhipūrvakṛtānyapi prāyaścittāpanodyāni bhavanti| ataḥ cāndrāyaṇādiprāyaścittapariharaṇāya madhyamavṛttyāśraṇamāvaśyakaṃ vidhīyata iti|
ānukūlyasya saṃkalpaḥ prātikūlyasya varjanam|
rakṣiṣyatīti viśvāso goptṛtvavaraṇaṃ tathā || 60 ||
ātmanikṣepakārpaṇye ṣaḍvidhā śaraṇāgatiḥ|
evaṃ māṃ śaraṇaṃ prāpya vītaśokabhayaklamaḥ || 61 ||
nirārambho nirāśīśca nirmamo nirahaṃkṛtiḥ|
māmeva śaraṇaṃ prāpya taret saṃsārasāgaram || 62 ||
satkarmaniratāḥ śuddhāḥ sāṃkhyayogavidastathā|
nārhanti śaraṇasthasya kalāṃ koṭitamīmapi || 63 ||
satkarmaniratāḥ; karmayoganiṣṭhāḥ| sāṃkhyavidaḥ; jñānayoginaḥ| yogavidaḥ; bhaktiyoginaḥ|
iti tasya vacaḥ śrutvā devadevasya śārṅgiṇaḥ|
prītāhamabhavaṃ śakra tadidaṃ varṇitaṃ tava || 64 ||
śakraḥ
devapriye mahādevi namaste paṅkajāsane|
ānukūlyādikaṃ bhāvaṃ mama vyācakṣva vistarāt || 65 ||
śrīḥ
ānukūlyamiti proktaṃ sarvabhūtānukūlatā|
antaḥ sthitāhaṃ sarveṣāṃ bhāvānāmiti niścayāt || 66 ||
ānukūlyasaṃkalpo na kevalamāvayorviṣaye| kiṃtu asmaccheṣabhūteṣu sarveṣvapi bhūteṣvityāha sarveti|
mayīva sarvabhūteṣu hyānukūlyaṃ samācaret|
tathaiva prātikūlyaṃ ca bhūteṣu parivarjayet || 67 ||
tyāgo garvasya kārpaṇyaṃ śrutaśīlādijanmanaḥ|
aṅgasāmagryasaṃpatteraśakterapi karmaṇaām || 68 ||
adhikārasya cāsiddherdeśakālaguṇakṣayāt|
upāyā naiva sidyanti hyapāyā bahulāstathā || 69 ||
iti yā garvahānistaddainyaṃ kārpaṇyamucyate|
śakteḥ sūpasadatvācca kṛpāyogācca śāśvatāt || 70 ||
īśeśitavyasaṃbandhādanidaṃprathamādapi|
rakṣiṣyatyanukūlānna iti yā sudṛḍhā matiḥ || 71 ||
sa viśvāso bhavecchakra sarvaduṣkṛtanāśanaḥ|
karuṇāvānapi vyaktaṃ śaktaḥ svāmyapi dehinām || 72 ||
aprārthito na gopāyediti tatprārthanāmatiḥ|
gopāyitā bhavetyevaṃ goptṛtvavaraṇaṃ smṛtam || 73 ||
tena saṃrakṣyamāṇasya phale svāmyaviyuktatā|
keśavārpaṇaparyantā hyātmanikṣepa ucyate || 74 ||
nikṣepāparaparyāyo nyāsaḥ pañcāṅgasaṃyutaḥ|
saṃnyāsastyāga ityuktaḥ śaraṇāgatirityapi || 75 ||
upāyo'yaṃ caturthaste proktaḥ śīghraphalapradaḥ|
asmin hi vartamānānāṃ vidhau vipraniṣevite || 76 ||
pūrve traya upāyāste bhaveyuramanoharāḥ|
ānukūlyetarābhyāṃ ca vinivṛttirapāyataḥ || 77 ||
kārpaṇyenāpyupāyānāṃ vinivṛttirihoditā|
rakṣiṣyatīti viśvāsādabhīṣṭopāyakalpanam || 78 ||
goptṛtvavaraṇaṃ nāma svābhiprāyanivedanam|
sarvajño'pi hi viśveśaḥ sadā kāruṇiko'pi san || 79 ||
śaraṇyasya sarvajñatvāt karuṇākaratvācca svayamevārtān rakṣiṣyatītyāśaṅkyāha sarvajño'pīti| saṃsāratantravāhitvaṃ nāma līlāvibhūtinirvahaṇam| vyājānapekṣarakṣaṇe sukṛdduṣkṛtsādhāraṇyena sarvarakṣaṇaprasaktau dharmādharmakṛtyākṛtyanyāyānyāyasaṃkaraprasaṅgaḥ| svecchayā kāṃścideva rakṣan anyān na rakṣati cet "samo'haṃ sarvabhūteṣu" ityuddhoṣayatastasya vaiṣamyanairghṛṇyaprasaṅga iti bhāvaḥ|
saṃsāratantravāhitvādrakṣāpekṣāṃ pratīkṣate|
ātmātmīyabharanyāso hyātmanikṣepa ucyate || 80 ||
hiṃsāsteyādayaḥ śāsrairapāyatvena darśitāḥ|
karmasāṃkhyādayaḥ śāsrairupāyatvena darśitāḥ || 81 ||
apāyopāyasaṃtyāgī madhyamāṃ sthitimāsthitaḥ|
rakṣiṣyatīti niścitya nikṣiptasvasvagocaraḥ || 82 ||
budhyeta devadeveśaṃ goptāraṃ puruṣottamam|
śakraḥ
upāyāpāyayormadhye kīdṛśī sthitirambike || 83 ||
apāyopāyatāmeva kriyā sarvāvalambate|
svīkāre vyatirike ca niṣedhavidhiśāsrayoḥ || 84 ||
niṣiddhasvīkāre vihitāsvīkāre cāpāyaḥ| vihitasvīkāre niṣiddhāsvīkāre copāyaḥ|
dṛśyate karmaṇo vyaktamapāyopāyarūpatā|
śrīḥ
trividhāṃ paśya deveśa karmaṇo gahanāṃ gatim || 85 ||
niṣedhavidhiśāsrebhyastāṃ vidhāṃ ca nibodha me|
anarthasādhanaṃ kiṃcitkiṃciccāpyarthasādhanam || 86 ||
hiṃsādi anarthasādhanam| jyotiṣṭomādi svargādyarthasādhanam| prāyaścittādi anarthaparihārakam|
anarthaparihāraṃ ca kiṃcit karmopadiśyate|
trairāśyaṃ karmaṇāmevaṃ vijñeyaṃ śāsracakṣuṣā || 87 ||
apāyopāyasaṃjñau tu pūrvarāśī parityajet|
tṛtīyo dvividho rāśiranarthaparihārakaḥ || 88 ||
anarthaparihārakaṃ tṛtīyaṃ dvividham cāndrāyaṇādi prāyaścittam utpannānarthanāśakam| nityanaimittikādi bhāvyanarthaparihārakamiti|
prāyaścittātmakaḥ kaścidutpannānarthanāśanaḥ|
tamaṃśaṃ naiva kurvīta manīṣī pūrvarāśivat || 89 ||
tṛtīye prathamāṃ vidhāmāha prāyascitteti|
kriyamāṇaṃ na kasmaicidyadarthāya prakalpate|
akriyāvadanarthāya tattu karma samācaret || 90 ||
tṛtīye dvitīyāṃ vidhāmāha kriyamāṇetyādi| nityanaimittikarūpamityarthaḥ|
eṣā sā vaidikī niṣṭhā hyupāyāpāyamadhyamā|
asyāṃ sthito jagannāthaṃ prapadyeta janārdanam || 91 ||
sakṛdeva hi śāsrārthaḥ kṛto'yaṃ tārayennaram|
upāyāpāyasaṃyoge niṣṭhayā hīyate'nayā || 92 ||
apāyasaṃplave sadyaḥ prāyaścittaṃ samācaret|
prāyascittiriyaṃ sātra yatpunaḥ śaraṇaṃ śrayet || 93 ||
apāyasaṃplava iti| buddhipūrvāparādhasaṃbhava ityarthaḥ|
upāyānāmupāyatvasvīkāre'pyetadeva hi|
aviplavāya dharmāṇāṃ pālanāya kulasya ca || 94 ||
jyotiṣṭomādīnāṃ mokṣopāyatvasvīkāre'pi niṣṭhāpracyutirbhavatyeva| tasmāt tāni na kurvīta| ayamatra nirgalitārthaḥ bhaktiyoganiṣṭhānāṃ tadyogamahimneva prapattiyoganiṣṭhānāmapi prapattimahimnaiva pūrvatanāni sarvāṇyapi buddhipūrvakāṇyabuddhipūrvakāṇi ca mokṣavirodhikarmāṇi samūlanāśaṃ naśyanti| prapannānāṃ prāyaśaḥ prapatteranantaraṃ tādṛśāni karmāṇi na saṃbhavantyeva| kadācijjātānyapyabuddhipūrvakāṇi aśliṣṭāni bhavanti| buddhipūrvakāṇi tu punaḥ prapadanena naśyanti| akṛtapunaḥprapadanānāṃ tu svalpadaṇḍena tadbhogāttannāśaḥ| prārabdhakarmāṇyapi prapannasyārtitāratamyena sadyo vā taddehāvasāne vā naśyanti| bhaktiyogāt nyāsayogasyāyaṃ mahimātiśayaḥ bhaktiyogaḥ prārabdhakarmāṇi nāpohayitumalam| nyāsayogastu prārabdhakarmāṇyapyapohayitumalam| yadyārtyatiśayāt prapattikāle sadyastannāśo'pi prārthitaḥ, tadā sadya eva tāni nasyanti| yadi taddehāvasane prārthitaḥ, tadā taddehāvasāna eva naśyanti| na tu bhaktiyoganiṣṭhānāmiva tadbhogārthadehāntarāpādakāni| tathā cāhuḥ "sādhyabhaktistu sā hantrī prārabdhasyāpi bhūyasī" iti| kiṃca vaidikāni kāmyakarmāṇi niṣkāmanayā bhaktiyogāṅgatayānuṣṭhātumabhyanujñāyante| prapannānāṃ tu niṣkāmanayāpi teṣāmanuṣṭhānaṃ svarūpaviruddhamiti|
saṃgrahāya ca lokasya maryādāsthāpanāya ca|
priyāya mama viṣṇośca devadevasya śārṅgiṇaḥ || 95 ||
manīṣī vaidikācāraṃ manasāpi na laṅghayet|
yathā hi vallabho rājño nadīṃ rājñā pravartitām || 96 ||
lokopayoginīṃ ramyāṃ bahusasyavivardhinīm|
laṅghayañśūlamārohedanapekṣo'pi tāṃ prati || 97 ||
evaṃ vilaṅghayan martyo maryādāṃ vedanirmitām|
priyo'pi na priyo'sau me madājñāvyativartanāt || 98 ||
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ nityanaimittikakarmāṇi na sarvathā phalarahitāni| kiṃtu pratyavāyotpattinirodhaphalāni| ānuṣaṅgikamapi hi prājāpatyādilokaprāptirūpaphalaṃ smaryate| tatra viniyogapṛthaktvāt pratyavāyotpattinirodhaphalakāni tānyādriyante| pratyavāyaśca bhagavannigraha eva| evaṃca bhagavannigrahotpattinirodhāya tāni kriyanta iti na sarvātmanā nirarthakānīti| ataḥ "prayojanamanuddiśya na mando'pi pravartate" iti codyasya nāvakāśaḥ|
upāyatvagrahaṃ tatra varjayan manasā sudhīḥ|
caturthamāśrayannevamupāyaṃ śaraṇāśrayam || 99 ||
atītya sakalaṃ kleśaṃ saṃviśatyamalaṃ padam|
apāyopāyanirmuktāṃ madhyamāṃ sthitimāsthitā || 100 ||
śaraṇāgatiragryaiṣā saṃsārārṇavatāriṇī|
idaṃ śaraṇamajñānāmidameva vijānatām || 101 ||
idaṃ titīrṣatāṃ pāramidamānantyamicchatām|
prāyaścittaprasaṅge tu sarvapāpasamudbhave || 102 ||
māmekāṃ devadevasya mahiṣīṃ śaraṇaṃ śrayet|
upāyādvirataḥ śaśvanmāṃ caiva śaraṇaṃ vrajet || 103 ||
tanūkṛtyākhilaṃ pāpaṃ māṃ cāpnoti naraḥ śanaiḥ|
athopāyaprasaktaśca bhuktvā bhogānanāmayān || 104 ||
ante viraktimāsādya viśate paramaṃ padam|
upāyaḥ sukaraḥ so'yaṃ duṣkarasca mato mama || 105 ||
nanu cirakālasādhyasya bhaktiyogasya kṣaṇakālasādhyasya nyāsayogasya ca mokṣākyatulyaphalatvaṃ na ghaṭate, gurulaghūpāyayorvikalpāyogāt| tathā sati prekṣāvantaḥ sarve'pi laghūpāya eva pravarteran, na gurūpāya iti tadvidherananuṣṭhānalakṣaṇamaprāmāṇyaṃ syādityatrāha upāyaḥ sukara iti| yadyapyayaṃ nyāsayogaḥ kṣaṇakālakartavyatvāt karmādyanaṅgakatvācca sukara eva, tathāpyatyantadurlabhamahāviśvāsādyaṅgāpekṣatvāt durūhamadhyamavṛttyāśrayatvācca duṣkara eveti bhāvaḥ| atra yadyapi jñānaśaktyādipauṣkalyatadrāhityābhyāmadhikāribhedāt vyavasthā suvacā, tathāpi `idameva vijānatām' ityādinā jñānādimatāmapi nyāsayogavidhānāt sā rītirnādṛtā|
śiṣṭairniṣevyate so'yamakāmahatacetanaiḥ|
akāmaiśca sakāmaiśca tasmātsiddhyarthamātmanaḥ || 106 ||
arcanīyā naraiḥ śaśvanmama mantramayī tanuḥ|
praviśya vidhivaddīkṣāṃ gurorlabdhvārthasaṃpadaḥ|
manmayairarcayenmantrairmāmikāṃ māntrikīṃ tanum || 107 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre rahasyopāyaprakāśo nāma saptadaśo'dhyāyaḥ
iti saptadaśo'dhyāyaḥ

<18>
aṣṭādaśo'dhyāyaḥ
śakraḥ
namaste padmanilaye namaste padmasaṃbhave|
viditaṃ veditavyaṃ me vedānteṣvapi durlabham || 1 ||
brūhi mantramayaṃ mārgamidānīṃ viṣṇuvallabhe|
yaṃ vijñāyārcayeyaṃ te divyāṃ mantramayīṃ tanum || 2 ||
kuto mantrasamutpattiḥ vka ca mantraḥ pralīyate|
mantrasya kiṃ phalaṃ padme kena madhye prapūryate || 3 ||
kiyatyaśca vidhā asya parimāṇaṃ kiyat kila|
kṣetrakṣetrajñabhāvaśca kīdṛśaḥ paramo'mbuje || 4 ||
mantraśca kena saṃgrāhya upadeṣṭā ca kīdṛśaḥ|
upāsanaprakāraśca kathamasyābjasaṃbhave || 5 ||
upāsanopayogī ca yāvānartho'mbujāsane|
siddhisādhanayogaśca pratyayāśca tathā tathā || 6 ||
yogaḥ svādhyāyayogaśca rakṣāyogastathaiva ca|
prāyaścittavidhiścaiva śrāddhakalpastathaiva ca || 7 ||
dīkṣāpratiṣṭhayoḥ kalpo yantrakalpastathaiva ca|
etacca nikhilaṃ yaccāpyadṛṣṭamupayujyate || 8 ||
prabrūhi tadaśeṣeṇa namaste padmasaṃbhave|
tavaiṣa śirasā pādau nato'smi kamalārūṇau || 9 ||
śaraṇaṃ ca prapanno'smi paṅkaje tvamadhīhi bho|
śrīḥ
praśabhāro'yamatulastvayoddiṣṭaḥ puraṃdara || 10 ||
vācyaste prītisaṃyogācchṛṇu vakṣyāmyaśeṣataḥ|
ahamityeva yaḥ pūrṇaḥ puruṣaḥ puṣkarekṣaṇaḥ || 11 ||
svabhāvaḥ sarvabhāvānāmabhāvānāṃ ca vāsava|
idaṃtayāvalīḍhaṃ yat sadasajjagati sthitam || 12 ||
idaṃtayeti| idamiti pratītiviṣayatayetyarthaḥ| parāktveneti yāvat| yuṣmadasmatpratyayagocaratvaṃ hi krameṇa parāktvapratyaktvayorlakṣaṇamāmananti|
tattallakṣaṇavanto ye tadahaṃtve vilīyate|
vilīnedaṃpadadvīpaḥ prāptaikadhyaścidambudhiḥ || 13 ||
vilīnetyādi; idaṃpadārthabhūtāḥ sarve'pi acitpadārthāḥ; ta eva dvīpāḥ cidambudhau vāsudeve vilīya tena sahaikatvaṃ prāptā ityarthaḥ|
nistaraṅgodayo'nanto vāsudevaḥ prakāśate|
pūrṇāhaṃtāsmi tasyaikā śaktirīśvaratāmayī || 14 ||
nityoditā sadānandā sarvataḥ samatāṃ gatā|
sarvabhāvasamudbhūtiḥ sarvapratyakṣasaṃmatā || 15 ||
nityoditeti| udayo dvividhaḥ śāntodayaḥ nityodayaśceti| avatārāvasthāyāmādyaḥ| parāvasthāyāṃ dvitīyaḥ|
yā hyeṣā pratibhā tattatpadārthakramarūṣitā|
uddhṛteṣu padārtheṣu sāhamakramaśālinī || 16 ||
avabodhātmikāyā me yā pratyagavamarśitā|
sā sphurattā mahānandā śabdabrahmeti gīyate || 17 ||
pratyagavamarśitā; ahaṃpratyayagamyatā|
prakāśānandasārāhaṃ sarvamantraprasūḥ parā|
śabdānāṃ jananī śaktirudayāstamayojjhitā || 18 ||
vyāpakaṃ yatparaṃ brahma nārāyaṇamanāmayam|
śāntatā nāma yāvasthā sāhaṃ śāntākhilaprasūḥ || 19 ||
tasyā me ya udeti sma sisṛkṣākyo'lpa udyamaḥ|
sa śabdārthavibhedena śānta unmeṣa ucyate || 20 ||
śabdodayapuraskāraḥ sarvatrārthodaya smṛtaḥ|
arthaśabdapravṛttyātmā śabdasya sthūlatā hi sā || 21 ||
śabdasyodayaḥ prathamam; anantaramarthasyeti tāntrikasiddhāntaḥ| śrutāvapi "nāmarūpe vyākaravāṇi" iti nāmavyākaraṇaṃ pūrvaṃ, tato rūpavyākaraṇamuktaṃ veditavyam|
bodhonmeṣaḥ smṛtaḥ śabdaḥ śabdonmeṣo'rtha ucyate|
udyacchabdodayaḥ śaktoḥ prathamaḥ śāntatātmanaḥ || 22 ||
sa nāda iti vikhyāto vācyatāmasṛṇastadā|
nādena saha śaktiḥ sā sūkṣmeti parigīyate || 23 ||
evaṃ ca śabdabrahma, parā vāk, nāda iti paryāyāḥ
nādāt paro ya unmoṣo dvitīyaḥ śaktisaṃbhavaḥ|
bindurityucyate so'tra vācyo'pi masṛṇaḥ sthitaḥ || 24 ||
paśyantī nāma sāvasthā mama divyā mahodayā|
tataḥ paro ya unmeṣastṛtīyaḥ śaktisaṃbhavaḥ || 25 ||
bindoranantaraṃ śaktyunmeṣasya madhyameti nāma|
madhyamā sā daśā tatra saṃskārayati saṃgatim|
vācyavācakabhedastu tadā saṃskāratāmayaḥ || 26 ||
caturthastu ya unmeṣaḥ śaktermādhyamikāt paraḥ|
vaikharī nāma sāvasthā varṇavākyasphuṭodayā || 27 ||
asti śaktiḥ kriyātmā me bodharūpānuyāyinī|
sā prāṇayati nādādiṃ śaktyunmeṣaparaṃparām || 28 ||
śāntarūpātha paśyantī madhyamā vaikharī tathā|
catūrūpā catūrūpaṃ vacmi vācyaṃ svanirmitam || 29 ||
vāsudevādayaḥ sūkṣmā vācyāḥ śāntādayaḥ kramāt|
ahamekapadī jñeyā prakāśānandarūpiṇī || 30 ||
vācyavācakabhedena punaḥ sā dvipadī smṛtā|
ūṣmāntaḥsthasvarasparśabhedāccāhaṃ catuṣpadī || 31 ||
śaṣasahā ūṣmāṇaḥ| yaralavā antaḥsthāḥ| acaḥ svarāḥ| kādayo māvasānāḥ sparśāḥ|
aṣṭavargavibhedācca sāhamaṣṭapadī smṛtā|
aghoṣarūpeṇānyena yuktā navapadī smṛtā || 32 ||
aṣṭa vargāḥ svaravargaḥ, kādipāntavargāḥ pañca, ūṣmavargaḥ, antaḥsthavargaśceti| aghoṣāśca yamāḥ, jihvāmūlīyopadhmānīyau visargaśceti|
ahamekapadī divyā śabdabrahmamayī parā|
ghoṣavarṇasvarūpeṇa varte'haṃ dvipadī punaḥ || 33 ||
prakārāntareṇaikadhyādikamucyate| śabdabrahmarūpeṇaikarūpā| dhvanivarṇātmanā dvirūpā|
takṣatī salilaṃ sarvaṃ dravyajātiguṇakriyāḥ|
caturdhābhidadhānāhaṃ catuṣpadyuditā budhaiḥ || 34 ||
takṣatī; takṣantī tanūkurvatī| sarvaṃ salilaṃ saṃsaraṇahetuṃ prākṛtatuṣṭim| salilākyastuṣṭiviśeṣaḥ sāṃkhyasamayaprasiddhaḥ| atra "gaurīrmimāya salilāni takṣatyekapadī dvipadī sā catuṣpadī| aṣṭāpadī navapadī babhūvuṣī sahasrākṣarā parame vyoman||" iti śrutiranukriyate| catuṣpadī; dravyajātiguṇakriyāśabdātmanā catūrūpā|
nāmabhāvadvayopetā sāhamaṣṭapadī smṛtā|
avikalpavikalpasthā sāhaṃ navapadī smṛtā || 35 ||
teṣāmeva śabdārthātmanā pratyekaṃ dvaividhye saṃkalayyāṣṭarūpā|
vyomnyahaṃ parame divyā hyanantākṣaramālinī|
iyadvitativistīrṇā pūrṇāhaṃtāhamādimā || 36 ||
eṣā ca vyavasthā prākṛtaloka eva| aprākṛtaloke tu anantākṣararūpā|
mantrāṇāṃ jananī jñeyā bhuktimuktipradāyinī|
udyanti mantrakallolā matta eva cidambudheḥ || 37 ||
māmāśritya vivartante yānti cāstaṃ muhurmayi|
saṃvidānandasaṃdohasundarāḥ śabdadehakāḥ || 38 ||
vivartante; pariṇamanti| nātrānyaparibhāṣito vivarto vivakṣitaḥ, `traiguṇyaṃ pariṇāmi tat' ityuktatvena triguṇapariṇāmatvāt śabdasya|
sāmarthyapūrṇāḥ phaladā mantrātmāno hi manmayāḥ|
varṇāḥ padāni vākyāni sahaprakaraṇāhnikaiḥ || 39 ||
adhyāyāśca paricchedāḥ sargā uchvāsakāstathā|
paṭalādyā avacchedāḥ praśravākānuvākakāḥ || 40 ||
maṇḍalāni ca kāṇḍāni saṃhitā vividhātmikāḥ|
ṛco yajūṃṣi sāmāni sūktāni ca khilaiḥ samam || 41 ||
śāsratantrātmakāḥ śabdā bāhyābāhyāgamāstathā|
bhāṣāśca vividhāstāstā vyaktāvyaktagiraḥ smṛtāḥ || 42 ||
mantrarūpabhidaṃ śakra viddhi madrūpavedinām|
bhāvanātāratamyena mantramantrivyavasthitiḥ || 43 ||
māṃ trāyate'yamityevaṃ yogena svīkṛto dhvanīḥ|
guptāśayaḥ sadā yaśca mantrajñaṃ trāyate bhayāt || 44 ||
sa mantraḥ saṃsmṛto'haṃtāvikāsaḥ śabdajaiḥ kramaiḥ|
pūrṇāhaṃtāsamudbhūtaiḥ śuddhabodhānvayo yataḥ || 45 ||
sarve mantrā madīyāḥ syuḥ prabhavāpyayavedinām|
madīyāścānyadīyāśca bhāvanātāratamyataḥ || 46 ||
prakṛtyanvayino mantrā madīyāḥ syuḥ pradhānataḥ|
bhavadbhāvātmakaṃ brahma svārasyena viśanti ye || 47 ||
prakṛtyanvayino mantrāstārikottārikādayaḥ|
mantrāḥ svarasato yānti ye bhāvaṃ bhavaduttaram || 48 ||
te'pavargapradā jñeyāstāraprāsādakādayaḥ|
bhāvottarāṃ samāṃ vāpi ye bhajanti bhavatsthitim || 49 ||
bhogāpavargadā mantrā jñeyāste tārikādayaḥ|
viśanti bhāvamevaike yāntyeke bhavadeva ca || 50 ||
bhuktidā muktidāścaiva dvitaye te vyavasthayā|
prakṛtyanvayināmevaṃ svabhāvaḥ parikīrtitaḥ|
abhisaṃdhibalāt sarvaṃ dvitaye te vitanvate || 51 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre mantrasvarūpakathanaṃ nāma aṣṭādaśo'dhyāyaḥ
ityaṣṭādaśo'dhyāyaḥ

<19>
ekonaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ
sisṛkṣālakṣaṇā pūrvā pūrṇāhaṃtā hareraham|
sṛṣṭirūpā parā śaktirūpetyevoditāsmyaham || 1 ||
sisṛkṣāvasthāyām ahaṃteti nāmnā prathitā| sṛṣṭyavasthāyāṃ parā śaktiriti nāmnā prathitā|
daśa pañca ca tulyā me daśāsridaśanandana|
anuttaraṃ svasaṃvedyaṃ cidrūpaṃ mama śāśvatam || 2 ||
daśetyādi; akārādibindvantāḥ pañcadaśa daśā jñeyāḥ| visargasya daśāprakṛtitvāt daśāsu na parigaṇanamiti jñeyam|
vāktattvaṃ tadakārātmā sarvavāṅmayasaṃbhavaḥ|
tadevānandarūpeṇa dvitīyaḥ svara iṣyate || 3 ||
icchātmanā tṛtīyaḥ syādīśānātmā turīyakaḥ|
unmeṣaḥ pañcamaḥ ṣaṣṭha ūrjatārūpa ucyate || 4 ||
catuṣkaṃ madhyamaṃ yattadicchādereva vikriyā|
anuttarecchāsaṃyogādekāro nāma jāyate || 5 ||
catuṣkamiti| ṛṝlṛlṝkārarūpamityarthaḥ| tasmāt teṣāṃ na pṛthak grahaṇaṃ kṛtam|
tasyaivānandasaṃyogājjāgadyonirudāhṛtā|
anuttaronmeṣayogādokāro nāma jāyate || 6 ||
jagadyoniḥ aikāraḥ|
tasyaivānuttaraśleṣāt sadyojātasamudbhavaḥ|
anuttarādbhavantyete vikāsā vedyasaṃśrayāḥ || 7 ||
sadyojātaḥ aukāraḥ|
tādṛktādṛksamunmeṣā ānandādyāsrayodaśa|
trayodaśatayonmeṣāḥ śritā vedanaśeṣitām || 8 ||
vedanaśeṣitām; saṃvidrūpeṇa pariśeṣitām| asya sūkṣmadaśāmityuttareṇānvayaḥ|
anuttarīṃ sūkṣmadaśāṃ sa tu pañcadaśaḥ svaraḥ|
etāḥ pañcadaśāvasthā visṛṣṭeḥ sphuraṇodyamāḥ || 9 ||
sūkṣmadaśā binduḥ| sa ca pañcadaśaḥ akāramārabhya gaṇane bhavati|
daśabhiḥ pañcabhiścaivamaṅgaiḥ pūrṇā sisṛkṣayā|
daivī sṛṣṭimayī śaktiḥ kṛtye kṛtye kṛtodyamā || 10 ||
sisṛkṣayeti hetau tṛtīyā|
visṛjatyāstu tasyā me tattvānāṃ pañcaviṃśatim|
puruṣādyāḥ pṛthivyantāḥ kādimāntāḥ samudgatāḥ || 11 ||
me iti pañcamyarthe vibhaktipratirūpakamavyayam; madityarthaḥ| samudgatā ityanenānveti| kādimāntā iti vyutkrameṇānvayaḥ|
tattadakṣarasaṃsphūrtestattattattvaṃ samudgatam|
catuṣkaṃ dhārāṇārūpaṃ yādivāntamudīryate || 12 ||
dhāraṇeti| paradaśāyā aparadaśāmavatarataḥ puruṣasya dhāraṇāt dhāraṇeti samākhyā|
dhārayanti yato madhye puruṣaṃ dhāraṇāḥ smṛtāḥ|
kalā kiṃcitkriyārūpā yakāro vātasaṃjñitaḥ || 13 ||
kiṃcijjñānātmikā vidyā rephaḥ pāvakasaṃjñitaḥ|
stambhamohātmikā māyā lakāraḥ pṛthivī mataḥ || 14 ||
rañjanātmā rāgaśaktirvakāro varuṇātmakaḥ|
parāparadaśāmadhye dhārayantyo naraṃ sadā || 15 ||
catasro dhāraṇā jñeyāstā etāstattvakovidaiḥ|
śādikṣāntaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ viśuddhaṃ brahmapañcakam || 16 ||
śaṣasaho'niruddhādyā vijñeyāsridaśeśvara|
sṛjantyāḥ kṣubhitaṃ rūpaṃ sṛṣṭyādau yanmamādbhutam || 17 ||
aniruddhādyā iti krameṇānvayaḥ| śakāraḥ aniruddhātmakaḥ|
kṣobhikā sā mahāśaktiḥ kṣātmā satyāparāhvayā|
pṛthivyādyā viyatprāntā yā divyāḥ pañca śaktayaḥ || 18 ||
kṣakārasya satyātmakatvamāha kṣobhiketi|
balādipañcakātmāno divyā matsattvanāmikāḥ|
jñānātmāno mamodyatyāstā etāḥ śādiśaktayaḥ || 19 ||
balādīti| balaiśvaryavīryaśaktitejāṃsītyarthaḥ|
visargo nāma yaḥ proktaḥ purā pañcadaśāṅgavān|
sāhaṃ somamayī śaktiḥ kiraṇāyutasaṃkulā || 20 ||
saṃkocaśca vikāsaśca tāveva parikīrtitau|
aṅgānāmantimo yastu proktaḥ pañcadaśo mayā || 21 ||
binduḥ saṃkocaḥ| visargaḥ vikāsaḥ| antima iti| bindurityarthaḥ|
ādānaśīlaṃ taṃ viddhi sūryaṃ bhoktāramañjasā|
sūryācandramasāvetau bindusargau puraṃdara || 22 ||
bhoktāram; saṃhārakamityarthaḥ| binduḥ sūryaḥ| visargaścandramā ityarthaḥ|
kiraṇāḥ sapta sapta syurdevayoranayordvayoḥ|
caturdaśa svarāḥ śiṣṭāḥ sapta yugmāni kalpayet || 23 ||
teṣu saptasu yugmeṣu pūrve sapta puraṃdara|
śoṣakāḥ sūryarūpāyā bhoktrākyāyā mamāṃśavaḥ || 24 ||
pūrve sapteti| a, i, u, ṛ, lṛ, e, o iti saptetyarthaḥ|
uttare sapta yugmeṣu śītalāhlādakāriṇaḥ|
poṣakāḥ somarūpāyā bhogyākhyāyā mamāṃśavaḥ || 25 ||
uttare sapteti| ā, ī, ū, ṝ, lṝ, ai au iti saptetyarthaḥ|
ālokastīkṣṇatā vyāptirgrahaṇaṃ kṣepaṇeraṇe|
pāka ityuditāḥ pūrve kiraṇāḥ sūryasaṃbhavāḥ || 26 ||
akārādyokārāntānāṃ saptānāṃ yugmasthapūrvavarṇaānāṃ sūryakiraṇātmakatvaṃ guṇāṃścāha āloka ityādi| tathā ca jayākhye "ālokastīkṣṇatā vyāptirgrahaṇaṃ kṣepaṇeraṇe| pākaḥ prāptiriti hyaṣṭau sūryabhāge vyavasthitāḥ| akārādiṣu hrasveṣu varṇeṣveteṣvanukramāt"|| iti| atrānusvāramapi saṃyojyāṣṭatvamuktam|
dravatā śītabhāvaśca śāntiḥ kiraṇaiḥ kāntiśālinī|
rasatānanda ityete sapta cāndramasāḥ karāḥ || 27 ||
evamuttaravarṇānāṃ somakiraṇātmakatvaṃ guṇāṃścāha dravatetyādinā| jayākhye ca "dravatā śaityabhāvaśca tṛptiḥ kāntiḥ prasannatā| rasatāsvāda ānando hyaṣṭau cāndrā ime matāḥ||" iti| atra visargaṃ saṃyojyāṣṭatvaṃ bhāvyam|
agnīṣomātmakairebhiḥ kiraṇaiḥ kāntiśālinī|
pumāṃsaṃ bindurūpaṃ tamaṅgīkṛtya viśeṣaṇī || 28 ||
somarūpottarā śaktikoṭimaṇḍalamaṇḍitā|
mahāsṛṣṭirmahānandā pravarte'ntyasvarātmanā || 29 ||
tasyāḥ pravartamānāyā udgataṃ brahmapañcakam|
kṣādi śāntaṃ sureśāna śaktyunmeṣaviśeṣitam || 30 ||
kṣa ityeva mahākṣobha uditaḥ satyasaṃjñayā|
vāsudevākhyayā ho'bhūtā sākhyaḥ saṃkarṣaṇodayaḥ || 31 ||
kṣādiśāntādhiṣṭhāyibrahmapañcakanāmānyāha satyetyādinā|
pradyumnaḥ ṣākhyayā jñeyo hyaniruddhastu śākhyayā|
tā etāḥ śaktayaḥ pañca pañcabrahmātmikāḥ parāḥ || 32 ||
brahmātmikāḥ; brahmamayya ityarthaḥ|
sphūrtayo madabhinnāstā jagadutpattihetavaḥ|
jvālā iva mahāvahnerbrahmaṇo mama śaktayaḥ || 33 ||
brahmaṇo mameti| brahmābhinnāyā mametyarthaḥ|
catasro dhāraṇā jātā vādyā yāntāḥ puraṃdara|
turyādyā jāgradantāstā avasthāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 34 ||
pumāṃsaṃ dhārayantyetā madhyato daśayordvayoḥ|
yaiṣā brahmadaśā proktā prākṛtī bhādikā ca yā || 35 ||
madhyata iti| parāparadaśayormadhye ityarthaḥ| bhādiketi| bhakārādikakāraparyantādhiṣṭhātṛbhūtāḥ prakṛtyādipṛthivyantā jñeyāḥ|
madhye tayormakārākhyo dhāraṇānāṃ catuṣkataḥ|
dhriyate sa pumān prokto jāgradādivibhedavān || 36 ||
yadi na dhriyate tābhirdaśāmanyatarāṃ vrajet|
brāhnīṃ vā prākṛtīṃ vāpi naiva syāt saṃsṛtistataḥ || 37 ||
ityarthaṃ dhāraṇā mattaḥ prādurbhūtā mamājñayā|
tato daśācatuṣkasthaḥ puruṣo bhoktṛsaṃjñakaḥ || 38 ||
dhāraṇāḥ antaḥsthāḥ| brahmaprākṛtadaśāmadhyasthatvāt antaḥsthā ityuktāḥ| ahirbudhnye tu "antaḥsthā iti ca proktā antaḥsthapuruṣeśayāḥ" iti vyutpattyantaramuktam|
matto jajñe ma ityevaṃ yogyo bhogāpavargayoḥ|
bhogānāṃ prasavārthāya puruṣasyāsya vāsava || 39 ||
matto jajñe ma ityevamityanena maśabdasya jīvavācakasya vyutpattirabhipretā| manadhātorniṣpattirapyanyatroktā|
acaitanyaṃ paraṃ sūkṣmaṃ guṇasāmyamanulbaṇam|
yonisvabhāvasaṃjñātaṃ matto'bhūdbha iti svayam || 40 ||
acaitanyamiti bahuvrīhiḥ| acetanamityarthaḥ| "acetanā parārthā ca nityā satatavikriyā" ityanyatroktam| anulvaṇam; avyaktamityarthaḥ| yoniḥ svabhāva iti ca tannāma| bha iti; bhakāravācyatvenetyarthaḥ|
bhogyabhogādisiddhyarthaṃ bhuñjānasya vipaścitaḥ|
bādyāt kakāraparyantādvarṇagrāmāt puraṃdara || 41 ||
tattvāvirbhāvakramamanurudya bādyādityādinā varṇānāṃ vyatkrameṇa nirdeśaḥ kṛtaḥ|
vyaktāni jajñire matto viṃśatisrīṇi ca kramāt|
buddhyahaṃkāramanasāṃ sṛṣṭirbāditrayāttathā || 42 ||
śrotrādernāditānteṣu pañcakasya samudbhavaḥ|
vāgāderṇādiṭānteṣu pañcakasya samudbhavaḥ || 43 ||
śabdādyāḥ pañcatanmātrā ñādicānteṣu jajñire|
viyadādīni bhūtāni ṅādikānteṣu jajñire || 44 ||
bodhaḥ śabdātmanodeti śabdasttvarthātmanā tataḥ|
viddhi bodhaṃ tu madrūpaṃ sarveyaṃ mattatistataḥ || 45 ||
varṇādhvanastviyaṃ rītirmadhyamā kathitā tava|
ādyāmantāṃ ca deveśa gadantyā me niśāmaya || 46 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre varṇotpattinirūpaṇaṃ nāma ekonaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
ityekonaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

<20>
viṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śakraḥ
namo nikhilanirmāṇatrāṇasaṃhāraśaktaye|
hareḥ svarūpabhūtāyai namaste jñānarūpiṇi || 1 ||
tvatprasādānmayā padme rahasyaṃ paramaṃ śrutam|
varṇādhvānaṃ yathāvanme bhūyastvaṃ vaktumarhasi || 2 ||
śrīḥ
śṛṇu varṇādhvano rītimādyāṃ tridaśapuṃgava|
prāpnoti yatparijñānātsādhako matsarūpatām || 3 ||
vedyavedakanirmuktamacyutaṃ brahma yat param|
anastamitabhārūpaṃ sarvābhinnamahaṃpadam || 4 ||
ahaṃtā nāma sā śaktistadabhinnā sadoditā|
anastamitabhārūpā vedyavedakavarjitā || 5 ||
prakāśānandasārāhaṃ sarvataḥ samatāṃ gatā|
koṭikoṭyayutaikāṃśakoṭyaṃśe kṣubhitā satī || 6 ||
śabdabrahmasvarūpeṇa svaśaktyā svayameva hi|
muktaye'khilajīvānāmudemi parameśvarāt || 7 ||
tadavyaktākṣaraṃ viddhi tantrīśabdo yathā kalaḥ|
pṛthagvarṇātmanā yāti sthitaye naikadhā sa tu || 8 ||
sūkṣmavarṇasvarūpo'sau dhārāsaṃtānarūpadhṛt|
pañcādhvakośamuktasya manniṣṭhasya vivekinaḥ || 9 ||
bhuvanādhvapadādhvamantrādhvatattvādhvakalādhvānaḥ pañcādhvānaḥ| māyāprasūtiprakṛtibrahmāṇḍajīvadehākhyāḥ pañca kośāḥ|
anubhūtipadaṃ yāti prasādāt paramātmanaḥ|
maccāturātmyanicayo vijñeyo hi tadātmanā || 10 ||
cāturātmyaṃ vāsudevasaṃkarṣaṇādikam| tadātmanā; varṇātmanā| taccānantarameva vakṣyate|
prabhavāpyayayogena bhārūpadhvanilakṣaṇaḥ|
sakārāntastvakārācca hakārādānta eva ca || 11 ||
utpattikrame dhvanilakṣaṇaḥ| apyayakrame parārūpeṇa cillakṣaṇaḥ| akāramārabhya sakāraparyantaṃ gaṇane aṣṭacatvāriṃśat varṇā bhavanti| yathā svarāḥ ṣoḍaśa, sparśāḥ pañcaviṃśatiḥ, antaḥsthāścatvāraḥ, hakāraṃ vihāyoṣmāṇasraya iti| evaṃ hakāramārabhya dīrghākāraparyantagaṇane'pi|
prabhave dvādaśāntastu hakāraścaturātmanām|
akārastvapyaye caitau dvādaśāntāvubhau samau || 12 ||
caturātmanāṃ vāsudevādīnāṃ prabhave hakāro dvādaśānto bhavati| apyaye tu akārastathā| ayaṃ bhāvaḥ pūrvoktāṣṭacatvāriṃśadvarṇānāṃ dvādaśadhā vibhāge prativibhāgaṃ catvāro varṇāḥ saṃpadyante| ete ca krameṇa vāsudevādibhiścaturbhiradhiṣṭhitā bhavanti| dvādaśabhāgānte prabhave hakāraḥ laye akāraśca bhavata iti|
vārṇe vyūhasamūhe'smin jñeyaṃ jñānasamādhinā|
viśrāma udayo vyāptirvyaktirā vāsudevataḥ || 13 ||
prativibhāgamāsthitā vāsudevādayaḥ krameṇa viśrāmodayavyāptivyaktirūpā jñeyāḥ|
atraikaikopari jñeyā mūrtirvai tvevameva hi|
yuktā viśrāmapūrveṇa catuṣkeṇa samāsataḥ || 14 ||
viśrāmaṃ cintayeddevaṃ vāsudevaṃ sanātanam|
akāraṃ puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ pūrvadevaṃ sanātanam || 15 ||
tadeva viśadayati viśrāmamityādinā|
saṃkarṣaṇāditattvāni viśrāmyanti laye'tra hi|
tataḥ saṃkarṣaṇaṃ devamākāramudayaṃ smaret || 16 ||
udito hi sa sarvātmā prathamaṃ sarvakṛt svayam|
vyāptaṃ pradyumnadevaṃ tamikāraṃ paricintayet || 17 ||
vividhaṃ prāpyate tena trayīkarmātmanā jagat|
aniruddhaṃ vyaktirūpamīkāraṃ tamanusmaret || 18 ||
vyajyante śaktayo hyatra jagatsṛṣṭyādayo'khilāḥ|
daṇḍavatsaṃniveśena saṃsthitā hyevameva hi || 19 ||
ā sakārāccatūrūpayuktā me caturātmatā|
smaret prabhavacintāyāṃ hakāraṃ dvādaśāntakam || 20 ||
ā sakārāditi| sakāraparyantamityarthaḥ|
hakāraṃ vāsudevaṃ tu viśrāmaṃ paricintayet|
saṃkarṣaṇaṃ sakāraṃ tamudayaṃ tvapyaye smaret || 21 ||
layakrame cāturātmyaniveśamāha hakāramiti|
evamākārato divyāṃ cintayeccaturātmatām|
dviṣaṭkaṃ dhāraṇānāṃ ca dvādaśādhyātmalakṣaṇam || 22 ||
ākārata iti| ākāraparyantamityarthaḥ|
sopānabhūtaṃ yatkrāntvā dvādaśāntādviśet param|
eṣā sā prathamā rītirvarṇamārgasya darśitā || 23 ||
sūkṣmā taccāturātmīyā bhārūpā manmayī parā|
madhyamā pūrvamevoktā viśeṣaṃ tatra me śṛṇu || 24 ||
dhāraṇāḥ pūrvamuktā yāścatasro matsvarūpikāḥ|
vakārākhyāniruddhasya śaktiḥ sā rāgasaṃjñitā || 25 ||
māyā nāma mahālakṣmīrlakārāparanāmikā|
vidyā yā rephasaṃjñātā mahāvāṇī tu sā smṛtā || 26 ||
vātasaṃjñā mahākālī kriyāśaktiryakāriṇī|
brahmādyā mūrtayastisraḥ patnyasrayyādayaśca yāḥ || 27 ||
tajjñeyaṃ sakalaṃ sūkṣmamakārasyādimeṃ'śake|
madhyame bhoktṛkūṭasthaḥ puruṣoṃ'śe pratiṣṭhitaḥ || 28 ||
saṃsārī puruṣaḥ sarvaścaramāṃśe'vatiṣṭhate|
eṣā te madhyamā rītirvarṇamārgasya darśitā || 29 ||
caramāmatha vakṣyāmi rītiṃ balaniṣūdana|
vaikharī caramā rītiḥ prayatnasthānabhedinī || 30 ||
vyaktavācāṃ samuccāre sā sphuṭībhavati dhravam|
jīvānāṃ dehabaddhānāṃ tattatsanmārgadarśikā || 31 ||
mātṛkā jāyate seyaṃ viṣṇuśaktyupabṛṃhitā|
viṣṇuvattatra pañcāśacchaktayaḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 32 ||
adhitiṣṭhanti ye yāṃ ca mātṛkāṃ varṇamālinīm|
vāsudevādayo vyūhā daśa dvau keśavādayaḥ || 33 ||
svarādhiṣṭhāyino devāḥ śaktīsteṣāmimāḥ śṛṇu|
lakṣmīḥ kīrtirjayā māyā vyūhaśaktaya īritāḥ || 34 ||
śrīśca vāgīśvarī kāntikriyāśāntivibhūtayaḥ|
icchā prītī ratiścaiva māyā dhīrmahimeti ca || 35 ||
śaktayaḥ keśavādīnāṃ tā etāḥ svaraśaktayaḥ|
kādyadhiṣṭhāyino devān gadantyā me niśāmaya || 36 ||
padmanābhādināmāno viṣṇavaḥ kādidevātaḥ|
padmanābho dhruvo'nantaḥ śaktīśo madhusūdanaḥ || 37 ||
padmanābhādayo vibhavabhedāḥ pūrvamevaikādaśādhyāye paṭhitā jñātavyāḥ|
vidyādhidevaḥ kapilo viśvarūpo vihaṃgamaḥ|
kroḍātmā baḍabāvakttro dharmo vāgīśvarastathā || 38 ||
ekārṇavaśayo devaḥ kūrmaḥ pātāladhārakaḥ|
varāho nārasiṃhaścāpyamṛtāharaṇastathā || 39 ||
śrīpatirdivyadeho'tha kāntātmāmṛtadhārakaḥ|
rāhujit kālanemighnaḥ pārijātaharo mahān || 40 ||
lokanāthastu śāntātmā dattātreyo mahāprabhuḥ|
nyagrodhaśāyī bhagavānekaśṛṅgatanustataḥ || 41 ||
devo vāmanadehastu sarvavyāpī trivikramaḥ|
naro nārāyaṇaścaiva hariḥ kṛṣṇastathaiva ca || 42 ||
jvalatparaśudhṛdrāmo rāmaścānyo dhanurdharaḥ|
vedavidbhagavān kalkī pātālaśayanaḥ prabhuḥ || 43 ||
eteṣvantyeṣu catvāro devā rāmādayo hi ye|
te raṅgayamayorjihvāmūlopadhmānayorapi || 44 ||
raṅgaḥ anunāsikaḥ| yamāḥ prātiśākhye prasiddhā varṇaviśeṣāḥ|
dhīstārā vāruṇī śaktiḥ padmā vidyā tathaiva ca|
saṃkhyā viśvā khagā bhūrgaurlakṣmīrvāgīśvarī tathā || 45 ||
amṛtā dharaṇī chāyā nārasiṃhī sudhā tathā|
śrīḥ kīrtirviśvakāmā mā satyā kāntiḥ saroruhā || 46 ||
māyā padmāsanā kharvā vikrāntirnarasaṃbhavā|
nārāyaṇī hariprītirgāndhārī kāśyapī tathā || 47 ||
vaidehī vedavidyā ca padminī nāgaśāyinī|
madaṃśakā imā devyo vijñeyāḥ kādiśaktayaḥ || 48 ||
bhavopakaraṇaiśceyaṃ mātṛkādhiṣṭhitā suraiḥ|
śrīkaṇṭhānandasūkṣmādyairlambodaryādiśaktibhiḥ || 49 ||
vinanāyakaiśca durgābhiḥ kṣetreśairmātṛbhistathā|
samayasthaistathā bauddhairārhatairapi cāparaiḥ || 50 ||
yathā hi kṣudhitā bālā mātaraṃ paryupāsate|
evaṃ sarve surā devīṃ mātṛkāṃ paryupāsate || 51 ||
iyaṃ yonirhi mantrāṇāṃ vidyānāṃ janmabhūriyam|
tattvānāṃ tāttvikānāṃ ca jñānānāṃ prasavasthalī || 52 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre mātṛkāprakāśo nāma viṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
iti viṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

<21>
ekaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śakraḥ
śabdārthavyaktirūpāyai ṣaḍadhvaparivartini|
adhvātītāvabodhākhye namaste harivallabhe || 1 ||
ṣaḍadhveti| varṇakalātattvamantrapadabhuvanākhyāḥ ṣaḍadhvānaḥ|
varṇāḥ prakāśitā devi yathāvat sarvahetavaḥ|
mantramārgamidānīṃ me yathāvadvaktumarhasi || 2 ||
sarveti| śabdānāmarthānāṃ cetyarthaḥ|
śrīḥ
eka eva paro devaḥ śrīmān puruṣasattamaḥ|
ṣāḍguṇyāmbhonidhirdivyaḥ sarvātmā sarvatomukhaḥ || 3 ||
tasyāhaṃ paramā śaktirahaṃtā śrīrabhedinī|
sarvādhārā sarvaśaktiḥ sarvajñā sarvatomukhī || 4 ||
mayi prakāśate viśvaṃ darpaṇodaraśailavat|
bodha eva svarūpaṃ me nirmalānandalakṣaṇaḥ || 5 ||
icchāparavatī sāhaṃ bodhakāṃśavivartinī|
śabdabrahmamayī bhūtvā vivarte'haṃ kalādhvanā || 6 ||
icchāparavatīti| svacchandetyarthaḥ| vivartaḥ pariṇāmaḥ| kalādhvaneti| jñānādiguṇātmanetyarthaḥ|
kalā jñānādayaḥ proktāḥ ṣaḍ guṇāḥ pārameśvarāḥ|
tāsāṃ trikadviyogena vivarte tattvavartmanā || 7 ||
trikadviyogena; yugalatrayayogenetyarthaḥ|
saṃkarṣaṇādayo devāstattvāni surasattama|
varṇavyatikarairbhūyo vivarte mantravartmanā || 8 ||
tasya mantrādhvano vyaktiṃ gadantyā me niśāmaya|
śabdabrahmavivarto'yaṃ kiraṇāyutasaṃkulaḥ || 9 ||
cillakṣaṇaḥ ṣaḍguṇātmā tasya bhedaścaturvidhaḥ|
vkacidbījaṃ vkacitpiṇḍaṃ vkacitsaṃjñā vkacitpadam || 10 ||
turyaṃ suṣuptiḥ svapnaśca jāgradbījādayaḥ kramāt|
ekasvaraṃ dvisvaraṃ vā svaravyañjanayordvayam || 11 ||
bījapiṇḍasaṃjñāpadamantrāḥ krameṇa turyādijāgradantapadacatuṣṭayasaṃgatā jñeyā ityarthaḥ|
bījaṃ bahusvaraṃ vāpi vijñeyaṃ vibudheśvara|
antarā harayaḥ piṇḍaṃ vkacitsvarasamāyutam || 12 ||
antarā madhye sthitāḥ harayaḥ vyañjanāni piṇḍamantra ityarthaḥ| tasyāpavādamāha vkaciditi|
tattadvācyābhidhā saṃjñā namaḥpraṇavasaṃyutā|
kriyākārakasaṃyogastutisaṃbodhalakṣaṇaḥ || 13 ||
padamantrasvarūpamāha kriyetyādi| saṃbodhaḥ saṃbodhanam|
nānābhijñāsamāyuktaḥ padātmā mantra ucyate|
etaccatuṣṭayaṃ mantraṃ saṃpūrṇaṃ devatātmani || 14 ||
abhijñā saṃjñā|
sā catuṣṭayasaṃbaddhā siddhimiṣṭāṃ prayacchati|
kṣetrakṣetrajñabhāvaṃ ca mantrāṇāṃ tridaśeśvara || 15 ||
sā; devatā|
vijñāya tattvato mantrān prayuñjīta vicakṣaṇaḥ|
śakraḥ
kṣetrakṣetrajñasadbhāvaṃ mantrāṇāṃ vada me'mbuje || 16 ||
yadvijñāya na muhyanti siddhimeṣyanti cācirāt|
śrīḥ
bījaṃ bījavatāṃ jīvaḥ śiṣṭaṃ kṣetraṃ prakīrtitam || 17 ||
bījavatāṃ mantrāṇāṃ bījākṣaraṃ jīva ityarthaḥ|
nirbījānāmādi jīvaḥ kṣetraṃ tu pariśeṣitam|
bījānāṃ caiva piṇḍānāmastu kṣetrajña ucyate || 18 ||
ādīti| ādyakṣaramityarthaḥ| astu; akārastvityarthaḥ|
śiṣṭaṃ tu kṣetramuddiṣṭamakārarahite punaḥ
kṣetrajñaḥ svara uddiṣṭaḥ kevale ca svare punaḥ || 19 ||
akārarahite punariti uttaratrānveti| svaraḥ; yaḥ kaścit svara ityarthaḥ| kevale ca svare ityuttaratrānveti|
jīvaḥ syāt prathamā mātrā dvitīyādi tanurbhavet|
ekamātre tu jīvaḥ syāt saṃskāro'dbhutalakṣaṇaḥ || 20 ||
saṃskāra iti| madhyamā vāgityarthaḥ| atrāṣṭādaśādhyāyasthaḥ ṣaḍviṃśaḥ śloko'vadheyaḥ|
uccāryamāṇaṃ kṣetraṃ syānniḥsvare piṇḍake punaḥ|
prathamo jīva uddiṣṭaḥ śiṣṭaṃ kṣetraṃ pracakṣate || 21 ||
uccāryamāṇamiti| vaikharītyarthaḥ|
kṣetrakṣetrajñasadbhāva eṣa te saṃpradarśitaḥ|
ādau madhye tathānte ca triṣu vānyataratra vā || 22 ||
eṣāṃ piṇḍo'thavā bījaṃ te mantrāḥ sārvakālikāḥ|
bījābhāve tu mantrāṇāṃ bījaṃ kṛtvādimākṣaram || 23 ||
yatra mantre bījaṃ nāsti tatra prathamākṣaramanusvārayutaṃ bījamityarthaḥ| yathā gaṇapataye namaḥ ityatra gaṃ iti bījamantraḥ|
anusvārayutaṃ paścāt sakalaṃ mantra ucyate|
prakrīḍayanti puruṣaṃ mantrā rāgeṇa rañjitam || 24 ||
caturdaśavibhāgasthe prākṛte bhuvanādhvani|
turyavarjaṃ suṣuptyādyo prākṛte ca padādhvani || 25 ||
ācāryadṛṣṭipātasthaṃ puruṣaṃ saṃyatendriyam|
prasādasumukhā mantrā uttārya bhuvanādhvanaḥ || 26 ||
padādhvanaśca vairāgyaṃ janayantaḥ pade pade|
kramāttattvakalāvarṇapadavīṣu nayanti tam || 27 ||
māntraṃ prāsādamāsādya nirdhūtāśeṣabandhanaḥ|
lakṣmīnārāyaṇākhyaṃ tadviśati brahma śāśvatam || 28 ||
śakraḥ
ācāryaḥ kīdṛśo devi śiṣyastasya ca kīdṛśaḥ|
mantreṣu katamo mantraḥ prabhavet paramāptaye || 29 ||
kathaṃ sa copadeṣṭavya etad brūhi namo'stu te|
śrīḥ
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyukto brāhnaṇe vedapāragaḥ || 30 ||
ṣaṭkarmanirataḥ śāntaḥ pañcakālarataḥ śuciḥ|
pañcarātrārthavinmaunī mantrākṣarakṛtaśramaḥ || 31 ||
na sthūlo na kṛśo hrasvo na kāṇo naiva rogavān|
nāndho na badhiro mūḍho na khalvāṭo na paṅgukaḥ || 32 ||
na hīnāṅgo'tiriktāṅgo na śvitrī na ca ḍāmbhikaḥ|
na krodhano na duścarmā na lobhahatacetanaḥ || 33 ||
akulīnaṃ durācāraṃ śaṭhaṃ jihnaṃ ca varjayet|
dayādāntiśamopetaṃ dṛḍhabhaktiṃ kriyāparam || 34 ||
satyavākchīlasaṃpannaṃ rekhākarmasu kauśalam|
jitendriyaṃ susutuṣṭa karuṇāpūrṇamānasam || 35 ||
āryalakṣaṇasaṃpannamārjavaṃ cāruhāsinam|
evaṃguṇagaṇākīrṇaṃ guruṃ vidyāttu vaiṣṇavam || 36 ||
śiṣyaśca tādṛśo jñeyaḥ sarvalakṣaṇalakṣitaḥ|
kṣāntiśīlaṃ sudhīmantaṃ krodhalobhavivarjitam || 37 ||
snānārcanarataṃ nityaṃ guruśuśrūṣaṇodyatam|
viprāgnidevapitṛṣu bhaktaṃ tarpaṇaśīlinam || 38 ||
kulīnaṃ ca tathā prājñaṃ śāsrārthanirataṃ sadā|
brāhnaṇaṃ kṣatriyaṃ vaiśyaṃ śūdraṃ vā bhagavatparam || 39 ||
īdṛglakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ śiṣyamārjavasaṃyutam|
varṇadharmakriyopetāṃ nārīṃ vā sadvivekinīm || 40 ||
vidyādanumate patyurananyāṃ patimāninam|
evaṃlakṣaṇakaṃ śiṣyamācāryo bhagavanmayaḥ|
jñāpayedvidhivanmantrān gurudṛṣṭyā samīkṣya tu || 41 ||
anena srīśūdrayorapi tāntrikamantragrahaṇe adhikāro vidhīte| paraṃ tu tāraviṣaye viśeṣo granthāntareṣvavagantavyaḥ|
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre guruśiṣyalakṣaṇaṃ nāma ekaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
ityekaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

<22>
dvāviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śakraḥ
anādinidhane devi sarvajñe harivallabhe|
kathaṃ vai jñāpayenmantrāṃsteṣāṃ rūpaṃ ca kīdṛśam || 1 ||
samapradhānatā vaiṣāmuta jyeṣṭhakaniṣṭhatā|
etat pṛṣṭhā mayā brūhi namaste sarasīruhe || 2 ||
śrīḥ
śṛṇu sarvamaśeṣeṇa mattastvaṃ pākaśāsana|
yathāsmi mantrarūpāhaṃ yathā ca jñāpayāmi tān || 3 ||
jñāpayāmīti| ācāryarūpeṇa bodhayāmītyarthaḥ|
paraṃ brahma paraṃ dhāma ṣāḍguṇyamamalojjvalam|
deśakālānavacchinnamanākāramanūpamam || 4 ||
anūpamamiti| anupamamityarthaḥ|
ahamityeva tadbrahma svātmasaṃbodhi nirguṇam|
anādinidhanaṃ divyaṃ lakṣmīnārāyaṇaṃ mahat || 5 ||
nirguṇam; sattvādiprākṛtaguṇahīnam|
cidānandarasaṃ divyamakhaṇḍamajarāmaram|
anunmiṣadbhavadbhāvaṃ grāhyagrāhakavarjitam || 6 ||
anunmiṣadityādi| śāntaparāvasthāyāṃ bhavadbhāvarūponmeṣarahitamityarthaḥ| tathā grāhyetyādikamapi bhāvyam|
stimitaṃ tat paraṃ brahma tasya stimitatāsmyaham|
tat kadācitparaṃ brahma bhavadbhāvavyavasthayā || 7 ||
unmiṣatyajahadrūpaṃ svecchayaiva kadācana|
sāhaṃ bhāvātmikāhaṃtā saṃpūjyā paramātmanaḥ || 8 ||
unmiṣati; prakāśata ityarthaḥ| ajahadityādi| svābhāvikaṃ nirvikāratvamajahadityarthaḥ|
udemi bhavato devādicchayaiva vivasvataḥ|
saṃbhṛtyākhilasaṃbhāramicchayaiva svanirmitam || 9 ||
vivasvataḥ; tejomayādbhagavata ityarthaḥ|
svabhittau likhitaṃ nītvā prabhavāmi ṣaḍadhvanā|
vārṇaḥ kalāmayaścaiva tāttviko māntrikastathā || 10 ||
pādiko bhauvanaścaiva ṣaḍadhvānaḥ prakīrtitāḥ|
paramaṃ yadahaṃtākhyaṃ saṃvidrūpamanāmayam || 11 ||
unmeṣaḥ prathamastasya varṇādhvā parikīrtitaḥ|
vyākṛtirdaśitā tasya pūrvaṃ te pākaśāsana || 12 ||
tadeva paramaṃ rūpaṃ mama saṃvinmayaṃ mahat|
vivartate'dhvabhāvena jñānādyena kalātmanā || 13 ||
jñānādīnāṃ kalānāṃ tu guṇānāṃ paramātmanaḥ|
purvaṃ te kathitā sūktiryāvanto yādṛśāśca te || 14 ||
adhvadvayamupādāya tadrūpaṃ mama cinmayam|
vāsudevādirūpeṇa vartate tattvavartmanā || 15 ||
adhvadvayam; varṇādhvānaṃ kalādhvānaṃ cetyarthaḥ|
vyūhāśca vibhavāścaiva yaccānyadbhagavanmayam|
tattvādhvano vivṛttiḥ sā kīrtitā paramātmanaḥ || 16 ||
anyat; vyūhāntarāṇi vibhavāntarāṇi cetyarthaḥ|
pūrvādhvadvayamādāya tadeva mama cinmayam|
rūpaṃ vivartate śaśvanmāntreṇa paramādhvanā || 17 ||
uttāraṇāya jīvānāṃ magnānāṃ bhavasāgare|
bhogāya bhavasaṃsthānāṃ vairāgyajananāya ca || 18 ||
mantrādhvaprayojanamāha uttāraṇāyetyādinā|
ārādhanasya siddhyarthaṃ mānasālambanāya ca|
mantrādvā paramodāro mama cidrūpalakṣaṇaḥ || 19 ||
mānasālambanāyeti| śubhāśrayarūpapradarśanena yogināṃ mānasasya dhyānālambanapradānāyetyarthaḥ| etacca śrīviṣṇupurāṇe prapañcitam|
vāsudevādidevānāṃ mūrtibhāvaṃ vrajatyasau|
mantrāḥ sarve cidātmānaḥ sarvagāḥ sarvasādhakāḥ || 20 ||
atha prakṛtaṃ mantramahimānamāha mantrā ityādinā|
trāyamāṇāśca mantāraṃ guptarūpāśca śāstrataḥ|
bhogāpavargadā hyete devadevasya śārṅgiṇaḥ || 21 ||
mahimavarṇanamukhena mantraśabdayaugikārthamāha trāyamāṇāśceti| mandhātoḥ traidhātośca vyutpanna iti bhāvaḥ| vyutpattyantaramāha gupteti| "matri guptabhāṣaṇe" iti dhātuḥ|
rūpaṃ me bhagavanmantrā vijñeyā mūrtayo'malāḥ|
jāgratsvapnau suṣuptiśca turyaṃ ceti catuṣṭayam || 22 ||
jñeyaṃ padādhvano rūpaṃ jāgradbāhyendriyakramaḥ|
bāhyendriyāṇāṃ tamasābhibhūte vibhave sati || 23 ||
svapnāvasthāṃ nirūpayati bāhyetyādinā|
antaḥkaraṇavṛttiryā saṃskārapariśeṣiṇī|
sā svapna iti vijñeyā tadabhāve suṣuptikā || 24 ||
tadabhāve; saṃskārābhāve|
tamasānabhibhūtasya sattvasthasya vipaścitaḥ|
bāhyāntaḥkaraṇasthāyā vṛtteruparame sati || 25 ||
śuddhasattvaprasādasya saṃtatisturyasaṃjñitā|
evaṃ caturvidhe mārge nirdiṣṭe'smin padābhidhe || 26 ||
turyavarjaṃ suṣuptyādiraśuddhāṃ bhajate gatim|
māyādikṣitiparyantā yoktā bhuvanapaddhatiḥ || 27 ||
bhuvanādvānaṃ nirūpayati māyādīti|
bhuvanādhvā sa vijñeyo hyaśuddho malapaṅkilaḥ|
prakrīḍayanti mantrāste śaśvadrāgaparaṃ naram || 28 ||
tattadbhogendrajālāni darśayanto vimohitam|
guruṇā sadayaṃ samyagvīkṣitaṃ karuṇādṛśā || 29 ||
uttārayanti vairāgyaṃ janayantaḥ pade pade|
tataḥ śuddhamayān mārgān prāpayantaḥ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ || 30 ||
pade pade; jāgradādika ityarthaḥ|
śabdabrahmaṇi niṣṇātaṃ prāpayeyuḥ parāṃ śriyam|
evaṃvidhā mahātmāno mantrāḥ paramapāvanāḥ || 31 ||
śabdabrahmaṇi; mantreṣvityarthaḥ|
trividhāste tu vijñeyā adhamā madhyamāḥ pare|
bhavopakaraṇānāṃ ye devānāṃ mūrtitāṃ gatāḥ || 32 ||
pare iti| uttamā ityarthaḥ| bhavopakaraṇāni; sargopakaraṇabhūtāni|
antavatphaladā mantrāste jñeyā adhamā budhaiḥ|
mantrāvibhavadevānāṃ saśaktīnāṃ tu mūrtayaḥ || 33 ||
te jñeyā madhyamā mantrā uttamā vyūhamūrtayaḥ|
ye tu brahmāvagāhante lakṣmīnārāyaṇātmakam || 34 ||
bhavadbhāvavyavasthānāste mantrā uttamottamāḥ|
evaṃ ca jyaiṣṭhyakāniṣṭhyaṃ vijñeyaṃ mantrakovidaiḥ || 35 ||
uttamāḥ pañcarātrasthā madhyamāstu trayīmayāḥ|
tantrāntarasthā vijñeyā adhamāḥ śāsracakṣuṣā || 36 ||
pañcarātrasthā iti| ananyaparasāttvikatamabhāgavatoddeśapravṛttatvāt teṣāmuttamatvam| trayīmayā iti| traiguṇyaviṣayatvātteṣāṃ madhyamatvam|
kalāṅgā uttamā jñeyā anyāṅgā madhyamāḥ smṛtāḥ|
anaṅgā adhamā mantrā bhūyaḥ śṛṇu sureśvara || 37 ||
kalāḥ ; jñānādayaḥ|
bījapiṇḍādisaṃyuktā uttamāḥ parikīrtitāḥ|
bījādyanyatamāntaḥsthā mantrā madhyamasaṃjñitāḥ || 38 ||
abījādiyutā jñeyā mantrā adhamasaṃjñitāḥ|
evaṃ mantravidhā jñātvā hyācāryaḥ śāsralocanaḥ|
yathāsvarūpataḥ śiṣyān jñāpayedarthito manūn || 39 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre ṣaḍadhvamantrasvarūpakathanaṃ nāma dvāviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
iti dvāviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

<23>
trayoviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ
vyāpakaṃ yat paraṃ brahma lakṣmīnārāyaṇaṃ mahat|
ahaṃtā paramā tasya śaktirnārāyaṇī hyaham || 1 ||
anugrahāya lokānāmahamācāryatāṃ gatā|
saṃkarṣaṇasvarūpeṇa śāsraṃ pradyotayāmyaham || 2 ||
punaśca gurumūrtisthā samyagvijñānaśālinī|
śaktimayyā svayā dṛṣṭyā karuṇāmantrapūrṇayā || 3 ||
pālayāmi gururbhūtvā śiṣyānātmopasarpiṇaḥ|
tasmājjñeyaḥ sadā śiṣyairācāryo'sau madātmakaḥ || 4 ||
śiṣyaṃ saṃjñāpayenmantrān yathā tadavadhāraya|
ādau śuddhe same snigdhe deśe bhūdoṣavarjite || 5 ||
bhūdoṣāḥ keśanakhāsthisaṃsargajāḥ, ūṣaratvādayaśca|
varṇānurūpavarṇāḍhye gomayenopalepite|
dhūpite śalyanirmukte gandhapuṣpādyalaṃkṛte || 6 ||
varṇānurūpetyādi| brāhnaṇādivarṇānuguṇasitādivarṇayukte ityarthaḥ| tathā coktaṃ paramasaṃhitāyām "brāhnaṇasya sitā bhūmiḥ kṣatriyasyāruṇā bhavet| pītavarṇā ca vaiśyasya kṛṣṇaā śūdrasya kīrtitā||" iti| śalyāni kaṇṭakādīni|
pañcagavyena saṃsikte candanādyanulepite|
sumṛnmayīṃ ca ghaṭikāṃ kevalaiḥ kusumaiḥ śubhaiḥ || 7 ||
kevalaiḥ; visrastaiḥ, agrathitairityarthaḥ|
praṇavādinamo'ntena svena mantreṇa pūjayet|
vyāhṛtyā parijapyātha pūrvayā praṇavādyayā || 8 ||
pūrvayeti| vyāhṛtyetyarthaḥ|
mṛdaṃ bhūmau prasāryātha gandhadhūpādhivāsitam|
caturaśraṃ suvṛttaṃ vā dvihastaṃ hastameva vā || 9 ||
susamaṃ mātṛkāpīṭhaṃ kṛtvā prastārayettu tām|
ekaiva bhinnavargā yā devī pañcadaśākṣarā || 10 ||
bhinnavargā; kavargādyātmanā bhinnetyarthaḥ|
mantrāṇāṃ jananī sākṣānmama śabdamayī tanuḥ|
padmākāreṇa vā mantrī cakrākāreṇa vā staret || 11 |
pauruṣe cakrarūpaṃ tu pādmīṃ lakṣmīmanukramet|
agnīṣomamayī śaktirvisṛṣṭākhyā dviraṣṭadhā || 12 ||
svarākhyāṃ tāṃ likhetpatramaraṃ vā pūrvadiggatam|
pṛthivyādipumantā ye pañca vargāstu kādayaḥ || 13 ||
pṛthivī kakāraḥ| pumān makāraḥ|
agnyādivāyuparyante tāṃ likhedarapatravat|
antaḥsthadhāraṇārūpaṃ yādivāntacatuṣṭayam || 14 ||
udaggataṃ likhet patramaraṃ vā pūrvavadbudhaḥ|
śādyaṃ kṣāntaṃ turīyāntaṃ yaduktaṃ brahmapañcakam || 15 ||
tallikhedaiśadiksaṃsthamaraṃ patramathāpi vā|
śabdākhyaṃ yatparaṃ brahma jyotirmayamanāmayam || 16 ||
dhyāyedālokarūpeṇa paryante cakrapadmayoḥ|
praṇavādyairnamo'ntaistairakṣaraistattvasaṃjñakaiḥ || 17 ||
prakṛtiṃ tvarcayettatra tattvarūpāṃ tu māṃ budhaḥ|
tatastatkarṇikāmadhye cintayenmantramātṛkām || 18 ||
anādinidhanāṃ devīmahaṃtāṃ puruṣottamīm|
pāśāṅkuśadharāṃ devīṃ padminīṃ padmamālinīm || 19 ||
puruṣottamasya srī puruṣottamī|
prasannāṃ padmagarbhābhāṃ sarvalokamaheśvarīm|
varṇapraklṛptāvayavāṃ varṇālaṃkārabhūṣitām || 20 ||
śabdabrahma tanuṃ vidyāt praṇavaṃ tu śiraḥ smaret|
a ā iti bhruvauvidyādi ī vidyāttu cakṣuṣī || 21 ||
u ū karṇau ṛ ṝ nāsāpuṭāvanyau kapolakau|
e ai oṣṭhau ca vijñeyau o au daśanapaṅktike || 22 ||
anyau; lṛlṝvarṇāvityarthaḥ| anayornapuṃsakatvānna nāmnā nirdeśaḥ|
aṃ jihvāmaḥ samuccāraṃ kacavargau karau smaret|
ṭatavargau padau vidyāt paphau pārśve smaredbudhaḥ || 23 ||
babhau paścātpurobhāgau maṃ nābhiṃ paricintayet|
prāṇoṣmāṇau yarau vidyāllaṃ hāraṃ paricintayet || 24 ||
vakāraṃ kaṭisūtraṃ tu kuṇḍale tu śaṣau smaret|
sakāraṃ hṛdayaṃ vidyāddhṛdayasthaṃ tu haṃ smaret || 25 ||
prasarantīṃ prabhāṃ vidyāt kṣakāraṃ vidyudujjvalām|
raṅgaṃ nāsāgragaṃ vidyādyamākhyaṃ hṛdaye smaret || 26 ||
raṅgam; anunāsikam|
jihvāmūlīyakaṃ jihvāmūle vidyādanantaram|
upadhmānīyakaṃ vidyādoṣṭhayoḥ kramaśastathā || 27 ||
śubhairvarṇamayaiḥ padmairagnīṣomamayaiḥ kṛtām|
bibhratīṃ vanamālāṃ ca kaṇṭhātpādāvalambinīm || 28 ||
agnīṣomārkakoṭyābhaṃ sphuradratnavibhūṣitam|
makuṭaṃ cintayedvidvān hakāraṃ pārameśvaram || 29 ||
evaṃ saṃsmṛtya tāṃ devīṃ mātṛkāṃ mantramātaram|
pūjayedarghyapuṣpādyairauṃ namo mantramātṛke || 30 ||
idamarghyaṃ gṛhāṇeti bhogairevamanukramāt|
tataḥ kṛtāñjaliḥ prahvaḥ praṇamyāṣṭāṅgavadbhuvi || 31 ||
padmasthe padmanilaye padme padmākṣavallabhe|
sarvatattvakṛtādhāre mantrāṇāṃ jananīśvari || 32 ||
vyākuru tvaṃ paraṃ divyaṃ rūpaṃ lakṣmīmayaṃ mama|
prārthyaivaṃ prayato mantrī svayaṃ lakṣmīmayo bhavet || 33 ||
mātṛkākṛtavinyāsaḥ svayaṃ sanmātṛkāmayaḥ|
uddharedīpsitaṃ mantraṃ śiṣyasyopadiśettataḥ || 34 ||
mātṛketyādi| akārādikṣakārāntairvarṇaiḥ kṛtaḥ vinyāsaḥ padmacakrayo nikṣepaḥ yena saḥ|
bījapiṇḍātmakā mantrā mantreṣu śreṣṭhatāṃ gatāḥ|
tatra śreṣṭhāni bījāni piṇḍebhyo'pi sureśvara || 35 ||
bījeṣu ratnabhūtāni sapta bījāni vāsava|
tārakaḥ prathamaṃ bījaṃ dvitīyaṃ tārikā smṛtā || 36 ||
tārādimantroddhārakramo'nantarādhyāye vakṣyate|
tayostu tejasā tulyaṃ tṛtīyamanutārikā|
caturthaṃ tu jagadyoniḥ paramaṃ bījamucyate || 37 ||
prādyumnaṃ pañcamaṃ bījaṃ ṣaṣṭhaṃ sārasvataṃ matam|
mahālakṣmīmayaṃ bījaṃ saptamaṃ parikīrtitam || 38 ||
sthūlasūkṣmaparatvaṃ tu pratisvaṃ parikīrtitame|
tvaṃ śakrāvahito bhūtvā śrṛṇu bījānyanukramāt || 39 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre mātṛkāprakāśo nāma trayoviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
iti trayoviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

<24>
caturviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ
paraṃ brahma paraṃ dāma paraṃ jyotiranūpamam|
lakṣmīnārāyaṇaṃ brahma doṣasūnyaṃ nirañjanam || 1 ||
ekaṃ sarvamidaṃ vyāpya sthitaṃ sarvottaraṃ mahaḥ|
ahaṃtāhaṃ parā tasya brahmaṇaḥ paramātmanaḥ || 2 ||
hitāya sarvajīvānāmunmiṣantī svavāñchayā|
śabdabrahmamayī bhūtvā mātṛkāmantravigrahā || 3 ||
bhavāmi mantrarūpāhaṃ tattadvācyānukāriṇī|
prathamaṃ tārarūpeṇa yathāsmyevaṃ samuddharet || 4 ||
