prathamaṃ dhruvamādāya tataḥ karṇaṃ samuddharet|
nābhiṃ samuddharetpaścāt trayamekatra yojayet || 5 ||
omityetat samutpannaṃ prathamaṃ brahmatārakam|
bindunā bhūṣayet paścānnādena tadanantaram || 6 ||
dhyāyet saṃtatanādena tailadhārāmivātatām|
etattadvaiṣṇavaṃ rūpaṃ tryakṣaraṃ brahma śāśvatam || 7 ||
aniruddhastvakāro'tra pradyumnaḥ pañcamaḥ svaraḥ|
saṃkarṣaṇo makārastu vāsudevastu bindukaḥ || 8 ||
caturṇāmavibhāgastu nādastatra sureśvara|
nādasya yā parā kāṣṭhā sāhaṃtā parameśvarī || 9 ||
śaktiḥ sā paramā sūkṣmā nādāntagaganāhvayā|
śabdabrahmamayī sūkṣmā sāhaṃ sarvāvagāhinī || 10 ||
nādāntetyādi| pūrvoktasya nādasyānte yat gaganaṃ daharākāśaḥ, tadrūpetyarthaḥ| devyā daharākāśarūpatvāt parabrahmaṇastanniyatavasatitvaṃ śrūyate "tatrāpi dahraṃ gaganaṃ viśokastasmin yadantastadupāsitavyam" iti| śrīsāttvate ca "nādāvasānagagane devo'nantaḥ samanvitaḥ" iti|
virāme sati nādasya yaḥ sphuṭībhavati svayam|
jyotistatparamaṃ brahma lakṣmīnārāyaṇāhvayam || 11 ||
etatte vaiṣṇavaṃ dhāma kathitaṃ pauruṣaṃ param|
śāntamasyaiva yadrūpaṃ tasya tattvaṃ niśāmaya || 12 ||
visṛṣṭiṃ pūrvamādāya sūryamante niyojayet|
saṃnikarṣe pare jāte tadomityuditaṃ mahaḥ || 13 ||
visṛṣṭiḥ visargaḥ| sūryaḥ aṃkāraḥ| aḥ aṃ iti sthite "ato roḥ" ityuttve guṇe pūrvarūpe ca omiti bhavati|
etat tat paramaṃ dhāma śāktisaṃhāralakṣaṇam|
smaryamāṇaṃ paraṃ tattvaṃ prakāśayati yad dhruvam || 14 ||
visargabindusaṃyogarūpatvāt sṛṣṭisaṃhāralakṣaṇamityarthaḥ|
saṃhṛtya sarvasaṃbhāraṃ śuddhāśuddhādhvasaṃbhavam|
sṛṣṭau samudyatā śaktiḥ sūrye puṃsi sanātane || 15 ||
sūrye puṃsi; bindurūpe|
parame bhoktṛrūpe sā vidhāya pratisaṃcaram|
agnīṣomamayādbhāvāt sthūlātsā pratinirgatā || 16 ||
dāṃpatyaṃ madhyamaṃ śaśvadbindunādamayaṃ śritā|
śaktiḥ śāntātmakaṃ divyaṃ sūkṣmadāṃpatyamāśritā || 17 ||
pratitiṣṭhati sā divye vyāpake paramātmani|
asya mātrā vidhānajñaiḥ sārdhāstisra udāhṛtāḥ || 18 ||
trayo'gnayasrayo lokāsrayo vedāsrayo guṇāḥ|
trayo devāsrayo vyūhāsrayo varṇāsrayaḥ svarāḥ || 19 ||
tritayaṃ tritayaṃ śakra yat kiṃcijjagatīgatam|
tadādi tritayaṃ jñeyamardhamātrā nirañjanā || 20 ||
ādi tritayamiti| akārādi trayamityarthaḥ| ardhamātrā binduḥ|
sarve śabdā akārotthā ukārāttejasāṃ trayam|
pṛthivyādi prakṛtyantaṃ makārotthaṃ puraṃdara || 21 ||
tejasāṃ trayam; sūryasomāgnirūpam|
jyotirmayyardhamātrā sā cinmayī paramā kalā|
yugbhiḥ svaraiḥ sabindvantairādyantasvaraṣaṭkayoḥ || 22 ||
paramā kalā; nādaḥ| ādisvaraṣaṭkam akārādyūkārāntam| antasvaraṣaṭkam lṛkārādyaukārāntam| teṣu yuksvarāḥ ā, ī, ū, lṝ, ai, au iti| taiḥ sabindubhi raṅganyāsaḥ| upāṅganyāsastu satāraiḥ jñānādipadaiḥ|
jñānādiguṇaṣaṭkāntairaṅgaklṛptiramuṣya tu|
nābhau pṛṣṭhe tathā bāhvorūrujānupadeṣu ca || 23 ||
tārapūrvān guṇān bhūyo vinyaset pākaśāsana|
evaṃ vinyasya tanmantramaṅgopāṅgasamanvitam || 24 ||
svadehe gururātmasthaṃ cintayet puruṣottamam|
viśvādilayapūrvaṃ tu yathāvat tannibodha me || 25 ||
viśvaṃ jāgratpadeśānaṃ sarvendriyasamīrakam|
bhoktāraṃ śabdapūrvāṇāṃ pañcānāṃ viṣayātmanām || 26 ||
viśveti jāgratpadasthātmanāma|
aniruddhātmakaṃ taṃ ca cintayet prathamākṣaram|
taṃ sopakaraṇaṃ devamakāre pravilāpya tu || 27 ||
aniruddhapradyumnasaṃkarṣaṇavāsudevalakṣmīnārāyaṇāḥ viśvataijasaprājñaturyaturyātītasaṃjñakajāgradādyadhiṣṭhātāro devāḥ|
akāraṃ taijase deve pradyumne svapnavartmage|
antaḥkaraṇavṛttīnāṃ prerake pravilāpayet || 28 ||
taṃ sopakaraṇaṃ devamukāre pravilāpayet|
ukāraṃ cesvare prājñe saṃkarṣaṇatanusthite || 29 ||
suṣuptipadage śaśvat prāṇādiprerake vibhau|
vilāpya taṃ ca deveśaṃ turyasaṃsthe'rthamātrake || 30 ||
jñānānandamaye deve vāsudeve vilāpayet|
turyātīte ca tatturyaṃ lakṣmīnārāyaṇātmani || 31 ||
pravilāpya svayaṃ divyāmahaṃtāṃ vaiṣṇavīṃ śrayet|
tanmayastādṛśaṃ prāpya layasthānaṃ tataḥ kramāt || 32 ||
tanmayaḥ; ahaṃtāmayaḥ|
jāgarāmavatīryātha dīkṣitaṃ śiṣyamagrataḥ|
sadgururmanmayo bhūtvā tāramadhyāpayetsvayam || 33 ||
layasthānāt sṛṣṭisthānāvataraṇacintāmāha jāgarāmiti|
sāṅgopāṅgakramaṃ śaśvat sasamādhiṃ savistaram|
sa ca dadyāt svamātmānaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ gurave dhanaiḥ || 34 ||
labdhānujñastataḥ kurvan pauraścaraṇikaṃ vidhim|
mahānadītaṭaṃ gatvā siddhādyāyatanaṃ tu vā || 35 ||
puraścaraṇaṃ nāma gurorlabdhasya mantrasya vīryavattaratvasaṃpādanāyānuṣṭheyo japahomādiḥ| tacca pañcāṅgam| tathā coktam "japahaumau tarpaṇaṃ cābhiṣeko viprabhojanam| pañcāṅgopāsanaṃ loke puraścaraṇamucyate||" iti|
pālāśaṃ vā vanaṃ samyakparyantādṛṣṭabhūtalam|
snānaṃ triṣavaṇaṃ kurvan brahmacārī jitendriyaḥ || 36 ||
payoyāvakabhaikṣāṇāmaśannanyatamaṃ sakṛt|
kuśoccaye niṣaṇṇaḥ san kāśacīrakuśeśayaḥ || 37 ||
pālāśaṃ dhārayeddaṇḍaṃ saṃvītaḥ kṛṣṇacarmaṇā|
maccitto manmayo bhūtvā gurvādiṣṭena vartmanā || 38 ||
nityaṃ yogaparo bhūtvā samyagjñānasamādhimān|
daśalakṣaṃ japenmaunī tāraṃ saṃsāratārakam || 39 ||
daśāṃśaṃ juhuyāt parṇaiḥ samidbhiḥ sarpiṣāpi vā|
prītā tasya prakāśe'hamahaṃtā vaiṣṇavī parā || 40 ||
daśāṃśamiti| lakṣakṛtva ityarthaḥ|
sādhakasya tataḥ samyak sadvivekini cetasi|
lakṣmīnārāyaṇākhyaṃ tat sāmarasyaṃ prakāśate || 41 ||
jīvanneva bhavenmuktaḥ punīte cakṣuṣā jagat|
siddhāḥ syustasya mantrāste laukikā vaidikāśca ye || 42 ||
snātaḥ sarveṣu vedeṣu vidyāsu sakalāsu ca|
siddhānteṣu ca sarveṣu tīrtheṣu ca bhavedasau || 43 ||
prayogāḥ sarvamantrāṇāṃ yāvanto yādṛśāśca ye|
tāvantastādṛśāste'sya prayogā iti nirṇayaḥ || 44 ||
asya vyāhṛtayastisro varṇatrayasamudgatāḥ|
pabhdyaḥ samudgatā hyasyāḥ sāvitrī sarvapāvanī || 45 ||
asyāḥ padbhyasrayo vedā ṛgyajuḥsāmalakṣaṇāḥ|
ityetanmayamevedaṃ laukikaṃ vaidikaṃ vacaḥ || 46 ||
yathā nyagrodhadhānāyāmantarbhūto mahādrūmaḥ|
tathedaṃ vāṅmayaṃ viśvamasminnantaḥ sthitaṃ sadā || 47 ||
tārasya samastavāṅmayarūpatve dṛṣṭān ucyate yatheti| dhānā sūkṣmaṃ bījam| mahādrumasya bīje sūkṣmatayāvasthānakathanaṃ cātra sāṃkhyakatkāryavādamanurudhya| aupaniṣadamate tu dhānāyā mahādrumasya copādānaikyāt satkāryavāda iti avasthābhedāt kāryakāraṇabhāvavyavahāra iti ca bhidā|
etadādyaṃ mahābījaṃ śabdānāṃ prakṛtiḥ parā|
śabdabrahma paraṃ dhāma pavitraṃ paramaṃ mahat || 48 ||
oṃkāraḥ praṇavastāro haṃso nārāyaṇo dhruvaḥ|
vedātmā sarvavedādirādityaḥ sarvapāvanaḥ || 49 ||
mokṣado muktimārgaśca sarvasaṃdhāraṇakṣamaḥ|
evamādīni nāmāni śāsre śāsre vicakṣaṇaiḥ || 50 ||
adhītāni mahāpuṇyānyoṃkārasya mahātmanaḥ|
idaṃ śaraṇamajñānāmidameva vijānatām || 51 ||
ayamanvicchatāṃ svargaḥ potaḥ pāraṃ titīrṣatām|
hakāraukārasaṃyogādayaṃ prāsādasaṃjñakaḥ || 52 ||
piṇḍo'yaṃ sarvatattvānāṃ piṇḍabhūtaḥ sanātanaḥ|
sādhanaṃ pratipattiśca viniyogho'tha dhāraṇā || 53 ||
bījasyeva sureśāna prāsādasyāsya viddhi tat|
asyaiva saṃjñāmantro'yaṃ haṃso nāma mahāmanuḥ || 54 ||
bhoktāraṃ prathamaṃ varṇaṃ viddhi bhogyaṃ dvitīyakam|
nārāyaṇamayaṃ pūrvamakṣaraṃ śrīmayaṃ param || 55 ||
prathamaṃ varṇamiti| hakāramityarthaḥ| dvitīyakamiti| sakāramityarthaḥ|
agnīṣomātmakāvetau varṇau viddhi sanātanau|
anayorantarā śakra bindudharmau vyavasthitau || 56 ||
bindudharmau sṛṣṭisaṃhārau|
ādhārānmūrdhaparyantaṃ bhoktāraṃ varṇamunnayet|
visṛjenmukhato varṇaṃ dvitīyaṃ bhogyasaṃjñakam || 57 ||
ādhārasthānamārabhya mūrdhaparyantagāminā pavanena hakāramuccaret| sakāraṃ mukhato visṛjet|
sarvā sṛṣṭiḥ kṛtā tena haṃsoccāraprayogataḥ|
ajapeyaṃ samākhyātā vidyā sarvāṅgaśobhanā || 58 ||
haṃsamantrasyājapāmantra iti nāma|
catuḥṣaṣṭyadhikāśītikoṭisaṃkhyāsu yoniṣu|
tadbhedeṣu ca mantro'yaṃ svayamuccarate sadā || 59 ||
niśvāsena samaṃ vidyā samudetyantarujjvalā|
udayāstamayāvasyāḥ śvāsaniśvāsatulyakau || 60 ||
ṣaṣṭiḥ śvāsā bhavetprāṇāḥ ṣaṭ prāṇā nāḍikā matā|
nāḍyaḥ ṣaṣṭirahorātramevaṃ kālakriyāgatiḥ || 61 ||
evaṃ haṃsodayādviddhi sahasrāṇyekaviṃśatim|
śatāni ṣaṭ ca deveśa tāvantaḥ syurjapāḥ kṛtāḥ || 62 ||
haṃsamantre sakṛjjapte sati 21600 saṃkhyākā japāḥ kṛtā bhavantīti bhāvaḥ|
kiṃtu saṃkalpanaṃ kuryādaharādau manīṣayā|
evaṃsaṃkhyān japānasya kariṣyāmīti buddhimān || 63 ||
evaṃsaṃkhyāniti| iyatsaṃkhyākānityarthaḥ|
vinyaset pañca cāṅgāni teṣāṃ rūpaṃ nibodha me|
sūryasomau caturthyantau namaḥ svāhāsamanvitau || 64 ||
sūryasomau hakārasakārau|
nirañjanau nirābhāsau vauṣaḍhuṃphaḍantakau|
phaḍantaṃ mūlamevāsramityaṅgānyasya pañca tu || 65 ||
ayameva viparyastaḥ paramātmamanuḥ smṛtaḥ|
smṛtvā śaktiṃ sasaṃbhārāṃ sūrye bhoktari saṃnayet || 66 ||
viparyasta iti| so'hamiti mantra ityarthaḥ| sakāreṇa śaktimādāya hakāre paramātmani yojayedityarthaḥ|
śiṣṭaṃ praṇavavaccintyamiti saṃjñāmanorvidhiḥ|
padamantrāsrayo'sya syurvidhāne pāñcarātrike || 67 ||
traya iti| yadyapi catvāro mantrā vakṣyante| tathāpi teṣu kaṃcidbhedamādāya trayāṇāṃ pṛthak nirdeśaḥ kriyate|
viṣṇave nama ityevaṃ namo nārāyaṇāya ca|
namo bhagavate pūrvaṃ vāsudevāya cetyapi || 68 ||
mantrasvarūpāṇyāha viṣṇave ityādi| ādau namo bhagavate iti| antevāsudevāyeti| namo bhagavate vāsudevāyeti mantra ityarthaḥ|
jitaṃ te puṇḍarīkākṣa namaste viśvabhāvana|
namaste'stu hṛṣīkeśa mahāpuruṣa pūrvaja || 69 ||
padamantraścaturtho'yaṃ praṇavasya puraṃdara|
oṃkārasahitānetān mantrān pūrvavido viduḥ || 70 ||
jñānādiguṇasaṃyuktairakṣaraiḥ praṇavādibhiḥ|
namo'ntairaṅgaklṛptiḥ syāttathaivopāṅgakalpanā || 71 ||
nyūnākṣarasya mantrasya varṇena carameṇa tu|
upāṅgakalpanā kāryā tattadguṇapadairyutā || 72 ||
nyūnākṣarasyeti| ṣaḍakṣarāṣṭākṣaramantrayoḥ nyūnākṣaratvam| tatra caramākṣareṇa pūraṇaṃ kartavyamityarthaḥ|
tathaiva sphītavarṇasya śiṣṭaistu dvādaśādhikaiḥ|
samastaiścaramopāṅgaṃ kalpayettejasā saha || 73 ||
sphītavarṇasyeti| adhikavarṇasyetyarthaḥ; yathā jitaṃ te iti mantre|
kevalastārakaścaiva catvāraśca tadādikāḥ|
pañcaite vyāpakā mantrāḥ pāñcarātre prakīrtitāḥ || 74 ||
nāsādhyaṃ kiṃcidastīha mantrairebhirmahātmabhiḥ|
niśreṇī pañcaparvaiṣā parabrahmādhirohaṇe || 75 ||
eṣā divyā mahāsattā pañcamantrī tu manmayī|
arcanājjapato dhyānādimāṃ samyak samāśritaḥ|
svāṃ sattāṃ vaiṣṇavīṃ prāpya paraṃ brahmādhigacchati || 76 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre tāraprakāśo nāma caturviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
iti caturviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

<25>
pañcaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ
itthaṃ te kathitaḥ śakra tārakasyaiṣa vistaraḥ|
saṃsāratārikāyā me tārikāyāḥ śṛṇu kramam || 1 ||
varṇānāṃ vidhisiddhyarthaṃ saṃjñāḥ pūrvaṃ niśāmaya|
śrutāsu vidhivadyāsu vidhirmāntraḥ pravartate || 2 ||
akāraścāprameyaśca prathamo vyāpakaḥ smṛtaḥ|
ādidevastathākāra ānando gopanaḥ smṛtaḥ || 3 ||
rāmasaṃjña ikāraśca iddha iṣṭaḥ prakīrtitaḥ|
īkāraḥ pañcabindurvai viṣṇurmāyā puraṃdara || 4 ||
ukāro bhuvanākhyaśca uddāma udayastathā|
ūkāra ūrjo lokeśaḥ prajñādhārākhya eva ca || 5 ||
satyaśca ṛtadhāmā ca ṛkāraḥ sa tu cāṅkuśaḥ|
ṝkāro viṣṭarākhyaśca jvālā caiva prasāraṇam || 6 ||
liṅgātmā bhagavān prokto lṛkārastārakaḥ smṛtaḥ|
lṛkāro dīrghaghoṇaśca devadattastathā virāṭ || 7 ||
tryaśra ekārasaṃjñaśca jagadyoniravigrahaḥ|
aiśvaryaṃ yogadhātā ca ai sa airāvaṇaḥ smṛtaḥ || 8 ||
okāra otadehaśca odanaścaiva vikramī|
aurvo'tha bhūdharākhyaśca au smṛto hyauṣadhātmakaḥ || 9 ||
trailokyaiśvaryado vyāpī vyomeśoṃ'kāra eva ca|
visargaḥ sṛṣṭikṛtkhyāto hyaḥkāraḥ parameśvaraḥ || 10 ||
kamalaśca karālaśca kakāraḥ prakṛtiḥ parā|
khakāraḥ kharvadehaśca vedātmā viśvabhāvanaḥ || 11 ||
gadadhvaṃsī gakārastu govindaśca gadādharaḥ|
ghakārastvatha gharmāṃśustejasvī dīptimāṃstathā || 12 ||
ṅakāra ekadaṃṣṭrākhyo bhūtātmā bhūtabhāvanaḥ|
cakāraścañcalaścakrī candrāṃśuḥ sa tu kathyate || 13 ||
chandaḥpatiśchaladhvaṃsī chakāraśchanda eva ca|
janmahantājitaścaiva jakāraścaiva śāśvataḥ || 14 ||
jhakāro jhaṣasaṃjñaśca sāmagaḥ sāmapāṭhakaḥ|
īśvaraścottamākhyaśca ñakārastattvadhārakaḥ || 15 ||
candrī ṭakāra āhlādo viśvāpyāyakarastathā|
dhārādharaṣṭhakāraśca nemiḥ kaustubha ucyate || 16 ||
daṇḍadhāro ḍakāraśca mausalo'khaṇḍavikramaḥ|
ḍhakāro viśvarūpaśca vṛṣakarmā pratardanaḥ || 17 ||
ṇakāro'bhayadaḥ śāstā vaikuṇṭha iti kīrtitaḥ|
takārastālalakṣmā ca vairājaḥ sraghdharaḥ smṛtaḥ || 18 ||
dhanvī bhuvanapālaśca thakāraḥ sarvarodhakaḥ|
dattāvakāśo damano dakāraḥ śāntidaḥ smṛtaḥ || 19 ||
dhakāraḥ śārṅgadhṛddhartā mādhavaśca prakīrtitaḥ|
naro nārāyaṇaḥ panthā nakāraḥ samudāhṛtaḥ || 20 ||
pakāraḥ padmanābhaśca pavitraḥ paścimānanaḥ|
phakāraḥ phullanayano lāṅgalī śvetasaṃjñitaḥ || 21 ||
bakāro vāmano hrasvaḥ pūrṇāṅgaḥ sa ca kathyate|
bhallātako bhakāraśca jñeyaḥ siddhiprado dhruvaḥ || 22 ||
makāro mardanaḥ kālaḥ pradhānaḥ paripaṭhyate|
caturgatiryakāraśca susūkṣmaḥ śaṅkha ucyate || 23 ||
aśeṣabhuvanādhāro ro'nalaḥ kālapāvakaḥ|
lakāro vibudhākhyaśca dhareśaḥ puruṣeśvaraḥ || 24 ||
varāhaścāmṛtādhāro vakāro varuṇaḥ smṛtaḥ|
śakāraḥ śaṃkaraḥ śāntaḥ puṇḍarīkaḥ prakīrtitaḥ || 25 ||
nṛsiṃhaścāgnirūpaśca ṣakāro bhāskarastathā|
sakārastvamṛtastṛptiḥ somaśca parikīrtitaḥ || 26 ||
sūryo hakāraḥ prāṇastu paramātmā prakīrtitaḥ|
ananteśaḥ kṣakārastu vargānto garuḍastathā || 27 ||
aśeṣasaṃjñā varṇānāmityetāḥ kīrtitā mayā|
anulomavilomena varṇyā varṇasya vai punaḥ || 28 ||
saṃjñāyāmanulomena saṃkhyāyāṃ vilomena cetyarthaḥ| "aṅkānāṃ vāmato gatiḥ" iti prasiddham|
saṃjñā saṃkhyā ca yā śakra sāmānyā sā mahāmate|
cidaṃśāḥ sarva evaite varṇā bhāsvaravigrahāḥ || 29 ||
kāraṇaṃ sarvamantrāṇāṃ lakṣmīśaktyupabṛṃhitāḥ|
stutā saṃpūjitā dhyātā varṇāḥ saṃjñābhirādarāt || 30 ||
prayacchanti parāmṛddhiṃ vijñānaṃ bhāvayantyapi|
parasparāṅgabhāvaṃ ca mantrotpattau vrajantyamī || 31 ||
carācare'smiṃstannāsti yadamībhirna bhāvitam|
nityā yadyapi tā divyā mantrāṇāṃ mūrtayaḥ parāḥ || 32 ||
tathāpyevaṃvidhairvarṇairbhāvitā iti cintanā|
bhavanti pūrṇasāmarthyā mantrāḥ śāsranidarśanāt || 33 ||
ālambanaṃ dhiyāṃ caiva bhavantyevaṃ mahāmate|
abhāge'pi yathā vyomni dhiyā bhāgaḥ prakalpyate || 34 ||
saukaryāya tathā mantre varṇabhāgo'nucintyate|
kṛtvaiva bhāvagāṃ vyāptiṃ varṇānāṃ pūjanaṃ tridhā || 35 ||
bhūmau padme tathā devyāstanau mantrān samuddharet|
paramātmānamādāya yojayet kālavahninā || 36 ||
tārikāmantramuddharati paramātmānamiti| paramātmā hakāraḥ| kālavahniḥ rephaḥ| māyā īkāraḥ| trailokyaiśvaryadaḥ anusvāraḥ| eṣāṃ yoge hrīṃ iti tārikāmantraḥ|
trailokyaiśvaryadopetamāyāmasminniyojayet|
iyaṃ sā paramā śaktirvaiṣṇavī sarvakāmadā || 37 ||
sattā pūrṇā cidānandā mama mūrtirnirantarā|
iyaṃ sā paramā niṣṭhā yā sā brahmavidāṃ dhruvā || 38 ||
asyāṃ niṣṭhāya tattvajñā viśanti brahma manmayam|
saiṣā tattvavidāṃ mukhyaiḥ śāsre śāsre vicintyate || 39 ||
otaṃ protamamuṣyāṃ vai jagacchabdārthatāmayam|
anayaiva sadā sāṃkhyaiḥ saṃkhyāye'haṃ sanātanī || 40 ||
anayaiva samādhisthaiḥ samādhīye samādhinā|
abhidhīye'nayaivāhaṃ śaivaiḥ ṣaṭtriṃśadantimā || 41 ||
mahārājñī tathaivāhamanayaiva trayī parā|
ṛgyajuḥsāmasaṃghāte cintye saure ca maṇḍale || 42 ||
taruṇīṃ rūpasaṃpannāṃ sarvāvayavasundarīm|
anayaiva vyavasyanti lokāyatavicakṣaṇāḥ || 43 ||
kṣaṇabhaṅgavidhānajñaiścintye nirviṣayā ca dhīḥ|
ārhataiścānayaivāhaṃ yakṣīnāmnā sadoditā || 44 ||
paramā tārikā śaktistāriṇī tārikākṛtiḥ|
lakṣmīḥ padmā mahālakṣmīstārā gaurī nirañjanā || 45 ||
hṛllekhā paramātmasthā yā śaktirbhuvaneśvarī|
cicchaktiḥ śāntirūpā ca ghoṣaṇī ghoṣasaṃbhavā || 46 ||
kāmadhenurmahādhenurjagadyonirvibhāvarī|
evamādīni nāmāni śāsre śāsre vicakṣaṇaiḥ || 47 ||
tārikāyā niruktāni vede vede ca paṇḍitaiḥ|
asyā evāparā mūrtirvijñeyā tvanutārikā || 48 ||
śāntaṃ niyojayet sthāne pūrvasya paramātmanaḥ|
śeṣamanyatsamaṃ hyeṣā tanurme'nyānutārikā || 49 ||
śāntaḥ śakāraḥ| anyat samamiti| tārikayā samam| rīṃ iti yojanīyamityarthaḥ| śrīṃ ityanutārikāmantraḥ|
tārikāyāmivāsyāṃ ca vijñeyaṃ vaibhavaṃ mahat|
ime śaktī pare divye mama tanvau puraṃdara || 50 ||
yatkiṃcidetayā sādhyaṃ sādhanīyaṃ tadanyayā|
ime pūrvāparībhāvaṃ prajato'nyonyavāñchayā||
samyaksādhayataścaiva sādhakānāmabhīpsitam || 51 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre tārānutārāprakāśo nāma pañcaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
iti pañcaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

<26>
ṣaḍviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śakraḥ
paraṃ brahma paraṃ dhāma padmasthe padmamālini|
namaste padmaje padme govindagṛhamedhini || 1 ||
dve ete kathite devi tava tanvau sanātane|
viśeṣastvasti vā kaścidanayoḥ sūkṣmarūpataḥ || 2 ||
śrīḥ
ekamādau paraṃ tattvaṃ lakṣmīnārāyaṇātmakam|
pūrṇastimitaṣāḍguṇyaṃ svacchasvacchandaciddhanam || 3 ||
tasyāhaṃ paramā śaktiḥ sarvāvasthānusāriṇī|
devī sā paramā divyā sthūlasūkṣmaparāhvayā || 4 ||
mama tanvāvime śaktī tārikā cānutārikā|
duhāte sakalān kāmānubhe ete puraṃdara || 5 ||
ubhe ete mate divye ubhe niṣṭhe pare smṛte|
ubhe saṃsthā mate sarvā ubhe te viṣṇuvallabhe || 6 ||
ubhe ete vicintyātha gacchanti paramāṃ gatim|
tattvaṃ tu paramaṃ sūkṣmaṃ gadantyā me niśāmaya || 7 ||
paraṃ brahma tataḥ śāntaṃ tato nāda iti kramaḥ|
sarvatrāvasthitā sāhaṃ nimeṣonmeṣarūpiṇī || 8 ||
ādyaṃ yat paramaṃ brahma sūkṣmaṃ stimitaśaktikam|
tārastatra pratiṣṭhāya tanoti vitatāṃ gatim || 9 ||
bhavato brahmaṇo yo'yamunmeṣaḥ paramātmanaḥ|
bhavadbhāvātmake tasmiṃstārikā pratitiṣṭhati || 10 ||
brahmaṇastvavaroho yaḥ śāntarūpaḥ sisṛkṣayā|
śāntākhye bhāvabhūyiṣṭhe tasminnāste'nutārikā || 11 ||
dvitīyastvavaroho yaḥ śaktyākhyo bhāva ūrjitaḥ|
vāgbhavādīni bījāni tatra tiṣṭhanti vāsava || 12 ||
etāvānanayorbhedaḥ proktaste sūkṣmadhīmayaḥ|
vāgbhavādīni bījāni gadantyā me niśāmaya || 13 ||
trailokyaiśvaryadopetamaiśvaryaṃ varṇamuddharet|
jagadyoniridaṃ bījaṃ vāgbhavākhyamudāhṛtam || 14 ||
vāgbhavabījamāha trailokyetyādinā| aiśvaryam aikāraḥ| tena sahānusvārayoge aiṃ iti bījamantraḥ| asya jagadyonitvamanantaraṃ vakṣyate|
saiṣā kuṇḍalinī śaktiryasyāṃ kuṇḍalitaṃ jagat|
śabdaśaktisvarūpeṇa yathā tadavadhāraya || 15 ||
yasyāṃ kuṇḍalitaṃ jagaditi kuṇḍalinīśabdasya yogavyutpattirabhipretā|
ī māyā paramā śaktirjagadyonirnirañjanā|
aprameyasya sā hi śrīrgṛhiṇī gṛhamedhinaḥ || 16 ||
asyāḥ pūrvamikāraṃ tu yojayet sūkṣmacakṣuṣā|
itthaṃ yadiṣṭaṃ yad dravyaṃ yattattatra pratiṣṭhitam || 17 ||
ānandamasya pūrvaṃ tu cintayet sūkṣmacakṣuṣā|
aprameyaṃ tataḥ pūrvaṃ yojayet sūkṣmackṣuṣā || 18 ||
aikārasya varṇacatuṣṭayasamāhārarūpatvamāha ī māyeti| asmāt pūrvamiṣṭākhyamikāraṃ, tataḥ pūrvamānandākhyamākāraṃ, tataḥ pūrvamaprameyākhyamakāraṃ ca yojayitvā saṃdhau kṛte ai iti rūpamiti bhāvaḥ|
śaktireṣā jagadyonisrailokyaiśvaryadojjvalā|
aprameyādanādyantādvyāpakāt paramātmanaḥ || 19 ||
gopanī sarvabhūtānāṃ śaktirānandanirbharā|
icchājñānakriyārūpairikārotthaiḥ samanvitā || 20 ||
trailokyaiśvaryadā devī viṣṇupatnī jagatprasūḥ|
iti vācyāṃ jagadyonibijasyābjāṃ vicintayet || 21 ||
ratiḥ krīḍābhidhā loke krīḍā ca syāt kriyā mama|
indhanaṃ dīpanaṃ jñānamicchā cekāradarśitāḥ || 22 ||
trailokyaṃ tu trayo lokāste ca jīvāsridhā sthitāḥ|
teṣāmaiśvaryadānena trailokyaiśvaryadāhvikā || 23 ||
aprameyādinā lokān vitatya bhuvanādhvanā|
tasminneva pare bhūyo vyomeśe paramātmani || 24 ||
bījākṣaraghaṭakasya dīrgekārasyārthamāha ratiriti| ratirīkāraḥ| indhanādayastadarthāḥ| trailokyaiśvaryadānāma nirvakti trailokyamiti| jīvāsridheti| baddhamuktanityā ityarthaḥ| vyomeśe; bindau|
saṃtiṣṭhate paretyevamudayāstamayau mama|
īdṛśīyaṃ mahāvidyā jagadyonirgirāṃ prasūḥ || 25 ||
pañcamī kāmasūrvidyā kāmabījāparāhvayā|
prādyumnī paramā śaktistasyā rūpaṃ nibodha me || 26 ||
madhyamaṃ guṇatattvānāṃ yat proktaṃ paścimānanam|
rañjanaṃ sattvatamasorbhogenālpena rañjitam || 27 ||
sā parā prakṛtiḥ kākyā kalpayantī jagadvidhim|
puruṣeśvarayogena sā sṛṣṭyai saṃprakalpate || 28 ||
avyaktapuruṣeśākyarūpatrayavibhāvinī|
māyā śrīḥ sā punardevī vyomeśe pratitiṣṭhati || 29 ||
prakṛtiṃ kakāramādāya tena puruṣeśvaraṃ lakāraṃ, māyāmīkāraṃ, vyomeśaṃ binduṃ ca yojayet| tatasca klīṃ iti kāmabījam|
iti rūpaprabhāvau tau kāmabījasya darśitau|
ṣaṣṭhīṃ sārasvatīṃ vidyāṃ gadantyā me niśāmaya || 30 ||
prajñādhāro hyahaṃ śakra prakṛṣṭajñānajanmabhūḥ|
sāhaṃ prajñāprasūrviṣṇorudayena samanvitā || 31 ||
ānandaṃ yojayet tasyāḥ purastāt sūkṣmayā dṛśā|
aprameyamataḥ pūrvaṃ bhāvayet bhūkṣmayā dṛśā || 32 ||
aprameyoditā sāhaṃ mahānandamayī śubhā|
ādhārabhūtā prajñāyā vyomeśe saṃsthitā punaḥ || 33 ||
punarvisṛṣṭiyogāya parameśvaramāgatā|
ṣaṣṭhī samuddhṛtā vidyā śabdataścārthataśca te || 34 ||
sārasvatabījamāha ṣaṣṭhīmityādinā| prajñādhāra ūkāraḥ| tataḥ pūrvamudaya ukāraḥ| tataḥ pūrvamānanda ākāraḥ| tataḥ pūrvamaprameyaḥ akāraḥ| ante vyomeśo binduḥ| punarvisargaḥ| eṣāṃ yoge auḥ iti bhavati| avayavārthamāha aprameyoditetyādinā|
iyaṃ bījatrayī vidyā kathitā tripurāhvayā|
vyutkramānukramābhyāṃ sā hyātmasāmyapradāpi ca || 35 ||
vidyeyaṃ kāmadhuk proktā japahomādisādhitā|
vyañjanasvarasaṃyogāt tasyā bhedān bahūnviduḥ || 36 ||
caturṇāṃ puruṣārthānāṃ hetūn vyasya samasya ca|
saptamī tu mahālakṣmīrvidyā sā sarvasādhanī || 37 ||
parāṃ prakṛtimādāya bhāskaraṃ tatra yojayet|
mardanena samāyojya yojayet kālavahninā || 38 ||
bhūṣayenmāyayā piṇḍamante vyāpinamānayet|
kathitaṃ te mahālakṣmībījametat puraṃdara || 39 ||
mahālakṣmībījoddhāramāha parāmityādinā| prakṛtiṃ kakāramādāya tena bhāskaraṃ ṣakāraṃ, mardanaṃ makāraṃ, kālavahniṃ rephaṃ, māyāmīkāraṃ, vyāpinaṃ binduṃ ca yojayet| kṣmrīṃ iti mantraḥ| tathā ca tantrarāje "grāso nabho dāhavahnisvairyuktaḥ kaulinīmanuḥ" iti| tatra grāsaḥ kṣakāraḥ, nabho makāraḥ, dāhavahniḥ rephaḥ, svamīkāro bhinduśca|
karṣantī vyākṛtāvasthāmahaṃ hi svena tejasā|
pradhānabhūmikāṃ gatvā mūrtitrayavibhāvinī || 40 ||
nirmāya sakalaṃ bhāvaṃ vyomeśe saṃpratiṣṭhitā|
iti bhāvyamidaṃ bījaṃ japatā sādhakena tu || 41 ||
ityete raśmayo jñeyo vidyāyāstārikākṛteḥ|
anutārādayo vidyā itīdaṃ tārikāmayam || 42 ||
tāmimāṃ tārikāṃ vidyāṃ bhajamāno yathāvidhi|
aihikāmuṣmikān bhogagānakṣayān pratipadyate || 43 ||
sapta vidyāḥ tāraḥ, tārikā, anutārikā, vāgbhavaḥ, kāmabījaṃ, mahālakṣamībījaṃ ceti|
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre saptavidyāprakāśo nāma ṣaḍvaliṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
iti ṣaḍviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

<27>
saptaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śakraḥ
namastubhyaṃ jagannāthe puṇḍarīkākṣavallabhe|
aśeṣajagadīśāne sarvajñe sarvabhāvini || 1 ||
śrutametanmayā samyagvidyānāṃ tattvamuttamam|
bhūyaśca tārikāyā me vidhiṃ vyākhyātumarhasi || 2 ||
śrīḥ
ādyamekaṃ paraṃ brahma sarvajñaṃ saccidātmakam|
svaśakticaritaṃ divyaṃ lakṣmīnārāyaṇaṃ mahaḥ || 3 ||
ahaṃ sā paramā śaktirahaṃtākhyā sanātanī|
taddharmadharmiṇī nityā prabhā bhānorivāmalā || 4 ||
tadīyāni vidhīyante pañca kṛtyāni sarvadā|
tadunmeṣasvarūpiṇyā mayaivāditinandana || 5 ||
mama divyā parā śaktirnityaṃ maddharmadharmiṇī|
hṛllokhā paramā vidyā matsvarūpā puraṃdara || 6 ||
hṛllekhā pañcaviṃśādhyāye nirdiṣṭatārikāparanāmā hrīṃmantraḥ| atrāpyanupadameva mantrasvarūpaṃ nirdekṣyate|
asyā vyākhyāmimāṃ śaśvat sāvadhānena cetasā|
śraddadhānaḥ prapannastvamupasanno gṛhāṇa me || 7 ||
āmananti yamātmānaṃ jagatastasthuṣaḥ param|
prasavasthitisaṃhārakāraṇaṃ sūryasaṃjñitam || 8 ||
nityaṃ prerayitāraṃ ca prāṇasaṃjñaṃ sanātanam|
taṃ viddhi prathamaṃ varṇam̐ hakāraṃ puruṣottamam || 9 ||
yastasya prathamonmeṣaḥ kroḍīkṛtajagattrayaḥ|
aśeṣabhuvanādhāro jvaladrūpo'mito'nyataḥ || 10 ||
rephaṃ tatparamaṃ viddhi tejorūpaṃ sanātanam|
unmeṣa eva samyak sa vyāpnuvan sakalāṃ gatim || 11 ||
vyāpārān pañca bibhracca bindūn sṛṣṭyādilakṣaṇān|
āscaryajñānarūpaśca nimeṣonmeṣasaṃtateḥ || 12 ||
icchājñānakriyārūpaṃ bibhracca vitatikramam|
iīrūpasya yugmasya sthitireṣā sanātanī || 13 ||
tadevaṃ paramonmeṣarūpāhaṃ vitatodayā|
icchājñānakriyārūpā pañcakṛtyakarī vibhoḥ || 14 ||
nānāvidhāścaryamayī ciddhanā sukharūpiṇī|
vijñeyā paramātmasthā vyāpinī viṣṇuvallabhā || 15 ||
vidhāya kṛtyamakhilaṃ trailokyaiśvaryadāyinī|
tasminneva punardeve vyomeśe paramātmani || 16 ||
ādāya sarvasaṃbhāraṃ pratitiṣṭhāmi niṣkalā|
asyā rūpāṇi pañceha tattvajñāḥ saṃpracakṣate || 17 ||
tāni rūpāṇi deveśa gadantyā me niśāmaya|
vyomeśāntamidaṃ rūpamekaṃ yattatprakīrtitam || 18 ||
vyomeśāntamiti| bindvantamityarthaḥ|
vyomeśāt parataḥ kecidvāñchanti parameśvaram|
vyomeśamapahāyānye pradhānaṃ viniyojya tu || 19 ||
parameśvaraḥ visargaḥ| pradhāno makāraḥ|
bindunādau ca vāñchanti tadante praṇavopamam|
ante pradhānamevaikaṃ keciddhīrāḥ pracakṣate || 20 ||
sṛṣṭikartāramante'nye vaidikāḥ samadhīyate|
evaṃ pañca svarūpāṇi tārikāyā vidurbudhāḥ || 21 ||
sṛṣṭikartā visargaḥ|
śāntasthāyāḥ sureśāna rūpāṇyevaṃvidhānyapi|
pradhānānte visṛṣṭyante vyomeśānte tathaiva ca || 22 ||
mantrāṇāmeṣāṃ phalatāratamyamāha pradhānānte ityādinā|
vyomeśordhvavisṛṣṭyanta iti rūpacatuṣṭaye|
aihikī paramā siddhistattaccāmutrikī parā || 23 ||
pradhānabindunādānte mokṣaśrīreva kevalā|
ityevamanusaṃdhāya tārikāyāḥ parāṃ sthitim || 24 ||
śiṣyāya sādhuśīlāya gurubrahmahitaiṣiṇe|
ācārya ādiśedvidyāṃ parabrahmasvarūpiṇīm || 25 ||
hastadehāṅgavinyāsaṃ vidhāyātmani vai purā|
vinaysya śiṣyadehe ca tataścopadiśenmanum || 26 ||
sthāpayeddhṛdi śiṣyasya bhāvapūrvaṃ manuṃ param|
punaśca sthāpayet svasya hṛdaye mantramuttamam || 27 ||
dīkṣābhiṣekapūrvaṃ ca sarvametat samācaret|
ācāryādatha saṃprāpya vidyāṃ śiṣyo vicakṣaṇaḥ || 28 ||
ātmānamātmanaścaiva vittaṃ dattvā tu dakṣiṇām|
sakalaṃ tvardhamaṃśaṃ vā yena vā toṣyate guruḥ || 29 ||
vaidike ca samācāre laukike ca vyavasthite|
apramādyan sadācārye guruṣu brāhnaṇeṣu ca || 30 ||
adrohaṃ śīlayan śaśvadbhūtagrāme caturvidhe|
nityamātmaguṇopeto dharmalakṣaṇasevakaḥ || 31 ||
saṃskāraiḥ saṃskṛtaḥ śubhrairdevapitratithikriyaḥ|
divyaśāsrāṇyadhīyāno nigamāṃścaiva vaidikān || 32 ||
alolupena cittena siddhāntānanusaṃcaran|
yāvadarthaṃ tu vijñānamādadānastatastataḥ || 33 ||
adūṣayaṃśca śāsrāṇi pramāṇairanusaṃcaran|
āhnikaṃ vidhivat kurvan śāsrārthaṃ karmaṇāṃ kramaiḥ || 34 ||
aharādi tvahorātraṃ punā rātryavasānakam|
avandhyaṃ satataṃ kurvaṃścoditaiḥ karmaṇāṃ kramaiḥ || 35 ||
pañcakālarato nityaṃ pañcayajñaparāyaṇaḥ|
bhūṣito damadānābhyāṃ satyenāhiṃsanena ca || 36 ||
coditena krameṇemāṃ vidyāṃ saṃsādhya yatnataḥ|
prasannayā dhiyā yukto nityaṃ svārthaparārthayoḥ || 37 ||
bhūtimeva parāmicchan vibhūtiṃ parivarjayet|
pāvanaḥ sarvabhūtānāṃ manasā cakṣuṣā girā || 38 ||
cittaprasādanīrdevīścatasraḥ pariśīlayan|
maitryādyāḥ śāntimanvicchan japayajñaparāyaṇaḥ || 39 ||
apramādyan svakarmasthaḥ pramāde sati daivataḥ|
prāyaścittaṃ caran samyagyasminyasmiṃstu yādṛśam || 40 ||
karmaṇā manasā vācā devadevaṃ janārdanam|
prapannaḥ śaraṇaṃ śaśvanmāṃ ca taddharmadharmiṇīm || 41 ||
uttamaṃ puruṣaṃ srīṃ ca saṃdṛṣṭvā māmanusmaran|
daṃpatī pūjayannityaṃ dāṃpatyaṃ cāpyalopayan || 42 ||
śabdasthamarthagaṃ vāpi puṃbhāvaṃ vividhātmakam|
srībhāvaṃ viddhi tadrūpaṃ lakṣmīnārāyaṇaṃ smaran || 43 ||
uttamāṃ guṇasaṃpannāṃ rūpayauvanaśālinīm|
alolupena cittena dṛṣṭvā māmeva cintayan || 44 ||
alolupeneti| atyāsaktirahitenetyarthaḥ|
srīṣu kṣāntamanā nityamavadannapriyaṃ sadā|
atikramaṃ pariharan saṃjātaṃ cāpyatarkayan || 45 ||
kṣālayan pāvanaiḥ srīṇāṃ jāyamānaṃ vyatikramam|
kubjāṃ vā vikalāṃ vāpi sarvāvasthāṃ gatāṃ sriyam || 46 ||
pāvanairiti| śuddhikarmabhirityarthaḥ| vyatikramaḥ pramādaḥ|
avinindaṃścaraṃsrīṇāṃ priyaṃ śāsrānukūlataḥ|
evaṃvṛttaḥ sadācāro naro vigatakalmaṣaḥ || 47 ||
madbhakto matpriyakaro madyājī matparāyaṇaḥ|
prāpnoti paramaṃ dhāma tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padam || 48 ||
iti te kathitā śakra tārikāyāḥ parā sthitiḥ|
vidyānāmapi cānyāsāṃ kiṃ bhūyaḥ śrotumicchasi || 49 ||
tārikāyā iti| hṛllekhāparanāmnyā ityarthaḥ|
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre sadācāraprakāśo nāma saptaviṃśodhyāyaḥ
iti saptaviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

<28>
aṣṭāviṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śakraḥ
namaste kamalāvāse namasrayyantavāsini|
tvatprasādena vidhivacchrato mantraḥ samādhinā || 1 ||
pratipattiśca sakalā svarūpaṃ ca yathāsthitam|
āhorātrikamācāramidānīṃ vaktumarhasi || 2 ||
śrīḥ
eko nārāyaṇaḥ śrīmānanādiḥ puṣkarekṣaṇaḥ|
jñānaiśvaryamahāśaktivīryatejomahodadhiḥ || 3 ||
ātmā sa sarvabhūtānāṃ haṃso nārāyaṇo vaśī|
tasya sāmarthyarūpāhamekā taddharmadharmiṇī || 4 ||
sāhaṃ sṛṣṭyādikān bhāvān vidadhānā punaḥ punaḥ|
ārādhitā satī sarvāṃstārayāmi bhavārṇavāt || 5 ||
dadāmi vividhān bhogān dharmeṇa paritoṣitā|
saddharmaparasaṃsthānā mama sattvādikā tanuḥ || 6 ||
vividhāniti| apavargapradāpyānuṣaṅgikānaihikānāmutrikāṃśca bhogān dadāmītyarthaḥ| saddharmapareṣu saṃsthānaṃ yasyā iti bahuvrīhiḥ|
ācārarūpo dharmo'sāvācārastasya lakṣaṇam|
tamācāraṃ pravakṣyāmi yaḥ sadbhiranupālyate || 7 ||
hitvā yogamayīṃ nidrāmutthāyāpararātrataḥ|
prapadyeta hṛṣīkeśaṃ śaraṇyaṃ śrīpatiṃ harim || 8 ||
yogamayīmiti| abhigamanādiṣu dharmeṣu yogākhyaḥ pañcamo dharmaḥ pūrvadinarātrimārabhya paradinabrāhnamuhūrtāvadhikakālānuṣṭheyaḥ|
prapatteśca svarūpaṃ te pūrvamuktaṃ sureśvara|
bhūyaśca śṛṇu vakṣyāmi sā yathā syāt sthirā tvayi || 9 ||
ācamya prayato bhūtvā smṛtvāsraṃ jvalanākṛti|
tat praviśya viniṣkrāntaḥ pūto bhūtvāsratejasā || 10 ||
prapattiṃ tāṃ prayuñjīta svāṅgaiḥ pañcabhiranvitām|
prātikūlyaṃ parityaktamānukūlyaṃ ca saṃśritam || 11 ||
mayā sarveṣu bhūteṣu yathāśakti yathāmati|
alasasyālpaśakteśca yathāvaccāvijānataḥ || 12 ||
upāyāḥ kriyamāṇāste naiva syustārakā mama|
ato'haṃ kṛpaṇo dīno nirlepaścāpyakiṃcanaḥ || 13 ||
lakṣmyā saha hṛṣīkeśo devyā kāruṇyarūpayā|
rakṣakaḥ sarvasiddhānte vedānte'pi ca gīyate || 14 ||
mahāviśvāso'tra vivakṣitaḥ| sarvasiddhānte; sarveṣu divyaśāsreṣu| vedānte; upaniṣatsu|
yanme'sti dustyajaṃ kiṃcit putradārakriyādikam|
samastamātmanā nyastaṃ śrīpate tava pādayoḥ || 15 ||
ātmātmīyanikṣepamāha yanme'stītyādinā| ātmanā; ātmanā sahetyarthaḥ|
śaraṇaṃ bhava deveśa nātha lakṣmīpate mama|
sakṛdevaṃ prapannasya kṛtyaṃ naivānyadiṣyate || 16 ||
goptṛtvavaraṇamāha śaraṇamityādinā|
upāyāpāyamuktasya vartamānasya madhyataḥ|
narasya buddhidaurbalyādupāyāntaramiṣyate || 17 ||
madhyato vartamānasya naivānyat kṛtyamiti pūrveṇānvayaḥ| buddhidaurbalyāditi| svādhikāramanālocya duṣkare karmaṇi pravṛttirhi buddhidaurbalyaprayukteti bhāvaḥ| upāyāntaramatra bhaktiyogaḥ| anena bhaktiyogasya nindā kriyata iti vā mumukṣorbhaktiyogaḥ svarūpaviruddha iti vā na mantavyam| "narasya buddhidaurbalyādindubimbagrahe spṛhā" ityukte vacanamidamindubimbaṃ nindatīti na kaścidapi prekṣāvān brūyāt| kiṃca "bhaktyā paramayā vāpi prapattyā vā mahāmate" iti bhaktervaikalpikasādhanatvoktirvirudhyeta| aśaktasya mumukṣoḥ svarūpaviruddha iti cet, nātra kasyāpi vipratipattiḥ|
ataḥ paraṃ sadācāraṃ procyamānaṃ nibodha me|
āśaṃsānaḥ samuttiṣṭhet sarvabhūtasukhodayam || 18 ||
bhavantu sarvabhūtāni sāttvike vimale pathi|
bhajantāṃ śrīpatiṃ śaśvadviśantu paramaṃ padam || 19 ||
ityāśāsya priyaṃ samyagbhūtebhyo manasā girā|
śarīraśodhanaṃ kṛtvā dharmaśāsravidhānataḥ || 20 ||
śaucaṃ ca vidhivat kṛtvā bhakṣayeddantadhāvanam|
athācamya vidānena pavitraiḥ śāsracoditaiḥ || 21 ||
plāvayitvābhyupāsīta saṃdhyāṃ trailokyapāvanīm|
manmayī trividhā śaktiḥ sūryasomāgnirūpiṇī || 22 ||
śuddhaye sarvabhūtānāṃ saṃdhyā devī pravartate|
upasthāya vivasvantamantaḥsthaṃ puruṣottamam || 23 ||
vivasvantaṃ viśiṣṭadīptimantamiti puruṣottamaviśeṣaṇam| antaḥstham; sūryamaṇḍalāntarvartinam| "arkamaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ sūryakoṭisamaprabham" ityādidhyānamatrābhipretam|
kuryādagnividhiṃ samyagupādānamathācaret|
sati vitte na kurvītopādānaṃ tu vicakṣaṇaḥ || 24 ||
upādānaṃ nāma bhagavadārādhanopayuktadravyārjanam| tacca niḥsvasya pañcayajñapañcakālaparāyaṇabhāgavatottamādayācitopanatataṇḍulādisvīkārarūpam| āḍhyasya tu tadupayuktaphalapuṣpatulasyādisaṃgraharūpam|
sapta vittāgamā dharmyā dāyo lābhaḥ krayo jayaḥ|
prayogaḥ karmayogaśca satpratigraha eva ca || 25 ||
snānaṃ kṛtvā vidhānena trividhaṃ śāsracoditam|
bhūtaśuddhiṃ vidhāyātha yāgamāntaramācaret || 26 ||
svayamutpāditaiḥ sphītairlabdhaiḥ śiṣyāditastathā|
bhogairyajeta māṃ viṣṇumubhau vā śāsrapūrvakam || 27 ||
aṣṭāṅgena vidhānena hyanuyāgāvasānakaiḥ|
svādhyāyamācaret samyagaparāhṇe vicakṣaṇaḥ || 28 ||
aṣṭāṅgayajanaṃ yathā jayākhye antaryāgaḥ, bhogayāgaḥ, madhvādiyāgaḥ, annayāgaḥ, saṃpradānam, vahnisaṃtarpaṇam, pitṛyāgaḥ, anuyāgaśceti |
divyaśāsrāṇyadhīyīta nigamāṃścaiva vaidikān|
sarvānanucaret samyak siddhāntānātmasiddhaye || 29 ||
siddhāntāniti| mantrāgamādisiddhāntabhedānityarthaḥ|
alolupena cittena rāgadveṣavivarjitaḥ|
na nindenmanasā vācā śāsrāṇyuccāvacānyapi || 30 ||
tāvanmātrārthamādadyāda yāvatā hyartha ātmanaḥ|
bhūtānāṃ śreyase sarve sarvaśāsrāṇi tanvate || 31 ||
tāṃ tāmavasthāṃ saṃprāpya tāni śreyo vitanvate|
ādau madhye ca sarveṣāṃ śāsrāṇāmantime tathā || 32 ||
śrīmānnārāyaṇaḥ prokto vidhayaiva tayā tayā|
ahaṃ nārāyaṇasthāpi sarvajñā sarvadarśinī || 33 ||
sarvaśāsrāṇāṃ bhūtahitatvaṃ paratatvaprakāśanena tadārādhanapratipādanena cetyabhisaṃdhāyāha śrīmāniti|
nidānajñā bhiṣakkalpā tattadgurvādirūpiṇī|
pravartayāmi śāsrāṇi tāni tāni tathā tathā || 34 ||
adhikārānurūpeṇa pramāṇāni tathā tathā|
atyantaheyaṃ na vkāpi śāsraṃ kiṃcana vidyate || 35 ||
sarvatra sulabhaṃ śreyaḥ svalpaṃ vā yadi vā bahu|
tataḥ kāryo na vidveṣo yāvadarthamupāśrayet || 36 ||
samayaṃ na viśettatra naiva dīkṣāṃ kadācana|
tataḥ saṃdhyāmupāsīta paścimāṃ sārdhabhāskarām || 37 ||
vidhāyāgnyarthakāryaṃ tu yogaṃ yuñjīta vai tataḥ|
suvivikte śucau deśe niḥśalāke manorame || 38 ||
mṛdvāstaraṇasaṃkīrṇe celājinakuśottare|
antarbahiśca saṃśuddhe yamādipariśodhitaḥ || 39 ||
āsanaṃ cakramāsthāya padmaṃ svastikameva vā|
yatra vā ramate buddhirnāḍīmārgān nipīḍayan || 40 ||
vijitya pavanagrāmaṃ pratyāhārajitendriyaḥ|
dhāraṇāsu śramaṃ kṛtvā māṃ dhyāyet susamāhitaḥ || 41 ||
anaupamyāmanirdeśyāmavikalpāṃ nirañjanām|
sarvatra sulabhāṃ lakṣmīṃ sarvapratyayatāṃ gatām || 42 ||
sākārāmathavā yogī varābhayakarāṃ parām|
padmagarbhopamāṃ padmāṃ padmahastāṃ sulakṣaṇām || 43 ||
yadvā nārāyaṇāṅkasthāṃ sāmarasyamupāgatām|
cidānandamayīṃ devīṃ tādṛśaṃ ca śriyaḥ patim || 44 ||
bahudhā yogamārgāste veditavyāḥ sureśvara|
teṣvekaṃ dharmamāsthāya bhaktiḥ śraddhā ca yatra te || 45 ||
samyaṅ nidhyānamutpādya samādhiṃ samupāśrayet|
dhyātā dhyānaṃ tathā dhyeyaṃ trayaṃ yatra vilīyate || 46 ||
yogasya parā kāṣṭhocyate dhyātetyādinā| yatreti| yasyāṃ samādhidaśāyāmityarthaḥ|
ekaivāhaṃ tadā bhāse pūrṇāhaṃtā sanātanī|
aikadhyamanusaṃprāpte mayi saṃvinmahodadhau || 47 ||
aikadhyamekabhāvaṃ yogini saṃprāpte sati nānyat kiṃcit prakāśate| kiṃtu ahameva bhāsa ityuttareṇānvayaḥ|
nānyat prakāśate kiṃcidahameva tadā parā|
yogācchrānto japaṃ kuryāttacchrānto yogamācaret || 48 ||
tasya kṣipraṃ prasīdāmi japayogābhiyoginaḥ|
nītvaivaṃ prathamaṃ yāmaṃ japayogādinā sudhīḥ || 49 ||
yoganidrāṃ nirvakti nitvaivamiti|
yogastha eva taddhīrastato yāmadvayaṃ svapet|
utthāyāpararātre tu pūrvoktamanusaṃcaret || 50 ||
yogo nāma paramātmanyātmanikṣepapuraḥsaraṃ paramātmaguṇagaṇānusaṃdhānam| tatkurvāṇa eva svāpamanubhavedityarthaḥ|
iti vyāmiśrakṛtyaṃ tat proktaṃ te balasūdana|
acchidrān pañcakālāṃstu bhagavatkarmaṇā nayet || 51 ||
vyāmiśreti| bahuvidhakarmakalāpasahitaṃ bhagavadārādhanamityarthaḥ| acchidrāniti| "yanmuhūrtaṃ kṣaṇaṃ vāpi vāsudevo na cintyate| sā hānistanmahacchidraṃ sā bhrāntiḥ sā ca vikriyā||" ityuktacchidrarahitānityarthaḥ|
dīkṣitaḥ pañcakālajño lakṣmīmantraparāyaṇaḥ|
antaraṃ nānayoḥ kiṃcinniṣṭhāyāṃ balasūdana || 52 ||
ubhāvetau matau bhaktau viśato māṃ tanukṣaye|
cakrapadmadharo nityaṃ bhavellakṣmīparāyaṇaḥ || 53 ||
svadāranirataśca syād brahmacārī sadā bhavet|
manmantramabhyasennityaṃ maccitto matparāyaṇaḥ || 54 ||
sarvānuccāvacāñchabdāṃstadbhāvena vibhāvayet|
agnīṣomavibhāgajñaḥ kriyābhūtivibhāgavit || 55 ||
sthūlasūkṣmaparatvānāṃ vedi

<29>
ekonatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śakraḥ
śuddhāśuddhaṣaḍadhvākyacitranirmāṇabhittaye|
namaḥ śrīvatsabhāminyai bhavasaṃtāpaśāntaye || 1 ||
agnīṣomavibhāgaṃ me kriyābhūtividhāmapi|
brūhi me tattvataḥ padme vidyāyāstārikākṛteḥ || 2 ||
śrīḥ
vyāpakaṃ yat paraṃ brahma śaktirnārāyaṇī hi yā|
sā hyahaṃ pariṇāmena bhavāmi praṇavākṛtiḥ || 3 ||
agnīṣomavibhāgaṃ me tārikāyā niśāmaya|
kriyābhūtividhānaṃ ca yathāvat suranandana || 4 ||
unmeṣaḥ paramo viṣṇorādyaḥ ṣāḍguṇyacinmayaḥ|
sāhaṃ saṃbhṛtasaṃbhārā śaktiste kathitā purā || 5 ||
sṛṣṭavatyā jagat kṛtsnaṃ tasyā me rakṣaṇodyame|
dvidhā pravartate rūpaṃ mukhenaiśvaryatejasoḥ || 6 ||
mukheneti| prādhānyenetyarthaḥ|
ṣāḍguṇyameva me rupaṃ kiṃcittejomukhaṃ matam|
ṣāḍguṇyameva me rupaṃ paramaiśvaryasaṃmukham || 7 ||
tejomukhaṃ tu yadrūpaṃ sā kriyāśaktirucyate|
saivāgnirucyate śaktiḥ sarvopaplavadāhanāt || 8 ||
aiśvaryasaṃmukhaṃ rūpaṃ bhūtirlakṣmīritīritā|
śaktiraiśvaryabhūyiṣṭhā sā me somamayī tuḥ || 9 ||
śoṣaṇāt sarvadoṣāṇāmagniśaktiḥ kriyāmayī|
jagadāpyāyayantyanyā bhūtiḥ soma ihocyate || 10 ||
icchājñānakriyāmayyāste ime vyūhaje mama|
ṣāḍguṇyavigrahā sāhaṃ vyūhinī parameśvarī || 11 ||
yā sā śaktiḥ kriyākhyā me ṣāḍguṇyaṃ tejasojjvalam|
tasyā āsaṃsrayo vyūhāḥ sūryasomāgniśaktayaḥ || 12 ||
tāsāmādyā parā divyā sūryākhyā śaktirujjvalā|
nirvahantī jagatkṛtyamaniśaṃ parivartate || 13 ||
adhyātmamadhidaivaṃ ca tathā caivādhibhautikam|
tasyā rūpatrayaṃ viddhi sūryākhyāyāḥ sureśvara || 14 ||
adhyātmasthā tu sūryākhyā piṅgalāmārgagāminī|
ālokenādhibhūtasthā sūryaśaktiḥ pravartate || 15 ||
sūryamaṇḍalasaṃsthānā śaktiḥ sānyādhidaivikī|
sūryamaṇḍalasaṃsthānā arciṣo yāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 16 ||
sūryamaṇḍalāntarvartino devasya trayīrūpatvaṃ vaktuṃ prathamatastasya ṛgrūpatvamucyate uttarārdhe sūryetyādinā|
ṛcastā viddhi deveśa tapantīstapanātmikāḥ|
dīptayo yāstadantaḥsthāstāni sāmāni viddhi me || 17 ||
tasya yajuḥsvarūpatvamāha antaḥstheti| anena "ādityo vā eṣa etanmaṇḍalaṃ tapati tatra tā ṛcastadṛcāṃ maṇḍalam" iti taittirīyaśrutirupabṛṃhitā bhavati| viṣṇupurāṇe coktam |
antaḥsthā yā parā śaktiḥ pauruṣīṃ tanumāsthitā|
tāṃ viddhi puruṣaṃ divyaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ yajurmayam || 18 ||
śaṅkhacakradharaṃ śrīśaṃ pīnodāracaturbhujam|
prasannavadanaṃ padmaviṣṭaraṃ puṣkarekṣaṇam || 19 ||
mūrdhāntaḥpuruṣasyāsya daśahotā nigadyate|
pādapāṇi caturhotā devasyāsya sureśvara || 20 ||
antaḥsthasyāsya puruṣasya daśahotā mūrdhā bhavati| daśahotrādipadaistattannāmāno mantrā vivakṣitāḥ| "cittiḥ sruk" ityārabhya taittirīyāraṇyake tṛtīyaprapāṭhake prathamānuvākamantro daśahotā| tatraiva "pṛthivī hotā" ityārabhya dvitīyānuvākapaṭhitaścaturhotā| pādapāṇītyekavadbhāvaḥ|
lomamāṃsāsthimajjāsṛk pañcahotā sureśvara|
stanāvaṇḍau ca puṃstvaṃ ca ṣaḍḍhotāpāna eva ca || 21 ||
"agnirhotā" ityārabhya tṛtīyānuvākapaṭhitaḥ pañcahotā| "sūryaṃ te cakṣuḥ" ityārabhya turīyānuvākapaṭhitaḥ ṣaḍḍhotā|
śīrṣaṇyāḥ sapta ye prāṇāḥ saptahotā nigadyate|
śobhāstu dakṣiṇāstasya saṃbhārāḥ saṃdhayaḥ smṛtāḥ || 22 ||
"mahāhavirhotā" ityārabhya pañcamānuvākapaṭhitaḥ saptahotā| "devasya tvā" ityārabhya daśamānuvākapaṭhitā dakṣiṇāḥ| "agniryajurbhiḥ" ityārabhyāṣṭamānuvākapaṭhitāḥ saṃbhārāḥ|
nāḍayo devapatnyo'sya hotṝṇāṃ hṛdayaṃ manaḥ|
cetanaḥ pauruṣaṃ sūktaṃ śaktiḥ śrīsūktamucyate || 23 ||
"senendrasya" ityārabhya navamānuvākapaṭhitā devapatnyaḥ| "suvarṇaṃ gharmam" ityārabhyaikādaśānuvākapaṭhito hotṛhṛdayam| puruṣasūktaśrīsūktamantrāḥ prasiddhāḥ|
oṃkāraḥ praṇavastāro guhyaṃ nāma sanātanam|
yajūṃṣi rudraśukrāṇi sthūlanāmāni tasya tu || 24 ||
"namaste rudra manyave" ityārabhya paṭhitā rudriyāḥ| evaṃ śukriyā api mantraviśeṣāḥ| atra ahirbudhnyasaṃhitāyāḥ 58, 59 adhyāyāvanusaṃdheyau|
yajurmayamanuṃ divyamabhyasyan puruṣaṃ naraḥ|
anuvyāhṛtyabhīcārapāpebhyo'pi pramucyate || 25 ||
yajurmayeti sāvitrī| anuvyāhṛti; vyāhṛtibhyaḥ paścāt| asya `abhyasyan' iti pūrveṇānvayaḥ|
tapatyevaṃ parā śaktisrayī sūryākhyayāmbare|
trividhaiṣā parā śaktiḥ prakhyātā sūryasaṃjñayā || 26 ||
trayīti| "vedairaśūnyasribhireti sūryaḥ" iti śrutiranusaṃdheyā|
sāvitrī nāma vedānāṃ jananī parivartate|
trivarṇapraṇavādhārā bhūrbhuvaḥsvasrināḍikā || 27 ||
tadādivarṇapavanā śiraḥkalpitaśekharā|
kṣityādipuruṣāntārṇā prakāśānandavigrahā || 28 ||
tadādīti| tat ityādayo varṇā ityarthaḥ| śiraḥ gāyatrīśiromantraḥ| kṣityādīti| kādimāntavarṇā|
uditā brahmaṇo bhūyo brahmaṇi pratitiṣṭhati|
vedānāṃ jananī saiṣā varṇānāṃ jananī parā || 29 ||
anulomavilomābhyāṃ saumyāgneyī nigadyate|
saiṣā sūryavapurdivyā sāvitrī manmayī kalā || 30 ||
gāyatrī nāma gāyantaṃ trāyate mahato bhayāt|
ādāya jīvanaṃ gobhirbhūsaritprāṇisaṃbhavam || 31 ||
ādāyeti| atra "yābhirādityastapati tābhiḥ parjanyo varṣati" iti śrutiranusaṃdheyā|
punarmuñcati megheṣu navamāsadhṛtaṃ karaiḥ|
uktā sūryamayī śaktiḥ śṛṇu vahnimayīṃ parām || 32 ||
eṣāpi trividhā śakra śaktirvahnimayī mama|
divyekābindhanā hyanyā kṣitau kṣitimayendhanā || 33 ||
abindhanā vidyut divyā| kāṣṭhādīndhanā bhaumī| jaṭharasthā kauṣṭhīti jñeyam|
tathā bhuktendhanā koṣṭhe śaktirvahnimayī tridhā|
sarvadevamayī saiṣā devānāṃ mukhamucyate || 34 ||
trivargasthaiḥ stutā saiṣā triṣṭubityucyate budhaiḥ|
durgāṇi tārayantyātmapārāyaṇaparaṃ naram || 35 ||
śaktiḥ somamayī tvanyā sāpi tredhā nigadyate|
divi bimbātmanā tvekā tathānyauṣadhirūpiṇī || 36 ||
somamayyapi tridhā ākāśe bimbātmanā, bhūmāvoṣadhyātmanā, prāṇiśarīre iḍātmaneti|
carati prāṇināmantariḍayaikāmṛtātmikā|
anuṣṭubbhiḥ stūyamānā saiṣānuṣṭubudīritā || 37 ||
mṛtyuṃjaya iti proktā sā vidyā mṛtyunāśinī|
sūryasomāgnirūpāṇāṃ tāsāmāsāṃ puraṃdara || 38 ||
haviṣkṛduditā sūryāt somarūpā havirmayī|
haviratti tathāgnyākhyā vahnivipramukhātmanā || 39 ||
tisṛbhirvartate kṛtsnaṃ lokatantramaharniśam|
iti vyūhatrayopetā vyūhinī sā kriyātmikā || 40 ||
śaktiḥ paramagambhīrā mama tejomukhodgatā|
sūryenduvahnikoṭyoghaniyutārbudabhāsvarā || 41 ||
cakraṃ sudarśanaṃ nāma sā bhavatyaridāraṇam|
asraṃ paramatejiṣṭhamāgneyaṃ nāma vaiṣṇavam || 42 ||
parodyamasvarūpaṃ tat prāṇādiprāṇanaṃ param|
udyanti sarvāṇyasrāṇi tasmāt sarvāśca śaktayaḥ || 43 ||
karaṇaṃ sādhakatamaṃ pañcakṛtyavidhau hareḥ|
na tat kṛtyaṃ vinā tena yat syād viṣṇormahātmanaḥ || 44 ||
saṃkalpādisvarūpeyaṃ sṛṣṭau viṣṇoḥ pravartate|
rakṣaṇe saṃhṛtau caiva dhatte cakramayīṃ tanum || 45 ||
mamāṃśajā parāgneyī kriyāśaktirhi vaiṣṇavī|
ābrahmastambaparyantaṃ ṣoḍhā viṣṭabhya tiṣṭhati || 46 ||
ṣoḍheti| kalātattvavarṇapadamantrabhuvanākhyacakrairityarthaḥ|
tatra varṇamayaṃ cakraṃ prathamaṃ śṛṇu vāsava|
tārikātārakadvandvamakṣagaṃ pārameśvaram || 47 ||
tārikā hrīṃmantraḥ| tārakaḥ praṇavaḥ|
nābhau sūryendubhārūpaṃ śritaṃ svāraṃ dviraṣṭakam|
kādibhāntaṃ triraṣṭāraṃ mādihāntaṃ tu nemigam || 48 ||
svāram; svarasamūhaḥ| dviraṣṭakam; ṣoḍaśakam| triraṣṭāram; caturviṃśatyararūpam|
pradhiḥ paryantavahnyātmā vargāntaḥ piṇḍasaṃnibhaḥ|
varṇacakramidaṃ divyaṃ vartate varṇavartmanā || 49 ||
kalācakramidaṃ śakra jñānākṣaṃ śaktinābhikam|
aiśvaryāraṃ balādyena nemyādyaṃ triguṇena tu || 50 ||
tattvaṃ tu vāsudevākṣaṃ nābhiḥ saṃkarṣaṇojjvalaḥ|
pradyumnāraṃ tathā śakra pradhirūpāniruddhakam || 51 ||
akṣanābhyaranemisthaisturyādyaiḥ padacakrakam|
mantramakṣādikairbījapiṇḍasaṃjñāpadātmabhiḥ || 52 ||
kālākṣaṃ nābhigāvyaktaṃ mahadādyarapañjaram|
manaḥśrotratadarthādivikāraparimaṇḍalam || 53 ||
lokalaukikaparyantaṃ bhuvanaṃ cakramantimam|
ṣaṭcakraṃ dadhatī hastaiḥ pauruṣīṃ tanumāsthitā || 54 ||
sudarśanaḥ kriyāśaktirvaiṣṇavī cakramadhyagā|
bījaṃ piṇḍaṃ padaṃ saṃjñā asyāḥ śṛṇu catuṣṭayam || 55 ||
somaṃ prathamamādāya prāṇamante niyojayet|
tato'mṛtamupādāya yojayet kālapāvakam || 56 ||
tatsaṃsthamanalaṃ kuryānmāyāṃ vyāpinamantataḥ|
etat saudarśanaṃ bījaṃ matkriyāśaktijṛmbhitam || 57 ||
somaḥ sakāraḥ| prāṇo hakāraḥ| amṛtaṃ sakāraḥ| kālapāvakaḥ rākāraḥ| analaḥ rakāraḥ| māyā vyāpī ca īṃkāraḥ| sahasrāra īṃ iti bījamantraḥ|
saptavarṇātmakaṃ divyamidaṃ bījaṃ maharddhidam|
etadeva mahat piṇḍaṃ māyāvyāpisamujjhitam || 58 ||
saptavarṇātmakamiti| somaprāṇāmṛtakālapāvakānalamāyāvyāpirūpasaptavarṇaghaṭitamityarthaḥ| māyāvyāpisamujjhitam; īkārarahitam| sahasrāra iti piṇḍamantraḥ|
kālāgnyarkāyutākārametadvajrāmbudadhvani|
pañcavarṇaṃ mahāpiṇḍaṃ durdharaṃ devadānavaiḥ || 59 ||
śucinā tu sakṛt smaryamajitendriyaduḥsmaram|
smṛtvā tu śāntaye smarye bīje me tārikādike || 60 ||
smarye; smaraṇīye ityarthaḥ|
yo'sau piṇḍordhvabhāgastho varṇaḥ kālānalābhidhaḥ|
dahyante tena daityendrā lokāścaiva yugakṣaye || 61 ||
dvitīyaśaktisaṃsthena tvagnināgniḥ samidhyate|
śaktiṃ prāṇayati prāṇaḥ pūrito'mṛtatejasā || 62 ||
iti piṇḍasvarūpaṃ te kathitaṃ surapuṃgava|
vyāpakaiḥ pañcabhiḥ piṇḍaṃ kalpitaṃ tvantarāntarā || 63 ||
varmāsrāntaṃ dhruvādyaṃ ca saṃjñāmantratvamṛcchati|
vyāpakau yojayedante piṇḍādyoḥ somasūryayoḥ || 64 ||
pūrvoktameva viśadayati vyāpakāviti|
ate somāgnikūṭasya hyekaṃ vyāpakamānayet|
ādyantayostathāntyasya varṇasya vyāpakau smṛtau || 65 ||
varma prāṇorjayorvyoma duṣṭopadravamardanam|
yaḥ saṃkarṣaṇastu saṃhāraḥ kalpānte'khilagocaraḥ || 66 ||
varma kavacam| prāṇorjayorvyoma; huṃmantraḥ|
sa phaṭkārastadante cāpyāhlādaḥ śāntikārakaḥ|
iti piṇḍavikarṣātmā saumyāgneyo manuḥ smṛtaḥ || 67 ||
vikarṣaḥ anusyūtiḥ|
svavarṇairaṅgavāneṣa balī saudarśano manuḥ|
divyāntarikṣabhaumānāṃ bhogānāmāptisādhanam || 68 ||
svavarṇairiti| pūrvoktasya ṣaḍakṣaramantrasya ye ṣaṭ varṇāḥ, taiḥ hṛdayādiṣaḍaṅgakalpanaṃ kāryamityarthaḥ| mantramahimānamāha divyetyādinā|
kalpadrumo manuḥ so'yamāśritānāṃ puraṃdara|
nārāyaṇāt samudyatyā mama nityaṃ jagaddhite || 69 ||
jagaddhite iti nimitte saptamī| jagaddhitanimittamityarthaḥ|

kriyābhūtivibhedaśca kriyāśaktibhidāpi ca || 70 ||

bhūyaḥ śakra kriyāśakte ṛddhimetāvatīṃ śṛṇu || 71 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre agnīṣomavibhāgaprakāśo nāmaikonatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
