ekatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ
yā sā ṣāḍguṇyatejaḥsthā kriyāśaktiḥ prakāśitā|
āgneyaṃ rūpamāśritya sā dhatte pauruṣaṃ vapuḥ || 1 ||
ṣāḍguṇye tatrāpi tejasi pradhānatayāvasthitetyarthaḥ|
sarvāsraśasrasaṃsyūtaṃ sūryasomāgnimūlakam|
mahat sudarśanaṃ nāma nānārūpopaśobhitam || 2 ||
sudarśanasya sarvaśasrāsranibiḍatvamahirbudhnyasaṃhitāyāṃ viśādyāye uktam| sahasrāretyakṣarasvarūpamabhipretya sūryasomāgnimūlakatvoktiḥ|
tasya madhye sthitā śaktiḥ pauruṣīṃ tanumāsthitā|
saumyāgneyasvarūpeṇa tattatkālavyavasthayā || 3 ||
bhaktaparipālanaduṣṭaśikṣaṇarūpakāryamanurudhya saumyāgneyasvarūpatvoktiḥ|
purastāddarśitastasyā vācakaste caturvidhaḥ|
tatra saṃjñāmayo mantro bhūyasā balavattaraḥ || 4 ||
bījapiṇḍasaṃjñāpadarūpeṇa cāturvidhyam|
tasya vyākhyāmimāṃ śakra gadantyā me niśāmaya|
yā sā somātmikā śaktirunmeṣaḥ prathamo hare || 5 ||
mūlaśaktirahaṃ śrīḥ sā prathamākṣarasaṃsthitā|
amṛtā tṛptirūpā ca somātmā cākhileśvarī || 6 ||
prathamākṣaraḥ sakāraḥ| tasyāmṛtādirūpatvamāha amṛtetyādinā| tṛptisomāvapi sakāraparau|
amṛtīkaraṇaṃ kuryāt sargasyendukalāsthitā|
śiraḥpadmādayo mantrāḥ parameśvarayuktayā || 7 ||
anayā sṛtayā kuryāttṛptiṃ saṃsiddhimeva ca|
sṛṣṭikṛtsaṃyutā seyaṃ jīvaśaktiḥ sanātanī || 8 ||
trailokyaiśvaryadopetā vāyuveśmākṣarasthitā|
tārākārā ripormūrdhni cintyocchedanasiddhaye || 9 ||
aprameyopagūḍhāyā agnīṣomamayījuṣaḥ|
asyāḥ śakteḥ samudbhūtaṃ sūktaṃ pauruṣamujjvalam || 10 ||
asyā iti| prathamākṣararūpāyā ityarthaḥ| sakārāt pauruṣaṃ sūktamudbhūtamityarthaḥ| tathā cāhirbudhnyasaṃhitā "akṣarādādimādasyāḥ sūktaṃ pauruṣamudgatam| dvitīyākṣarasaṃbhūtaṃ śrīsūktaṃ nāma yanmune||" iti|
etadādīni sūktāni sahasramṛṣayo viduḥ|
nityamāpyāyate mantraḥ so'yamagnijuṣā mayā || 11 ||
tattatkāryajuṣā tattadvarṇaśaktitrayījuṣā|
anayā yanna sādhyeta na tadansita jagattraye || 12 ||
śaktitrayī sūryasomāgniśaktayaḥ|
sūte sā sakalāḥ śaktīranujānāti tāḥ punaḥ|
saṃharantī punaścaitā iti ca smaryate tataḥ || 13 ||
svamunmeṣamadhiṣṭhāya paramātmā sa śaktirāṭ|
udito jagato'rthāya hetidevaḥ sanātanaḥ || 14 ||
hetidevaḥ sudarśanaḥ|
sūryaśca bhoktṛtāṃ prāpya prāṇayan prāṇa iṣyate|
ātmānaṃ darśayatyeṣa trailokyaiśvaryadānvitaḥ || 15 ||
sūryo hakāraḥ| prāṇo'pi sa eva|
hṛtpuṇḍarīkamadhyasthaṃ yaṃ vicinvanti yoginaḥ|
yathāvad dhyāyato vedhyaṃ mukteṣorvegavattayā || 16 ||
muktasyetyādi| ativegenetyarthaḥ|
prakarṣeṇonnayan prāṇān prāṇa ityeṣa śabdyate|
indumaṇḍalasaṃvīto vyāpimānaprameyavān || 17 ||
vyāpī anusvāraḥ|
jihvāmūlasthito dhyāto vākpravṛttiṃ niyacchati|
aṃmaṇḍale sthito dhyātaḥ sa eva hi sudhāṃ sravan || 18 ||
jihvāmūleti kaṇṭhasthānam| aṃmaṇḍalamanusvāraḥ|
nirviṣīkaraṇaṃ dhyātaḥ karoti jagatāmapi|
rāmopagūḍhādasmāddhi śriyaḥ sūktaṃ samudgatam || 19 ||
rāmopagūḍhāditi| ikārasahitādityarthaḥ|
etadādīni sūktāni sahasramṛṣayo viduḥ|
tattatkālajuṣā tattadvarṇaśaktitrayījuṣā || 20 ||
anena yanna sādhyeta na tadasti jagattraye|
hanyate sakalaṃ lokaṃ gamayatyamalaṃ padam || 21 ||
hanyata ityādinā hakāranirvacanaṃ kriyate| "hana hiṃsāgatyoḥ" iti dhātvarthamabhipretyāha gamayatīti|
tyājayatyakhilaṃ kleśaṃ gamyate yogacintakaiḥ|
hetyevaṃ kathyate sadbhirevaṃ nirvacanasthitaiḥ || 22 ||
aśeṣabhuvanādhārā saṃkalpaprabalīkṛtā|
pratyabhijñāyate saiva śaktiryā paramātmanaḥ || 23 ||
aśeṣabhuvanādhāro rephaḥ|
saratyasyāścalaṃ sarvaṃ sriyate sakalaiḥ sadā|
pṛthivī saṃsthitā seyaṃ stambhe sṛtyā niyujyate || 24 ||
srā ityasya nirvacanamāha saratīti| sarati; gacchatītyarthaḥ| sriyate; gamyate prāpyata ityarthaḥ| sṛtyāḥ gamanāt stambhe stambhane niścalatva iti yāvat| gamanāt nivartya stabdhatayā niyamyata ityarthaḥ|
saho nāma balaṃ tatra ramate tat sahasradhā|
sahasrati sahasrā syādagnīṣomātmano mama || 25 ||
sahasrā iti samudāyasya prakārāntareṇa nirvacanamarthaṃ cāha saha ityādi| sahasśabdāt balārthakādupari ramadhātorniṣpannasya ra ityasya saṃyojane sahasreti rūpam|
agnīṣomamayī sā me śaktiḥ sarvakriyākarī|
susaṃkalpasamiddhā sā tejasāṃ rāśirūrjitā || 26 ||
ra ityasya nirvacanamāha agnītyādinā| kālapāvakatāṃ repharūpatām| kevalīti śaktiviśeṣaṇam|
saṃprāpyaivānalaṃ bhāvaṃ kālapāvakatāṃ gatā|
retyevaṃ kevalī bhūtvā jvalatyaviratodayā || 27 ||
parameśvarabhūtā sā paramāścaryakāriṇī|
ra ityeva mahāśaktirmadīyādyā kriyāhvayā || 28 ||
saṃkhyānantyaṃ sahasraṃ syādarānantyaṃ taducyate|
varmāsrayoḥ svarūpaṃ ca darśitaṃ te puraṃdara || 29 ||
nirvacanāntaramāha saṃkhyetyādinā| sahasrāṇyanantāni arāṇi yasyeti samāsaḥ|
dhruvaśca praṇavo'syādiḥ pūrvameva nirūpitaḥ|
evameva mahāmantraḥ śabdabrahmodgato rasaḥ || 30 ||
dhruvaḥ praṇavaḥ|
ātharvaṇī mahāśaktiḥ kriyāśakteḥ priyā tanuḥ|
trayīsāro hyatharvākhyā pañcaparvā mahāśrutiḥ || 31 ||
pañcaparveti| nakṣatravidhānavidhividhānasaṃhitāśāntirūpapañcakalpetyarthaḥ|
mantreṇa sūyate'nena sāreṇeva vanaspatiḥ|
asya tvaṅgavidhānajñāḥ ṣaḍaṅgāni pracakṣate || 32 ||
bhūsāreṇa vṛkṣa ivābhivṛddhetyarthaḥ| ṣaḍaṅgāni hṛdayādiṣaḍaṅgāni|
gāyatrīmapi cakrākhyāṃ prākāraṃ cāgnisaṃjñitam|
gopanādvaruṇotsedhāt sodayādamṛtāttathā || 33 ||
gopana ākāraḥ| varuṇo vakāraḥ| udaya ukāraḥ| amṛtaḥ sakāraḥ| vāṃ suṃ iti mantraḥ|
ūrdhvaṃ cakrāya ca svāhā hṛdādistu śikhāvadhi|
sūryajvālāpadāccordhvaṃ mahāyuktāt sudarśanāt || 34 ||
hṛdādiḥ hṛdayaśiraḥśikhārūpaḥ| mahāyukteti| mahāsudarśanāyeti vivakṣitam|
ūrdhvaṃ cakrāya ca svāhā varmādyo'srāntako vidhiḥ|
namaścakrāya tasyānte vidmahe'sau caturyugaḥ || 35 ||
varmādya iti| kavacanetrāsrāṇi| caturyugaḥ; aṣṭākṣaraḥ| `namaścakrāya vidmahe' iti|
jvālāya ca sahasrānte dhīmahīti navārṇakaḥ|
tannaḥ pracodayānmadhye cakra ityaṣṭavarṇakaḥ || 36 ||
‘sahasrajvālāya dhīmahi| tannaścakraḥ pracodayāt’ iti mantraḥ|
muṣṭiṃ vitarjanīṃ kṛtvā tarjanīṃ tarjasaṃsthitām|
parito bhrāmayedvahniṃ dhyāyan prākārasaṃsthim || 37 ||
anyonyasaṃmukhe pāṇitale vai dakṣiṇottare|
kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhayoragre śliṣṭe dīrghāstathāparāḥ || 38 ||
parito bhrāmayedevaṃ cakramudreyamīritā|
ahgamudrāstu vakṣyante śaktigrāsamanuṃ śṛṇu || 39 ||
pavitramanalārūḍhaṃ savyāpi praṇavāt param|
mahāsudarśanetyevaṃ cakrarājaṃ mahādhvagam || 40 ||
tato'stagatatetyasmāt sarvaduṣṭabhayaṃkara|
chindhi chindhītyataḥ paścādbhindhi bhindhi prakīrtayet || 41 ||
vidārayadvayaṃ paścāt paramantrān grasa grasa|
dvirbhakṣayeti bhūtāni trāsayeti dviruccaret || 42 ||
varmāsravahnijāyāḥ syuḥ śaktigrasanakṛnmanuḥ|
svayaṃ sudarśano bhūtvā mantramuccārayannimam || 43 ||
pavitramityādi| `oṃ praṃ mahāsudarśana cakrarāja mahādhvaga astagata sarvaduṣṭabhayaṃkara chindhi chindhi bhindhi bhindhi vidāraya vidāraya, paramantrān grasa grasa bhakṣaya bhakṣayaya bhūtāni trāsaya trāsaya' ityasyānantaraṃ kavacamantramasramantraṃ ca saṃyojyānte svāhāpadaṃ saṃyojayet| eṣa śaktigrāsamantraḥ|
śaktiṃ mukhahṛdādibhyaḥ parasyācūṣayeddhiyā|
ṣaḍakṣarasya mantrasya śṛṇu dhyānaṃ puraṃdara || 44 ||
nyastāṅgaścakramudrābhirvahniprākāramadhyagaḥ|
sahasrāramahācakramayutāgnicayotkaṭam || 45 ||
ṣaḍadvamayamudbhrāntaṃ dhyāyenmacchaktijṛmbhitam|
akṣasthaṃ paramātmānaṃ nārāyaṇamanāmayam || 46 ||
cakrarūpiṇamīśānaṃ dhyāyet kuṅkumasaṃnibham|
pītāmbaradharaṃ divyaṃ muktālaṃkāramaṇḍitam || 47 ||
enamaṣṭabhujaṃ dhyāyenmahāvyasanasaṃplave|
udyajjānumanekāsraṃ sthitaṃ paramaśobhanam || 48 ||
cakraṃ musalamuddāmamaṅkuśaṃ sarasīruham|
yāmye karacatuṣke'tha vāme bhujacatuṣṭaye || 49 ||
śaṅkhaṃ bāṇayutaṃ cāpaṃ pāśaṃ gurvīṃ gadāmapi|
dadhānaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ divyaṃ daṃṣṭrābhāsvaritānanam || 50 ||
piṅgākṣaṃ piṅgakeśāḍhyaṃ jvālāmālāpariṣkṛtam|
atha ṣoḍaśahastaṃ ca dhyāyeddevaṃ sudarśanam || 51 ||
sudarśanadhyānamāha nyastāṅga iti| kṛtāṅganyāsa ityarthaḥ| cakramudrā cātraivādyāye aṣṭatriṃśe śloke pratipāditā; vahniprākāraśca saptatriṃśe śloke| sudarśanavarṇanaṃ sahasrāretyādinā| cakramadhye cakrarūpiṇaḥ paramātmanaḥ dhyānaṃ prastauti akṣasthamityādinā| dakṣiṇakaradhṛtāyudhānyāha cakramityādinā| vāmakarasthāyudhānyāha śaṅkhamityādinā| dakṣiṇaṃ; samarthamityarthaḥ| aṣṭabhujasudarśanadhyānaṃ vistarato'hirvudhnyasaṃhitāyāṃ catuścatvāriṃśādhyāye pratipāditamanusaṃdheyam| udyajjānumiti pratyālīḍhākhyā samarodyamāvasthitiḥ vivakṣitā|
paraiḥ paribhave prāpte pratīkāravivarjite|
sthitāvanavaklṛptāyāmapi nirjitya vairiṇaḥ || 52 ||
bhaye mahati saṃjāte coravyāghradvipādibhiḥ|
pratyālīḍhasthitaṃ devaṃ vairivargadigunmukham || 53 ||
prahārodyogibhiḥ pīnairbhujairūrdhvairalaṃkṛtam|
śaktyā dīptena khaḍgena vahninā ca śatārcipā || 54 ||
aṅkuśenātha daṇḍena kuntenātha jvalattviṣā|
paraśvadhena cakreṇa dakṣiṇādhaḥkaraiḥ kramāt || 55 ||
śaṅkhena cāpamukyena pāśenātha halena ca|
kuliśena gadāsroṇa musalenātha śūlataḥ || 56 ||
ūrdhvādadhaḥ sthitairvāmaiḥ pradīptairāyudhairyutam|
daṃṣṭāniṣṭhyūtaghorāgnijvālākolāhalākulam || 57 ||
saṃsyūtatattvayākīrṇaṃ divyayā vanamālayā|
ghorāṭṭahāsasaṃtrāsadravdadaityendradānavam || 58 ||
jvālākulajvaladdaityamedomedurapāvake|
ayutāyutavahnīnāmāspade dīptatejasām || 59 ||
adhvaṣaṭkamaye cakre cakriṇaṃ cakramuttamama|
dhyāyedevaṃvidhaṃ devaṃ bhaye mahati mānavaḥ || 60 ||
pratyālīḍhākhyā sthitiḥ udyajjānumiti pūrvaṃ sūcitā| ṣoḍaśu bhujeṣu dakṣiṇabhujāṣṭakāyudhānyāha śaktyetyādinā | vāmabhujāṣṭakāyudhānyāha śaṅkhenetyādinā| saṃsyūtatattvayeti| caturviśatitattvamayyetyarthaḥ| vanāmālāsvarūpakathanametat| ata evāsyā bhūtamāletyapi vyavahāraḥ| adhvaṣaṭkaṃ varṇādhvādi|
evaṃ dhyātvā punardhyāyeccaturbāhuṃ sudarśanam|
anyathā naiva śāntiḥ syādasti tejastathā hareḥ || 61 ||
etasya dhyānena sarvāriṣṭaśāntirahirbudhnyasaṃhitāyāṃ saptacatvāriṃśādhyāye varṇitā draṣṭavyā|
ghoraśāntavibhedena pauruṣaṃ dhyānamīritam|
iti te suraśārdūla dhyānamanyacca me śṛṇu || 62 ||
prakārāḥ pauruṣā ye ye dhyāne'smin parivarṇitāḥ|
tān sarvāna manmayāneva saṃsmarecchīghrasiddhaye || 63 ||
atyadbhutamidaṃ śakra rahasyaṃ te prakīrtitam|
bhūyo rahasyamanyacca śṛṇu me surapuṃgava || 64 ||
āgneyī yā madīyā te purā śaktiḥ prakīrtitā|
sūryakoṭyarbudābhāsā vahnikoṭyarbudopamā || 65 ||
indukoṭyarbudābhāsā mama spandamayī tanuḥ|
amṛtaṃ paramātmānamaśeṣabhuvanādhṛtim || 66 ||
spandamayī kriyāmayī| amṛtaṃ sakāraḥ| paramātmā hakāraḥ| aṣeṣabhuvanādhṛtiḥ rephaḥ|
āsthāya pañcabindvātmā spṛśantī vyāpinaṃ param|
hitāya sarvabhūtānāmudeti parameśvarāt || 67 ||
pañcabinduḥ īkāraḥ| vyāpī anusvāraḥ|
tāmakṣaṃ kalpayecchaktiṃ tatprabhāṃ nābhimaṇḍalam|
arāṇi ṣaṇmanorarṇān sūryoddāmau sabindukau || 68 ||
arāṇi ṣaḍiti ṣaḍaracakraṃ vivakṣitam| sūryoddāmau hu iti|
susthitau nemigau dhyāyeccheṣaṃ tu pradhimaṇḍalam|
ātmānaṃ madhyato dhyāyet svaṃ māyāparamātmanoḥ || 69 ||
śeṣamiti| phaḍityetadityarthaḥ|
sūryānalāntarasthaṃ ca nirasyan saṃsmarejjanam|
dhyāyannaniśamevaṃ hi yogī dhyānaparāyaṇaḥ || 70 ||
vidhūya nikhilaṃ doṣaṃ sāṃsārikamaśeṣataḥ|
mayi bhaktiṃ parāṃ prāpya māmevānte samaśnute || 71 ||
antarā paramātmānamamṛtaṃ ca sthito japan|
manīṣī manasā nityaṃ pīyūṣāpyāyanaṃ smaret || 72 ||
sudhayāplāvyamāno hi srutayā śaktikoṭarāt|
prāṇena prāṇyamānaśca dagdhadoṣo'nalatviṣā || 73 ||
prāṇena hakāreṇa| analo rephaḥ|
pañcabindukriyālābhādaiśvaryaṃ paramāsthitaḥ|
saṃtatābyāsayogena vaśī yukto jitendriyaḥ || 74 ||
vihāya sakalaṃ kleśaṃ veṣamāsthāya māmakam|
dṛpto jātabalo yogī kriyayā sarvato vaśī || 75 ||
īśvaraḥ paramo bhūtvā sarvavyāptimayaḥ sthitaḥ|
māmeva māmakaṃ dhāma matprasādādupāśnute || 76 ||
yā kriyā sā cidākhyātā yā cittiḥ sā parā kriyā|
ete saparamānandāsrayaste parikīrtitāḥ || 77 ||
svasvarūpasya kriyācidānandarūpatāmāha yeti|
akhaṇḍaikā parā śaktiścitkriyānandarūpiṇī|
vaiṣṇavī sā parāhaṃtā sāhaṃ sarvārthapūraṇī || 78 ||
svācchandyānmama saṃkalpo dvidhaivaṃ pravijṛmbhate|
ekā śaktiḥ kriyāhvānā mahābhūtirathāparā || 79 ||
dvidheti| kriyāvibhūtibhedenetyarthaḥ|
sāmānayto'nyoḥ śakra sthitāhaṃ parameśvarī|
eṣā te sakalā śaktiḥ kriyārūpā pradarśitā || 80 ||
sthūlasūkṣmaparatvena tārikāyā niśāmaya|
nimīlitakriyākārā spaṣṭaiśvaryasvarūpiṇī || 81 ||
anantarādhyāyaviṣayabhūtabhūtiśaktivarṇanamavatārayati nimīliteti| bhūtiśaktirūpe kriyāśaktiḥ nimīlitāsta ityarthaḥ| spaṣṭeti| aiśvaryaṃ vispaṣṭaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ|
tanuḥ ṣāḍguṇyarūpā me bhūtiḥ sā tārikāhvayā|
tasyāḥ sthūlādirūpāṇi yathāvanme niśāmaya ||
ucyamānāni deveśa sāvadhānena cetasā || 82 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre sudarśanaprakāśo nāmaikatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
ityekatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

<32>
dvātriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ 
yā hyeṣā paramā vidyā tārikā bhavatāriṇī|
sthūlaṃ sūkṣmaṃ paraṃ ceti tasyā rūpatrayaṃ śṛṇu || 1 ||
vidyā cidrūpetyartaḥ| bhavatāriṇītyanena tārikāyā anvarthatvamāha|
pañcavarṇaṃ caturvarṇamiti sthūlātmakaṃ vapuḥ|
trivarṇā sūkṣmasaṃjñā me parā viṣṇumayī sthitā || 2 ||
hrī iti bindorupari ghaṇṭānādavat śrūyamāṇaṃ nādaṃ saṃyojya pañcavarṇatvaṃ, taṃ vihāya caturvarṇatvamupapādanīyam| trivarṇeti| īṃrūpetyarthaḥ| pararūpamāha viṣṇumayīti| kevalekārarūpetyarthaḥ|
imāstisro hravasthā me pratyekaṃ tu tridhā tridhā|
sthūle tu yā parāvasthā paramātmāśrayā tu sā || 3 ||
pratyekaṃ tridheti| sthūlarūpe sthūlasūkṣmapararūpeṇa, sūkṣmarūpe sthūlasūkṣmapararūpeṇa, pararūpe'pi sthūlasūkṣmapararūpeṇeti traividhyamiti bhāvaḥ| sthūle pararupamāha sthūle tviti| paramātmā hakāraḥ|
aśeṣabhuvanādhāraviṣṇuvyāpisamāśrayā|
bhoktṛbhogyātmikā ceyaṃ tasyā rūpaṃ nibodha me || 4 ||
aśeṣabhuvanādhāro rephaḥ| viṣṇuḥ īkāraḥ| vyāpī anusvāraḥ| āhatya hrīṃ bhavati|
aśeṣabhuvanādhāranilayavyāpijanmanā|
paramātmādikenaiva lalāṭataṭameyuṣā || 5 ||
lalāṭataṭameyuṣetyasya rūpeṇeti śeṣaḥ|
ākramya vaiṣṇavaṃ rūpaṃ sve punaḥ pratitiṣṭhati|
tārakāraṇanādena śobhamānā haripriyā || 6 ||
ākramyeti vaiṣṇavarūpasyānabhivyaktirucyate|
bhoktṛbhogyādikaṃ sarvaṃ bhuvanaṃ bibhratī dhiyā|
vyāpinaṃ sarvato devī paramātmānamāśritā || 7 ||
tāvattayā sthitā viṣṇurūpā sthūlā gatiḥ parā|
pañcakṛtyakarī śaktisrividhaiśvaryabṛṃhitā || 8 ||
tāvattayeti| bhoktṛbhogyarūpeṇetyarthaḥ| atha sthūle sūkṣmamāha pañcakṛtyetyādi| sargādīni pañca kṛtyāni|
prāṇayantī śriyā devaṃ jṛmbhamāṇodadhiprabham|
āśritya hyānalaṃ bhāvamiyaṃ sūkṣmā gatirmatā || 9 ||
sṛṣṭyādikaṃ vidhāyātha vyomasthaṃ paramāsthitā|
sarvāścaryakarī devī sṛṣṭisthityantakāraṇam || 10 ||
sthūle sthūlarūpamāha sṛṣṭyādikamiti| sarvāścaryetyādi| pūrvaṃ sūkṣme jagataḥ sṛṣṭisaṃkalpa uktaḥ| atra tu tasya sṛṣṭirucyata iti dheyayam|
sūryaṃ samāśritā viṣṇuṃ kalayantījagatsthitim|
kālapāvakatejobhiḥ sthūlā sthūlamayādbhutā || 11 ||
kathitā gatayastisraḥ sthūlāyā mama vāsava|
sūkṣmāyāstu gatīstisraḥ śṛṇu vṛtraniṣūdana || 12 ||
akālakalanā seyaṃ sūkṣmā tu parameśvarī|
vyāpinaṃ paramātmānaṃ śrayantī vartate dhruvā || 13 ||
sūkṣmarupasya sādhāraṇaṃ svabhāvamāha akāleti| anena pūrvoktasthūlarūpasya kālakālyatvamabhimanyate|
śuddho vargastathāśuddho dvividhaṃ sṛjyamucyate|
śuddhetare sthitā sthūlā śuddhe sūkṣmā pratiṣṭhitā || 14 ||
viśeṣāntaramāha śuddha iti|
tisro'pyāsāṃ gatīḥ samyak sthūlāyā iva lakṣayet|
parā yā me tanuḥ śakra tasyā rūpaṃ niśāmaya || 15 ||
prastute trivedhe sūkṣmarūpepūrvoktasthūlagatīratidiśati tisra iti| paramiyān bhedaḥ pūrvoktasthūlagatiraśuddhaviṣayā; iyaṃ tu śuddhaviṣayeti|
sarvavyāptimatī divyā niṣkalā sā nirañjanā|
sā parā manmayī śaktiḥ kathitā viṣṇusaṃjñayā || 16 ||
atha parāyāḥ sādhāraṇaṃ rūpamāha sarvavyāptimatītyādi|
eṣā sā vaiṣṇavī sattā saiṣāhaṃtā harermatā|
eṣā sā yogināṃ niṣṭhā saiṣā sāṃkhyātmanāṃ gatiḥ || 17 ||
sāṃkhyātmanām; prakṛtipuruṣavivekādijñānavatāmityarthaḥ|
iyaṃ sā paramā mūrtiriyaṃ sā paramā gatiḥ|
śaktiḥ kuṇḍalinī cādyā bhramarī yogadāyikā || 18 ||
tasyā nāmabhedānāha śaktirityādinā|
anāhatā hyaghoṣā ca nirmaryādā nadodgatā|
śabdabrahma tathā śaktirmātṛkāyoniruttamā || 19 ||
nadaḥ; nādaḥ|
gāyatrī ca kalā gaurī śacī devī sarasvatī|
vṛṣākapāyī satyā ca prāṇapatnī yaśasvinī || 20 ||
indrapatnī mahādhenuraditirdevanandinī|
rudrāṇāṃ jananī devī vasūnāṃ tu hitā tathā || 21 ||
ādhityānāṃ svasā nābhiramṛtasya dhṛtiḥ parā|
iḍā ratiḥ priyākārā gurudhātrī mahīyasī || 22 ||
mahī ca viśrutiscaiva trayī gauḥ prāṇavatsalā|
śaktiśca prakṛtiścaiva mahārājñī payasvinī || 23 ||
tārā sītā tathā śrīśca kāmavatsā priyavratā|
taruṇī ca varārohā nīrūpā rūpaśālinī || 24 ||
ambikā sundarī jyeṣṭhā vāmā ghorā manomayī|
siddhā siddhāntikā yogā yoginī yogabhāvinī || 25 ||
evamādīni nāmāni śāsre śāsre manīṣibhiḥ|
kathitāni rahasyāni śakteḥ siddhāntapāragaiḥ || 26 ||
saiṣā śaktiḥ parā divyā tridhā rūpairavasthitā|
sthūlasūkṣmaparatvena traidhametat pradarśyate || 27 ||
asyāḥ svarūpamī proktā sā ca tredhāvatiṣṭhate|
aprameyādirūpeṇa tasyā vyākhyāmimāṃ śṛṇu || 28 ||
ī; īkāra ityarthaḥ|
ṣāḍguṇyaṃ yat paraṃ brahma vāsudevākhyamavyayam|
saṃhṛtākhilabhedaṃ tadekameva yadā tadā || 29 ||
parāyāḥ pararūpamāha ṣāḍguṇyamiti| saṃhṛteti| bhedaḥ; bhinnaḥ prapañcaḥ| pratisaṃcare sakalasyāpi prapañcasya brahmāṇi layāt tathoktiḥ| ekameveti| sūkṣmāvasthacidacidviśiṣṭaṃ brahmaikameva tattvamabhavadityarthaḥ|
aprameyākhyayā devastadā yogibhirīryate|
vyāpyavyāpakabhedo vā sṛjyasṛṣṭividhāpi vā || 30 ||
na tadānyaprameyatvaṃ na kiṃcittasya vidyate|
tadā śūnyamivākāraiḥ prasuptamiva sarvataḥ || 31 ||
prameyatvaṃ pramāviṣayatvam| śūnyamiveti| sarvavyāpāroparamāt asatkalpamityarthaḥ| bāhyābhimataśūnyākhyatattvanirāsāyevaśabdaḥ|
ṣāḍguṇyaṃ brahma tatreyaṃ saṃsthitā viṣṇusaṃjñayā|
ataraṅgārṇavākārā śaktiśaktimatorgatiḥ || 32 ||
atha parāyāḥ sūkṣmarūpamāha śaktīti| yadetyuttaravākyasthamatrādau jñeyam| yadā śakteḥ śaktimataśca vyaktā gatiravagatiḥ, tadetyuttaratrānvayaḥ|
tadā yadā punarbrahma vāsudevākhyamavraṇam|
unmiṣatyātmasaṃkalpāttadā prathama ucyate || 33 ||
prathama iti| saṃkalpa iti śeṣaḥ|
prathate hi yadā brahma śuddhāśuddhākhyavartmanā|
māyā nāma tadā tveṣā vrahnasaṃkalparūpiṇī || 34 ||
eṣā; parā śaktirityarthaḥ|
saṃkarṣaṇādikṣityantastasyā garbhamavasthitaḥ|
yadā punaḥ paraṃ brahma svecchāsaṃpāditaṃ pṛthak || 35 ||
parāyāḥ sthūlarūpamāha saṃkarṣaṇeti|
vyāpyavyāpakabhedena sadvitīyamavasthitam|
pañcabindustadā devī pañcakṛtyavidhāyinī || 36 ||
pañcabinduḥ; īkāraḥ|
parāyā iti te proktā mama tanvā gatisrayī|
rūpaṃ rūpaṃ vibhajyaiṣā tattattattvārṇabhedinī || 37 ||
trayī; sthūlasūkṣmapararūpeṇa trividhetyarthaḥ|
tattadvācakatāṃ nītā svakāmājjagatītanuḥ|
teṣu teṣu hi tattveṣu svātmabhūtāvatiṣṭhate || 38 ||
madaṃśaḥ sūkṣmarūpo yo gūḍho'gniriva dāruṣu|
tattadrūpamanuprāptā sevanī nāma śāśvatī || 39 ||
sevanīti jihvādisthāneṣu varṇoccāraṇādyupayogyavayavasaṃghaṭanāviśeṣa ucyate|
tattadvarṇoparāgeṇa tattadvyaktivapuḥ svayam|
adhidaivatabhāvena tatra yoṃ'śaḥ parātmakaḥ || 40 ||
yoṃ'śa iti| asya seyamityanantaraślokasthenānvayaḥ|
vaiṣṇavaḥ śaktirūpo me niyacchannavatiṣṭhate|
tattadvācakatāṃ yāti devī seyamanaśvarī || 41 ||
aśeṣabhuvanādhārā yoginī parameśvarī|
kevalastattvavarṇastu trailokyaiśvaryadāṃ gataḥ || 42 ||
tattat sthūlamayaṃ tattvaṃ madīyaṃ vakti śāśvatam|
tattadbhāvābhidhānena tanniyantṛtvadarśane || 43 ||
sarvamapi sthūlatattvaṃ madīyaśaktyā śāśvataṃ bhavati| anena kṣaṇabhaṅgavādanirāso'bhipretaḥ|
iyameveśvarā devī dvidhā saktāvatiṣṭhate|
kṣādi śāntaṃ purā yatte darśitaṃ brahmapañcakam || 44 ||
sakteti| āsaṅgayuktetyarthaḥ| tadevāha kṣādīti|
kṣa ityādisvarūpeṇa trailokyaiśvaryadāṃ gatā|
svarūpe niyame caiva dvedhā seyamavasthitā || 45 ||
dvedheti| sarveṣāṃ svarūpaṃ niyamanaṃ ca pradarśayantī dvedhāvatiṣṭhata ityarthaḥ|
dhāraṇānāṃ catuṣkaṃ yadvādi yāntamudīritam|
tatra sūkṣmapare bhāve sthiteyaṃ pūrvavad dvidhā || 46 ||
vādiyāntacatuṣkaṃ dhāraṇācatuṣkamityuktamityarthaḥ|
traiyavastho makāro'yaṃ proktaścaitanyavācakaḥ|
tatrāpi sūkṣmaparayordvedheyaṃ daśayordvayoḥ || 47 ||
tryavastha eva traiyavasthaḥ|
māyāprasūtitraiguṇyarūpo yo bhārṇa ucyate|
ī nāma pūrvavaddevī tatrāpi daśayordvayoḥ || 48 ||
buddhyahaṃkāramanasāṃ yadrūpaṃ bādikaṃ trayam|
tatrāpi pūrvavad dvedhā devīyaṃ daśayordvayoḥ || 49 ||
nādike ṇādike caiva tathendriyagaṇadvaye|
daśayoḥ sūkṣmaparayoriyaṃ dvedhāvatiṣṭhate || 50 ||
nādikaṃ tavargaḥ| ṇādikaṃ ṭavargaḥ|
ñādike ṅādike caiva sthūlasūkṣmasvarūpake|
vibhūtipañcake devī daśayoḥ pūrvavat sthitā || 51 ||
cavargapañcakaṃ kavargapañcakaṃ ca tanmātrasthūlabhūtarūpatayoktamityarthaḥ|
saptatyā vitatā bhedaiḥ śuddhāśuddhamayādhvani|
naṭīva svayamī śaktibibharti bahudhā vapuḥ || 52 ||
saptatyeti| śuddhāśuddhādhvanoḥ pratyekaṃ pañcatriṃśaditi āhatya saptatirbhedāḥ| ī śaktiḥ; īkārarūpā śaktirityarthaḥ|
iyadvistṛtimāpannāmīmimāṃ parameśvarīm|
vicintya paramaṃ yāti padaṃ viṣṇoḥ sanātanam || 53 ||
yatra yatra gatā seyaṃ śuddhāśuddhe tathādhvani|
tatra tatra tvajahatī viṣṇoḥ saṃbandhamī sthitā || 54 ||
ekadvitryādiyogena svaravyañjanarūṣitā|
śuddhāśuddhādhvavargasthā nānābhedopapāditā || 55 ||
jaṭoparāgahīnāyā asyā eva punasridhā|
jñeyaḥ sthūlādirūpeṇa vibhedastattvacintakaiḥ || 56 ||
jaṭeti| jakāraṭakārādītyarthaḥ| upalakṣaṇametat varṇāntaroparāgasyāpi|
sṛṣṭikṛtsaṃyutā sthūlā sūkṣmā vyomeśasaṃyutā|
nirañjanā parā seyamī ityevānurāgiṇī || 57 ||
īrūpasya sthūlādibhedamāha sṛ

<33>
trayasriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ
śṛṇu vatsa sureśāna vidyāyāstārikākṛteḥ|
aṅgopāṅgāni mantrāṇi nānāmantramayāni me || 1 ||
gopanaṃ pañcabinduṃ ca hyūrjamairāvaṇaṃ tathā|
aurvaṃ ca pañcakaṃ caite pratyekaṃ vyāpinānvitam || 2 ||
gopanaḥ ākāraḥ| pañcabinduḥ īkāraḥ| ūrjaḥ ūkāraḥ| airāvaṇa aikāraḥ| aurvaḥ aukāraḥ| vyāpī anusvāraḥ|
prāṇānaloparisthaṃ tu kṛtvaitat piṇḍapañcakam|
hṛdādinetraparyantamaṅgabījamidaṃ smaret || 3 ||
prāṇaḥ hakāraḥ| analaḥ rephaḥ| piṇḍapañcakam; hrāṃ, hrīṃ, hrūṃ, hraiṃ, rhrau iti mantrapañcakam| hṛdādīti| hṛdayaśiraḥ śikhākavacanetrāṇītyarthaḥ|
hṛdbījāt parato yojyaṃ jñānāyeti padaṃ tataḥ|
hṛdayāya namaścaiva mantro'yaṃ dhāraṇapradaḥ || 4 ||
mantraṃ nirdiśati hṛdityādi| oṃ hrāṃ jñānāya hṛdayāya namaḥ|
praṇavādinamo'nto'yaṃ mantra ekādaśākṣaraḥ|
paraṃ praṇavabījābhyāmaiśvaryāya padaṃ nyaset || 5 ||
śirase ca tathā svāhā hyeṣa ekādaśākṣaraḥ|
paraṃ praṇavabījābhyāṃ śaktaye ca padaṃ nyaset || 6 ||
śikhāyai vauṣaḍityevaṃ śaikho'yaṃ tu daśākṣaraḥ|
paraṃ praṇavabījābhyāṃ balāyeti padaṃ nyaset || 7 ||
kavacāya himityevaṃ mantro'yaṃ ca daśākṣaraḥ|
paraṃ praṇavabījābhyāṃ tejase ca padaṃ nyaset || 8 ||
netrābhyāṃ bauṣaḍityevaṃ netramantro daśākṣaraḥ|
prāṇānaloparisthaṃ tu vinyaset parameśvaram || 9 ||
tasmāt praṇavapūrvāttu padaṃ vīryāya vinyaset|
asrāya ca phaḍityevaṃ mantro'yaṃ ca daśākṣaraḥ || 10 ||
oṃ hrīṃ aisvaryāya śirase svāhā| oṃ hrūṃ śaktaye śikhāyai vauṣaṭ| oṃ hraiṃ balāya kavacāya huṃ| oṃ hrauṃ tejase netrābhyāṃ vauṣat| oṃ hraḥ vīryāya asrāya ca phaṭ|
aṅgaṣaṭkamidaṃ proktamupāṅgatriyugaṃ śṛṇu|
tārikānte kramāddadyāt pūrvavat ṣaḍguṇaṃ padam || 11 ||
jñānāditejaḥparyantaṃ tadante ca kramānnyaset|
udarāya ca pṛṣṭhāya bāhubhyāmiti vai padam || 12 ||
oṃ hrīṃ jñānāya udarāya namaḥ| oṃ hrīṃ śaktaye pṛṣṭhāya namaḥ| oṃ hrīṃ balāya bāhubhyāṃ namaḥ|
ūrubhyāmatha jānubhyāṃ caraṇābhyāmiti kramāt|
namaśca parato yojyamupāṅgānāmayaṃ vidhiḥ || 13 ||
oṃ hrīṃ aiśvaryāya ūrubhyāṃ namaḥ| oṃ hrīṃ vīryāya jānubhyāṃ namaḥ| oṃ hrīṃ tejase caraṇābhyāṃ namaḥ|
ityevamaṅgopāṅgānāṃ mantrā dvādaśa kīrtitāḥ|
alaṃkārāsramantrāṃstu bruvatyā me niśāmaya || 14 ||
kaustubho vyomasaṃbhinnaḥ paramātmā tataḥ param|
ūrdhvādho'nalasaṃbhinna ūrjenāpi samanvitaḥ || 15 ||
sṛṣṭikṛtsaṃyuto mūrdhni kaustubho vyāpisaṃyutaḥ|
namaskṛtistataḥ paścāttataḥ paścāt prabhātmane || 16 ||
kaustubhāya tataḥ svāhā praṇavādyastu kaustubhaḥ|
mantraḥ ṣoḍaśavarṇo'yaṃ sarvakarmaprasādhakaḥ || 17 ||
oṃ ṭhaṃ rhrūḥ ṭhaṃ namaḥ prabhātmane kaustubhāya svāhā|
uddharet prathamaṃ tāraṃ dhareśaṃ tata uddharet|
tadadhastṛptisaṃjñaṃ ca varāhaṃ tadadho nyaset || 18 ||
māyayā bhūṣayet paścādvyāpinā cāṅkayettataḥ|
pañcātmā varṇapiṇḍo'yaṃ namaskāraṃ tataḥ param || 19 ||
tataḥ sthalajalodbhūtabhūṣitepadamuddharet|
vanamāle tataḥ svāhā mantraḥ sarvārthasādhakaḥ || 20 ||
oṃ lsvīṃ namaḥ sthalajalodbhūtabhūṣite vanamāle svāhā|
ekonaviṃśatyarṇo'yaṃ vanamālāmayo mahān|
tārakasyāvasāne tu vāmanārṇaṃ samuddharet || 21 ||
tadadhastṛptimāyojya bhūṣayedudayena tu|
vyāpinā cāṅkitaḥ piṇḍaścaturarṇo mahādbhutaḥ || 22 ||
namaskārastataḥ paścācchrīnivāsapadaṃ tataḥ|
padmāya vahnijāyā ca pādmo'yaṃ tridaśākṣaraḥ || 23 ||
oṃ bsuṃ namaḥ śrīnivāsapadmāya svāhā| tryadhikāḥ daśākṣarāḥ yasyeti bahuvrīhiḥ|
ādāyādau tu vaikuṇṭhaṃ rephaṃ tadupari nyaset|
ānandenānvitaṃ paścādvyāpinā cāṅkayettataḥ || 24 ||
kasthakasthapadaṃ dadyānnemidvandvamataḥ param|
varapāśāya vai svāhā praṇavādyastu pāśarāṭ || 25 ||
oṃ rṇāṃ kastha kastha ṭhaṭha varapāśāya svāhā|
mantraḥ pañcadaśārṇo'yaṃ kāmināṃ kṣiprasiddhikṛt|
praṇavānte virāṭsaṃjñaṃ vyāpinā mūrdhni saṃyutam || 26 ||
dvayaṃ piṇḍatayā yojyaṃ rephaṃ kamalamaṅkuśam|
vyāpinā saṃyutaṃ mūrdhni tṛtīyamidamakṣaram || 27 ||
padaṃ niśitaghoṇāya cāṅkuśāya śikhipriyā|
iti pañcadaśārṇo'yamāṅkuśaḥ śīghrasiddhidaḥ || 28 ||
oṃ lṝṃrkṛṃ niśitaghoṇāya aṅkuśāya svāhā|
alaṃkārāsramantrāṇāmetat pañcakamīritam|
ādhārāsanamantrāṇāṃ śṛṇu rūpaṃ puraṃdara || 29 ||
yadvinā tārikāyāstu pūraṇaṃ naiva jāyate|
analadvayamadhyasthaḥ prāṇo māyī sa bindumān || 30 ||
tata ādhāraśaktyai ca praṇavādirnamo'ntimaḥ|
ādhāraśaktimantro'yaṃ vijñeyastu navākṣaraḥ || 31 ||
oṃ hrīṃ ādhāraśaktyai namaḥ|
analadvayamadhyastho bindvanto'pyūrjasaṃyutaḥ|
tataḥ kālāgnikūrmāya namo'ntaḥ praṇavādikaḥ || 32 ||
analadvayamadhyastha iti| prāṇa ityanuṣajyate| oṃ rhrūṃ kālāgnikūrmāya namaḥ|
mantraḥ kālāgnikūrmasya vijñeyo'yaṃ daśākṣaraḥ|
gopanenāṅkitaṃ prāṇaṃ mūrdhni ca vyāpinā yutam || 33 ||
praṇavānte samuddhṛtya hyanantāya namastataḥ|
aṣṭākṣaro hyayaṃ mantro nāgarājasya kīrtitaḥ || 34 ||
oṃ hāṃ anantāya namaḥ|
kamalaṃ cāgnirūpaṃ ca pradhānaṃ puruṣeśvaram|
piṇḍīkṛtya catuṣkaṃ tu gopanavyāpisaṃyutam || 35 ||
vasudhāyai namaḥ paścāt praṇavādirmanustvayam|
viśvaṃbharāyā vijñeya ādhāraḥ parikalpyate || 36 ||
oṃ kṣmlāṃ vasudhāyai namaḥ|
amṛtaṃ varuṇaṃ cārṇadvayaṃ piṇḍīkṛtaṃ saha|
gopanavyāpisaṃyuktaṃ praṇavānte samuddharet || 37 ||
oṃ svāṃ kṣīrārṇavāya namaḥ|
kṣīrārṇavāya ca namaḥ so'yaṃ mantro navākṣaraḥ|
pavitraṃ sodayavyāpisaṃyutaṃ praṇavāntagam || 38 ||
oṃ puṃ ādhārapadmāya namaḥ|
ādhārapadmāya namaḥ padmasyāyaṃ daśākṣaraḥ|
itthamādhāraṣaṭkasya mantraṣaṭkaṃ prakīrtitam || 39 ||
ādhāreśākhyamantrāṇāṃ vidhiṃ śṛṇu puraṃdara|
dhartājito'mṛtādhāro vibudhākhyaśca vāsava || 40 ||
etāṃścatura uddhṛtya varṇāneṣāmathopari|
vinyaseccaturo varṇān satyādīn sādhakottamaḥ || 41 ||
trailokyaiśvaryadaṃ dadyāccatvāryetāni vāsava|
bījāni praṇavādīni dharmādeḥ ṣoḍaśātmanaḥ || 42 ||
dharmādikamadharmādyamṛgādyaṃ ca kṛtādikam|
catuṣṭayāni catvāri yāni siddhāni lokataḥ || 43 ||
dhartā dhakāraḥ| ajito jakāraḥ| amṛtādhāro vakāraḥ| vibudho lakāraḥ| satyaḥ ṛkāraḥ| oṃ dhṛṃ, oṃ dhṝṃ, oṃ dhlṛṃ, oṃ dhlṝṃ iti krameṇa dharmajñānavairāgyaiśvaryākhyādhāreśabījamantrāḥ| oṃ jṛṃ, oṃ jṝṃ, oṃ jlṛṃ, oṃ jlṝṃ iti krameṇa adharmājñānāvairāgyānaiśvaryākhyādhāreśabījamantrāḥ| oṃ vṛṃ, oṃ vṝṃ, oṃ vlṛṃ, oṃ vlṝṃ iti ṛgyajuḥsāmātharvākhyādhāreśabījamantrāḥ| oṃ lṛṃ, oṃ lṝṃ, oṃ llṛṃ, oṃ llṝṃ iti kṛtatretādvāparakalyākhyādhāreśabījamantrāḥ|
catuścaturvibhāgena saṃjñāḥ ṣoḍaśa vinyaset|
bījopari namaschānte mantrāḥ ṣoḍaśa te smṛtāḥ || 44 ||
atha dharmādīnāṃ ṣoḍaśānāṃ śaṃjñāmantrā ucyante caturityādinā| oṃ dhṛṃ dharmāya namaḥ| oṃ dhṝṃ jñānāya namaḥ| ityādikrameṇa jñeyāḥ|
sodayaṃ sāmṛtaṃ hrasvaṃ praṇavopari vinyaset|
avyaktapadmāya namaḥ sa mantro'vyaktapadmakaḥ || 45 ||
hrasvaḥ bakāraḥ| oṃ bsuṃ avyaktapadmamāya namaḥ|
sūryendvagnipadebyastu pratyekaṃ maṇḍalāya ca|
namo'nte praṇavaścādau te mantrā maṇḍalatraye || 46 ||
oṃ sūryamaṇḍalāya namaḥ| oṃ indumaṇḍalāya namaḥ| oṃ agnimaṇḍalāya namaḥ| iti maṇḍalatrayamantrāḥ|
pratyagātmaparāmarśiśabdaḥ somo'tha sargavān|
cidbhāsanākhyamantro'yaṃ tryakṣaraḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 47 ||
ahaṃ saḥ| iti cidbhāsanamantraḥ|
ityāsanākhyamantrāṇāṃ kathitā tvekaviṃśatiḥ|
ityayaṃ pīṭhapūjānto mantragrāmo mayeritaḥ || 48 ||
ekaviṃśatiriti| ṣoḍaśādhāreśamantrāḥ| ekaḥ avyaktapadmamantraḥ| trayo maṇḍalamantrāḥ| ekaḥ cidbhāsanamantra iti jñeyam|
rahasyaṃ paramaṃ guhyamidānīṃ paramaṃ śṛṇu|
kṣetreśādyaṃ mantracayaṃ vighnanirmathanakṣamam || 49 ||
garuḍaṃ kālamanalaṃ piṇḍīkṛtyāṅkayet tataḥ|
savyāpinādidevena kṣetrapālāya vai namaḥ || 50 ||
oṃ kṣmrāṃ kṣetrapālāya namaḥ|
praṇavādyo manuḥ so'yaṃ kṣetreśasya navākṣaraḥ|
anutārā śriyai paścānnamastvādau ca tārakaḥ || 51 ||
oṃ śrīṃ śriyai namaḥ|
ṣaḍakṣaraḥ śriyo mantraścaṇḍādīnaparāñśṛṇu|
sacañcalānalastāraḥ kevalastvādito bhavet || 52 ||
oṃ cro caṇḍāya namaḥ|
caṇḍāya nama ityeva saptavarṇo manūttamaḥ|
sapavitrānalastāraḥ kevalastvādito bhavet || 53 ||
oṃ proṃ pracaṇjāya namaḥ|
pracaṇḍāya namo mantraḥ pracaṇḍo'yaṃ caturyugaḥ|
saśāśvatānalastāraḥ kevalastvādito bhavet || 54 ||
oṃ jroṃ jayāya namaḥ|
jayāya nama ityevaṃ jayasya munivarṇakaḥ|
varāhānalasaṃyuktastāraḥ śuddhastathādigaḥ || 55 ||
oṃ vroṃ vijayāya namaḥ|
vijayāya namaḥ so'yaṃ vijayasya caturyugaḥ|
govindaḥ sānalo māyī vyāpimān praṇavāntagaḥ || 56 ||
oṃ grīṃ gaṅgāyai namaḥ|
gaṅgāyai nama ityevaṃ gaṅgāyā munivarṇakaḥ|
samāyaḥ sānalaḥ sūkṣmo vyāpimān praṇavāntagaḥ || 57 ||
oṃ yrīṃ yamunāyai namaḥ|
yamunāyai namaścāyaṃ yāmuneyaścaturyugaḥ|
śaṃkaraḥ sānalaḥ sorjo vyāpimān praṇavāntagaḥ || 58 ||
oṃ śrūṃ śaṅkhanidhaye namaḥ|
tataśca śaṅkhanidhaye namaḥ so'yaṃ navākṣaraḥ|
pavitraḥ sānalaḥ sorjo vyāpimān praṇavāntagaḥ || 59 ||
oṃ prūṃ padmanidhaye namaḥ|
tataśca padmanidhaye namaḥ so'yaṃ navākṣaraḥ|
kṣetreśāt padmanidhyantaṃ mantrāṇāṃ daśakaṃ tvidam || 60 ||
gaṇeśādyādisiddhāntamatha mantragaṇaṃ śṛṇu|
ūrjavyāpisamāyukto govindaḥ praṇavāntagaḥ || 61 ||
tato govindavaikuṇṭhau pavitraḥ sragdharastathā|
jagadyonigataḥ śaṅkho naraḥ kālo visargavān || 62 ||
gaṇeśādīti| gaṇeśamārabhya ādisiddhaparyantānāṃ mantrā ityarthaḥ| oṃ gūṃ gaṇapataye namaḥ|
navākṣaro hyayaṃ mantro gāṇapatyaḥ prakīrtitaḥ|
ṣoḍhā saṃyojya govindaṃ yugmādyairgopanādibhiḥ || 63 ||
oṃ gāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ| ityādi jñeyam|
aṅgaklṛptiramuṣya syānnamaḥ svāhādisaṃyutā|
ādyantānalasaṃyuktaṃ māyāvyāpisamanvitam || 64 ||
garuḍaṃ tārakasyānte tadante tārikāṃ smaret|
rephaśaṅkhādidevāḍhyaṃ somaṃ vyāpisamanvitam || 65 ||
caturthaṃ saṃsmaredbījaṃ tadidaṃ balasūdana|
vairājānalaśaṅkhāḍhcaṃ gopanaṃ vyāpisaṃyutam || 66 ||
somavarṇaṃ smarecchakra pañcamaṃ paramādbhutam|
aprameyādidevādi yāvadgaruḍavarṇakam || 67 ||
yathāpāṭhaṃ samuccārya vāgīśvaryai tato namaḥ|
ekaṣaṣṭyarṇako mantro vāgīśvaryā ayaṃ smṛtaḥ || 68 ||
oṃ rkṣrīṃ hrīṃ syrāṃ stryāṃ akṣavāgīśvaryai namaḥ|
kuryāccaturthabījena gopanādivibhedinā|
antarjātiyutāṃ samyagaṅgaklṛptiṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ || 69 ||
oṃ syrāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ ityādayo'ṅgamantrāḥ| jātirityaṅgamucyate|
gopanādivibhinnasya bījasyādyantayornyaset|
praṇavaṃ ca namaścaiva tato jātiṃ prakalpayet || 70 ||
praṇavatritayaṃ vyāpisaṃyutaḥ sodayaśca gaḥ|
gurave'tha namaḥ so'yaṃ prathito gurupūjane || 71 ||
praṇavatritayasyānte pavitro vyāpisaṃyutaḥ|
paramagurave namaḥ prathitastu gurorguruḥ || 72 ||
praṇavatritayasyānte vyāpyānandayutastu paḥ|
parameṣṭhine'tha ca namo mantro gurugurorguruḥ || 73 ||
oṃ oṃ oṃ guṃ gurave namaḥ| oṃ oṃ oṃ paṃ paramagurave namaḥ oṃ oṃ oṃ pāṃ parameṣṭhine namaḥ|
tārapañcakamāhlādaṃ vyoma prāṇopari nyaset|
kālānalau tu tadadhaḥ sarvalokeśvaropari || 74 ||
yathākramoditairvarṇaiḥ piṇḍaṃ kṛtvā tataḥ svadhā|
pitṛbhyo'tha namaḥ so'yaṃ pitṛsaṃghasya mantrarāṭ || 75 ||
oṃ oṃ oṃ oṃ oṃ ṭhmrūṃ svadhā pitṛbhyo namaḥ|
ṣaṭ tārā ādidevo'tha vyomavān kevalo'tha saḥ|
rāmavān damanaścātha siddhebhyo'tha tato namaḥ || 76 ||
mantro'yamādisiddhānāṃ bhagavadbhāvitātmanām|
kṣetreśādyādisiddhāntān vighnanirmathanakṣamān || 77 ||
oṃ oṃ oṃ oṃ oṃ oṃ āṃ ādisiddhebyo namaḥ|
prāgeva pūjayenmantrī samārādhanakālataḥ|
atha lokeśamantrāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇu vāsava || 78 ||
prāṇaṃ dhareśamānandaṃ piṇḍīkṛtya kramasthitān|
mūrdhni vyomayutaṃ kṛtvā tata indrāya vai namaḥ || 79 ||
oṃ hlāṃ indrāya namaḥ|
aindro'yamīrito mantraḥ pāvakādīnnibodha me|
kuryāt prāṇānalānadairvyāpināpi ca piṇkam || 80 ||
prāṇakālādidevaiśca vyāpināpi ca piṇḍakm|
agnaye nama ityevaṃ yamāya nama ityāpi || 81 ||
oṃ hrāṃ agnaye namaḥ| oṃ hnāṃ yamāya namaḥ|
naraḥ sa bhagavān vyāpī piṇḍo nirṛtaye namaḥ|
yātudhāneśamantro'yaṃ jaleśasyāvadhārāya || 82 ||
oṃ nlṛṃ nirṛtaye namaḥ|
prāṇaṃ varuṇamānandaṃ vyāpinaṃ piṇḍayed budhaḥ|
varuṇāya namaḥ paścādvāruṇo'yaṃ manūttamaḥ || 83 ||
oṃ hvāṃ varuṇāya namaḥ|
prāṇaṃ sūkṣmaṃ tathānandaṃ vyāpinaṃ piṇḍayed budhaḥ|
vāyave nama ityevaṃ vāyavīyo manūttam || 84 ||
āṃ hyāṃ vāyave namaḥ|
gharmāṃśuvaruṇānandān vyāpinā saha piṇḍayet|
somāya nama ityevaṃ saumyo manurudāhṛtaḥ || 85 ||
oṃ ghvāṃ somāya namaḥ|
sūryamūrjaṃ tathā vyoma cakriṇaṃ piṇḍayet kramāt|
īśānāya namaḥ paścādīśānasya manūttamaḥ || 86 ||
oṃ hcūṃ īśānāya namaḥ|
prāṇaṃ vaikuṇṭhamānandaṃ vyāpinā piṇḍayet kramāt|
anantāya namaḥ paścānnāgarājamanustvayam || 87 ||
oṃ hṇāṃ anantāya namaḥ|
prāṇaṃ kharvaṃ tathānandaṃ vyāpinā piṇḍayet kramāt|
brahmaṇe nama ityevaṃ brahmaṇo manuruttamaḥ || 88 ||
oṃ hravāṃ brahmaṇe namaḥ|
sarveṣāṃ praṇavaḥ pūrvamāyudhānāmatho śṛṇu|
analaṃ janmahantāraṃ pradhānamanalorjakau || 89 ||
oṃ rjmrūḥ kuliśāya namaḥ|
sargeṇa piṇḍayet saṃjñāṃ namaḥ kuliśamantrarāṭ|
ūrjaṃ vihāya tatsthāne māyayā paribhūṣitam || 90 ||
oṃ jmrīḥ śaktaye namaḥ|
tadeva piṇḍaṃ saṃjñā ca namaḥ śaktimanustvayam|
akhaṇḍavikramaṃ kālaṃ lokeśaṃ parameśvaram || 91 ||
oṃ ḍmūḥ daṇḍāya namaḥ|
piṇḍīkṛtya tataḥ saṃjñā namo daṇḍamanustvayam|
viśvāpyāyakaraṃ kālaṃ lokeśaṃ parameśvaram || 92 ||
oṃ ṭmrūḥ khaḍgāya namaḥ|
piṇḍīkṛtya tataḥ saṃjñā namaḥ khaḍgamanustvayam|
candrī śāntādidevau ca sṛṣṭikṛt piṇiḍatā ime || 93 ||
oṃ ṭśāḥ pāśāya namaḥ|
pāśāya nama ityevaṃ pāśamantro'yamadbhutaḥ|
ajito varuṇānandau sṛṣṭikṛt piṇḍitā ime || 94 ||
oṃ jvāḥ dhvajāya namaḥ|
dhvajāya nama ityevaṃ dhvajamantra udīritaḥ|
paramātmānaloddāmān piṇḍayet sṛṣṭikṛdyutān || 95 ||
oṃ hrūḥ musalāya namaḥ|
musalāya namaḥ paścānmausalo'yaṃ manūttamaḥ|
analaṃ janmahantāramudayaṃ sṛṣṭikṛdyutam || 96 ||
oṃ rjuḥ śūlāya namaḥ|
piṇḍīkṛtya tataḥ saṃjñā namaḥ śūlamanustvayam|
karālamanalārūḍhamodanaṃ sṛṣṭikṛdyutam || 97 ||
oṃ kroḥ sīrāya namaḥ|
piṇḍīkṛtya tataḥ saṃjñā namaḥ sīramanustvayam|
varuṇaṃ ca naraṃ caiva gopanaṃ sṛṣṭikṛdyutam || 98 ||
oṃ vnāḥ padmāya namaḥ|
piṇḍīkṛtya tataḥ saṃjñā namaḥ padmamanustvayam|
sarveṣāṃ praṇavaḥ pūrvo viṣvaksenamanuṃ śṛṇu || 99 ||
analaprāṇalokeśān vyāpinā piṇḍayet kramāt|
varuṇaṃ bhūdharaṃ caiva vyāpinā piṇḍayet kramāt || 100 ||
oṃ rhūṃ rvau jñānadāya namaḥ|
piṇḍo'yaṃ jñānadaḥ paścādviṣvaksenasya mantrarāṭ|
praṇavādistvayaṃ mantraḥ sarvārthakṛdudīritaḥ || 101 ||
ūrjahīnaṃ tu yat pūrvaṃ tenānandādiyoginā|
aṅgaklṛptiramuṣya syājjātimudrāsamanvitā || 102 ||
oṃ rhrāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ ityādi|
somaṃ varuṇamīkāraṃ vyāpinā piṇḍayet kramāt|
surabhyai nama ityevaṃ tārapūrvo manustvayam || 103 ||
oṃ svīṃ surabhyai namaḥ|
surabhyāḥ kathitaḥ sarvabhogasaṃpūraṇārthakaḥ|
praṇavadvitayasyānte tārikādvayamuddharet || 104 ||
tataḥ paramadhāmā cāvasthite madanugrahā|
abhiyogodyate ceha tathaivāvatareti ca || 105 ||
ihābhimataśabdaṃ ca siddhide iti ca trayam|
tato mantraśarīre ca tārastārā namo namaḥ || 106 ||
oṃ oṃ hrīṃ hrīṃ paramadhāmāvasthite madanugrahābhiyogodyate ihāvatarehābhimatasiddhide mantraśarīre oṃ hrīṃ namo namaḥ| ityāvāhanamantraḥ|
pañcacatvāriṃśadarṇo mantra āvāhanārthakaḥ|
praṇavastārikā prāṇāsrayaḥ savyāpinastataḥ || 107 ||
tisraśca tārikāḥ paścādidaṃ śabdaṃ triruccaret|
gṛhāṇa vahnijāyā ca bhogadānamanustvayam || 108 ||
oṃ hnīṃ haṃ haṃ haṃ hnīṃ hnīṃ hnīṃ idamidamidamarghyaṃ gṛhāṇa svāhā ityādi|
oṃkāramuddharet pūrvaṃ viṣṇuṃ vyomānvitaṃ tataḥ|
tārikāmuddharet paścādbhūyo viṣṇuṃ tathāvidham || 109 ||
tato vyomānvitaṃ prāṇaṃ somanāmasamanvitam|
pare ca parameśe ca prasīda praṇavaṃ tataḥ || 110 ||
oṃ īṃ hrīṃ īṃ haṃsapare parameśe prasīda oṃ hnīṃ namaḥ| iti prasādanamantraḥ|
tārikā ca namaścānte prasādanamanustvayam|
praṇavastārikā caiva tato bhagavatīti ca || 111 ||
mantramūrte'tha svapadaṃ samāsādaya taddvayam|
kṣamasveti dviruccārya tārastārā namo namaḥ || 112 ||
oṃ hrīṃ bhagavati mantramūrte svapadaṃ samāsādaya samāsādaya kṣamasva kṣamasva oṃ hrī namo namaḥ| iti visarjanamantraḥ|
vaisarjanamanuḥ so'yamityete mantranāyakāḥ|
kathitāḥ suraśārdūla sarvapāpamalāpahāḥ || 113 ||
abhaktānāṃ ca ye naiva pradeyāḥ krūrakarmaṇām|
nāstikānāmasādhūnāṃ dhūrtānāṃ vañcanājuṣām || 114 ||
bhaktānāmāstikānāṃ ca śraddhāsaṃyamasevinām|
madīyakramasaktānāṃ saṃskṛtānāṃ viśeṣataḥ || 115 ||
tattvataścopasannānāṃ dṛḍhaśraddhāvalambinām|
vaiṣṇavānāmidaṃ vācyamavācyamitareṣu tu || 116 ||
anyathā vakti yo mohāllobhāt kāmādathāpi vā|
ajñānādbālabhāvādvā sa yāti narakān bahūn || 117 ||
tasmādālakṣya vai sarvaṃ guṇajātaṃ yathoditam|
prabrūyādupasannāya dharmeṇa nyāyatastathā || 118 ||
prabrūyādyo hyadharmeṇa yo vādharmeṇa pṛcchati|
tāvubhau narakaṃ ghoramṛcchataḥ kālamakṣayam || 119 ||
atra "adharmeṇa ca yaḥ prāha yaścādharmeṇa pṛcchati| tayoranyataraḥ praiti vidveṣaṃ vādhigacchati||" iti manuvacanamavadheyam|
pṛthivīṃ ratnasaṃpūrṇāṃ dadyādyadyapi vāsava|
naiva deyaṃ hyabhaktāya nāparīkṣitaśīline || 120 ||
iti te bhavato bhaktirmayi śakra mahīyasī|
tattvataścopasannasya mayeha prīyamāṇayā || 121 ||
aṅgopāṅgādimantrāṇāṃ mantrakośaḥ prakāśitaḥ|
mudrākośamidānīṃ tvaṃ gadantyā me niśāmaya || 122 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre aṅgopāṅgādimantraprakāśo nāma trayasriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
iti trayasriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

<34>
catusriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ
mudrākośaṃ pravakṣyāmi mantrakośasya vāsava|
yena vijñātamātreṇa mantrasiddhirmahīyasī || 1 ||
mudrāṃ vai bandhayenmantrī snānakāle jalāntare|
ātmano nyāsakāle ca pūjānte maṇḍalāvidhau || 2 ||
arcāyāṃ mantravinyāse hyarghyapātreṣu bhojane|
pūrṇāhutyavasāne ca mantre hyabhyantarasthite || 3 ||
hiṃsakānāṃ vighātāya sarvavighnopaśāntaye|
saṃmukhau tu karau kṛtvā suśliṣṭhau suprasāritau || 4 ||
saṃmukhaṃ madhyamāyugmanikṣiptāṅgulayaḥ parāḥ|
anyonyābhimukhāścaiva bhujabṛndena kalpitāḥ || 5 ||
mahāśrīriti vikhyātā sarvasaubhāgyadāyikā|
mocanī sarvadoṣāṇāṃ śīghrasiddhipradāyinī || 6 ||
jaḍabhūtasya vai jantorbodhanābhyudayātmikā|
prasārya vāmamuttānamaṅgulyo viralāḥ sthitā || 7 ||
kāryāstvākuñcitaprāntā aṅguṣṭhaṃ setuvadbhavet|
saṃmukhaṃ tāsu saṃlagnaṃ karaśākhāsu madhyamām || 8 ||
hṛtsaṃmukhaṃ tu badhnīyācchaktimudrāṃ sukhapradām|
ime mudre mahābhāge yogibṛndopavandite || 9 ||
parasūkṣmapadasthe tu mama prītipradāyike|
mama sthūlapadasthāyā yonimudrāṃ niśāmayā || 10 ||
saṃmukhau tu karau kṛtvā suśliṣṭau suprasāritau|
madhyato mūlataḥ pṛṣṭhe viparyaste hyanāmike || 11 ||
tarjanīmūlayornyasya tābhyāmagre nigūhayet|
madhyayoḥ śeṣayoragre kaniṣṭhāyugalaṃ puraḥ || 12 ||
anyonyaṃ pṛṣṭhato lagnaṃ talamadhyonnataṃ bhavet|
aṅguṣṭhāgradvayaṃ kuryānmadhyayoragraparvagam || 13 ||
mahāyoniriti khyātā mudrā sarvavaśaṃkarī|
trividhā trividhāyā me mudraiṣā saṃpradarśitā || 14 ||
sūkṣmākhyā śaktimudrā yā vikārastatra varṇyate|
pradeśinyādito'ṅguṣṭhamaikaikaśyena setuvat || 15 ||
vinyasyāṅguliṣu jñeyāścatasro mudrikā imāḥ|
śaktīnāṃ tu madīyānāṃ lakṣmyādīnāṃ puraṃdara || 16 ||
lakṣmīḥ kīrtirjayā māyā devyo macchaktayo hi tāḥ|
dakṣiṇena tu hastena muṣṭibandhaṃ prakalpayet || 17 ||
antaḥsthamunnataṃ kṛtvā vāmāṅguṣṭhaṃ surottama|
saṃmukhaṃ hṛdi hārdaiṣā mudrā buddhivivardhanī || 18 ||
prasṛtā aṅgulīḥ sarvā aṅguṣṭhena tu saṃspṛśet|
śairasyeṣā bhavenmudrā mantrasaṃnidhikāriṇī || 19 ||
muṣṭiṃ vitarjanīṃ kṛtvā tarjanī hyūrdhvasaṃsthitā|
śikhāmudreti vikhyātā sarvudaṣṭabhayaṃkarī || 20 ||
doṣavignavināśāya sadā hyetāṃ pradarśayet|
ādau tasmāt prayatnena yāgaveśmani darśayet || 21 ||
utsādaṃ sarvavighnānāṃ kurute mantrasaṃyutā|
ubhayoragrataḥ śākhāḥ saṃsthāstu karayordvayoḥ || 22 ||
tābhyāṃ madhyamasaṃlagnaṃ karabandhādito bhavet|
vārṇaṇyeṣā bhavenmudrā dvāvaṃsāvanayā spṛśet || 23 ||
abhedyā duṣṭasaṃghena bhūtavetālayogibhiḥ|
karmakāle nibadhyaiṣā mantriṇā ca prayatnataḥ || 24 ||
karayorgrathitāṅgulyaḥ saṃvṛtāḥ pāṇipṛṣṭhagāḥ|
tarjanyau prāntasaṃlagne suṣire cocchrite tayoḥ || 25 ||
aṅguṣṭhau mūlasaṃlagnau viparyastau parasparam|
cākṣuṣyeṣā bhavenmudrā nibadhyā cakṣurantike || 26 ||
tarjanīṃ sphoṭayeddikṣu daśasvaṅguṣṭhakena tu|
drutaṃ karadvayenaiva cakṣurbhyāṃ saṃnirīkṣayet || 27 ||
asramudreti vikhyātā trāsanī devavidviṣām|
aṅgānāmiyamuddiṣṭā ṣaṇmudrī sarvasādhanī || 28 ||
upāṅgatriyugasyātha mudrā etā niśāmaya|
aṅgulīḥ śleṣayet sarvāścatasro dakṣiṇasthitāḥ || 29 ||
tāsāṃ mūle tathāṅguṣṭhaṃ tiryañcaṃ viniveśayet|
upāṅgānāmiyaṃ mudrā tayā tattat spṛśet padam || 30 ||
alaṃkārāsramantrāṇāṃ mudrā atha niśāmaya|
kaniṣṭhānāmikāmadhyā muṣṭivat pāṇimadhyagāḥ || 31 ||
ubhayorhastayoḥ paścāttau muṣṭī śleṣayenmithaḥ|
prasārya tarjanīdvandvaṃ śleṣayedagratastataḥ || 32 ||
aṅguṣṭhāgre viparyasya tarjanyormadhyato nyaset|
mudraiṣā kaustubhī nāma mālāmudrāmimāṃ śṛṇu || 33 ||
kaṇṭhādāpādataḥ svāṃsau tarjanībhyāṃ paribhraman|
kuryādyugapadevaiṣā vanamālāmayī śubhā || 34 ||
vyaṅguṣṭhā aṅgulīraṣṭau grathayedagrato mithaḥ|
lambaṃ bāhudvayaṃ kuryādeṣā vā vanamālikā || 35 ||
aṅguṣṭhau pārśvato lagnāvaṅgulyo viralāḥ sthitāḥ|
eṣā pāṅkeruhī mudrā puṣṭisaubhāgyavardhinī || 36 ||
uttāne dakṣiṇe pāṇāvagre'ṅguṣṭhakaniṣṭhayoḥ|
melayet setuvacchiṣaṣṭaṃ susaṃśliṣṭaṃ latātrayam || 37 ||
ākuñcitaphaṇākārā mudrā pāśā bhavediyam|
muṣṭiṃ pṛṣṭhasthitāṅguṣṭhaṃ pāṇyormuṣṭidvayaṃ purā || 38 ||
kuryādadhomukhaṃ vāmaṃ tatpṛṣṭhe dakṣiṇaṃ tathā|
athādhārādiśakteḥ syānmudroktā kūrmavahnijā || 39 ||
adhomukhasya vāmasya hyanāmātarjanīdvayam|
ākuñcya madhyamāpṛṣṭhe vinyasettu susaṃsthitam || 40 ||
ṛjūmadhomukhīṃ kuryānmadhyamāmaṅgulīṃ tathā|
tataḥ kaniṣṭhikāṅguṣṭhau valavattu prasārayet || 41 ||
anantāsanamudreyamananto'yaṃ yathotthitaḥ|
iyamāsanamudrāṇāṃ pradhānā parikīrtitā || 42 ||
avibāgā parā śaktirādhārādhārasaṃjñitā|
kūrmamudrā tadunmeṣā nādātmānantamudrikā || 43 ||
bhāvanīyamidaṃ śaśvaditthamāsanakarmaṇi|
karadvayena badhnīyāllagnamuṣṭidvayaṃ puraḥ || 44 ||
aṅgulītritayenaiva aṅguṣṭhau tarjanīdvayam|
prāntalagnaṃ tu tat kuryāttadyugaṃ melayet punaḥ || 45 ||
eṣā sā pārthivī mudrā sarvabhūtavidhāriṇī|
maṇibandhau tu saṃlagnau nakhāgrāṇi karadvaye || 46 ||
kāryāṇi sāṅgulīkāni parasparamukhāni tu|
aṅguṣṭhāgre nirādhāre tanmadhye cālayed drutam || 47 ||
madye kuryācca karayoragādhaṃ suṣiropamam|
kṣīrārṇavasya mudraiṣā pādmī pūrvaṃ pradarśitā || 48 ||
karadvayamasaṃlagnaṃ kṛtvā tadanu yojayet|
mukhe mukhaṃ tu tarjanyorevaṃ madhyamayoḥ kramāt || 49 ||
anāmayostataḥ paścādvaktre vaktraṃ kaniṣṭhayoḥ|
aṅguṣṭhayugalaṃ tattadaṅgulīmukhayornyaset || 50 ||
etanmudrācatuṣkaṃ tu dharmādye tu catuṣṭaye|
adharmādicatuṣkāṇāṃ tadvanmudrācatuṣṭayam || 51 ||
tadūrdhvasthasya padmasya mudrā pādmī puroditā|
dakṣiṇasya tu hastasya tarjanyaṅguṣṭhamelanam || 52 ||
kṛtvā tadanu tadbandhaṃ vikāsya ca śanaiḥ śanaiḥ|
samuttānaṃ punaḥ kuryācchākhāsaṃghaṃ pṛthak pṛthak || 53 ||
dhāmatrayasya mudraiṣā cidbhāsanagatāṃ śṛṇu|
sphuṭau prasāritau hastau kuryādañjalirūpakau || 54 ||
cidbhāsanasya mudraiṣā śuddhasattvamayī parā|
dvātriṃśaditi mudrāṇāṃ sarvadoṣavināśinī || 55 ||
bhūṣaṇaāsrāsanādīnāṃ tava śakra pradarśitā|
kṣetreśvarādimudrāṇāmidānīṃ daśakaṃ śṛṇu || 56 ||
grastamaṅgulisaṃghātaṃ kṛtvā pāṇidvayena tu|
balātsaṃpīḍayet kurvannaṅguṣṭhadvayamucchritam || 57 ||
mudreyaṃ kṣetrapālasya sarvaduṣṭanibarhaṇī|
uttānau tu karau kṛtvā nikaṭasthau puraṃdara || 58 ||
aṅgulīnāṃ gaṇaṃ sarvaṃ kuñcitaṃ madhyasaṃsthitam|
aṅguṣṭhau patitau kṛtvā kramaśaḥ sphuṭatāṃ nayet || 59 ||
śrībījasya tu mudraiṣā prathamaṃ kathitā tava|
samutthāpya karādvāmāttarjanīṃ caṇḍabījajām || 60 ||
tāmeva dakṣiṇāddhastāt pracaṇḍasya nidarśayet|
madhyamāṃ vāmahastādvai samutthāpya jayasya sā || 61 ||
dakṣiṇād vijayākhyasya bījasya parikīrtitā|
vāmāccānāmikāṃ prāgvatkṛtvā gāṅgasya viddhi tām || 62 ||
dakṣiṇādyāmunasyoktā mudrā bījasya vāsava|
mudrā śaṅkhanidheḥ proktā vāmahastāt kaniṣṭhikā || 63 ||
tathā padmanidherhastād dakṣiṇāt sā kaniṣṭhikā|
dakṣiṇena tu hastena sāṅguṣṭhena tu muṣṭinā || 64 ||
pradeśinīmanāmāṃ ca vāmahastasya pīḍayet|
prayatnīkṛtaśākhānāṃ pṛṣṭhe yojyātha madhyamā || 65 ||
lambamānakarākārā yathā saṃdṛśyate ca sā|
muṣṭernātisamīpasthāṃ vāmahastāt kaniṣṭhikām || 66 ||
dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭhapārśvena daṃṣṭrāvat paribhāvayet|
īṣattiryaggatispaṣṭau vāmo'ṅguṣṭhastathā paraḥ || 67 ||
yathā tau paridṛśyete gajakarṇopamau śubhau|
gaṇeśvarasya mudraiṣā sarvavighnakṣayaṃkarī || 68 ||
saṃśliṣṭau maṇibandhau tu kṛtvā pāṇidvaye purā|
saṃlagnamagradeśāttu pronnataṃ madhyamāyugam || 69 ||
pradeśinīyugaṃ tadvattathaivānāmikādvayam|
aṅguṣṭhaṃ dviguṇīkṛtya namayettadadhomukham || 70 ||
śanaiḥ śanaiḥ spṛśedyāvat svaṃ svaṃ pāṇidvayītalam|
sphuṭaṃ suviralaṃ kuryādaṅguṣṭhadvitayaṃ tathā || 71 ||
kaniṣṭhikādvayaṃ caiva samena dharaṇena tu|
iyaṃ vāgīśvarī mudrā vāṇīvibhavadāyinī || 72 ||
saṃmukhau saṃpuṭīkṛtya dvau hastau saṃprasāritau|
viniyojyau lalāṭāgre śirasāvanatena tu || 73 ||
gurvāditritayasyaiṣā mudrā cetaḥprasādinī|
prottānaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ pāṇiṃ kṛtvāṅguligaṇaṃ tataḥ || 74 ||
saṃlagnaṃ kuñcayet kiṃcidaṅguṣṭhaṃ saṃprasārya ca|
tiyak śanaiḥ śanaiḥ kiṃcit kuryāccādhomukhaṃ tataḥ || 75 ||
mudrā pitṛgaṇasyaiṣā nityatṛptikarī smṛtā|
iyaṃ śrāddhasahasrebhyaḥ pitṛprītikarī sadā || 76 ||
darśanīyā prayatnena pitṝṇāṃ pūjane sadā|
karadvayaṃ samuttānaṃ nābhideśe niyojayet || 77 ||
vāmasya dakṣiṇaṃ pṛṣṭhe mudraiṣā siddhasaṃsadaḥ|
suspṛṣṭaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ hastamātmanastu parāṅmukham || 78 ||
parāṅmukhaṃ lambamānaṃ vāmapāṇiṃ prakalpayet|
varābhayadamudre dve lokeśānāmime smṛte || 79 ||
ekaikena tu mantreṇa vajrādyeva kramādyutam|
asrākhyāṃ śaktisaṃyuktāṃ prāguktāṃ saṃpradarśayet || 80 ||
lokapālāyudhānāṃ tu pūjitānāṃ kramādiha|
vāmahastakaniṣṭhādyāstisraḥ svatalamadhyagāḥ || 81 ||
tāsāmaṅguṣṭhataḥ pṛṣṭhe tarjatī pronnatā bhavet|
nāsāvaṃśapradeśasthā tato dakṣiṇapāṇinā || 82 ||
aṅgulītritayenaiva muṣṭiṃ baddhā tu pūrvavat|
tarjanīṃ dviguṇīkṛtya tvaṅguṣṭhāgre niyojayet || 83 ||
prodyato dakṣiṇo bāhuścakrakṣepe yathodyataḥ|
viṣvaksenasya mudreyaṃ viśvabandhanakṛntatī || 84 ||
kiṃcidākuñcayeddhastaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ hṛdayopagam|
aṅguṣṭhau viralau spaṣṭau mudrā hyāvāhane smṛtā || 85 ||
khaḍgadhārāsamākārau viralāṅgulikāvubhau|
aṅguṣṭhau daṇḍavat kṛtvā muṣṭibandhaṃ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ || 86 ||
kuryāt kaniṣṭhikādibhyo mudraiṣā syādvisarjane|
prasṛtau dvau karau kṛtvā suśliṣṭau cāpyadhomukhau || 87 ||
kanīyasyau tathāṅguṣṭhau suśliṣṭau ca niyojya ca|
madhyamāṅguliyugmaṃ cāpyanyonyakarapṛṣṭhagam || 88 ||
prakṣipyānāmikāyugmaṃ tarjanīyugalaṃ tathā|
mudraiṣā kāmadhenvākhyā sarvecchāparipūraṇī || 89 ||
dviprakāraṃ tu mudrāṇāṃ prayogaṃ viddhi vāsava|
adhyātmaṃ saṃvidākāraṃ bāhyaṃ vākkarmacittajam || 90 ||
anena vidhinā mudrāṃ yo badhnāti vidhānavit|
tenedaṃ mudritaṃ sarvamapunarbhavasiddhaye || 91 ||
iti mudrāgaṇaḥ sarvastavoddiṣṭaḥ puraṃdara|
ārādhanādhikārārthaṃ śṛṇu snānavidhiṃ param || 92 ||
antarbahirmalopetamalakṣmīḥ pratipadyate|
tasyā nivāraṇārthāya snānaṃ sarvatra śiṣyate || 93 ||
tat punasrividhaṃ snānaṃ jalamantrasmṛtikramāt|
trividhaṃ puruhūtaitat smṛtaṃ śataguṇottaram || 94 ||
puṣkarādiṣu tīrtheṣu yat snānaṃ jalajaṃ smṛtam|
tataḥ śataguṇaṃ snānaṃ bhagavacchāsracoditam || 95 ||
tasmācchataguṇaṃ māntraṃ mantrāṅganyāsasaṃbhavam|
tasmācchataguṇaṃ dhyānaṃ śuddhasaṃvinmayaṃ param || 96 ||
ādau sāmānyavidhinā jalasnānaṃ samācaret|
viśeṣavidhinā pascādviśeṣasya vidhistvayam || 97 ||
pūrvaṃ snātvā mṛdāmbhobhiḥ paścādgandhādilepanapt|
snānaṃ syāttanmaladhvaṃsi prāṇāyāmaiḥ samācaret || 98 ||
dvādaśāvṛttayā kuryāt pūraṇaṃ tārayā purā|
dhārayet ṣoḍaśāvṛttyā dviṣaṭkena punastyajet || 99 ||
savyadakṣiṇaparyāyai recanāntamarutkramaiḥ|
malān kṣapayati prāṇanāḍīsthān pūrvasaṃcitān || 100 ||
tatastattvamayo bhūtvā malinaṃ bhūtapiṇḍakam|
ṣāṭkośikamasāraṃ ca śodhayeddhāraṇāvaśāt || 101 ||
pṛthivyādīni sarvāṇi tattvāni svasvakāraṇe|
dhāraṇābhirnayedastamavyaktāntāni vai kramāt || 102 ||
kṣīre kṣīramivātmānaṃ mayi saṃmiśrayettataḥ|
bhūtvā lakṣmīmayaḥ paścādbhavennārāyaṇātmakaḥ || 103 ||
dhāraṇābandhamāsādya śuddhasattvena cetasā|
paramātmātmakattvaṃ yat sā śaktiḥ paramā matā || 104 ||
tatstha eva svakaṃ piṇḍaṃ saṃdaheddhāraṇāgninā|
cidāśuśukṣaṇestejaḥ puñjamarciḥkaṇālayam || 105 ||
patantaṃ saṃsmarenmūrdhni piṇḍaṃ prajvalitaṃ tataḥ|
śāntamantaḥsthasatsattvaṃ bhasmībhūtarajastamam || 106 ||
rajastamomayaṃ bhasma tvapohyodbodhavāyunā|
cidānandamahāmbhodherataraṅgaguṇākarāt || 107 ||
prasṛtaṃ sṛṣṭimārgeṇa saṃsmaredamṛtodakam|
tenāpyāyitamantaḥsthaṃ tatsattvaṃ dehatāṃ nayet || 108 ||
śuddhaṃ tatsṛṣṭimārgeṇa saṃśrayedbhautikaṃ vapuḥ|
antaḥśuddhiriyaṃ proktā bāhyaśuddhimatho śrṛṇu || 109 ||
vakṣyamāṇakramairaṅgairmantranyāsaṃ samācaret|
bahiḥ śuddhirbhavedevaṃ tadā snānaṃ samācaret || 110 ||
pavitrapāṇirādāya mṛtkalāṃ mantramantritām|
tāṃ tridhā vāmahastāgre mūlamadhyāgrato nyaset || 111 ||
bodhaśaktyātmanā pūrvaṃ tīrthaśuddhiṃ samācaret|
tīrthaṃ tat trividhaṃ proktaṃ sthūlasūkṣmaparātmanā || 112 ||
tarpayatyakhilaṃ sthūlaṃ sthūlarūpeṇa tajjagat|
sattvātmanā tu sūkṣmeṇa sasurāṃstarpayetpitṝn || 113 ||
pareṇānandarūpeṇa nayenmatkarmayogyatām|
tasmācchuddhiḥ purā kāryā tīrthe śāsradṛśā svayam || 114 ||
jñānadhāraṇayākṛṣya tīrthasattāṃ tu vaiṣṇavīm|
mayi śaktau layaṃ nītvā cidānandamahāspade || 115 ||
sthūlaṃ jñānāgninā dagdhvā brahmānandena pūrayet|
jñānaśaktyāvaropyātha tīrthe sattvaṃ tu vaiṣṇavam || 116 ||
prathame madhyame'thānte mṛdbhāge kramaśaḥ sudhīḥ|
asraṃ ca mūlamantraṃ cāpyaṅgamantraṃ ca saṃsmaret || 117 ||
daśa dikṣu kṣipedasraṃ sarvavighnopaśāntaye|
mūlamantrānvitaṃ bhāgaṃ tīrthamadhye vinikṣipet || 118 ||
saṃnidhānaṃ bhavettena mantramūrterma kṣaṇāt|
mṛdbhāgamāṅgamantraṃ yattenāṅgāni vilepayet || 119 ||
jalamadhyaṃ samāviśya nimajjyonmajjya vai punaḥ|
karābhyāmasrajaptāmbhaḥ pūrvaṃ mūrdhni vinikṣipet || 120 ||
dvitīyaṃ mūlamantreṇa tṛtīyaṃ cāṅgamantrakaiḥ|
tatastīraṃ samāsādya samyagācamya vai tataḥ || 121 ||
snānaṃ samācarenmantrairyathāvannyāsakarmaṇaā|
dhyānasnānaṃ tataḥ kuryāt sāvadhānena cetasā || 122 ||
svasthitaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ smṛtvā lakṣmīdharaṃ param|
tatpādodakajāṃ dhārāṃ saṃsmarecchirasi cyutām || 123 ||
tayā saṃplāvayedantarbahiśca sakalaṃ vapuḥ|
ekaikaśo dviśo vāpi triśo vāpi samāhitaḥ || 124 ||
yathāśakti yathākālaṃ trividhaṃ snānamācaret|
praṇavādyairnamo'ntaiśca nāmabhirmanmayān surān || 125 ||
ṛṣīṃśca tarpayitvātha svadhānte tarpayet pitṝn|
evaṃ snānaṃ vidhāyātha tīrthasthaṃ mantranāyakam || 126 ||
ākṛṣya pūrakeṇātha saṃsmareddhṛdayāntare|
ākṛṣya manasāśraṃ ca digvidiksthāpitaṃ purā || 127 ||
yāgāṅgāni samādāya prayāyādyāgamandiram|
svayaṃ siddhamathārṣaṃ vā siddhairvā parikalpitam || 128 ||
manuṣyaiḥ kalpitaṃ vātha manmayairbhāvitāntaraiḥ|
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃpūrṇaṃ vimānaṃ pāñcarātrikam || 129 ||
śubhaṃ kamalinītīraṃ vijanaṃ vā manoharam|
yadvā viviktamudyānaṃ puṇyavṛkṣopaśobitam || 130 ||
pulinaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ vā sikatopari vistṛtam|
avātamajanasparśamasamīpasthadoṣavat || 131 ||
yatra vā rocate cittaṃ tatra yāyānmanovaśāt|
hṛnmadhyasthe pare mantre prabuddhānandavigrahe || 132 ||
digantaramapaśyan vai maunī saṃrodhitānilaḥ|
prāpya sthānaṃ śubhaṃ tatra nāsāgreṇa virecayet || 133 ||
manmantraṃ paramātmānaṃ pradīptānalavigraham|
bahirasraṃ ca vinyasya caraṇenāhanet kṣitim || 134 ||
smaran mantramayīṃ lakṣmīṃ māmekāṃ parameśvarīm|
ekāntadeśamāsādya badhnīyādrucirāsanam || 135 ||
darbhe carmaṇi vasre vā phalake yajñakāṣṭhaje|
abhivandya hariṃ māṃ ca bhaktyaiva gurusaṃtatim || 136 ||
gṛhītvā mānasīmājñāṃ tebhyastu śirasā nataḥ|
mānasīṃ nirvapet sarvāṃ kriyāṃ jñānasamādhinā || 137 ||
jñānena kriyate yadyat karma brahmasamādhinā|
śuddhasattvamayaṃ tattadakṣayaṃ bhavati dhruvam || 138 ||
bāhyadravyāśritā yasmād doṣā rājasatāmasāḥ|
tatastacchodhanamapi karmaṇā manasā girā || 139 ||
tasmādekāntanirdoṣaṃ bhāvanāvāsitaṃ tathā|
tasmājjñānaṃ samāsthāya śuddhaṃ saṃvitsamudbhavam || 140 ||
jñānabhāvanayā karma kuryādvai pāramārthikam|
iti snānavidhiḥ samyak kīrtitaste sureśvara|
aṅganyāsādikaṃ sthānaṃ tava vakṣyāmyataḥ param || 141 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre snānavidhiprakāśo nāma catusriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
iti catusriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

<35>
pañcatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ
antaryāgādisiddhyarthaṃ bhūtaśuddhiṃ niśāmaya|
kathyamānaṃ mayā śakra sāvadhānena cetasā || 1 ||
bhūtaśuddhirnāma sādhakasya prākṛtadehasthānāṃ māṃsamedo'sthibhūyiṣṭhānāṃ bhūtānāṃ śuddhīkaraṇam| tacca bhagavadārādhanayogyatāsaṃpattyai| tatprakāraśca prākṛtānāṃ teṣāṃ svasvakāraṇeṣu layacintanapūrvakaṃ bhagavati samarpaṇaṃ kṛtvā punastatsakāśāt bhagavanmayāprākṛtabhūtāvirbhāvapūrvakaṃ tadārabdhadehāvasthitibhāvanam| eṣā ca bhūtaśuddhiḥ sarvairapi tāntrikaiḥ svasvasamayānusāreṇa svasvatantreṣu pratipāditā dṛsyate|
prakṛtyantasya pṛthvyādeḥ kādibhāntatayaiva ca|
manmayīkaraṇaṃ buddhyā bhūtaśuddhirihocyate || 2 ||
kādi bhāntamiti| kakāreṇa pṛthivī bhakāreṇa prakṛtiśca gṛhyate|
pṛthivyādi prakṛtyantaṃ yat prakṛtyaṣṭakaṃ sthitam|
sthūlasūkṣmavibhedena tatra rūpadvayaṃ viduḥ || 3 ||
cakṣurgocarasaṃsthānaṃ sthūlarūpaṃ tu varṇyate|
kāraṇākāratā yatra tattu tanmātramucyate || 4 ||
sthūlasūkṣmavibhedena tattvametad dviraṣṭakam|
viṣayendriyavṛttīśca tatra tatra niveśayet || 5 ||
ghrāṇādi pāyūpasthādi gandhādīti trayaṃ trayam|
tanmātravarge pṛthvyādau prātilomyācchamaṃ nayet || 6 ||
taraṅgā jaladhau yadvadastaṃ yānti samīraṇāt|
viṣayendriyakallolā mahābhūtamahodadhau || 7 ||
pūrvoktaṃ layaṃ nidarśanamukhenāha taraṅgā iti|
susamīcīnayā buddhyā tadvadastaṃ nayed budhaḥ|
mano'bhimāna ityetāvahaṃkāre śamaṃ nayet || 8 ||
prāṇamadhyavasāyaṃ ca buddhitattve nibarhayet|
sattvaṃ rajastamaścaiva traiguṇyaṃ prakṛtau nayet || 9 ||
yattat traiguṇyamavyaktaṃ kāraṇaṃ mahadādike|
traiguṇyaṃ ca prasūtiśca māyetyetat tridhā tu tat || 10 ||
traiguṇyamiti| eṣāṃ krameṇa pradhānamavyaktaṃ tama ityaupaniṣadau vyavahāraḥ|
evaṃ tattvavidāṃ jñeyā etāḥ prakṛtayo daśa|
sthūlasūkṣmavibedena tāḥ punarviṃśatiḥ smṛtāḥ || 11 ||
sthūlānāṃ prakṛtīnāṃ tu daśa mantrā ime smṛtāḥ|
tattatsaṃjñā huṃphaḍantāstārikādyā dhruvādikāḥ || 12 ||
tattatsaṃjñā iti| ādau praṇavaḥ, tatastārikā, tato bhūtasaṃjñāḥ, tato huṃ phaṭ| āhatya oṃ hnīṃ pṛthivyai huṃ phaṭ iti mantraḥ| jayākhye tu tārikāsthāne mantrāntarāṇyuktāni|
māṃsaṃ medastathā smṛtvā raso vyomākṣaratrayam|
parāt paradvayaṃ caiva bindunādavibhūṣitam || 13 ||
daśānāṃ sūkṣmarūpāṇāṃ śaktyādyāstā nava smṛtāḥ|
deheṣu jīvabhūtā yāḥ śaktayaḥ parasaṃjñitāḥ || 14 ||
māyāvyomayutānetān śaktyādīṃstadanu smaret|
nivṛttiśca pratiṣṭhā ca vidyā śāntistathaiva ca || 15 ||
jīvaśaktīnāṃ nāmānyāha nivṛttirityādi|
śāntyatītābhimānā ca prāṇā guṇavatī tathā|
guṇasūkṣmā nirguṇā ca etāḥ saṃjñāḥ kramāt smṛtāḥ || 16 ||
bījānāṃ daśake tasmin vahniviṣṇvardhacandrakān|
saṃyojya mantrān jānīyādadhiṣṭhātrīgatānimān || 17 ||
vahnītyādi| rīṃ ityetat saṃyojyetyarthaḥ|
gandhaśrīśca rasaśrīśca rūpaśrīḥ sparśapūrvikā|
śabdaśrīrabhimānaśrīḥ prāṇaśrīrguṇapūvikā || 18 ||
gandhaśrīrityādīni śaktināmāni|
tathaiva guṇasūkṣmaśrīrmāyāśrīriti saṃjñayā|
adhiṣṭhātryo'pi vartante śaktayo daśake mama || 19 ||
evaṃ parikaraṃ buddhvā bhūtaśuddhiṃ samācaret|
sthānaśuddhiṃ purā kuryādyathā tadavadhāraya || 20 ||
pureti| bhūtaśuddheḥ pūrvamityarthaḥ|
kālāgnyarkasahasrābhāṃ nirdhūmāṅgārasaṃnibhām|
māṃ smṛtvā manmukhotthena vahninā nirdahedbhuvam || 21 ||
somāyutābhamadvaktrajenāsiñcedathāmbunā|
sthānaśuddhirbhavedevaṃ bhūtaśuddhimatho śṛṇu || 22 ||
evaṃ nirdagdhasya sthānasya punarujjīvanamāha sometyādi|
caturaśrāṃ samāṃ pītāṃ vajracihnāṃ vasuṃdharām|
mantreṇākṛṣya dehāntaḥ svasthānasthāṃ layaṃ nayet || 23 ||
mantreṇeti| atra mantraḥ ślāṃ iti jayākhye uktaḥ.
gandhamātre tatastacca svabījenāstamānayet|
mayyadhiṣṭhānabhūtāyāṃ māṃ ca bāhyāmbhasi kṣipet || 24 ||
mantreṇārdhendusaṃkāśaṃ padmāṅkaṃ tacca daivatm|
svasthāne vilayaṃ nītvā rasamātre svamantrataḥ || 25 ||
mantreṇa taṃ pratiṣṭhāpya tacca mayyānayellayam|
māṃ ca bāhye kṣipedvahnāvāmāyaṃ tatkramastvayam || 26 ||
mantreṇeti| ṣvāṃ iti jayākhye mantra uktaḥ| āmāyamiti| māyāparyantamityarthaḥ|
sarvatra naikaṃ budhyeta macchaktervilayaṃ budhaḥ|
yathā hi sarpirāsiñcet kṣīre tanmathanodbhavam || 27 ||
naikamiti| layaṃ sarvatraikavidhaṃ na budhyetetyanvayaḥ| tadeva nidarśanamukhenāha yathā hīti| tanmathanodbhavamiti sarpirviśeṣaṇam|
sarpiranyatra ca kṣīre tatsarpiṣyapi cānyakam|
evamā prakṛteḥ śaktīradhiṣṭhātrīḥ smared budhaḥ || 28 ||
evaṃ māṃ paramāṃ śaktiṃ navaśaktisamanvitām|
nirguṇāntavidhāṃ dhyātāṃ māyādhiṣṭhānakāriṇīm || 29 ||
daśamīṃ tatparaṃ nītvā śaktimekādaśīṃ sthitām|
mahākṣobhamayīṃ lakṣmīṃ vyūhābhyudayarūpiṇīm || 30 ||
ekādaśīṃ ca tāṃ nītvā dvādaśīṃ paramātmikām|
anirdeśyāmanaupamyāṃ dvādaśīṃ tāṃ mayi kṣipet || 31 ||
evaṃ tāṃ paramāṃ śaktiṃ dvādaśīmaśilātmikām|
dvādaśāntāntamunnīya varṇamayyāṃ mayi smaret || 32 ||
dvādaśānteti| mūrdhna upari māyāto dvādaśāṅgulādūrdhvaṃ sthānaṃ dvādaśāntamiti sāṃpradāyiko vyavahāraḥ|
agnīṣomārkakoṭyābhā sarvato'kṣiśiromukhī|
dhārāsaṃtānarūpā me sūkṣmā varṇamayī tanuḥ || 33 ||
varṇamayīṃ tanuṃ viśiniṣṭi agnītyādi| dhārāsaṃtāneti| amṛtadhārāsaṃtānarūpetyarthaḥ|
sarvajīvopakārāya sarvasaṃbhārasaṃbhṛtā|
uditā sā purā viṣṇormeghādvidyudivojjvalā || 34 ||
śakraḥ
kāni sthānāni dehe'smin yatra kāryo layaḥ kramāt|
kīdṛśāni ca bimbāni bhūmyādīnāṃ vadāmbuje || 35 ||
śrīḥ
ā jānuto bhuvaḥ sthānamā kaṭyāḥ payasaḥ smṛtam|
ā nābhestejasaḥ sthānaṃ vāyoḥ sthānaṃ tadā hṛdaḥ || 36 ||
ā karṇānnabhasaḥ sthānamā bilāccāpyahaṃkṛteḥ|
ā bhruvormahataḥ sthānamākāśe tu paraṃ smṛtam || 37 ||
tataḥ prādeśamātrāgre mūrdhataścaturaṅgule|
sthāne prakṛtyāḥ śeṣaṃ tadaṅgulīnāṃ dviraṣṭakam || 38 ||
prakṛtyāḥ sthānamākāśe āha tata iti| mūrdhnaścaturaṅgulordhvabhāge prakṛtyā avasthānam| tataḥ ṣoḍaśāṅgulopari bhāge avyaktasyāvasthānam|
ekādaśyāṃ dviṣaṭkāyāṃ sthānaṃ tattvakṣaraśriyaḥ|
caturaśraṃ bhavedbimbaṃ vajrāṅkaṃ pārthivaṃ mahat || 39 ||
tata ekādaśāṅgulopari bhāge māyāsthānam| tato dvādaśāṅgulopari bhāge śabdabrahmasthānamiti vivekaḥ| idameva dvādaśāntamityucyate|
ardhendusadṛśaṃ śuklaṃ padmāṅkaṃ payasaḥ smṛtam|
trikoṇaṃ svastikāṅkaṃ ca raktaṃ taijasamucyate || 40 ||
dhūmraṃ ṣaḍbindusaṃyuktaṃ vṛttaṃ vāyavyamucyate|
añjanābhaṃ tathākāśaṃ bimbamātraṃ smṛtaṃ param || 41 ||
bimbamātramiti| pradhānādīnāṃ bimbamātrameva| na varṇādikamityarthaḥ|
evaṃ tattvopasaṃhāre kṛte hṛtkuharodgatam|
jñānarajjvavalambaṃ ca suṣumnāmadhyamānugapt || 42 ||
ūrdhvamājānumunnīya śaktisopānapaṅktibhiḥ|
dvādaśāntāntarājīvaṃ madhyasthāyāṃ mayi kṣipet || 43 ||
mayi kṣipediti| dehasya dahanacintane tadantaḥsthajīvasyāpi dahancintanaprasaktau tannivāraṇāya jīvasya devyāṃ nikṣepa ucyate|
tadante ca mahāpadmaṃ sahasradalasaṃyutam|
sūryakoṭisahasrābhamindukoṭyayutaprabham || 44 ||
tadante dvādaśānte| mahāpadmamiti| astīti śeṣaḥ|
agnīṣomamayāntaḥsthā mahānandamayī tanuḥ|
anirdeśyopamā saṃvinmayī sā māmikā parā || 45 ||
aṃśataḥ prasarantyasyā jīvānandā saridvarā|
svānandamenamānīya mahānandamayīṃ nayet || 46 ||
asyāḥ; mama tanvāḥ|
tato lavaṇakūṭābhaṃ piṇḍamasmanmukhodgataiḥ|
mahājvālairmahāvegaiścinmayaiḥ parito dahet || 47 ||
jīve dehānnirgamathya dvādaśāntaṃ prāpite sati, dehasya dahanacintanamucyate tata ityādinā| lavaṇakūṭābhamiti nirgamitajīvasya dehasya nirdeśaḥ|
yuktaḥ sarasakaḥ ṣaṣṭho bindumān dāhapāvakaḥ|
tārikānatimadhyastho vijñeyaḥ śāsracakṣuṣā || 48 ||
dāhakāgnimantra ucyate| hrīṃ rthūṃ nama iti|
somamayyā mamāsyotthaiḥ pīyūṣaiḥ plāvayettataḥ|
candrī sūkṣmastu savyomā piṇḍasyāpyāyane smṛtaḥ || 49 ||
punaḥ aprākṛtadehasaṃpatticintanamāha somamayyā iti| tatra mantraḥ ṭhyaṃ iti| jayākhye tu vsaṃ ityuktaḥ |
sisṛkṣayā mayodyatyā saṃvitprāṇopagūḍhayā|
preritāstāḥ smarecchaktīrvarṇamayyāṃ mayi sthitāḥ || 50 ||
tatastābhiḥ svaśaktībhiścodanādvārapūrvakam|
māyādi kṣitiparyantaṃ nirmitaṃ saṃsmaret kramāt || 51 ||
māyā pradhānasya susūkṣmāvasthā; yāṃ tama ityaupaniṣadā vadanti|
tataḥ piṇḍasamutpattiṃ karaṇavyañjanojjvalām|
evaṃ piṇḍaṃ samutpādya śuddhalakṣmīmayaṃ mahat || 52 ||
pūrvoktamārgeṇa tato hṛdayaṃ jīvamānayet|
piṇḍabhūtāsrayo varṇā analaḥ somacandriṇau || 53 ||
jīvamantramāha piṇḍabhūtā iti| rṭsa iti jātaḥ| jayākhye'pyevamevoktaḥ|
tārikānatimadyasthā jīvamantra udāhṛtaḥ|
viśuddhavigrahastvevaṃ mantranyāsaṃ samācaret || 54 ||
adhikārāya pūjāyāṃ pūjakānāṃ mude'pi ca|
nibarhaṇāya daityādevignānāṃ vijayāya ca || 55 ||
kṣitāvupari vinyastaṃ yat purā phalakādikam|
svena svena tu mantreṇa taṃ mantrī pañcadhā smaret || 56 ||
ādhāraśaktikūrmorvīdugdhābdhyambujarūpataḥ|
tārkṣyaṃ tatra sthitaṃ dhyāyet kharvabījātmanā sthitam || 57 ||
kharvabījamiti| khamityākārakamityarthaḥ|
tatropaviśya lakṣmīśaṃ rūpaṃ svamanucintya ca|
diśo nibadhya cāsreṇa punarevaṃ muhurmuhuḥ || 58 ||
svaṃ rūpamiti| "ātmeti tūpagacchanti" ityuktarītyā svātmakamityarthaḥ|
śarajālopamaṃ kṛtvā prākāraṃ cāsanādbahiḥ|
saprākāraṃ tu tat sthānaṃ kavacenāvakuṇṭhayet || 59 ||
asreṇa; asramantreṇa vīryāyāsrāya phaḍityanena| evaṃ kavacena; kavacamantreṇa balāya kavacāya humityanena|
gaganasthairadṛśyaṃ syādyathā nyāsaṃ samācaret|
karanyāsaṃ purā kṛtvā dehanyāsaṃ samācaret || 60 ||
aṅguṣṭhe tārikāṃ nyasyettacchaktīraṅgulīṣu tu|
tarjanyāṃ tu nyasellakṣmīṃ madhyāyāṃ kītimapyatha || 61 ||
anāmāyāṃ jayāṃ māyāṃ kaniṣṭhāyāṃ tato nyaset|
satāratārikāsaṃjñānatyantastanmanukramaḥ || 62 ||
satāretyādi| oṃ hrīṃ lakṣmyai tarjanībhyāṃ namaḥ ityādi prakāreṇetyarthaḥ|
kaniṣṭhikādyāsu tato hṛdayādīnyanukramāt|
aṅgāni pañca vinyasya netramaṅgeṣu saṃsmaret || 63 ||
kaustubhaṃ dakṣiṇatale vanamālāṃ tathāpare|
dakṣiṇe madhyataḥ padmaṃ nyasedvāmatale'pi ca || 64 ||
atha karanyāsaḥ kaustubhamityādinā| madhyataḥ; tala ityarthaḥ|
aḍkuśaṃ dakṣiṇe nyasyet pāśaṃ vāmatale tathā|
anena vidhinā pūrvaṃ hastanyāsaṃ samācaret || 65 ||
tatastu vigrahanyāsaṃ yathā kuryāttathā śṛṇu|
ā mūrdhnaścaraṇāntaṃ cācaraṇācca śiro'vadhi || 66 ||
mūrdhānamārabhya caraṇaparyantaṃ caraṇamārabhya mūrdhaparyantaṃ ca dehanyāsamāha ā mūrdhna iti|
tārakaṃ tārikāṃ caiva tau ca candrātapopamau|
vāmaskandhe tato lakṣmīṃ nyasetkīrtiṃ ca dakṣiṇe || 67 ||
jayāṃ dakṣiṇapāṇisthāṃ māyā vāmakarasthitām|
nāsāntare ca hṛnmantraṃ śiromantraṃ ca mūrdhani || 68 ||
śikhāṃ caiva śikhāsthāne skandhayoḥ kavacaṃ tataḥ|
vinyasya netrayonetramaśraṃ pāṇitaladvaye || 69 ||
nābhau pṛṣṭe karadvandva ūrujānupadeṣu ca|
nyaset samyagupāṅgāni triyugaṃ mantravittamaḥ || 70 ||
vakṣaḥsthaṃ kaustubhaṃ madhye kaṇṭhe ca vanamālikām|
paṅkaje karayornyasya mantramāvartayan dvidhā || 71 ||
vāmahaste tataḥ pāśamaṅkuśaṃ dakṣiṇe kare|
pādayostārikāṃ sthūlāṃ sūkṣmāṃ caivopasaṃdhike || 72 ||
caramāṃ brahmarandhre tu mantrāṇāṃ nāyikā hi sā|
candrikātapasaṃkāśaṃ nyāse nyāse manuṃ smaret || 73 ||
manasā bhāvayenmudrāstatra tatra ca sādhakaḥ|
tārikā yā parā devī tayā vyāptānanusmaret || 74 ||
vinyastān sakalān mantrān sarvataḥ saṃplutodavat|
evaṃ kṛte'nusaṃdhāne mantrāṇāṃ bhinnavartmanām || 75 ||
apāmivodadhisthānāmekīkāraḥ prajāyate|
evaṃ nyāse kṛte mantrī sākṣāllakṣmīmayo bhavet || 76 ||
sarvādhikārabhāgī syādāśritya dhyānajaṃ balam|
dhyāyinaḥ sarvasiddhīnāmāvirbhāvaśca jāyate || 77 ||
nyastāṅgo nirbhayastiṣṭheddeśe duṣṭasamākule|
vijayetāpamṛtyūṃśca sarvāṃścaivaupasargikān || 78 ||
nyastāṅgo vidhivanmantrī yathāśāsreṇa cetasā|
tanuṃ mantramayīṃ tāṃ tu māṃ dhyāyet parameśvarīm || 79 ||
ahaṃ sa bhagavān viṣṇurahaṃ lakṣmīḥ sanātanī|
ityevaṃbhāvavān yogī bhūyo naiva prajāyate || 80 ||
itīyaṃ bhūtaśuddhiste yathāvacchakra varṇitā|
antaryāgamatho vakṣye tattvatastanniśāmaya || 81 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre bhūtaśuddhiprakāśo nāma pañcatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
iti pañcatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

<36>
ṣaṭtriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ
brahmānandamayī śakra bhogairyat pāramārthikaiḥ|
ijyeyaṃ hṛdayāntaḥsthā so'ntaryāga iti smṛtaḥ || 1 ||
padmaṃ vā svastikaṃ vāpi baddhvā yonyāsanaṃ tu vā|
nābhimeḍhrāntare dhyāyecchaktimādhārarūpiṇīm || 2 ||
ādhāraśaktisthānamucyate nābhītyādinā|
devīṃ kenāpyanādeyāṃ nīrūpāṃ jyotirātmikām|
tadūrdhve kālakūrmaṃ tu vimalaṃ dīptavigraham || 3 ||
tadūrdhve iti| ādhāraśakterūrdhvabhāga ityarthaḥ|
kūrmākāraṃ paraṃ devaṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam|
tasya copari nāgendraṃ pūrṇacandranibhānanam || 4 ||
tasyeti| kūrmasyoparītyarthaḥ|
phaṇāsahasrasaṃpūrṇaṃ madāghūrṇitalocanam|
cakralāṅgalahastaṃ ca praṇamettaṃ parāt param || 5 ||
tadūrdhvaṃ vasudhāṃ devīṃ kuṅkumakṣodasaṃnibhām|
hemaratnavicitrāṅgīṃ prasannavadanekṣaṇām || 6 ||
tadūrdhvamiti| nāgendrāsanādūrdhvamityarthaḥ|
baddhāñjaliṃ śirodeśe saṃsmarantīṃ vibhuṃ smaret|
caturdhā bhājite kṣetre nābhimeḍhrāntarasthite || 7 ||
saṃsmarantīmiti| dhyāyantīmityarthaḥ|
evamādhāraśaktyādidevatāḥ saṃsmaredimāḥ|
nābhau kṣīrārṇavaṃ dhyāyet kundendudhavalākṛtim || 8 ||
srotoraśmibhirākīrṇaṃ pūrṇacandranibhānanam|
gambhīravigrahaṃ dhyāyedrūpavantamarūpiṇam || 9 ||
tataḥ samutthitaṃ padmaṃ dhyāyet kṣīrārṇavodarāt|
praśāntapāvakākāramudayādityavarcasam || 10 ||
lambodaraṃ hasantaṃ ca sitadantaṃ śubhānanam|
dvibhujaṃ veṣṭitaṃ śaśvacchubhairvividhaṣaṭpadaiḥ || 11 ||
sahasradalasaṃpannaṃ sahasrakiraṇāvṛtam|
sahasraraśmisaṃkāśaṃ tatpṛṣṭhe cāsanaṃ nyaset || 12 ||
tatpṛṣṭha iti| padmapṛṣṭha ityartaḥ| nyaset; nyastaṃ dhyāyedityarthaḥ|
dharmaṃ jñānaṃ ca vairāgyamaiśvaryaṃ ca kramādimān|
āsanasya smaret pādānāgneyādividiggatān || 13 ||
āsanasya pādāḥ dharmajñānādayaḥ āgneyādividikṣu jñeyāḥ|
puruṣākṛtayaḥ sarve sitāḥ siṃhānanā ime|
mahotsāhā mahāvīryā maddhāraṇakṛtodyamāḥ || 14 ||
pūrvādidiggatā jñeyā dharmādīnāṃ viparyayāḥ|
adharmaṃ ca tathājñānamavairāgyamanaisvaram || 15 ||
viparyayāḥ adharmājñānādayaḥ prāgādidikṣu|
puruṣākṛtayaśceme bandhūkakusumopamāḥ|
prāgīśānadiśormadhye prāgāgneyadigantare || 16 ||
yātavīvāruṇīmadhye pāśivāyudigantare|
ṛgvedādyaṃ catuṣkaṃ ca pītaṃ hayanarākṛti || 17 ||
yātavī nirṛtidik| pāśī varuṇaḥ|
īśānasomadiṅmadhye vahnyantakadigantare|
rākṣasāntakadiṅmadhye vāyusomadigantare || 18 ||
kṛtādyaṃ yugabṛndaṃ tu kṛṣṇaṃ vṛṣanarākṛti|
sarve caturbhujā ete dvābhyāṃ pīṭhadhṛtastathā || 19 ||
dvābhyāmañjalibaddhābhyāṃ praṇamantyāsanasthitām|
teṣāmupari saṃcintyaṃ pīṭhaṃ buddhimayaṃ param || 20 ||
dvābhyāmiti| bhujābhyāmiti śeṣaḥ| añjalibaddhābhyāmiti| baddhāñjalibhyāmityarthaḥ|
avyaktamambujaṃ śvetaṃ tadūrdhve'ṣṭadalaṃ smaret|
tadūrdhve sūryabimbaṃ tu sūryakoṭisamaprabham || 21 ||
tadūrdhve candrabimbaṃ tu candrakoṭisamaprabham|
tadūrdhve vahnibimbaṃ tu vahnikoṭyayutaprabham || 22 ||
tamo rajaśca sattvaṃ ca guṇānetān kramāt smaret|
praṇavasya nateścaiva tattatsaṃjñāstu madhyagāḥ || 23 ||
sūryenduvahnibimbāni kramāt tamorajaḥsattvamayāni jānīyāt| oṃ sūryabimbāya namaḥ ityādayo mantrāḥ|
buddhyādisattvaparyantatattvamantragaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ|
tataścidāsanaṃ dadyāt parāhaṃtāsvarūpakam || 24 ||
ā nābherhṛdayāntāttu pañcadhā bhājite pade|
samudrādyāsanāntaṃ tu caturbhiḥ kalpayet padaiḥ || 25 ||
nābhimārabhya hṛdayaparyantaṃ bhāgaṃ pañcadhā vibhajya tatrādau caturṣu bhāgeṣu kṣīrārṇavādyāsanāntaṃ bhāvayet|
ekena pañcamenaiva padmādyāsanakalpanam|
bhūtānyādhāraśaktau tu kūrme tanmātrakaṃ gaṇam || 26 ||
pañcame tu bhāge avyaktapadmaṃ bhāvayet| ukteṣu sthāneṣu padārthānāmavasthitimāha bhūtānīti| sthūlāni pañca bhūtānītyarthaḥ|
vāgādikaṃ tathānante bhuvi śrotrādipañcakam|
manaḥ kṣīrārṇave dhyāyedahaṃkāraṃ tato'mbuje || 27 ||
dviraṣṭakaṃ ca dharmādyamāsanaṃ cāpi dhīḥ smṛtā|
avyaktaṃ ca tadūrdhvasthamavadātaṃ saroruham || 28 ||
avadātaṃ saroruham; avyaktapadmamityarthaḥ|
tamaḥprabhṛtayaścoktā guṇā hyabjatrayātmakāḥ|
eke kālaṃ vadantyetad bhūtāditritayātmakam || 29 ||
etat; avyaktapadmamityarthaḥ| pārameśvare kālacakramityasya vyavahāraḥ|
cidāsanamanantākhyaṃ pumāṃsamapare jaguḥ|
eke cidāsanādūrdhvaṃ garuḍaṃ paricakṣate || 30 ||
kharvavyomānvitaṃ paścāt khagānananamoyutam|
mantramāhuḥ sureśāna tanmantraṃ navamaṃ budhāḥ || 31 ||
khaṃ khagānanāya namaḥ|
itthaṃ bahuvidhairmantrairāsane vihite kramāt|
viṣṇuṃ viśvātmakaṃ devaṃ nārāyaṇamanāmayam || 32 ||
bhāvayet paramātmānaṃ śaṅkhacakragadādharam|
caturbhujaṃ pītavasraṃ puṇḍarīkanibhekṣaṇam || 33 ||
"tasya yathā kapyāsaṃ puṇḍarīkamevamakṣiṇī" iti śrutyarthopabṛṃṇamatra|
uditaṃ saṃsmareddeva svaśakteḥ spandanātmanaḥ|
samyag dhyeyaṃ yathaitatte tathā bhūyo nibodha me || 34 ||
susamyaṅ nyastamantrāṅgaḥ susaṃklṛptāntarāsanaḥ|
darśitāśeṣamudraśca saṃsmarenmantravaibhavam || 35 ||
paraṃ brahma paraṃ dhāma yat pāre tamasaḥ sthitam|
śaktimacchaktibhāvena lakṣmīnārāyaṇaṃ mahaḥ || 36 ||
sarvaṃ sarvātigaṃ sarvasaṃsthitaṃ sarvayantṛ ca|
cidānandaghanaṃ pūrṇaṣāḍguṇyāmṛtavigraham || 37 ||
sarvāntarātmatvāt sarvaniyantṛnatvācca sarvarūpiṇamityarthaḥ|
bhāvānāṃ cidacidrūpabhāvābhāvādibhedinām|
antarātmatayā tattadahaṃkārapadāspadam || 38 ||
tadevocyate antarātmatayetyādinā|
acintyānanuyojyena hetunā yena kenacit|
śaktimacchaktibhāvena tad dvidhā vyavatiṣṭhate || 39 ||
ahamityeva yaḥ proktaḥ padapratyayayordvayoḥ|
nārāyaṇaḥ sa bhavitā tasyāhaṃtā tu yā parā || 40 ||
taddharmadharmiṇī lakṣmīḥ śaktiḥ sā bhāvarūpiṇī|
sarvakāryakarī saiva śaktirvitanute jagat || 41 ||
śaktimantamadhiṣṭhāya jyotsneva himadīdhitim|
sā vitistīrṣamāṇā hi śabdabrahmātmanā purā || 42 ||
devyā jagadvyāpārakartṛtvaṃ bhagavantamāśrityaiva, na svātantryeṇetyanena jagajjanmādikāraṇatvasya bhagavadasādhāraṇalakṣaṇasya nānupapattirityuktaṃ bhavati| prayojakakartā sarveśvaraḥ, prayojyakartrī devītyuktaṃ bhavati| anenāsya pāñcarātrasiddhāntasya śāktamatapraveśaśaṅkāyā nāvakāśaḥ|
vitatyātmānamatha sā vitanotyarthavartmanā|
śabdabrahmamayaḥ pūrvo yo nāma prathamodayaḥ || 43 ||
akalaṅgaḥ kalādvātmā yogasthairanubhūyate|
dhārāsaṃtānavarṇātmā varṇamārgaḥ sa śabdyate || 44 ||
kalādhvātmā; jñānaśaktyādiguṇātmā| dhārāsaṃtāneti varṇādhvanirdeśaḥ|
kalādhvānamadhiṣṭhāya sa punarmantravartmanā|
udeti sakalaṃ śakticakramādāya vaiṣṇavam || 45 ||
śakticakraṃ lakṣmyādikam|
parātmanā paraṃ jyotirmayamānandalakṣaṇam|
trivighenaiva rūpeṇa yathā te varṇitaṃ purā || 46 ||
rūpaṃ paraṃ tadevātha vartate sūkṣmavartmanā|
mantraprasararūpeṇa tacca te darśayiṣyate || 47 ||
sūkṣmaṃ tat trividhaṃ bhūyo vartate sthūlavartmanā|
sthūlā cāditanuryeyamaṅgopāṅgavibhedinī || 48 ||
lakṣmīnārāyaṇasyaiṣā mūrtiḥ ṣāḍguṇyabṛṃhitā|
cetanācetanaṃ viśvamanusaṃdheyamatra tu || 49 ||
atra tu; mantrātmakasthūlamūrtāvityarthaḥ|
tattadaiśvaryadatvaṃ tadadhiṣṭhātṛtvameva ca|
mantrasya vaiṣṇavaṃ rūpaṃ tadvijñeyaṃ vipaścitā || 50 ||
mantramūrtau sarvatattvāvasthitimāha tattadityādinā|
tattatkāryakarī tasyā yā śaktiḥ sāhamambujā|
mantrasya yaddhi caitanyaṃ pumāṃsaṃ taṃ pracakṣate || 51 ||
phalaprasavasāmarthyaṃ prākṛtaṃ rūpamucyate|
yad dṛḍhavyavasāyitvaṃ mahato yo guṇastu saḥ || 52 ||
mahataḥ ; mahattatvasya|
mantrāṇāṃ yadahaṃyutvamahaṃkāraguṇastu saḥ|
mantrāṇāṃ mānasaṃ rūpamaindriyajñānahetutā || 53 ||
yacchabdarūpatā mantre sā jñeyā nabhasi sthitā|
kampo mantrasamāveśe vāyavyaṃ rūpamucyate || 54 ||
prakāśakaratā dhyāne māntrī yā taijasātmikā|
yā tṛptirmantrasadbhāve parijñeyāmbhasī tu sā || 55 ||
yo hi mantrasthito bhāvaḥ sa bhaumo guṇa ucyate|
ityevaṃ sarvagāṃ vyāptiṃ mantrī māntrīṃ susaṃsmaret || 56 ||
acirānmantrasāmarthyāttena bhāvena jāyate|
itthaṃ saṃvittisāmarthyāttārikāśaktimātatām || 57 ||
tena bhāveneti| tattaddharmeṇetyarthaḥ|
spandamānāṃ purā paśyedvyomni saudāminīmiva|
hṛdambujaguhāmadhye pūrvokte citprabhāsane || 58 ||
pūrvokta iti| ādhāraśaktyādinirūpaṇāvasarokta ityarthaḥ|
sphurantyāṃ tārikāmūrtau śabdabrahmaṇi saṃsmaret|
mithunaṃ śāśvataṃ divyaṃ yathāvadavadhāraya || 59 ||
anena tārikāyāḥ śabdabrahmarūpatvamuktaṃ bhavati|
sūryānalāṃśasaṃsthānaṃ nārāyaṇamanāmayam|
smareccidāsanāsīnaṃ puṇḍarīkāyatekṣaṇam || 60 ||
nārāyaṇākyapuruṣottamasya mānasīṃ pūjāṃ vaktuṃ tadrūpamāha sūryetyādinā|
pītāmbaramudārāṅgaṃ kāñcīnū puraśobhitam|
hārakuṇḍalakeyūrakirīṭakaṭakojjvalam || 61 ||
yugānusārikāntiṃ vā nīlāmbaranibhaṃ tu vā|
śaṅkhacakradharaṃ devaṃ varadābhayadāyinam || 62 ||
prabhayeva maṇiṃ pūrṇaṃ gāmbhīryeṇeva sāgaram|
prabhayeva vivasvantaṃ jyotsnayeva niśākaram || 63 ||
purā dhyātvā hṛṣīkeśaṃ prasannamukhapaṅkajam|
purāṣṭādaśabhirbhogairarcayet puruṣottamam || 64 ||
sūktena pauruṣeṇātha praṇavena ca vāsava|
dviṣṭcatusrikārṇaaisca tathaiva ca jitaṃtayā || 65 ||
dviṣaḍityādi| ṣaḍakṣarāṣṭākṣaradvādaśākṣaramantrairityarthaḥ| jitaṃtayā; "jitaṃ te puṇḍarīkākṣa" ityādiślokarūpamantreṇetyarthaḥ|
arcayitvātha deveśaṃ lakṣmīṃ sarvāṅgagāṃ smaret|
layātmanārcayitvātha lakṣmīmāvāhayettataḥ || 66 ||
vāmotsaṅge niṣaṇṇāṃ tāmatha devasya śārṅgiṇaḥ|
arcayedvividhairbhogairyathāvacchāsracoditaiḥ || 67 ||
śakraḥ
devapriye devadevi namaste paṅkajekṣaṇe|
vidhiṃ puruṣasūktasya tārādīnāṃ ca me vada || 68 ||
śrīḥ
eko nārāyaṇo devaḥ śrīmān kamalalocanaḥ|
ekāhaṃ paramā śaktiḥ sarvakāryakarī hareḥ || 69 ||
tāvāvāṃ parame vyomni kṣemāya sakalātmanām|
āsīnau sakaleśānau sūribhiḥ sevitau sadā || 70 ||
tayornau hṛdi saṃkalpaḥ kaścidāvirbabhūva ha|
uttāraṇāya jīvānāmupāyo'nviṣyatāmiti || 71 ||
āvābhyāmutthitaṃ tejaḥ śabdabrahmamahodadhiḥ|
mathyamānāttatastasmādabhūt sūktadvayāmṛtam || 72 ||
puruṣasya hareḥ sūktaṃ mama suktaṃ tathaiva ca|
anyonyaśaktisaṃpṛktamanyonyārṇapariṣkṛtam || 73 ||
mama sūktamiti| śrīsūktamityarthaḥ|
nārāyaṇārṣamavyaktaṃ pauruṣaṃ sūktamiṣyate|
anyanmadārṣakaṃ sūktaṃ śrīsūktaṃ yat pracakṣate || 74 ||
nārāyaṇaḥ puruṣasūktasya ṛṣirityarthaḥ| madārṣakamiti| śrīrahamṛṣirityarthaḥ|
praṇavādyāḥ purā mantrāḥ pañca samyak pradarśitāḥ|
idānīṃ śṛṇu saṃkṣepātteṣāmārādhanakramam || 75 ||
pañceti| tārānutārāṣaḍakṣarāṣṭākṣaradvādaśākṣarā ityarthaḥ|
aṣṭādaśa ṛcaḥ proktāḥ pauruṣe sūktasattame|
tābhistu praṇāvādyābhirbhogānaṣṭādaśottamān || 76 ||
kuryādāvāhanādyāṃśca ta ete'ṣṭādaśa smṛtāḥ|
āvāhanāsane sārghyaṃ pādyamācamanaṃ tathā || 77 ||
snānaṃ ca paridhānaṃ ca sottarīyopavītakam|
gandhaḥ sumanaso dīpo dhūpaśca madhuparkakaḥ || 78 ||
prāpaṇaṃ sendu tāmbūlaṃ pādayoḥ kusumāñjaliḥ|
ātmārādhanadānaṃ ca yatheṣṭasthānacintanam || 79 ||
prāpaṇam; annādikaṃ naivedyam| induḥ; ghanasāraḥ|
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃpannā sarvādhāramayī parā|
nityā saṃpūrṇaṣāḍguṇyā yā viṣṇormūrtirujjvalā || 80 ||
saiveyaṃ kathyate mūrtirmāntrī mantraiścidātmakaiḥ|
svīkārayati tāṃ mantrī devaṃ yat svena tejasā || 81 ||
tadāvāhanamityuktaṃ mantravijñānapāragaiḥ|
svastikṛtyai svabhāvena cetanācetanaṃ hariḥ || 82 ||
āvāhayati svīkārayati mantratanūmiti yaugikārthaḥ|
adhitiṣṭhati yadviśvamāsanaṃ tadanu kriyā|
mamānandamayī śaktirdevastvāpyāyate yayā || 83 ||
saivārghyācamanīyādirūpetyarghyādicintanam|
ammayāḥ sakalā lokā jīvāścaiva tadāśrayāḥ || 84 ||
saiveti| ānandamayī śaktirevetyarthaḥ| ammayāḥ; apāṃ pariṇāmabhūtāḥ; "apa eva sasarjādau" iti sarvalokānāṃ tadupādānakatvokteḥ| tadāśrayāḥ; adbhirjīvanadhāriṇaḥ| jīvānāmappariṇāmatvābhāvāt tadāśrayatvoktiḥ|
taccheṣā ubhaye'pīti dyotyate pādyadānataḥ|
ṣoḍhā vibhajya rūpaṃ svaṃ tarpayāmi sanātanam || 85 ||
śabdādirūpeṇāhameva bhagavantamārādhayāmītyarthaḥ|
śabdādyaiḥ pañcabhirbhāvairantaścāpyabhimānataḥ|
samāveśitasadbhāvairbhogaiḥ sāṃdṛṣṭikādikaiḥ || 86 ||
ārādhayejjagannāthaṃ sāvadhānena cetasā|
dṛṣṭyaiva janyate prītiryeṣāṃ sāṃdṛṣṭikā matāḥ || 87 ||
bhogān sāṃdṛṣṭikābhyavahārikasāṃsparśikābhimānikabhedena caturdhā vibhajya varṇayati dṛṣṭyaivetyādinā|
śubhā rūpolbaṇāste ca dīpapravahaṇādayaḥ|
bhogāḥ śubhakarāḥ śaśvattarpayanti rasaurhi ye || 88 ||
prāpaṇācamanīyādyāste syurābhyavahārikāḥ|
sukhā ramyā mṛdusparśāḥ sparśairye tarpayantyajam || 89 ||
bhogāḥ sāṃsparśikāste syuḥ pādyārghyāsanapūrvakāḥ|
gandhāḥ sāṃsparśike kecit kecidābhyavahārike || 90 ||
niviṣṭā anilādyāḥ syurantyāḥ pākajagandhinaḥ|
stutivāditragītādyā bhogāḥ śabdamayā hi ye || 91 ||
dainyāñjalipuṭādyāśca te smṛtā ābhimānikāḥ|
itthaṃ caturvidhairbhogaiḥ śāsradṛṣṭena vartmanā || 92 ||
ṛgbhiḥ sapraṇavādyābistoṣayet puruṣottamam|
mantrānte bhoganirdeśaprītiśca tadanantaram || 93 ||
oṃkṛtyarcamathoccārya praṇavādīni pañca ca|
āvāhayāmi lakṣmīśaṃ paramātmānamavyayam || 94 ||
praṇavādīni pañceti| tārānutārikāṣaḍakṣarāṣṭākṣaradvādaśākṣaramantrānityarthaḥ|
ātiṣṭhatāmimāṃ mūrtiṃ madanugrahakāmyayā|
śriyā sārdhaṃ jagannātho divyo nārāyaṇaḥ pumān || 95 ||
ityañjaliprasūnasthaṃ svamūrtāvavatārya ca|
praṇamya svāgataṃ pṛṣṭvā kṣāmayedanayā dhiyā || 96 ||
svamūrtau; svaparikalpitāyāṃ mūrtau|
praṇamya bhagavan paścādāsanenārcayāmi ca|
uktvā mantrānathoccārya trirnirdiśyedamityataḥ || 97 ||
paścāt sanāma nirdiśya prītiṃ paścāt samācaret|
prīyatāṃ bhagavān paścādvāsudevastataḥ param || 98 ||
sanāmeti| idamidamidaṃ pādyamityādirītyā bhoganāmanirdeśapūrvakamityarthaḥ|
arghyaṃ niḥsrāvayedvedyāmayaṃ bhogavidhikramaḥ|
āsanārghyādi bhogeṣu yathāliṅgaṃ vinirdiśet || 99 ||
ādyayāvāhanaṃ kuryādāsanaṃ ca dvitīyayā|
arghyaṃ tṛtīyayā deyaṃ mūrdhnyāpaḥ kusumoddhṛtāḥ || 100 ||
puruṣasūktaṛcāṃ bhogapradāne viniyogamāha ādyayetyādinā|
pādyaṃ deyaṃ caturthyā tu pañcamyācamanīyakam|
ṣaṣṭhyā snānavidhiḥ kāryaḥ saptamyā paridhānakam || 101 ||
aṣṭamyottaravāsaśca navamyā gandhalepanam|
daśamyā sragalaṃkārā ekādaśyā tu dīpakaḥ || 102 ||
dvādaśyā surabhirdhūpaḥ parayā madhuparkakaḥ|
prāpaṇaṃ tu caturdaśyā pañcadaśyānuvāsanam || 103 ||
anuvāsanaṃ nāma bhojanānantaradeyaṃ sugandhi tāmbūlādi|
ṣoḍasyā tu namaskāraḥ parayā kusumāñjaliḥ|
aṣṭādaśyā pradānaṃ ca samārādhanakarmaṇaḥ || 104 ||
snānavāsaḥpradīpeṣu dadyādācamanakriyām|
purastādarhaṇaṃ kāryaṃ madhuparkānnadānataḥ || 105 ||
arhaṇam; āpośanam|
tarpaṇācamane paścāt praṇavadvitayena tu|
itthaṃ snānādibhogeṣu deśakālādyapekṣayā || 106 ||
kuryādrājavadācāraṃ tattanmantreṇa sādhakaḥ|
saṃkalpaśca pradānaṃ ca prītiśceti trayaṃ trayam || 107 ||
kuryāt sarveṣu bhogeṣu deśakālādyapekṣayā|
samāhito'ñjaliṃ kṛtvā tata oṃ bhagavanniti || 108 ||
āsanenārcayiṣyāmītyuktvā dadyādathāsanam|
mantramuccārya nirdiśyedidamāsanamityataḥ || 109 ||
omoṃ prīyatāṃ bhagavān vāsudeva iti bruvan|
arghyādijalamādāya sthāpayeddevasaṃnidhau || 110 ||
ābhirarghyābhirityevamābhiḥ pādyābirityapi|
ābhirācamanīyābhirarhaṇīyābhirityapi || 111 ||
ābhirarghyābhirityādīnāmadbhiriti viśeṣyamuttaravākyādanuṣañjanīyam|
tarpaṇīyābhiradhbhiśca snānīyābhiritīdṛśam|
prītisaṃkalpayorvācyamimā arghyā itīdṛśam || 112 ||
vācyaṃ pradānavelāyāṃ yathāliṅgamiti kramaḥ|
arghyādikalpanaṃ cāgre bahiryāge vidhāsyate || 113 ||
iti sragādibhiḥ ṣaḍbhirdevadevaṃ janārdanam|
pañcabhirvā caturbhirvā tribhirdvabyāmathāpi vā || 114 ||
srak, dīpaḥ, dhūpaḥ, madhuparkaḥ, prāpaṇam; anuvāsanamiti ṣaḍbhirityarthaḥ|
ekena vā samārādhya deśakālānukūlataḥ|
mayaiva pūjitaṃ dhyātvā devadevaṃ janārdanam || 115 ||
candrodaya ivāmbhodhiṃ sarvāṅgaparibṛṃhitam|
anirdeśyāmanaupamyāmanantāṃ bhāvinīṃ sadā || 116 ||
anaupamyāmiti| anupamāmityarthaḥ| upamaivaupamyam| svārthe ṣyañpratyayaḥ|
āmodamiva puṣpasthaṃ dīpasthāmiva ca prabhām|
viniṣkraṣṭumaśakyāṃ māmananyāṃ puruṣottamāt || 117 ||
viniṣkraṣṭum; vinipkramayitumityarthaḥ|
bhāvayan vidhivanmantrī layayāgena māṃ yajet|
tārikāvidhimanviṣya tayā māṃ tārikāṃ yajet || 118 ||
atha meghādivodyantīṃ vidyutaṃ puruṣottamāt|
samudyantīṃ tadicchāto vibāvya manasā sudhīḥ || 119 ||
vāmotsaṅge niṣaṇṇāṃ māṃ devadevasya cintayet|
aikadhyamāvayorjñātvā svabāvaṃ ca suśītalam || 120 ||
aikadyam; ekavidhatvam|
ṛgbhyāṃ hiraṇyapūrvābhyāṃ prapadyeta janārdanam|
sāntvayecca parābhyāṃ tāṃ māṃ tāvadapṛthakkṛtām || 121 ||
ṛgbhyāmiti| hiraṇyavarṇām, tāṃ ma āvaheti dvābhyāmityarthaḥ|
pañcamyā ca prapadyeta prasannāṃ bhāvayan dhiyā|
jñātvā pūrvoktasāmarthyaṃ tārikāyā yathārthataḥ || 122 ||
mudrāsamanvito mantro ya āvāhanasaṃjñitaḥ|
pūrakeṇa sureśāna manasā samudīrayan || 123 ||
māmathāvāhayeddevādutsaṅge paramātmanaḥ|

padmagarbhanibhāṃ kāntāmasitāyatalocanām|

gambhīranābhiṃ trivalīvibhūṣitatanūdarām|
sukarkaśadṛḍhottuṅgapīnavṛttaghanastanīm || 126 ||
caladvirephapaṭalasamākrāntālakāvalim|
āraktādharabimbāṃ ca vaṃśamuktāphaladvijām || 127 ||
dvijāḥ dantāḥ|
ardhacandralalāṭastharājamānalalāṭikām|
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃpannāṃ kṛṣṇakuñcitamūrdhajām || 128 ||
lalāṭikā tilakam| alaṃkāre kan|
varadāṃ paṅkajakarāṃ padmamālāvibhūṣitām|
viṣṇuvāmabhujāsliṣṭāṃ tadaṃsasthakarāmbujām || 129 ||
vāmena bāhunā divyāṃ vahantīṃ puṣpamañjarīm|
varadābhayapāṇiṃ vā pāśāṅkuśakarāṃ tu vā || 130 ||
ardhasvastikasaṃlīnāṃ sphuranmaulivirājitām|
dhyātvā māṃ saṃmukhīṃ kuryānmantramūrtiṃ sanātanīm | 131 ||
svastikam āsanaviśeṣaḥ|
mūlamantrādikairbhūyo mantraiḥ sarvaiśca pūrvavat|
karanyāsaṃ vinā dehanyāsaṃ mayi samācaret || 132 ||
puṣpamardhyaṃ tathā dīpaṃ dhūpaṃ mālyaṃ vilepanam|
cetasā sādareṇaiva pādyamācamanaṃ tataḥ || 133 ||
praṇāmamathavāṣṭāṅgaṃ jayaśabdāṃśca mānasān|
pradarśayettato mudrā yāste pūrvaṃ pradarśitāḥ || 134 ||
svāgataṃ tava padmākṣi saṃnidhiṃ bhaja me'mbuje|
gṛhāṇa mānasīṃ pūjāṃ yathārthaparibhāvitām || 135 ||
labdhvānujñāṃ tato matto mānasaṃ yāgamācaret|
saṃkalpajanitairbhogaiḥ pavitraiḥ pāramārthikaiḥ || 136 ||
bāhyaprakriyayā śaśvat parastādvakṣyamāṇayā|
māṃ yajeta suniṣṇāto bhogaiḥ sāṃsparśikādikaiḥ || 137 ||
prāpaṇāntaṃ vidhāyānte kāriṇaṃ saṃsmared gurum|
