jīvantamathavātītaṃ tasmai dadyāttato'khilam || 138 ||
vittaṃ saṃvibhajeccaiva prāpaṇāṃśena mantravit|
jīvato'pyathavātītānyathārthenaiva cetasā || 139 ||
parivārān smaret sarvān vakṣyamāṇān viśeṣataḥ|
kāraṇe mayi saṃlīnān dhānāsthāniva bhūruhān || 140 ||
tattanmantraprayogeṇa layaprakriyayā yajet|
kuryānmahānase homaṃ mokṣalakṣmīpradaṃ śubham || 141 ||
triguṇādhāramadhyasthe trikoṇe triguṇe'nale|
dhyānāraṇiṃ tu nirmathya cidagnimavatārya ca || 142 ||
saṃskāraiḥ saṃskṛtaṃ kṛtvā vakṣyamāṇadhiyā sudhīḥ|
trilakṣaṇādhāragate vaiṣṇave jātavedasi || 143 ||
saṃskārairiti| vaiṣṇavīkaraṇaprakriyayetyarthaḥ|
nādāvasānagaganāttārikāyāḥ parisratam|
brahma havirgṛhītvātha brahmarandhreṇa saṃviśet || 144 ||
tato vahnigṛhaṃ gatvā sarpiḥ saṃskṛtya śāsrataḥ|
kurvīta sakalaṃ kṛtyaṃ tenājyena yathāvidhi || 145 ||
tato homāvasāne tatsaṃkalpaḥ karma mānasam|
saṃnyasenmayi bhāvena vakṣyamāṇadhiyā sudhīḥ || 146 ||
yaḥ kramo'bihito bāhye sa sarvo mānase'tra tu|
avadhānena vā kāryo manmayairdravyasaṃcayaiḥ || 147 ||
sarvopasargaśamanaḥ so'yaṃ sarvaphalapradaḥ|
kathito mānaso yāgaḥ kārya ādehapātanāt || 148 ||
ādehapātanāditi| anena pāñcakālikadharmaniratasya paramaikāntinaḥ pratipuruṣaṃ pratyahaṃ ca bhagavadārādhanamavaśyakartavyaṃ nityakarmetyuktaṃ bhavati|
atha dravyāṇi sarvāṇi manmayīkṛtya yatnataḥ|
bāhyotthavāsana śāntyai bāhyayāgamathācaret || 149 ||
ityevamantaryāgaste madīyaḥ śakra varṇitaḥ|
bahiryāgasvarūpaṃ tu tattvato me niśāmaya || 150 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre antaryāgaprakāśo nāma ṣaṭtriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
iti ṣaṭtriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

<37>
saptatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ
bahirvedyādideśasthadravyairmadbhāvanekṣitaiḥ|
bhogabhūtairyadijye'haṃ bahiryāgastu sa smṛtaḥ || 1 ||
madbhāvanekṣitairiti| madātmanā bhāvitairityarthaḥ| bhogabhūtairiti| bhogabhūtairiti| bhogopakaraṇairityarthaḥ|
vāsanā dvividhā proktā bāhyābhyantarahetujā|
nirṇudatyāntarāḥ sarvā mānaso yāga uttamaḥ || 2 ||
bāhyāśayaviśuddhyarthaṃ bāhyayāgo vidhīyate|
śāsrīyāṃ kṣitimāsthāya maṇḍape tatra maṇḍite || 3 ||
āśayaḥ; vāsanā| brahmasthāne; brabrabhāvanābhāvite ityarthaḥ|
brahmasthāne vidhāyātha vediṃ sarvaguṇānvitām|
aṣṭahastaṃ tadardhaṃ vā tadardhaṃ vāpi sādhitam || 4 ||
prāgantaṃ susitaṃ sūtraṃ vedimadhye prasārayet|
tasminrubhayataḥ pārśve madhyataścārdhamadhyayoḥ || 5 ||
vidhāya trīṇi cihnāni pañca śaṅkūn nikhānayet|
antyayosriṣu madhyeṣu śaṅvkostatra hyupāntyayoḥ || 6 ||
sūtramadhyaṃ yataḥ prāptaṃ tatra śaṅkuṃ nidhāpayet|
pāśau dvau pratimucyātha madhyacihnaṃ samānayet || 7 ||
savye cāpyapasavye ca tatra cihne vidhāya ca|
madhyame pratimucya dve sūtramadhyaṃ samānayet || 8 ||
cihnasyopari deśe ca kramāt savyāpasavyayoḥ|
sūtramadhyaṃ yataḥ prāptaṃ tatra śaṅkuṃ nidhāpayet || 9 ||
pūrvasmin pratimucyaikaṃ pāśaṃ dakṣiṇato'param|
kuryāt pradakṣiṇaṃ koṇaṃ madhyacihne'nayostataḥ || 10 ||
aiśakoṇaṃ tathā kuryāt pratimucyottaratra tu|
pratīcyayāmyayorevaṃ pratīcyodīcyayorapi || 11 ||
pratimucya kramāt pāśau kuryāt kauṇau tathā parau|
tatkṣetrasphuṭatāyai tu sūtrayettu caturdiśam || 12 ||
bhakte ṣoḍaśadhā kṣetre caturbhirmadhyamaiḥ padaiḥ|
prāgādikaiḥ pradeśaiśca vidadhīta navāmbujam || 13 ||
bhakte; vibhājita ityarthaḥ|
madyamaṃ kamalaṃ kāryamaṣṭapatraṃ yathā śṛṇu|
caturdhā bhrāmayet kṣetraṃ varjayitvāṣṭamaṃ bahiḥ || 14 ||
karṇikā kesaraṃ patrasaṃdhiḥ parveti ca kramāt|
catvāryaṃśapramāṇāni tyaktoṃ'śo vyoma tadbahiḥ || 15 ||
tasmin sūtrāṣṭakaṃ padme digvidiksaṃsthitaṃ kṣipet|
sūtrāṇāmantare bhūyaḥ kṣipet sūtrāṣṭakaṃ tathā || 16 ||
padmasaṃdhisthasūtreṇa dikkrameṇa dalāṣṭakam|
sahajāḥ sucitā rekhā vyomarekhāgaṇāḥ smṛtāḥ || 17 ||
padmāntarāṇāmaṣṭānāmevameva vidhā bhavet|
evaṃ puravaraṃ ramyaṃ navapadmasulakṣaṇam || 18 ||
bahirdvārayutaṃ kāryaṃ koṇaśobhādisaṃyutam|
caturvarṇayutaṃ kāryaṃ karṇikādivibhaktaye || 19 ||
karṇikādītyādipadena kesarapatrasandhiparvāṇi gṛhyante|
pauṣpe vā prastare vāsre susite vātha dhūpite|
ahate gandhayukte'tha sthaṇḍile vopalepite || 20 ||
maṇḍalamiva puṣpaprastarādikamapi pūjāsthānatayā grāhyam|
yajan kāryavaśānmantrī navapadmaṃ vicintayet|
kuryāccādhyānamārgasthāṃ pratimāṃ śāsradarśanāt || 21 ||
sauvarṇae rājatādye vā dṛḍhe kālādivarjite|
ārādhayed ghaṭe pūrṇe dadhnā kṣīreṇa vāriṇaā || 22 ||
praśastapallavākrānte vasrapaṭṭādisaṃyute|
vibhāvya navapadmaṃ tu pūjayenmantravittamaḥ || 23 ||
ghaṭe pure tathārcāyāṃ yadvā mantrī kramādyajet|
sarvalokamayaṃ sarvagīrvāṇāśrayamuttamam || 24 ||
sarvādhāramayaṃ dhyāyennavapadmaṃ purottamam|
tārikākhyānamaḥśabdairarcyamarghyādinā tataḥ || 25 ||
tataḥ pātracatuṣkaṃ tu hemādidravyanirmitam|
pūtāmbu pūrṇaṃ gandhasragratnauṣadhikuśodakaiḥ || 26 ||
pūrvamarghyādinetyuktaṃ viśadayati tata iti|
vedyāmarcāpuraḥsthāyāṃ nyaset koṇacatuṣkake|
yātavīyādivahnyante kramādeva prakalpayet || 27 ||
nyasediti| ardhyādipātratveneti śeṣaḥ| yātavīyādīti| nairṛtyādyāgneyāntakoṇeṣvityarthaḥ|
arghyamācamanīyaṃ ca pādyaṃ snānīyameva ca|
kalpayet kramaśo mantrī mantrānetānudīrayan || 28 ||
tāraṃ ca kalpayāmīti madhye saṃjñāḥ syurañjasā|
anekārthaṃ ca tatrārghyaṃ kalpyaṃ pātre'tha madhyataḥ || 29 ||
oṃ arghyaṃ parikalpayāmītyādikrameṇeti bhāvaḥ| anekārthamiti| sarvārthatoyamiti tasya sāṃpradāyikī prasiddhiḥ|
siddhārthakāstilā dūrvāḥ sayavāḥ sitataṇḍulāḥ|
toyakṣīraphalopetā arghyadravyamudāhṛtam || 30 ||
pātraṃ tārikayāpūrya sudhāsaṃdohadehayā|
tasminniṣkalasaṃsthāne mantracakraṃ vicintayet || 31 ||
niṣkalaṃ madhyamārghyaṃ tu pūjya puṣpādinā purā|
bhāvanīyaṃ ca tat samyagagnīṣomamayātmanā || 32 ||
pūjya ; pūjayitvā| bhāvanīyam; dhyeyam|
eṣa prathamasaṃskāro dvitīyamavadhāraya|
pracaṇḍakiraṇavrātairbhāskarīyairdahettu tat || 33 ||
dvitīyamiti| dāhanaplāvanādirūpaṃ vakṣyamāṇamityarthaḥ| dahediti| śoṣaṇapūrvakaṃ dahediti saṃpradāyaḥ|
nirvāpayettato dagdhaṃ śītapūrṇenduraśmibhiḥ|
brahmānandāmṛtāmbhodhikallolaiḥ pūrayettu tat || 34 ||
abhimantrya tu mukhyaistanmanubistārikādibhiḥ|
tato jalamupādāya nyaset pātrāntareṣu tu || 35 ||
tata iti| saṃskṛtatoyapūrṇāt pātrādityarthaḥ|
pratisvamapi vā kuryādīdṛśaṃ saṃskṛtikramam|
āpyāyanaṃ ca pātrāṇāṃ prītiṃ cāsmāt samācaret || 36 ||
pratisvamiti| pratyekamarghyādipātramityarthaḥ|
mudrā kāmadughā kāryā saurabheyī svamantrataḥ|
surabhiṃ himaśailābhāṃ nirādhārapade sthitām || 37 ||
dhyātvā tatstanasaṃkāśāṃ mudrāṃ tāṃ paridarśayet|
karābhyāṃ gandhadigdhābhyāṃ sāvadhānena cetasā || 38 ||
argyapātrāmbhasā prokṣya maṇḍalaṃ maṇḍapaṃ tathā|
yāgadravyāṇyaśeṣāṇi tāḍayedasramantrataḥ || 39 ||
asreti| oṃ vīryāyāsrāya phaḍiti mantreṇetyarthaḥ|
mūlenāplāvayet paścāttat sarvaṃ bhogatāṃ vrajet|
tato viṣṇuṃ namaskṛtya tadaṅkasthāṃ ca paṅkajām || 40 ||
mūlena; mūlamantreṇetyarthaḥ|
arcayenmūlamantreṇa hyāmuktakusumādibhiḥ|
arghyapātramathādāya puṣpaṃ dhūpaṃ vilepanam || 41 ||
āmukteti| agrathitaiḥ viralairityarthaḥ|
dīpaṃ naivedyamapyevaṃ dvārayāgaṃ tataścaret|
udumbarasya mūle tu bahirdvārasya madhyataḥ || 42 ||
udumbaraśabdo dvārasyordhvādhaḥ pradeśanikṣiptadārvorvartate|
bhūmiṣṭhaṃ kṣetrapālaṃ tu yajeddhyānādisaṃyutam|
nīlajīmūtasaṃkāśaṃ daṇḍahastaṃ mahātanum || 43 ||
kṣetrapālarūpamāha nīleti|
muṣṭikṛdvāmahastena dhyeyaḥ kṣetreśvaraḥ sadā|
mudrā ca darśanīyātra pareṣvevaṃ vidhiḥ kramāt || 44 ||
dvāropari sthitāṃ lakṣmīmūrdhvasaṃstha udumbare|
paṅkeruhakarāṃ lakṣmīṃ padmoparigatāṃ yajet || 45 ||
dakṣiṇottaraśākhābhyāṃ mūle caṇḍapracaṇḍakau|
tadvajjayaṃ ca vijayaṃ bāhye dvārasya cāntare || 46 ||
śākhā dvārasya pārśvadārukhaṇḍaḥ|
caṇḍādyā vijayāntāśca sarve jñeyāścaturbhujāḥ|
gadācakradharāścaiva śaṅkhahastā mahābalāḥ || 47 ||
tarjayanto hyabhaktāṃśca doṣāṇāṃ dhvaṃsanodyatāḥ|
itthaṃ dhyeyāḥ samabhyarcya darśanīyāśca mudrikāḥ || 48 ||
abhaktān| devābhaktānityarthaḥ|
śākhādvayasya madhye tu vāmadakṣiṇataḥ kramāt|
gaṅgāṃ ca yamunāṃ caiva pūjayettadanantaram || 49 ||
śākhādvayasya; dvārapārśvadārukhaṇḍadvayasya|
hastābhyāṃ dadhatī kumbhaṃ tīrthena paripūritam|
navayauvanalāvaṇyā śvetarūpā smitānanā || 50 ||
gaṅgāyamunayoḥ rūpamucyate hastābyāmityādinā|
gaṅgā dhyeyā prasannā ca pūrṇacandranibhānanā|
tādṛśī nīlajīmūtasaṃnibhā yamunā nadī || 51 ||
tenaiva kramayogena dvārasyābhyantare sthitau|
nidhīśau śaṅkhapadmākhyāvarghyapuṣpādibhiryajet || 52 ||
nidhīśau bhāvanīyau tau nidhibhāṇḍoparisthitau|
sthūlodarau ca piṅgākṣau dvibhujau bhagavanmayau || 53 ||
kṛtvaivaṃ dvārayāgaṃ tu tataḥ puṣpaṃ ca saṃmukham|
aṅguṣṭhatarjanīmadhyātritayenāsramantrataḥ || 54 ||
ādāya cābhimantryātha cakraṃ tadupari smaret|
niśitāraṃ jvaladrūpaṃ pravarṣadanalāśani || 55 ||
kṣayakṛdvighnajālānāṃ nyasedyāgagṛhāntare|
dakṣiṇāṃ tarjanīṃ kuryāt samyagūrdhvamukhīṃ tataḥ || 56 ||
śikhāmantreṇa saṃyuktāṃ vidyudvilasitaprabhām|
smṛtvā bhrāmayamāṇastāṃ saṃviśedyāgamandiram || 57 ||
arghyapātrāmbhasāsreṇa prokṣayet svakamāsanam|
tasminnādhāraśaktyādi māntramāsanamarcayet || 58 ||
tāṃ tāṃ pradarśayenmudrāṃ yatra yatra ca yā ca yā|
tatropaviśya deveśamarcayeddhṛdaye sthitam || 59 ||
lakṣmīnārāyaṇākhyaṃ taddhṛdgataṃ paramaṃ mahaḥ|
dāṃpatyamanapāyaṃ tadyāpayennetrayordvayoḥ || 60 ||
yāpayet| gamayedityarthaḥ|
sarvaṃ tadbhāvamāpādya paśyenniscalalocanaḥ|
maṇḍape vedikāyāṃ vā yatra vā yaṣṭumicchati || 61 ||
arghyapuṣpādibhiḥ pūjyamādhārādikamāsanam|
dharmādikān yajet paścāttattaddigbhāgagocarān || 62 ||
padmārkamaṇḍalāṃścaiva tato bhāvāsanāvadhi|
uparyupari yogena dhyātvā puṣpādinārcayet || 63 ||
maṇḍalasthasya devasya dakṣiṇe maṇḍalopari|
kṛtādiyugaparyante vāyorīśāvadhi kramāt || 64 ||
vibhajya saptadhā kṣetramādau gaṇapatiṃ yajet|
padmāsanopaviṣṭaṃ tatkesare tu dale'mbuje || 65 ||
dadhānaṃ cordhvabāhubhyāmakṣasūtraparaśvathau|
varadābhayamudre ca pūrvahastadvayena tu || 66 ||
kiṃtu mudrā vicintyaivaṃ varadābhayahastayoḥ|
tarjanyaṅguṣṭhasaṃsargād vyākhyāmudrāsamākṛtiḥ || 67 ||
pīnaṃ lambodaraṃ sthūlamekadaṃṣṭraṃ gajānanam|
kesareṣvaṅgaṣaṭkaṃ ca ṣaṭsu padmasya vinyaset || 68 ||
svamantreṇārcayenmudrāṃ baddhvā puṣpādinā sudhīḥ|
tato vāgīśvarīṃ devīṃ svamantreṇārcayetsudhīḥ || 69 ||
sūryenduvahnibimbasthasitapaṅkeruhāsanām|
sarvopādhivinirmuktāmapi sākāratāṃ gatām || 70 ||
śaktiṃ śabdātmikāṃ sākṣānmadīyāṃ prathamodgatām|
dvinetrāṃ dvibhujāṃ śvetāṃ śaṅkhapaṅkajadhāriṇīm || 71 ||
sphuratpīyūṣakallolasadṛśābharaṇāmbarām|
tato guruṃ tadguruṃ ca lokasiddhākṛtī ca tau || 72 ||
lokasiddhākṛtiṃ paścāt tadguruṃ parameṣṭhinam|
tataḥ pitṛgaṇaḥ pūjyo hyamūrtaḥ piṇḍasaṃnibhaḥ || 73 ||
ādisiddhān yajet pascādbhagavaddhyānatatparān|
śāntān nimīlitākṣāṃśca śubhāṅgāṃstejasādhikān || 74 ||
anujñāṃ prāpya tebhyaśca labdhānujño yathākramam|
āvāhya māṃ yajet paścāddevadevāṅkasaṃsthitām||
yena yena prakāreṇa taṃ taṃ śṛṇu sureśvara || 75 ||
anujñāṃ prāpyeti| guruparamagurvādīnāmanujñāṃ prāptāmanusaṃdhāyetyarthaḥ|
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre bāhyayāgaprakāśo nāma saptatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
iti saptatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

<38>
aṣṭatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ
eko nārāyaṇo devaḥ pūrṇaṣāḍguṇyavigrahaḥ|
tasyāhaṃ paramā śaktirekāhaṃtā sanātanī || 1 ||
sādhakānugrahārthāya sāhaṃ sākāratāṃ gatā|
aṅkasthā devadevasya yathā pūjye tathā śṛṇu || 2 ||
prāpyānujñāṃ gaṇeśāderhṛtsthāmāvāhayettataḥ|
bhāvayet paramātmānamantaryāgābhipūjitam || 3 ||
pūrṇastimitaṣāḍguṇyaṃ mayā śaktyābhipūritam|
tārakaṃ tat triruccārya tārikāṃ tu triruccaret || 4 ||
āvāhayet tato mantraṃ puṃliṅgaṃ samudīrayan|
puruṣaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ pīnodāracaturbhujam || 5 ||
puṃliṅgaṃ mantramiti| praṇavamityarthaḥ|
uccaran paramaṃ mantraṃ recakena śanaiḥ śanaiḥ|
agnīṣomadvayāntaḥ sthavartmanā nāsikodarāt || 6 ||
divyaśaktisamāvāsaṃ lakṣmīśamavatārayet|
anāhatāt padādyadvā sarvataḥ samatāṃ gatam || 7 ||
anāhataṃ hṛdayasthaṃ dvādaśadalaṃ padmam|
nirgataṃ tu smareddevaṃ vidyutpuñcamivāmbudāt|
sapuṣpāñjalimadhyasthaṃ bhāvāsanagataṃ nyaset || 8 ||
saṃnidhiṃ saṃnirodhaṃ ca saṃstambhaṃ sthāpanaṃ tathā|
kurvan mudrācatuṣkaṃ tu manasā samyagācaret || 9 ||
saṃnidhyādīnāṃ mudrā anantarameva vakṣyante|
uttānau saṃhatau pāṇī kṛtvāṅguṣṭhadvayena tu|
svāṃ svāṃ kaniṣṭhikāṃ mṛjyāt saṃnidhīkaraṇaṃ tu tat || 10 ||
anāmayormadhyamayoḥ kuryādunmārjanaṃ tathā|
saṃstambhasaṃnirodhau ca dvau kṛtau bhavatastadā || 11 ||
kṛtvā catasṛbhirmuṣṭimaṅgulībhiḥ karadvaye|
tiryak ca saṃmukhīkṛtya nyasenmuṣṭidvayopari || 12 ||
aṅguṣṭhadvitayaṃ śakra saṃsthāpanamidaṃ bhavet|
evaṃbhūto bhavatyevaṃ tārādiṃ mantramuccaran || 13 ||
kālaṃ pādyārghyadānāntamutthitaṃ bhāvayeddharim|
snānābharaṇavasrasragdāne'laṃkaraṇe tathā || 14 ||
kālamiti| kāle ityarthaḥ| atyantasaṃyoge dvitīyā|
anyatra bhogayāgeṣu tattadbhogānukūlataḥ|
smarecchāsrīyasaṃsthānaṃ sānukampaṃ susaṃmukham || 15 ||
sānukampaṃ susaṃmukhamiti ca hariviśeṣaṇam|
klṛpte tu vigrahe pūrvaṃ tathārūpo'vatiṣṭhate|
bhogeṣu dīyamāneṣu śaktiryā manmayī parā || 16 ||
tatrasthāṃ tāṃ smaret sākṣādādadāno hariryathā|
āhūtasya tataḥ sārghyaṃ puṣpāṇāṃ pūrṇamañjalim || 17 ||
āhūtasyeti| sāṃnidhyaṃ prāpitasyetyarthaḥ|
vikṣipyādhomukhainaiva pāṇiyugmena mūrdhani|
tāreṇa tārayā dvābhyāṃ namasā cārcayet kramāt || 18 ||
puṣpārghyadhūpalepaistu mūlamantrādibhiḥ kramāt|
saṃkṣepaṃ vistaraṃ cāpi yathākālaṃ samācaret || 19 ||
lepaḥ candanādyanulepaḥ|
śaktiṃ tadaṅgasaṃpūrṇāṃ nirākārāmanūpamām|
tārayā pūjayet pascāttattadaṅgeṣu devatāḥ || 20 ||
anūpamām; anupamāmityarthaḥ|
asrabhūṣaṇaśaktyādyāḥ svaiḥ svairmantraiḥ samarcayet|
ayaṃ yāgo layo nāma mahān sarvārthasiddhidaḥ || 21 ||
tato bhagavato viṣṇoḥ śaktisaṃpūrṇavigrahāt|
sarvaśaktimayīṃ divyāmekāṃ paramabhāsvarām || 22 ||
ṣāḍguṇyavigrahāṃ devīmanirdeśyāmanūpamām|
sarvatra sarvadā viṣṇoḥ sarvathaivānapāyinīm || 23 ||
kṛpayā sādhakārthāya svayaṃ sākāratāṃ gatām|
avihāyaiva taṃ devamākāraṃ pṛthageyuṣīm || 24 ||
uktakrameṇa māṃ pūrvaṃ mantreṇāvāhayet sudhīḥ|
āvāhanavidhau proktaṃ kramaṃ sarvaṃ samācaret || 25 ||
vāmotsaṅge niṣaṇṇāṃ ca viṣṇormāṃ bhāvayet priyām|
bhāvanīyaṃ ca madrūpaṃ pūrvameva nidarśitam || 26 ||
tasyāṃ layaprakāreṇa mayi sarvaṃ yajet kramāt|
tato viniḥsṛtaṃ mattaḥ smarecchaktyādikaṃ kramāt || 27 ||
jvālāyāḥ supradīptāyāḥ sphuliṅganicayaṃ yathā|
bhogasthāne yathaikaikaṃ vinyasettattathā śṛṇu || 28 ||
madhyābjakarṇikāmadhye bhāvāsanagatau smaret|
puruṣaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ tadaṅkasthāṃ ca paṅkajām || 29 ||
prācyābjakarṇikāmadhye lakṣmīdehādvinirgatām|
dvibhujāṃ bhāvayellakṣmīṃ dvinetrāṃ cārukuṇḍalām || 30 ||
śvetamālyāmbaradharāṃ hārakeyūrabhūṣitām|
sarvalakṣaṇasaṃpannāṃ pīnonnatapayodharām || 31 ||
prabuddhotpalavistīrṇalocanāṃ susmitānanām|
caladvirephapaṭalaramaṇīyālakāvalim || 32 ||
lasallalāṭatilakāmāraktādharapallavām|
kṛṣṇakuñcitakeśāṃ ca vaṃśamuktāphaladvijām || 33 ||
padmāsanopaviṣṭāṃ ca pāśāhkuśakaradvayīm|
padmagarbhopamākārāṃ saṃmukhīmāvayoḥ smaret || 34 ||
smareddakṣiṇabhāgābjakarṇikodaramadhyagām|
veṣabhūṣādibhistulyāṃ lakṣmyāḥ kundanibhāṃ tu vā || 35 ||
kīrtiṃ madrūpaniṣkrāntāṃ sarvakīrtimayīṃ parām|
āvayoḥ paścimābjasthakarṇikāsanasaṃsthitām || 36 ||
madrūpaniḥsṛtāṃ dhyāyettādṛśīmaruṇāṃ jayām|
smareduttarapadmasthakarṇikodaramadhyagām || 37 ||
māyāṃ madrūpaniṣkrāntāṃ tādṛśīmaruṇāṃ tu vā|
āgneye hṛdayaṃ padme śiro yātusaroruhe || 38 ||
śikhāṃ tu vāyavīyābje varma ceśānapaṅkaje|
madhyapadmapuraḥpatre netramasraṃ tu koṇake || 39 ||
netrasya purato devaṃ vāsudevaṃ vicintayet|
madhyadakṣiṇadikpatre smaret saṃkarṣaṇaṃ prabum || 40 ||
smarenmadhyasarojasya pradyumnaṃ paścime dale|
aniruddhamudaksaṃsthe koṇapatrayugadvaye || 41 ||
asrasya pṛṣṭhato nāgān smaret pīyūṣavarṣmaṇaḥ|
gulguluṃ ca guruṇyaṃ ca madanaṃ śalalaṃ tathā || 42 ||
gulgulvādayaḥ nāgānāṃ saṃjñāḥ|
sudhākumbhakarān śaṅkhakundagaurāṃścatūradān|
lakṣmīkamalapatreṣu sarvataḥ saustubhaṃ nyaset || 43 ||
hṛtpaṅkajadaleṣvevaṃ vanamālāṃ niveśayet|
kīrtipaṅkajapatreṣu bhūṣāpadmaṃ niveśayet || 44 ||
jayāpaṅkajapatreṣu varma padmadaleṣu ca|
padmeṣu vinyasedevaṃ padmapatracatuṣṭaye || 45 ||
śiraḥpaṅkajapatreṣu nyasedaṅkuśamuttamam|
nyased dvāracatuṣkāgre garuḍaṃ patatāṃ varam || 46 ||
nāgān vā tata ātmīyadvārāgreṣu niyojayet|
pūrvadvārabhuvi nyasyet padmahastāṃ balākikām || 47 ||
śvetāṃ kamalapatrākṣīṃ prasannāṃ vāmanākṛtim|
dakṣiṇadvāratastadvannīlāṃ tu vanamālikām || 48 ||
paścimadvāramadhye tu raktavarṇāṃ vibhīṣikām|
udīcyadvāramadye tu śaṃkarīṃ madhudīdhitim || 49 ||
prāgādīśānaparyante bahirdvārapradeśataḥ|
indrādīn saṃsmaredaṣṭau lokapālān savāhanān || 50 ||
ata ūrdhvaṃ ca nāgeśaṃ brahmāṇaṃ ca vicintayet|
tadbahiśca tadasrāṇi vajrādīni vicintayet || 51 ||
somaśaṃkaradiṅmadhye khasthitaṃ saṃsmaret prabum|
viṣvaksenamudārāṅgamāyāntaṃ gaganāntarāt || 52 ||
anuktānāmidānīṃ me dhyānaṃ śṛṇu puraṃdara|
amṛtātmānamabhrāṅgaṃ puṇḍarīkāyatekṣaṇam || 53 ||
śaṅkhacakragadāpadmadharaṃ śrīvatsavakṣasam|
pītāmbaraṃ caturbāhuṃ vāsudevaṃ vicintayet || 54 ||
tuṣāranicayākāraṃ nīlavasraṃ caturbhujam|
smaret saṃkarṣaṇaṃ sīrasaunandakadharaṃ param || 55 ||
saunandakaḥ kāmapālasya gadā|
varadābhayahastaṃ ca nimagnoddharaṇakṣamam|
pradyumnaṃ saṃsmaredraktaṃ raktavāsasamīśvaram || 56 ||
śārṅgaṃ śarāṃśca bibrāṇaṃ varadaṃ cābhayapradam|
saṃsmaredaniruddhaṃ ca pītaṃ viśadavāsasam || 57 ||
khaḍgakheṭakahastaṃ ca varābhayakaraṃ param|
āsīnāḥ sarva evaite puruṣāḥ puṣkarekṣaṇāḥ || 58 ||
paśyanto diśamīśānāṃ māṃ ca viṣṇuṃ ca śāśvatam|
siñcanto'mṛtakumbhairnau himaśailanibhā gajāḥ || 59 ||
sitaśoṇitavarṇāṅgaṃ śuklavasraṃ caturbhujam|
padmāsanopaviṣṭaṃ ca padmaśaṅkhakarāṅkitam || 60 ||
hṛnmudrālaṃkṛtakaraṃ vāmenābhayadaṃ sadā|
sitābharaṇavasrāḍhyaṃ karpūrāliptavigraham || 61 ||
āvayoḥ saṃmukhāsīnaṃ hṛnmantraṃ saṃsmared budhaḥ|
vandhujīvopamaṃ raktaṃ padmacakradharaṃ param || 62 ||
kākālikajjalaśyāmaṃ padmakaumodakīdharam|
dakṣiṇenātmamudrāḍhyaṃ pareṇābhayadāyinam || 63 ||
kāka iva, aliriva, kajjalamiva ca śyāmaḥ ; tamityarthaḥ| athavā kākāliḥ atyantakṛṣṇavarṇaḥ kākaviśeṣaḥ|
liptaṃ mṛgamadenaiva puṣpādyairasitairyutam|
āvayoḥ saṃmukhāsīnaṃ kavacaṃ saṃsmaret prabhum || 64 ||
haridrāruṇasaṃkāśaṃ gadāpadmadharaṃ kramāt|
svamudrākaraṇavyagrapūrvabhāgakaradvayam || 65 ||
sauvarṇāmbarabhūṣāḍhyaṃ pralayānalavikramam|
āvayoḥ saṃmukhāsīnaṃ smaredasraṃ mahodyamam || 66 ||
nārīrvā saṃsmaredetān hṛdādīn sādhakottamaḥ|
uktabhūṣaṇaveṣāḍhyāḥ svānurūpānulepanāḥ || 67 ||
ityuktaṃ dhyānamaṅgānāṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ śubham|
kaustubhaṃ dvibhujaṃ dhyāyet sahasrārkasamaprabham || 68 ||
pradīptaveṣabhūṣāḍhyaṃ svamudrāṃ dadhataṃ hṛdi|
pañcavarṇakṛtāṃ kāntāṃ vanamālāṃ śubhekṣaṇām || 69 ||
vanamālāmiti| dhyāyediti śeṣaḥ|
prauḍhasrīsadṛśīṃ mudrāṃ dadhatīṃ hastayordvayoḥ|
padmaṃ padmamukhaṃ dhyāyet prasannaṃ candrasaṃnibham || 70 ||
dvibhujaṃ cārusarvāṅgaṃ karābhyāṃ mudrikāṃ dadhat|
navadūrvāṅkuraśyāmaṃ pāśeśaṃ pannagānanam || 71 ||
dvibhujaṃ yāmyahastena svāṃ mudrāṃ dadhataṃ param|
tathā varadahastaṃ ca bhīmarūpaṃ bhayāvaham || 72 ||
yāmyahastena| dakṣiṇahastenetyarthaḥ|
saṃsmaredaṅkuśaṃ tīkṣṇaṃ dīrghanāsaṃ bhayānakam|
yāmyena dadhataṃ mudrāṃ pāṇinā savyato varam || 73 ||
prasiddhā lokapālāste veṣākṛtivibhūṣaṇaiḥ|
narākṛtīni śasrāṇi cihnayuktāni mūrdhani || 74 ||
dvārapālikāḥ balākikādayaḥ pūrvaṃ 47,48,49 ślokeṣūktāḥ|
śaṅkhacakradharo dhyeyo viṣvaksenaścaturbhujaḥ|
sākṣādviṣṇusamo mudrāvyāpṛto'nyakaradvaye || 75 ||
dvārāgrasthaṃ tato dhyāyedgaruḍaṃ bīmalocanam|
raktatuṇḍaṃ mahāghoṇaṃ taptacāmīkaraprabham || 76 ||
pṛthudaṃṣṭraṃ gṛdhravaktraṃ pakṣamaṇḍalamaṇḍitam|
iti te parivārāṇāṃ dhyānamuktaṃ samāsataḥ || 77 ||
āvayoḥ sthūlayorūrdhve smaret sūkṣmāṃ tu tārikām|
dāṃpatyaṃ bibhratīṃ sūkṣmāṃ lakṣyālakṣyāmivānaghām || 78 ||
tadūrdhve tu parāṃ tārāṃ pūrṇaṣāḍguṇyacinmayīm|
dāṃpatyamāvayordivyaṃ tatparaṃ pārameśvaram || 79 ||
sarvataḥpāṇipādaṃ tat sarvato'kṣiśiromukham|
śabdārthau bhāsayat svena tejasā nityamudyatā || 80 ||
pratibimbamivaitasmin mantracakramaśeṣataḥ|
evaṃ kramotkramākāraistārikāṃ vitatāṃ parām || 81 ||
bhāvena tattvato buddhvā yathānyāsaṃ samarcayet|
āditaḥ sakalāvāsāvarcayennau dhiyānaghau || 82 ||
arghyādiprāpaṇāntena bhogajālena mantravit|
śaktyādiviṣvaksenāntaṃ parivāraṃ tato'rcayet || 83 ||
sarvādau garuḍaṃ devīścatasro dvārapālikāḥ|
bhogayāgakrameṇāyaṃ nyāsaste śakra darśitaḥ|
yāvanto yādṛśā ye ca bhogāstāṃstvaṃ nibodha me || 84 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre bahiryāgaprakāśo nāmāṣṭatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
ityaṣṭatriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

<39>
ekonacatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ
śṛṇu bhogān sureśāna dattaistuṣyāmi yairaham|
yeṣu me vitatā śaktirbhogyabhogāya tiṣṭhate || 1 ||
tiṣṭhate; svāśayaṃ prakāśayantī tiṣṭhatītyarthaḥ| prakāśanārthe taṅ|
yathāśaktyanurūpeṇa tattadbhogopakalpane|
divyaśaktiprabhedaistairvicitraiḥ styānatāṃ gataiḥ || 2 ||
saṃpūrṇamīsvarārhaṃ tadbhāvanīyamaśeṣataḥ|
mṛdvāstaraṇasaṃstīrṇamādāvāsanamuttamam || 3 ||
bhogaviśeṣā ucyante mṛdvityādinā| bhagavadārādhane ṣaḍāsanāni; yathā mantrāsanam, snānāsanam, alaṃkārāsanam, bhojyāsanam, punarmantrāsanam, paryahkāsanaṃ ceti| tatra prathamaṃ mantrāsanamatrocyate|
arghyaṃ pādyaṃ madhūparkastathaivācamanīyakam|
praṇāmapūrvakaṃ paścādātmātmīyanivedanam || 4 ||
tasminnāsane samarpaṇīyānāha arghyamityādinā| ātmātmīyanivedanaprakāramāha pādme  "dāso'haṃ te jagannātha saputrādiparigrahaḥ| preṣyaṃ praśādhi kartavye māṃ niyuṅkṣva hite sadā||" iti|
anujñāpya tataḥ paścāt snānāsanamanuttamam|
pādapīṭhamathārghyaṃ ca tataḥ pādyapratigrahaḥ || 5 ||
dvitīyaṃ snānāsanamatra| atra mantraḥ sāttvate  "sphuṭīkṛtaṃ mayā deva tvidaṃ snānapare tvayi| sapādapīṭhaṃ paramaṃ śubhaṃ snānāsanaṃ mahat| āsādayāśu snānārthaṃ madanugrahakāmyayā||" iti|
pādyāmbu pāduke snānaśāṭī mātrā ca śālikā|
darpaṇaṃ gandhatoyaṃ ca pāṇiprakṣālanārthakam || 6 ||
mātrā ca śāliketi| akṣatāgraśubhravarṇaśālitaṇḍulapūrṇapātramatra mātrāpadārthaḥ| tathā ca pādme  "śālitaṇḍulamātrayā" iti| etacca mātrādānamārādhanamadhyāpatitavaikalyaparihārāya|
dantakāṣṭhaṃ ca vadanaprakṣālācamanāmbunī|
gandhatailaṃ ca cūrṇaṃ ca śāligodhūmasaṃbhavam || 7 ||
haridrācūrṇasaṃmiśramīṣatpadmakabhāvitam|
udvartanārthaṃ tadanu snānārthaṃ khalisaṃyutam || 8 ||
khaliḥ mṛgamadādivāsanāvāsitaṃ ghanībhūtagandhatailakiṭṭakam|
uṣṇāmbu candanaṃ candramiśritaṃ lepanārthakam|
kṣīraṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ gavyaṃ madhvaikṣavarasaṃ tathā || 9 ||
candraḥ ghanasāraḥ|
sugandhāmalakābhiśca lodhratoyaṃ tataḥ param|
raktacandanatoyaṃ ca rajanīvāri cottamam || 10 ||
granthiparṇīpayaḥ paścāt tataśca tagarodakam|
priyaṅgujaṭilāsiddhārthakasarvauṣadhījalam || 11 ||
puṣpapatraphalāmbhāṃsi bījagandhodake tathā|
hemaratnasarittīrthakevalāmbūni ca kramāt || 12 ||
snānīyāṃmbusametāni deyānyambūnyamūni tu|
arghyapātrāttathaivārghyaṃ snānānāmantarāntarā || 13 ||
dadyāt sapuṣpatoyena kṣālanaṃ cāntarāntarā|
snānaśiṣṭāmbusaṃpūrṇaṃ haridrāśālisaṃbhṛtam || 14 ||
snapanānantaraṃ nīrājanamucyate snānaśiṣṭeti|
sragādisaṃyutaṃ kumbhaṃ haste kṛtvāparatra tu|
siddhārthairdhūpavat pātraṃ bhrāmayitvā tu mūrdhani || 15 ||
haste; vāmahaste| aparatra; dakṣiṇahaste|
bahiḥ kṣipettato dadyāt sudhaute cāṅgaśāṭike|
keśodakāpakarṣārthamambaraṃ dehavārihṛt || 16 ||
antarīyottarīye dve sudhaute vāsasī śubhe|
bhāvite gandhadhūpena dadyād bhadrāsanaṃ tataḥ || 17 ||
bhadrāsanam; snānāsanam|
śodhayet pūrṇakumbhaistu khaplutaṃ bhāvayeddharim|
alaṃkārāsanaṃ paścāddeyaṃ mṛdvāstarolbaṇam || 18 ||
khaplutam; dvādasāntasthitam| tṛtīyamalaṃkārāsanamāha alaṃkāreti|
tatra sarvaṃ pradātavyamarghyapādyādi pūrvavat|
vivecanaṃ ca keśānāṃ kaṅkatena praśodhanam || 19 ||
kaṅkataṃ keśaprasādhanī|
śīrṣaṇyāstāḥ sumanasastataścūḍopakalpanam|
candanādyāḥ sugandhāśca vyajanaṃ śoṣaṇārthakam || 20 ||
makuṭādyā alaṃkārāḥ pradeyāḥ paramādbhutāḥ|
srajo nānāvidhākārāḥ sāttvikaiḥ kusumaiścitāḥ || 21 ||
puṣpāñjaliḥ padadvandve prākāraḥ sumanaścayaiḥ|
gandhadravyasuśītena hyañjanenāñjanaṃ dṛśoḥ || 22 ||
tathaivālambhanaṃ cāpi lalāṭatilakaṃ tathā|
ādarśo vimalo mṛṣṭaḥ paritaśca samīkṣaṇam || 23 ||
pradīpaśca pradhūpaśca vāhanaṃ cetanetarat|
stutimaṅgalagītāni nṛttavāditradarśanam || 24 ||
cetanetarat vāhanamiti| suvarṇādinirmitaṃ hastyaśvādi|
mātrāśca ratnasaṃpūrṇā bhogacchidraprapūraṇāḥ|
buddhyā viracitāstāstā rājarājocitāḥ kriyāḥ || 25 ||
alaṃkārāsanasthāya tvete bhogā hi manmayāḥ|
atha bhojyāsanaṃ deyamargyapādyādikaṃ tathā || 26 ||
manmayā ityanenādhyāyādāvukto viṣayaḥ smāryate| turīyaṃ bhojyāsanamupavarṇyate atheti|
yajeta madhuparkeṇa yathā tadavadhāraya|
payaso madhuno dadhnaḥ saṃyogo madhuparkakaḥ || 27 ||
madhuparkamāha payasa iti|
pātraṃ puraḥ pratiṣṭhāpya madhuparkeṇa pūritam|
arhaṇaṃ tarpaṇaṃ cārghyāt pṛthak pātradvaye bhavet || 28 ||
arhaṇenārcanaṃ pūrvaṃ tataśca madhuparkakam|
tarpaṇaṃ tarpaṇīyābhistato niṣpuṃsanaṃ kare || 29 ||
arhaṇamāpośanam| tarpaṇam; pānīyatīrtham| niṣpuṃsanam; hastodvartanam|
deyamācamanaṃ paścānmātrā gaurmādhuparkikī|
yaṣṭavyamannayajñena sāṅgena madhuparkavat || 30 ||
punaḥ madhuparkadānasaṃbhāvitavaikalyaparihārāya gavā mātrādānamatrocyate|
pradeyā annayajñārthā mātrāḥ śālyannanirmitāḥ|
sakarpūraṃ ca tāmbūlaṃ pradeyamanuvāsanam || 31 ||
pañcame mantrāsane samarpaṇīyāni tāmbūlādīni|
tataśca viśramārthāya vimānaṃ paramādbhutam|
pradeyaṃ tatra dātavyaṃ sarvamarghyādi pūrvavat || 32 ||
ṣaṣṭhaṃ paryahkāsanamucyate tataśceti|
japaṃ samācaret paścādalakṣyamitarairjanaiḥ|
mantrasaṃskṛtayā samyak śubhayā hyakṣamālayā || 33 ||
japasaṃkhyāgaṇanopakaraṇeṣvakṣamālā praśastā|
prākṛtastvaṅgulībhistu parvabhistu daśottaraḥ|
śatottarādisaṃkhyastu vijñeyo hyakṣamālayā || 34 ||
vācikaḥ kṣudrakarmārthamupāṃśu siddhikarmaṇi|
mānaso mokṣalakṣmīdo dhyānātmā sarvasiddhikṛt || 35 ||
japaviśeṣo vācikādiḥ|
akṣamālā tathā kāryā na dṛśyā prākṛtairyathā|
akṣāsthimātrā maṇya uttamāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 36 ||
dhātrīphalāsthisadṛśā madhyamāḥ parikīrtitāḥ|
adhamā badarāsthyābhāḥ śreṣṭhamaṣṭaśatātmakam || 37 ||
tadardhaṃ madhyamaṃ proktaṃ tadardhamadhamaṃ smṛtam|
sauvarṇaṃ dravyasiddhyarthe puṣṭyarthe pitṛkarmaṇi || 38 ||
akṣamālāmaṇiviśeṣānāha sauvarṇamityādinā|
rājataṃ tāmrarūpaṃ tu medhāvīryajayāptaye|
trāpuṣaṃ yakṣiṇīsiddhau saisaṃ rakṣaḥpiśācayoḥ || 39 ||
vetālasādhanārthaṃ tu rītijaṃ kāṃsyajaṃ tu tat|
akṣasūtraṃ parijñeyaṃ nāgapannagasādhane || 40 ||
rītiḥ pittalalohaviśeṣaḥ|
āyasaṃ kṣudrakarmārthamiti dhātumayo maṇiḥ|
āyurārogyabhūtyarthaḥ sarvo maṇimayo maṇiḥ || 41 ||
mokṣāya śāntaye caiva sphāṭiko maṇirucyate|
saubhāgye vaidrumaḥ kāryaḥ sautraḥ kāryastu muktaye || 42 ||
saitraḥ; tantunirmitaḥ|
śāntaye muktaye puṣṭyai tulasīmūlajo maṇiḥ|
sarvasiddhipradaḥ pādmo maṇiḥ śāṅkhaḥ śriyai mataḥ || 43 ||
āyuḥprajāyaśodaḥ syānmauktiko maṇiruttamaḥ|
ādadyādekameteṣāṃ śubhakāle guṇādhike || 44 ||
asreṇa gandhatoyena kṣālayettadanantaram|
śāṇakārpāsajorṇānāṃ kṛtvā sūtraṃ navaṃ dṛḍham || 45 ||
triguṇaṃ triguṇīkṛtya caturdhā vā yathā dṛḍham|
kṣālayitvāsratoyena tenaiva grathayenmaṇīn || 46 ||
anūnānadhikāṃstulyānacalān grathitāntarān|
iṣṭasaṃkhyāmaṇiprotasūtrāntadvitayopamam||
maṇiṃ prakalpayenmerumakṣamālāvidhistvayam || 47 ||
meruḥ japasūtrapradhānagranthistho bṛhanmaṇiviśeṣaḥ|
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre bahiryāgaprakāśo nāmaikonacatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
ityekonacatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

<40>
catvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śakraḥ
namaste vārisaṃbhūte namaste padmasaṃbhave|
prakāramakṣamālāyāḥ pratiṣṭhāyā vadāmbuje || 1 ||
śrīḥ
sādhitāmakṣamālāṃ tu sthāpayedbhājane śubhe|
pūjayitvārghyapuṣpādyaistasyāḥ śuddhiṃ samācaret || 2 ||
sādhitām; nirmitām|
astreṇa dagdhvā nirvāpya varmaṇāpyāyayecchriyā|
paramāmṛtarūpiṇyā tāṃ mālāṃ bhāvayettataḥ || 3 ||
nirvāpya; viśoṣya| śriyā; śrīmantreṇa| bhāvayet; vakṣyamāṇarūpeṇa dhyāyet|
caturbhujāmanaupamyāṃ manmayīṃ māmivāparām|
varadābhayahastāṃ ca baddhāñjalikaradvayām || 4 ||
brahmadvārāmbujanmasthāṃ devīṃ dhyāyecchikhopamām|
tāṃ parāṃ vaiṣṇavīṃ śaktiṃ manmayīṃ madabhedinīm || 5 ||
brahmadvāraṃ brahmarandhrākhyanāḍī| śikhā vahniśikhā|
dvādaśānte vicintyātha kramāddhyāyeddhṛdambuje|
hṛtpadmādutthitāṃ būyo brahmarandhrādvinirgatām || 6 ||
dvādaśānte; mūrdhna upari dvādaśāṅgulaparimitasthāne|
śanaiḥ śanairullasantīṃ mālāsthāṃ tāṃ vicintayet|
aṅgopāṅgakramopetāṃ sthūlasūkṣmaparātmikām || 7 ||
māṃ dhyāyet tārikākārāṃ tatra śaktau sureśvara|
maṇīn sūtraṃ tathā mālāṃ mālāsthāṃ vaiṣṇavīmapi || 8 ||
ekārṇavīkṛtaṃ sarvaṃ māyāṃ dhyāyet sureśvara|
mayā dattāṃ vibhāvyaināṃ saṃskṛtāmakṣamālikām || 9 ||
japaṃ samācaret paścāt pūrvoktena vidhānataḥ|
anunmiṣat paraṃ sūkṣmaṃ sthūlamātmānameva ca || 10 ||
lakṣmīnārāyaṇākāraṃ pañcakaṃ bhāvayedidam|
turyātītaṃ tathā turyaṃ suṣuptisvapnajāgarāḥ || 11 ||
avasthāpañcakaṃ tadvat tatkartṛkaraṇādikam|
kartrunmeṣaṃ tathā tatsthaṃ karaṇaṃ bāhyakaṃ tathā || 12 ||
mantrākṣaraṃ tathā sthūlaṃ sarvaṃ tattanmayaṃ smaret|
hṛtpuṇḍarīkamadhyasthāṃ manmukhāmbhojaniḥsṛtām || 13 ||
smarecchabdamayīṃ śaktiṃ vaiṣṇavīṃ madabhedinīm|
tasyā vinirgatāṃ dhyāyenmātṛkāṃ mantramātaram || 14 ||
manmayīṃ saṃsmarenmālāṃ japyamantramayīṃ tataḥ|
latāyāmiva puṣpāṇi mantrān vai tatra saṃsmaret || 15 ||
sphuraṇaṃ maṇisaṃsparśe hṛllayaṃ ca tadatyaye|
bhāvayan mantranāthasya japaṃ kuryādvicakṣaṇaḥ || 16 ||
hṛdbhāsvarūpasaṃsūtibahirantaḥkramotkramāt|
iti smaran japedekavāraṃ tatkoṭisaṃmitam || 17 ||
japaṃ samāpya vidhivannyasyenmayi japaṃ kṛtam|
śaktiṃ tāṃ manmukhāntaḥsthāṃ japarūpāṃ vicintayet || 18 ||
dīpe dhūpe tathārghye ca ghaṇṭānādaṃ samācaret|
āvāhane tathārghye ca naivedye madhuparkake || 19 ||
prīṇane ca prayuñjīta ghaṇṭānādaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ|
na vinā pūjayā cālyā tāṃ vinā pūjayenna ca || 20 ||
kāryasiddhimabhīpsadbhiriha cāmutra cobhayoḥ|
sā hi ghaṇṭābhidhā śaktirvāgīśā ca sarasvatī || 21 ||
vāci mantrāḥ sthitāḥ sarve vācyaṃ mantreṣu cākhilam|
etasyāṃ cālyamānāyāṃ mantrā vatsā iva drutam || 22 ||
kāṅkṣamāṇāḥ samāyānti tāṃ ghaṇṭāṃ mantramātaram|
adhomukhaṃ tu brahmāṇḍaṃ dyāyellokakulākulam || 23 ||
nālaṃ tasyāstadūrdhve tu vṛttaṃ padmaṃ smaredbudhaḥ|
aṣṭapatraṃ śubhaṃ śvetaṃ karṇikākesarānvitam || 24 ||
tanmadhye cintayeddevīṃ ghaṇṭāmaṣṭabhujānvitām|
mukhyahastacatuṣkeṇa pāśaśaṅkhāmbujāṅkuśān || 25 ||
parabāhucatuṣkeṇa dadhatīmakṣasūtrakam|
vijñānapustakaṃ samyagabhayaṃ ca varaṃ tathā || 26 ||
padmāsanāmambujākṣīṃ padmagarbhasamatviṣam|
badmamālādharāṃ pītasitavasrānulepanām || 27 ||
mantraughamudgirantīṃ ca brahmādiparisaṃstutām|
tārikāmuccaran dīrghaṃ yugmāṃ saṃcālayedimām || 28 ||
anayā pūjayan mantrī mantrasiddhiṃ nigacchati|
tato gurūn samānīya manmayān vāpi vaiṣṇavān || 29 ||
pradadyāt prāpaṇārdhaṃ tu tebhyo manmantramuccaran|
atha vahnigatāṃ samyagagnīṣomamayīṃ parām || 30 ||
vahnisaṃtarpaṇamucyate athetyādinā|
tarpayenmāṃ sureśāna yathāvadavadhāraya|
tata uttaradigbhāge devāgārādbahistu vā || 31 ||
bhūbhāge lakṣaṇopete kuṇḍaṃ kuryāt salakṣaṇam|
caturaśraṃ samaṃ yadvā padmākāraṃ manoharam || 32 ||
śatārdhahomasaṃkhyāyāṃ kuṇḍaṃ syād dvādaśāṅgulam|
aṣṭottaraśate'ratnisamaṃ hastaṃ sahasrake || 33 ||
homasaṃkyānuguṇyena kuṇḍavistṛtirucyate śatārdhetyādinā|
ayutākhye dvihastaṃ ca lakṣahome catuṣkaram|
koṭihome'ṣṭahastaṃ syācchāsrataḥ kārayecca tat || 34 ||
trikoṇamapi vā kuryāddhomakuṇḍaṃ trimekhalam|
uddhṛtya tārayā pūrvaṃ tristayā prokṣayedbhuvam || 35 ||
tayā; tārayā|
śoṣaṇaṃ dāhanaṃ plāvaṃ tārayā samyagācaret|
ādhāraśaktyādyārabhya pūjyaṃ bhāvāsanāvadhi || 36 ||
bhāvāsanaṃ pūrvamādhāraśaktiprakaraṇoktam|
tatra nārāyaṇākhyāṃ vai śaktiṃ tejomayīṃ parām|
manmayīmamṛtākārāṃ sarvātiśayarūpiṇīm || 37 ||
sarvaśaktisamūhasthāṃ sarvavastvantarasthitām|
avatārya hṛdambhojāt sṛṣṭimārgeṇa śāśvatīm || 38 ||
tārikāsphuraṇākārāmavatīrya hṛdambujāt|
recakena vinikṣipya kuṇḍamadhyāmbujāntare || 39 ||
saṃpūjya gandhapuṣpādyaiḥ padmamudrāṃ pradarśya ca|
dhyāyedṛtamatīṃ śaktiṃ susnātāmahatāmbarām || 40 ||
dhyāyedityādinā agnervaiṣṇavīkaraṇamucyate|
dhyāyet sarvātmikāṃ śaktiṃ tāmeva tvadharāraṇim|
uttaraṃ cāraṇiṃ dhyāyet sarvatejomayaṃ harim || 41 ||
mathnīyāt tārayā samyak tathā caivānutārayā|
utpannaṃ tārayādāya kṛśānuṃ śaktisaṃbhavam || 42 ||
abhigūhyāṅgulībhistu saṃskuryādvaiṣṇavākyayā|
cūḍāṃ ca tārayā kuryādannaprāśanapūrvakam || 43 ||
abhigūhya; saṃvṛtya| annaprāśaneti jātakarmanāmakaraṇayorapyupalakṣaṇam|
upanīya tato vahniṃ tārayā cānutārayā|
tataḥ svāhāsvadhābhyāṃ tu devībhyāṃ jātavedasaḥ || 44 ||
pāṇigrahaṇakaṃ kuryāt tārayā tvanutārayā|
sarvaṃ dhyānamayaṃ kāryaṃ jātanāmādikarma tat || 45 ||
lohapāṣāṇamaṇyutthavahnau kāryavaśāt kṛte|
laukike vāpi saṃskāraṃ niṣekādi samācaret || 46 ||
ādhāya taijase pātre mṛnmaye'bhinave tu vā|
devīsahāyaṃ svāheśaṃ tārayaivārcayet sudhīḥ || 47 ||
pūraṇenopasaṃhṛtya svātmanyupaśamaṃ nayet|
kramādānandaśaktyā taṃ saṃhāreṇa tu yojayet || 48 ||
sṛṣṭimārgeṇa taṃ bhūyo'pyavatārya padāt padam|
kuṇḍamadhyāmbujāntaḥsthāṃ tārayā bhuvamānayet || 49 ||
vahniṃ svāhāsvadheśānamagnīṣomamayaṃ yajet|
samidbhistisṛbhistārāmuccarannekayaikayā || 50 ||
śālītilākṣataiḥ paścāt tata ājyāhutitrayam|
uccārya tārikāṃ pūrvaṃ bodhayeti dviruccaret || 51 ||
tatrastho budhyate vahniryathāvacca hutaḥ svayam|
paryagnikaraṇaṃ kāryaṃ tārayaivārdrapāṇinā || 52 ||
stṛṇīyāt tārayā darbhaisredhā tredhā caturdhiśam|
udagbhāge'gnikuṇḍasya stṛṇīyāddarbhasaṃcayam || 53 ||
praṇītāprokṣaṇīpātre darvīdhmau sruksruvau tathā|
ājyasthālīṃ pavitre ca sarvaṃ tatra nidhāpayet || 54 ||
praṇītāṃ tārayāpūrya gandhayuktena vāriṇā|
pavitrābyāṃ trirutpūya māṃ dhyāyet tārayā śriyam || 55 ||
vahnerudak pratiṣṭhāpya prokṣaṇīṃ pūrayecchriyā|
tāmutpūya tataḥ prokṣya yāgopakaraṇaṃ samam || 56 ||
ājyapātraṃ samādāya tasminnājyaṃ nidhāya ca|
brahma sarpiḥsamudrotthaṃ taddhyātvāgnerudaggatam || 57 ||
tārayā samabhijvālya darbhāgre vinidhāya ca|
tayā punarabhijvālya paryagnikṛtimācaret || 58 ||
vahneḥ paścāt pratiṣṭhāpya pavitradvitayena tat|
utpūya tārayāgnau tat pavitradvitayaṃ kṣipet || 59 ||
paridadhyāt paridhibhiryajñayogyaiścaturdiśam|
agniśarvadiśoḥ sthāpya kuṇḍamadhye samiddvayam || 60 ||
tata idhmaṃ samādhāya śuṣkaṃ pañcadaśātmakam|
tārayā pariṣicyāgniṃ sruksruvau paritāpayet || 61 ||
nimṛjya tārayā paścāttau prokṣyātha srucā tayā|
vāyoragnidiśaṃ yāvadyātoḥ śarvadiśāvadhi || 62 ||
dhārayā srāvayedvahnau madhye dadyāt sruvāhutim|
aṣṭottaraśataṃ vāpi tadardhaṃ vā tadarthakam || 63 ||
sruveṇājyāhutīrdadyāt svāhāntāṃ tārikāṃ gṛṇan|
ayaṃ yogyo bhavedvahnirbhagavaddhavyavāhane || 64 ||
ādadhyāt samidhaḥ sapta tatastārikayā sudhīḥ|
yajñakāṣṭhamayīrādau brahmakṣatratarūdbhavāḥ || 65 ||
brahmataruḥ palāśaḥ| kṣatrataruḥ mucukundanāmā taruḥ| daśāparā iti| avaśiṣṭā daśa samidha daśa samidha ityarthaḥ|
tārayā samidho dadyāt kāṣṭharūpā daśāparāḥ|
māmeva bhāvayenmadhye viṣṇoraṅkasthitāṃ parām || 66 ||
brahmānandamayāmbhojakarṇikāsthasya vai vibhoḥ|
aṅkasthāṃ bhāvayallam̐kṣmīṃ tā dadyātsamidho'khilaḥ || 67 ||
tataḥ puṣpamayīṃ dadyād dhūpadravyamayīṃ tathā|
etāstu trividhā deyā hastenaiva manīṣiṇā || 68 ||
madhuparkamayīṃ paścāt sruveṇa juhuyāt sudhīḥ|
srucyannaṃ caturādāya sarpiṣāpūryaṃ pūrvavat || 69 ||
tayaivānnāhutiṃ dadyāt svāhāntāṃ tārikāṃ gṛṇan|
sruveṇājyāhutīḥ pūrvasamitsaṃkhyāḥ samācaret || 70 ||
puṣpāñjalimupādāya vahnisthāmarcayeddhiyā|
nityayāgo mamaitāvānūrdhvaṃ kāmyāhutiṃ kṣipet || 72 ||
daśavāraṃ tārayaiva kṣamasveti vadan dhiyā|
praṇītāmupasaṃhṛtya śucisthāne ninīya ca || 73 ||
prahṛtya paridhīn sarvān sam̐srāvaṃ samācaret|
staraṃ prahṛtya vahnau ca ghṛtenāpūrya ca sruvam || 74 ||
dadyāt pūrṇāhutiṃ dīrghāṃ svāhāntāṃ tārikāṃ gṛṇan|
śaktyā sametāṃ tāṃ tārāṃ vahnisthāṃ manmayīṃ parām || 75 ||
māṃ cāpyaṅkasthitāṃ viṣṇornāsikāsaṃdhimārgataḥ|
marucchaktyā samākṛṣya hṛtpadme viniveśya ca || 76 ||
yāgabhūmiṃ sametyātha navapadmasthayā mayā|
ekīkṛtya mayi nyasyedarchāsthāyāṃ kṛtiṃ tu tām || 77 ||
prāgeva vibhajedannaṃ prāpaṇāt saṃpradānataḥ|
tenānnena yajet samyagviṣvaksena caturbhujam || 78 ||
maṇḍalāntamupānīya samāhūyāmbarāntarāt|
navāmrapatrasadṛśaṃ piṅgabhrūśmaśrulocanam || 79 ||
pītavasraṃ caturdaṃṣṭraṃ svamudrādvitayānvitam|
gadākhaḍgadharaṃ devamabhyarcya kramaśaḥ sudhīḥ || 80 ||
sāṅgamudrāmathādarśya gatvā kuṇḍasamīpataḥ|
viṣvaksenastato bhakatyā tarpaṇīyastilākṣataiḥ || 81 ||
ita ārabhya pañcārdhāni jayākhyāvacanamanukurvanti|
vauṣaḍantena mantreṇa dadyāt pūrṇāhutiṃ tataḥ|
maṇḍale pūjayitvātha kuryāttasya visarjanam || 82 ||
svamantreṇa suraśreṣṭha kṣamasveti padaṃ vadan|
mudrāṃ ca darśayettaṃ ca nabhasyutpatitaṃ smaret || 83 ||
viṣvaksenārcanaṃ sarvamagādhe'mbuni nikṣipet|
toyenāsraprajaptena plāvayenmaṇḍalaṃ ca tat || 84 ||
tataḥ saṃpūjayet sarvān lokapālānaśeṣataḥ|
puṣpārghyairannadānāntairekāhutyā ca vahnitaḥ || 85 ||
bahvibhirvā yathāśakti svaiḥ svairmantrairyathāvidhi|
pūjayitvā visṛjyaitāṃstadasrāṇyapi pūjayatet || 86 ||
sarvāṃśca parivārāṃstān svasvasthānakrameṇa tu|
rcayet kṣetrapālādīn vahnimadhye ca tarpayet || 87 ||
ādhāraśakterārabhya pīṭhaśaktīśca sarvaśaḥ|
gaṇeśādyāśca saṃpūjyāḥ saṃtarpyāśca ghṛtādikaiḥ || 88 ||
sakṛtkṛtyā trivṛtyā vā pūrṇāḥ sarveṣvatha kṣipet|
lakṣmyādīnpūjayetsarvān viṣvaksenārcanātpurā || 89 ||
viṣvaksenārcanāt paścāllokapālārcanaṃ kramāt|
tato vediṃ viśodhyātha darbhān saṃstīrya dakṣiṇān || 90 ||
pitṝn yajet krameṇaiva prāpaṇārthāvaśeṣataḥ|
kṛtvā piṇḍatrayaṃ tena pitṛbhyo nirvapet stare || 91 ||
arghyājjalaṃ tathā dadyādekaikasyaikamañjalim|
vaiṣṇavāyāthavā dadyādbrāhnaṇāya viśeṣiṇe || 92 ||
brāhnaṇāya viśeṣiṇe iti| viśiṣṭabrāhnaṇāyetyarthaḥ| etacca dhaniketaraviṣayam| dhanikaviṣaye tu caturo brāhnaṇān, tadalābhe ekaṃ vā śrāddhavidhinā bhojayediti sāttvate uktam |
pitṝnuddiśya vai bhaktaṃ tattannāmānudeśavat|
saṃnyasya mayi tat sarvamantardhānamavekṣya ca || 93 ||
arghyādyamupasaṃhṛtya varmāsre pratihṛtya ca|
upasaṃhṛtya ca nyāsamanuyāgaṃ samācaret || 94 ||
anuyāgaḥ prāpaṇāvaśeṣopayogo bhojanādiḥ|
asreṇa tārayā prokṣya tārayā pariṣicya ca|
upastīrya tayā cāpo dadyāt prāṇāhutīstayā || 95 ||
tayopastīrya; tārayā nipīyetyarthaḥ|
adīkṣitastvanuyajaṃstattanmantrānusaṃhitām|
tārikāmuccaran kuryānmāṃ cāntaḥsthāṃ vibhāvayet || 96 ||
pūrvoktaṃ tārayā prokṣaṇapariṣecanāpośanaprāṇāhutyādikaṃ dīkṣitaviṣayam| adīkṣitānāṃ tu tattatsūtrakṛduktamantraireva kartavyam|
somānandamayīṃ divyāṃ kramādannātmatāṃ gatām|
vīryarūparasākārāṃ tejovīryabalātmikām || 97 ||
aiśvaryaśaktivijñānarūpaṃ bhoktāramavyayam|
ātmānaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ bhāvayet puruṣottamam || 98 ||
tārikāmuccaran paścādapidhāyānnamambhasā|
ācamya dvistato nyasyedanuyāgaṃ tato mayi || 99 ||
apidhāyāmbhaseti| uttarāpośanaṃ pītvetyarthaḥ|
atha svādhyāyamabhyasyed dinaśeṣaṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ|
caturvidhāni śāsrāṇi tadutthaṃ tārikādikam || 100 ||
svādhyāyam; vedadivyaśāsrādiparicayam| caturvidhāni āgamasiddhāntamantrasiddhāntatantrasiddhāntatantrāntarasiddhāntapratipādakāni śāsrāṇi|
siddhāntānapi cāśeṣānasaṃlagnena cetasā|
svāśayapraviśuddhyarthaṃ samīkṣeta dhiyā svayā || 101 ||
saṃdhyāmupāsya vidhivadabhigamya ca māṃ dhiyā|
yogaṃ yuñjīta vidhivacchāsraśuddhena cetasā || 102 ||
pūrvapaścimayornaktaṃ yāmayordhātusāmyavān|
iti yāgavidhiḥ śakra vistareṇa pradarśitaḥ || 103 ||
naktaṃ prathamayāmaḥ caramayāmaśca yogakālaḥ| madhye jāyamānā nidrāpi bhagavati svātmasamarpaṇena yogarūpā bhāvanīyā|
vidhānasya tu saṃkṣepaṃ punarasya nibodha me|
saṃkṣepavistare kuryād deśakālānukūlataḥ || 104 ||
naiva kuryādapacchedaṃ yajedañjalināpi mām|
yajetobhau sahaivāvāṃ sūktena puruṣeṇa tu || 105 ||
apacchedaḥ; vicchedaḥ|
tathā madīyasūktena tābhyāṃ vā nau yajet pṛthak|
sthāne vā sarvamantrāṇāṃ tārikāmeva yojayet || 106 ||
kuryāt saṃkalpasaṃnyāsau dvāvevādyantayoḥ pṛthak|
akurvan bhoganirdeśaṃ kevalairvā yajettu taiḥ || 107 ||
tathā tathaiva kurvīta śaknuyāttu yathā yathā|
na tveva hāpayedyāgamapi dadyājjalāñjalim || 108 ||
śaknuyāditi| madārādhanasya nityatvāt nityasya ca yathāśaktyanuṣṭhānavidhānāt yathāśakti māmārādhayedityarthaḥ|
tārikāṃ vāpyadhīyīta dravyābhāve vicakṣaṇaḥ|
manasā bhāveyedrūpaṃ yattasyā yādṛśaṃ ca yat || 109 ||
nirāmiṣasya śuddhasya labdhalakṣyasya vai pade|
vikṣobhāya kriyāḥ sarvāḥ yā yāḥ śāsreṇa darśitāḥ || 110 ||
ātatasya ca sarvatra samatāmabhyupeyuṣaḥ|
kaḥ kiṃ kasmai kimarthaṃ vā kathaṃ vā śaknuyātkriyām || 111 ||
agnīṣomamayau hitvā panthānau sārvalaukikau|
tayorantaramāviśya mārgamūrdhvaṃ samācaret || 112 ||
layāgnidagdhadurmārgaḥ śītībhūto nirāmayaḥ|
sthūlasūkṣmaparātītapadākrāntivicakṣaṇaḥ || 113 ||
paśyañcchṛṇvanspṛśañjighrannaśnangacchansvapañśvasan|
pralapanvisṛjangṛhṇannunmiṣannimiṣannapi || 114 ||
akurvanneva tat sarvaṃ madbhūto hyanidaṃmayaḥ|
avasthādeśakālādyairanavasyūtayākhilaiḥ || 115 ||
anavasyūtayeti| aparicchinnayetyarthaḥ|
ahaṃtayā samākrānto dhūmapītāgnipītavat|
anatrā vartamāno hi sūryācandramasordvayoḥ || 116 ||
dhūmapītetyādi| yathā dhūmāgnyormadhye vartamāno dhūmenāgninā ca saṃbaddho bhavati, tathā sūryācandramasorantarā vartamāna ityarthaḥ|
vyaktamuddālya vai tālu dhruvasthāne niveśayan|
manaḥ śūnyamayaṃ bhāvamātiṣṭhan layasaṃmitam || 117 ||
evaṃ yo vartate yogī tārikāmananodyataḥ|
sa karmaṭhaḥ sa vai sāṃkhyaḥ sa yogī sa ca sāttvataḥ || 118 ||
sa ca pāśupato jñeyaḥ sarvasiddhāntagaśca saḥ|
ityevaṃ te mayoddiṣṭo madbhāvaḥ pāramārthikaḥ|
śṛṇu śeṣamaśeṣaṃ me yatte kiṃcid vivakṣitam || 119 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre nityavidhiprakāśo nāma catvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
iti catvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

<41>
ekacatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śakraḥ
namo nityānavadyāyai jananyai sarvadehinām|
ādhāreśātmarūpāyai śuddhāśuddhākhilādhvanām || 1 ||
bāhyāntaravibhāgena śruto yāgaḥ savistaraḥ|
saṃprati śrotumicchāmi tvatto dīkṣāvinirṇayam || 2 ||
śrīḥ
eko nārāyaṇaḥ śrīmān ṣāḍguṇyamahimojjvalaḥ|
tasya ṣāḍguṇyarūpāhaṃ śaktirekā sanātanī || 3 ||
ātmānaṃ vibhajāmyekā pañcadhā devasaṃvidā|
śabdarūpārtharūpābhyāṃ vṛttirūpeṇa vāsava || 4 ||
tathaivācāryarūpeṇa dīkṣākhyenāpareṇa tu|
yad dyati kleśakarmādīnīkṣayatyakhilaṃ padam || 5 ||
dīkṣāśabdanirvacanaṃ yadityādinā| dyati khaṇḍayati| "do avakhaṇḍane" iti dhātuḥ|
kṣapayitvā malaṃ sarvaṃ dadāti ca paraṃ padam|
dīkṣeti tena tattvajñairvarṇyate vedapāragaiḥ || 6 ||
nirvacanāntaraṃ kṣapayitveti|
dīkṣā sā trividhā tāvat sthūlasūkṣmaparātmanā|
punardīkṣyavibhedena trividhā sā caturvidhā || 7 ||
samayī putrakaścaiva tṛtīyaḥ sādhakastathā|
ācāryaśceti dīkṣyāste teṣāmanyatra vistaraḥ || 8 ||
mahāmaṇḍalayāgena havanādvātha kevalāt|
vācā kevalayā vāpi dīkṣaiṣā trividhā punaḥ || 9 ||
vittāḍhyasyālpavittasya dravyahīnasya ca kramāt|
ānīya dṛḍhasaṃkalpaṃ cirakālaparīkṣitam || 10 ||
ācāryaḥ praṇataṃ śiṣyaṃ saṃsārānalatāpitam|
navāmbujaṃ vidhāyādau tasmin kumbhaṃ samijya ca || 11 ||
samijyeti| saṃpūjyetyarthaḥ|
nirṇiktapāpmanaḥ śiṣyān prāyaścittaiḥ pṛthagvidhaiḥ|
susnātān dhautavasrāṃśca pavitrīkṛtavigrahān || 12 ||
ānīya bhagavadbhaktān śubhāḥ kanyāḥ sriyastathā|
pañcagavyena pūtāṃśca dantadhāvanapūrvakam || 13 ||
kanyāḥ sriyastatheti tāsāmapi tāntrikadīkṣādhikāramāha|
puṣpāñjalibhṛtaścaiva baddhākṣānnavavāsasā|
āpādamūrdhaparyantamekasūtraṃ prakalpayet || 14 ||
triguṇaṃ triguṇagranthīṃstatsūtre tattvasaṃkhyayā|
kṛtvā mūrdhādipādāntaṃ bāvayettattvapaddhatim || 15 ||
triguṇeti| sattvarajastamomayagranthīnityarthaḥ| tattvasaṃkhyā saptaviṃśatiḥ|
īśakālādi bhūmyantaṃ saptaviṃśatisaṃkhyayā|
tattvāni granthayo jñeyā granthisthāstu guṇāsrayaḥ || 16 ||
māyāvidyākriyātmānaste pāśāḥ parikīrtitāḥ|
sthūlasūkṣmātmako dehaḥ śubhāśubhaphalapradaḥ || 17 ||
rañjito'yaṃ guṇaiścitrairaśeṣakaluṣāspadam|
saṃpātahomakarmānte dehaṃ sūtramayaṃ svayam || 18 ||
saṃpātahomaḥ tattvāhutiḥ|
chitvā chitvā tu hotavyaṃ bhoganirmūlakāraṇam|
lalāṭe ceśvaraṃ dhyāyeccidrūpaṃ sarvatomukham || 19 ||
avayavabhedena tattvadhyānamāha lalāṭa iti|
svabījena sthitaṃ dhyātvā juhuyāttattvasaṃkhyayā|
sindūrapuñjasaṃkāśaṃ pradhānaṃ bhrūyuge smaret || 20 ||
tālumūrdhni sthitāṃ buddhiṃ pūrṇendukiraṇopamām|
tālumadhye tvahaṃkāraṃ kusumābhaṃ vicintayet || 21 ||
tālukarṇāntare dhyāyenmano rājopaladyuti|
kaṇṭhahṛtpadmayormadhye vibhakte pañcadhā same || 22 ||
rājopalaṃ vajram|
prasphurattārakākārāñchrotrādīn pañca cintayet|
hṛnnābyoḥ pañcadhā madye vāgādīni smarettathā || 23 ||
smarecchabdāditanmātrā nābhibastyagramadhyame|
ūrvorācaraṇadvandvāt sthūlabhūtāni saṃsmaret || 24 ||
svaiḥ svairbimbaiḥ sametāni tārākārāṇi tānyapi|
uccārya praṇavaṃ tatra tattadbījaṃ samuccaret || 25 ||
tattvasaṃjñāṃ tataḥ svāhā saṃpātāhutirīdṛśī|
guruḥ saṃpātahomānte svayaṃ lakṣmīmayo bhavan || 26 ||
pūrṇāhutiṃ tato dadyāttārayā vauṣaḍantayā|
evaṃ saṃpātahomānte sūtraṃ tadgranthimad dṛḍham || 27 ||
śarāvasaṃpuṭāntaḥsthaṃ vinivedya madantataḥ|
ānītasyātha śiṣyasya netrabandhaṃ vighaṭṭayet || 28 ||
pradattapustakaṃ samyagguruṃ svamabhivādayet|
sa śiṣyo'gnisamīpastho juhuyāttārayā dhiyā || 29 ||
aṅgairupāṅgairlakṣmyādiparivāraiśca sarvaśaḥ|
adhikārī bhavatyevaṃ jape'gnau śravaṇe'rcane || 30 ||
dīkṣāyāmadhvaśuddhyarthaṃ mūlādyairjuhṛyāttataḥ|
tilena cāpi juhuyācchatādyaṃ tu daśāvaram || 31 ||
pūrṇāhutiṃ ghṛtenaiva tārayaiva tu pātayet|
eṣā dīkṣā bhavenmāntrī sarvamantraviyojanī || 32 ||
etāvatyadhikāre tu śiṣyān bhogaikalampaṭān|
grāhayedīpsitān mantrān prakṛtiprākṛtāṃstu vā || 33 ||
sidhyanti saṃmukhā mantrāḥ sarve prakṛtisaṃbhavāḥ|
tattvāni tvasya śodhyāni vakṣyamāṇaprakārataḥ || 34 ||
śuddhatattvādhvavargasya mantragrahaṇamiṣyate|
eṣā dīkṣā bhavenmāntrī tattvadīkṣāṃ nibodha me || 35 ||
oṃ bījaṃ tattvasaṃjñāṃ ca śodhayasvābhidhānvayāt|
āhutīnāṃ daśāvṛttyā samyagdhyānasamanvayāt || 36 ||
kṣityādirīśvarāntaḥ syāttattvagrāmo viśodhitaḥ|
homādau dīkṣaṇīyasya saṃjñā yojyā yathārthataḥ || 37 ||
baddhapadmāsane śiṣye sadguruḥ svasamīpage|
dhyāyedbhūmyantamīśādyaṃ samagrāṃ tattvapaddhatim || 38 ||
nirīkṣya lakṣmīnetrābhyāṃ lakṣmīhastena saṃspṛśet|
upasaṃhṛtya bhūmyādyāmīśādyāṃ tu punaḥ sṛjet || 39 ||
gurorlakṣmīmayatvasya pūrvamuktatvāt tannetre lakṣmīnetre ityucyet| lakṣmīhastena tārikāyuktahastena|
itthaṃ śiṣyatanusthānāṃ tattvānāṃ jaḍarūpiṇām|
āvahatyāśu saṃbodhaṃ dīkṣā dhyānamayī tviyam || 40 ||
pāśasūtramathādhāya śarāvadvayamadhyagam|
gatvā kuṇḍasamīpaṃ tu tadutsārya nidhāya ca || 41 ||
tārayā sāṅgayā hutvā sahasrādyaṃ śatāvaram|
puṣpamekamathādāya bahuśo'pyabhimantrya ca || 42 ||
hṛdaye tāḍayecchiṣyaṃ tārayā huṃphaḍantayā|
śiṣyaṃ bhūtattvagaṃ smṛtvā tadbhogān bhojayeddhiyā || 43 ||
samāptākhilabhūbhogaṃ tata uddhṛtya yojayet|
aptattve tatra cāpyevaṃ matveśādvidhirīdṛśaḥ || 44 ||
tārayā sāṅgayā hutvā chitvā granthiṃ tu pārthivam|
nidhāya sruci saṃpūrya sarpiṣā juhuyāttayā || 45 ||
tārikāyāḥ pare bhāve kuṇḍasthe jvalanatviṣi|
aptattvaṃ krāmayetsūkṣmaṃ tadapyevaṃ tu homayet || 46 ||
tatsūkṣmaṃ krāmayedvahniṃ juhuyāt pūrṇayā ca tat|
evaṃ krameṇa hutvā tu pūrṇāhutiparaṃparām || 47 ||
prakṛtiṃ puruṣaṃ nītvā puruṣaṃ ceśvaraṃ nayet|
īśvarāt paramaṃ tattvaṃ tattvagrāme na vidyate || 48 ||
sthūlasūkṣmaparākārā śaktirīśvararūpiṇī|
puruṣo hīśvarāttattvādadho yāto yato'śuciḥ || 49 ||
prāpya tat paramaṃ tattvaṃ śucireva bhavatyayam|
viśvātmā viśvataścakṣustato dīkṣyo bhavatyayam || 50 ||
bhogamokṣaprasiddhyarthaṃ syātāṃ pūrṇāhutī tataḥ|
sakalaṃ niṣkalaṃ śiṣyaṃ dhyātvā sruci ghṛtaṃ tathā || 51 ||
dvayaṃ tadekīkurvan vai parātītaḥ sthito guruḥ|
dhyāyan vijñānaśabdātmā paśyantīrūpamuttamam || 52 ||
tārikāyāḥ paraṃ bhāvamanucchrāyamanāhatam|
tena śiṣyaṃ samīkurvaṃstārayā vauṣaḍantayā || 53 ||
pūrṇāhutyātha śiṣyasya sthitaye dehapātanāt|
namo'ntayā dhruvādyevaṃ tārayā juhuyādbahu || 54 ||
tatra pūrṇāhutiṃ datvā mahāpūrṇamatha kṣipan|
śiṣyaṃ guruḥ svamātmānaṃ māṃ ca lakṣmīṃ sanātanīm || 55 ||
kṣīre kṣīramiva dhyāyet sarvaṃ mayi samīkṛtam|
evaṃ lakṣmīmayīkṛtya śiṣyaṃ vijñānavāyunā || 56 ||
ākṛṣya kramato mantramimaṃ saṃśrāvayet puna|
hṛdaye sthāpayitvā māṃ tārāmupadiśettataḥ || 57 ||
aṅgopāṅgādikaṃ sarvaṃ śāsrīyaṃ kramameva ca|
diśet sāmayikaṃ dharmaṃ mantraguptyādikaṃ hi yat || 58 ||
viṣṇuhastaṃ tato dadyānmūrdhni pṛṣṭhe hṛdantare|
tadaṅgaṃ mudrayālakṣya śiṣyeṇārādhayettu mām || 59 ||
tārayā kumbhamādāya yatreṣṭaṃ guruṇā purā|
aṅgopāṅgādisaṃyuktāṃ tārikāṃ manasā gṛṇan || 60 ||
abhiṣiñcedguruḥ śiṣyaṃ prasannenāntarātmanā|
labdharūpastataḥ śiṣyaḥ saṃsārāmbudhipāragaḥ || 61 ||
mahatā vibhavenātha guruyāgaṃ samācaret|
kṛtvā tvādhāraśaktyādi guruṃ tatra niveśya ca || 62 ||
arghyādyaiḥ pūjayet sarvaiḥ svarṇaratnādibhistathā|
āśitaṃ tarpitaṃ paścānmantreṇānena pūjayet || 63 ||
ajñānagahanālokasūryasomāgnimūrtaye|
duḥkhatrayāgnisaṃtāpaśāntaye gurave namaḥ || 64 ||
gṛhītvā tu tato'nujñāṃ mantramāvartayettataḥ|
sādhayecca yathākāmaṃ yāvadyāvadabhīpsati || 65 ||
itaḥ paraṃ kāśyapādyanvayabhavā eva parārthayajanādhikāriṇa iti pratipādanaparo grantho na sārvatriko dṛśyate; kiṃ tu āndhrākṣaramudritapustaka eva dṛśyate| sa ca kuṇḍalitaḥ| ato bhāgo'yaṃ kaiścit devālayeṣu sveṣāmeva parārthayajanādhikāraṃ sthāpayitukāmaiḥ saṃyojitaḥ syādityastyunnetumavakāśaḥ| ādyatvikānuṣṭhānaviruddhaścāyaṃ bhāgaḥ|
iti dīkṣābhiṣekau te varṇitau balasūdana|
samyaksamādhisaṃpādyaṃ kiṃ bhūyaḥ śrotumiccasi || 66 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre dīkṣābhiṣekaprakāro nāmaikacatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
ityekacatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

<42>
dvicatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śakraḥ
saṃsārasāgarottārapotapādāmbujadvaye|
hṛṣīkeśamahiṣyai te bhūyo bhūyo namo namaḥ || 1 ||
tvatprasādānmayā devi śruto dīkṣāvidhiḥ kramāt|
tārikāyā vadābjasthe paurascaraṇikīṃ kriyām || 2 ||
puraścaraṇaṃ nāma gurūpadeśāt gṛhītasya mantrasya svābhīṣṭaphalapradatvasaṃpādanārthaṃ kriyamāṇo vratahomādiḥ|
śrīḥ
ahaṃ nārāyaṇī nāma śaktirnārāyaṇāśritā|
tasyā me paramā pūrtistārikā bhuvaneśvarī || 3 ||
tasyā me piṇḍabhūtāyāḥ śṛṇu sādhanasaṃpadam|
kṛṣṇāṣṭamīṃ samārabya yāvatkṛṣṇacaturdaśī || 4 ||
piṇḍabhūtāyā iti| piṇḍamantrarūpāyā ityarthaḥ|
sa kālastārikāsiddhau tantrajñaiḥ saṃpradarśitaḥ|
mahāpāpairasaṃspṛṣṭaḥ prakhyātairatipātakaiḥ || 5 ||
nāstikyātpracyuto bhāvānninditābhyāsavarjitaḥ|
bhūteṣu bhāvayanmaitrīṃ kṛtapāpānutāpavān || 6 ||
uccāpacāni pāpāni prāyaścittaiḥ śamaṃ nayet|
brahmacārī haviṣyāśī satyavādī dṛḍhavrataḥ || 7 ||
saṃnidhau manasā viṣṇoroṃkāraṃ niyutaṃ japet|
mahāvyāhṛtibhirhomānayutaṃ sarpiṣācaret || 8 ||
niyutaṃ daśa lakṣāṇi| ayutaṃ daśa sahasrāṇi|
sāvitryā ca tilairhomaṃ tāvatsaṃkhyaṃ samācaret|
mahāpāpātipāpmānau vihāya prathitau kṛtau || 9 ||
prathitau vihāyeti| aprakāśakṛtau ityarthaḥ|
etādṛśaṃ vidhiṃ kṛtvā praṇavāditrayeṇa tu|
mahāpāpātipāpādyairaprakāśairvimucyate || 10 ||
upavāsādibhistadvat prakāśairapi cetaraiḥ|
tisro vopavasedrātrīraghamarṣaṇatattvavit || 11 ||
upavāsādibhirityanena prakāśakṛtānāṃ prāyaścittamucyate|
trirahnasrirniśāyāśca savāsā jalamāviśet|
pratisaṃdye nimajjaṃ srisrirjapannaghamarṣaṇ || 12 ||
pratimajjanamevaṃ tu kṣapayettat trirātrakam|
caturthe'hani vai dadyād brāhnaṇāya payasvinīm || 13 ||
evaṃ pūto bhavet prāgvadaśeṣeṇāpi cāṃhasā|
ātmānamabhiṣiñcedvā trisaṃdhyaṃ pañcagavyataḥ || 14 ||
tisro nītvā kṣapā evaṃ mucyate sarvakilbiṣaiḥ|
tārādipañcakaṃ teṣāmekaikamathavā dhiyā || 15 ||
tārādipañcakamiti| tāraṃ vyāhṛtīḥ sāvitrīṃ tārikām anutārikāṃ cetyarthaḥ|
japan piban samīkṣeta vaiṣṇavaṃ vimalojjvalam|
ahorātrakṛtairevaṃ mucyate sarvapātakaiḥ || 16 ||
vaiṣṇavaṃ samīkṣeteti kṛtasya karmaṇaḥ sādguṇyārthamuktam|
bhuvane yānti ye viṣmumapāpā dharmatatparāḥ|
taspaṅktistho'pi vā bhuktvā mucyate sarvapātakai || 17 ||
vaiṣṇavamahimānamāha bhuvana ityādinā| viṣṇuṃ śaraṇyatvena prapannā vaiṣṇavā ityarthaḥ|
dharmaiḥ pāpaṃ kṣayaṃ nītvā maharṣigaṇasaṃmataiḥ|
tārikāmāśrayet paścādbhavasāgaratārikām || 18 ||
upoṣya vidhivanmantrī kṛṣṇapakṣasya saptamīm|
sthitvā saṃdhyāmathāṣṭamyāṃ tārikājapamācaret || 19 ||
anujjhana vihitaṃ karma kāmyāntaravivarjitaḥ|
divye saiddhe tathaivārṣe viṣṇorāyatane'male || 20 ||
parvatāgre nadītīre goṣṭhe bilvavane'pi vā|
payoyāvahaviṣyāṇāmaśannanyatamaṃ sakṛt || 21 ||
japaṃ dvādaśasāhasraṃ kuryādvai sapta vāsarān|
daśāṃśaṃ tarpaṇaṃ kuryādāhutīścāpi sarpiṣā || 22 ||
anena vartamānasya vidhinā tārikāvidhau|
caturdaśīniśīthe cecchubhaṃ paśyati darśanam || 23 ||
surākumbhasya lābho vā surāpānamathāpi vā|
sriyā kāmābiṣeko vā darśanaṃ sudṛśo'thavā || 24 ||
tayā vāliṅganaṃ bhāvāt saha bhogo'thavā tayā|
mantrasiddhestayoktirvā phalalābho'thavā tataḥ || 25 ||
saumyasya darśanaṃ vāpi mithunasya surūpiṇaḥ|
rājño vā darśanaṃ rājamahiṣyā vātha darśanam || 26 ||
nārāyaṇasya vā sākṣāt svapne dṛṣṭirmamāpi vā|
pativrātādarśanaṃ vā vaiṣṇavairvā samāgamaḥ || 27 ||
yaccānyat svapnaśāsreṣu śabdyate śubhadarśanam|
labdhāśastata utthāya tyaktanidro jitaklamaḥ || 28 ||
ācamya prayato mantrī smarenmāṃ saṃstare niśām|
atha prātaḥ samutthāya kṛtasaṃdyāvidhikramaḥ || 29 ||
saṃstare śayyāyām| niśāmityatyantasaṃyoge dvitīyā|
tulyaśīlavayorūpau surūpau dharmatatparau|
adīnākṛpaṇākārau rūpavantau manasvinau || 30 ||
daṃpatī yauvanāvasthau prasannamṛdubhāṣiṇau|
āhūya snāpayitvā tau lakṣmīnārāyaṇātmakau || 31 ||
bhūṣayitvā ca vasrādyairakurvan vittavañcanām|
āśitau liptagandhāṅgau dakṣiṇāparitoṣitau || 32 ||
prārthayettārikāsiddhiṃ lakṣmīnārāyaṇātmanā|
astvevamiti vākyānte tāvāvāṃ śaraṇaṃ vrajet || 33 ||
tāvāvāmiti| lakṣmīnārāyaṇākhyāsmadrūpiṇau tau daṃpatī ityarthaḥ|
vācayitvā tataḥ svasti vaiṣṇavān vedavittamān|
dvijāgryāṃstarpayitvātha vartetābhīṣṭasaṃpade || 34 ||
caturdaśīniśāyāṃ cenna paśyet svapnadarśanam|
amāvāsyāṃ samārabya yāvatkṛṣṇasya saptamī || 35 ||
tāvantaṃ vratavāneva vartayet kālamapyatha|
trisahasraṃ japaṃ kurvannekabhuktena vartayan || 36 ||
ekabhuktena ekavārabhojanena|
madye kaumāradārāṇāmṛtuṃ prāptamalaṅghayan|
anindan kāminīvṛttaṃ daṃpatī nandayan dhiyā || 37 ||
ṛte pāpaṃ priyaṃ kurvan kāminīnāmalolupaḥ|
tataḥ kṛṣṇāṣṭamīṃ prāpya pūrvavajjapamācaret || 38 ||
kurvan homādikaṃ sarvaṃ svapnadṛṣṭau nivartayet|
yāvaccihnāni saṃpaśyet tāvadevaṃ samācaret || 39 ||
siddhāyāṃ tārikāyāṃ tu sarvaṃ ca labhate naraḥ|
samyakkartā ca śāsrāṇāmadyātmagatikovidaḥ || 40 ||
sarvatantravidhānajñaḥ sarvavedāntapāragaḥ|
sarvasaṃdehanirbhedī sarvanirṇayapāragaḥ || 41 ||
yathārthavāgṛjurvāgmī dharmasāgarapāragaḥ|
parasya svasya vāyaṃ hi viniyogaṃ cikīrṣati || 42 ||
nigrahe'nugrahe vāpi sa sa sidhyati sarvadā|
śakraḥ
namaḥ saṃpūrṇaṣāḍguṇyavigrahāyai haripriye || 43 ||
aravindagṛhāyai te govindagṛhamedhini|
tvanmukhābjācchratā siddhistārikāyā viśeṣiṇī || 44 ||
sthūlasūkṣmaparākārā yathāvacca pradarśitāḥ|
sādhitāyā vidhānena tārāyāsrividhātmanaḥ || 45 ||
viniyogamidānīṃ me vaktumarhasi padmaje|
śrīḥ
ekaḥ ṣāḍguṇyapūrṇātmā haṃso nārāyaṇo vaśī || 46 ||
haṃsī śaktirahaṃ tasya vaśinī sarvakāmadā|
haṃso haṃsī ca tāvāvāmuditau tārikātmanā || 47 ||
tasyā asmatsvarūpāyā viniyogaṃ nibodha me|
nānāvidheṣu mantreṣu bāhyāntaravibhāgataḥ || 48 ||
yāvanto yādṛśā ye ca mantrāḥ santi parāvarāḥ|
tadīyā viniyogā ye yāvantaḥ santi yādṛśāḥ || 49 ||
tāvantastādṛśāste'syā viniyogā na saṃśayaḥ|
tathāpi viniyogānme kāṃścicchakra niśāmaya || 50 ||
dharmārthakāmamokṣeṣu sadyaḥ pratyayakārakān|
kṛṣṇājinottarāsaṅgāṃ kṛṣṇājinanivāsinīm || 51 ||
kṛṣṇājināmbarāṃ trastakṛṣṇaśābaśubhekṣaṇām|
pūrṇacandranibhāṃ dhyātvā brahmamudrākṣasūtriṇīm || 52 ||
saroruhe dadhānāṃ cāpyaparasmin karadvaye|
itthaṃ māmambujākṣaṃ vā devadevaṃ janārdanam || 53 ||
dhyātvā lakṣaṃ japettārāṃ dharmaḥ pratyakṣatāmiyāt|
vibhusaṃkhyāmitānprasthāñśālīnāṃ taṇḍulātmanām || 54 ||
sarpiṣastāvataḥ prasthān śarkarāyāḥ palāni ca|
guḍasya vāpi tāvanti pācayedekapātragān || 55 ||
payasā tāvatā tulyaṃ vidhinā suśṛtaṃ haviḥ|
śuklapratipadaḥ prātarudite'rdhena bhāskare || 56 ||
ekāhutyaiva juhuyānmahāpātrasthitaṃ haviḥ|
triṣṭubhā jātavedasyā tārikādyantaruddhayā || 57 ||
jātavedasyeti| "jātavedase sunavāma somam" ityādikayā ṛcā|
mahākuṇḍe mahāvahnau yantrayogena buddhimān|
atha prātaḥ samārabhya yāvadastamayaṃ raveḥ || 58 ||
avicchinnaṃ ca juhuyāt sruveṇaiva gataklamaḥ|
sapiṣā saṃskṛtenaiva tādṛsyā triṣṭubhā sudhīḥ || 59 ||
prātarārabhya śālyannaṃ payodadhyājyasaṃskṛtam|
ekaikaṃ bhojayedvipraṃ prātarārabhya saṃtatam || 60 ||
vācayitvā dvijānante tarpayedvedavittamān|
daṃpatī vaiṣṇavau caivaṃ lakṣmīlakṣmīśasaṃjñayā || 61 ||
ya evamācareddhīro vyavasāyasahāyavān|
koṭisaṃkyamanaupamyamakṣayaṃ labhate nidhim || 62 ||
imaṃ śṛṇu mahāścaryaṃ prayogaṃ pākaśāsana|
tārāmādāya pūrvaṃ tu yojayet subhagepadam || 63 ||
svāhāṃ saṃyojayet paścāttārikeyaṃ ṣaḍakṣarā|
upoṣyaiva caturdaśyāṃ paurṇamāsyāmupakramet || 64 ||
japeddaśasahasraṃ tāmārāme śobhanadrume|
āsīno madhyataḥ samyakpūrṇakumbhapradīpayoḥ || 65 ||
dhyāyanmāṃ padmagarbhābhāṃ paṅkajadvayadhāriṇīm|
dvibhujāmasitāpāṅgīṃ nīlakuñcitamūrdhajām || 66 ||
smitapūrṇamukhīṃ ramyāṃ pīnonnatapayodharām|
sarvābharaṇasaṃpūrṇāṃ dukūlottamavāsinīm || 67 ||
suśubhāṃ subhagāmitthaṃ dhyāyañjapamathācaret|
tarpayejjuhuyāccaiva daśāṃśaṃ tārayānayā || 68 ||
baliṃ dadyācca śālyannaṃ payoghṛtaguḍānvitam|
sriyaṃ lakṣaṇasaṃpannāṃ pūjitāṃ bhojayetsudhīḥ || 69 ||

<43>
tricatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ
śṛṇu vakṣye parāṃ siddhiṃ nānāyogasamutthitām|
vidyāyāḥ piṇḍabhūtāyāstārikāyāḥ sureśvara || 1 ||
yuñcīta vidhivadyokī tārikāṃ dehagocare|
nāsikāgre ca jihvāgre jihvāyā madhyamūlayoḥ || 2 ||
kaṇṭhorasoruro'nte ca dhārayettattvapaddhatim|
kṣityādyāṃ buddhiparyantāṃ svasvakāryasvabhāvavat || 3 ||
buddhirmahattattvam|
tattvāttattvāntaraṃ yāsyan saṃkhyayā saṃkhyayā japet|
dviścatuḥ ṣaṭ tathāṣṭau ca daśaṣoḍaśavārakam || 4 ||
tattvādityādi| pṛthivītattvāt aptattvagamane ityādirītyetyarthaḥ|
caturviṃśativāraṃ ca buddhyante prakṛtiṃ smaret|
aśītiṃ saṃsaṃmarettārāṃ tatra jīvaṃ vicintayet || 5 ||
yāvān yādṛk ca yaścāyaṃ śataṃ tatrācarejjapam|
avyaktacetanādhāraṃ puṣkaraṃ tadadhaḥ smaret || 6 ||
tattvādyavayavādhāramanantaṃ tattvavāhakam|
padmākāraṃ vicintyaitajjapetsārdhaṃ śataṃ sudhīḥ || 7 ||
tatastannālamyaktakālajīvākṣarātmakam|
khaṃ yattu tatra kālastattamo'ntaḥsuṣirūpakam || 8 ||
yastvantaścetanaḥso'yamiti tattritayātmakam|
cintayitvā yathāvattadācared dviśataṃ japam || 9 ||
nālādadho'niruddhākhyaśaktiṃ māṃ bhagavanmayīm|
saṃsmaran rūpakālādyairācarettu śataṃ japam || 10 ||
pradyumnaśaktiṃ tadadho japasaṃkhyā ca tādṛśī|
adhaḥ sāṃkarṣiṇīṃ śaktiṃ japasaṃkhyā ca tādṛśī || 11 ||
sarvodho vāsudevākhyaṃ dāṃpatyaṃ sthūlarūpakam|
tataḥ sūkṣmaṃ paraṃ tasmāt parātītaṃ tu sarvataḥ || 12 ||
lakṣmīnārāyaṇābhāsamanirdeśyamanaupamam|
sarvataḥ śaktiśaktīśaspandamānamanāvilam || 13 ||
prāpya yogamayīṃ nidrāṃ nidrāmevaṃ samāviśet|
utthāyāpararātre tu dvādaśaitāstu dhārayet || 14 ||
dvādaśeti| vāsudevādicatuṣkajīvaprakṛtibuddhibhūtānīti dvādaśa dhāraṇāḥ|
vāsudevādibhūmyantaṃ tatsaṃkhyānavidhikramāt|
hṛdi vā dhārayedetāḥ samastā api dhāraṇāḥ || 15 ||
dhāraṇā dvādaśaivaitāstārikāyāmathāpi vā|
eṣa sarvahito yogo yattattvaistatra bhūyate || 16 ||
pratisaṃhṛtya vā sarvaṃ vyaktāvyaktamayaṃ padam|
nirālambaṃ manaḥ kṛtvā śūnyabhāvaṃ samāviśet || 17 ||
kalpāntaramāha pratisaṃhṛtyeti| sarvāṇyapi tattvāni svasvakāraṇeṣu pravilāpya mano nirviṣayaṃ kuryāt| nirviṣaye manasi yaḥ paramānandānubhavaḥ sa eva paramo yoga iti|
śūnyākārasamākāraṃ saṃpūrṇamiva vāridhim|
āviśāmi mahāyogaṃ śūnyabhāvaniveśitam || 18 ||
yadā stimitaniḥśabdanīradānandasaṃnibham|
yogī bhavati yuñjānaḥ sa yogī matpriyaḥ sadā || 19 ||
yatra dhyeyamavacchinnaṃ naiva kiṃcana vidyate|
ajñeyamanavacchinnaṃ tadrūpaṃ madvapuḥ sphuṭam || 20 ||
avacchinnaṃ parimitam|
ātmasātkurute yogī jñeyaṃ sakalamakramam|
vitatā sā mahājvālā nistaraṅgā tanurmam || 21 ||
hūyante manasā yatra bhūtāni bhuvanāni ca|
sarvabhāvastadā śaśvadbhāvo me pravijṛmbhate || 22 ||
sarvabhāvaḥ viśvātmanā vartamānatā| bhāvaḥ sattā|
srucā ca manasā hutvā bhūtākṣabhuvanādikam|
tāṃ ca srucaṃ prahṛtyātha yoginā bhūyate mayā || 23 ||
mayā bhūyate; madātmanā bhūyata iti bhāve prayogaḥ|
yadavacchidyate yena tattu tasmānmahattaram|
dhiyāvacchidyate sarvaṃ yadyadasti ca nāsti ca || 24 ||
devyā jñānasvarūpatvamucyate yadavacchidyata ityādinā|
sā nāvacchidyate'nyena tanuḥ sā me nirañjanā|
dhiyo naitau bahirbhūtāvabhāvo bhāva eva vā || 25 ||
anālīḍhau dhiyā na staḥ sā me tanuranañjanā|
yadātikramya maryādāṃ jaladhiḥ plāvayejjagat || 26 ||
yogaparipākadaśāyāṃ sarvamapi jagat dhīrūpamevāvabhāsata ityatra laukikaṃ nidarśanamāha yadātikramyeti|
tadā na sthalanimne stastathā mayyakhilaṃ jagat|
nedamalpātmanā śakyamāsthātuṃ paramaṃ padam || 27 ||
kuryādvicchidya vicchidya yogametadavāptaye|
bhāve bhāve śubhe'nyatra bhūte bhavati bhāvini || 28 ||
smaret sārataraṃ rūpaṃ paramaṃ pārameśvaram|
saṃbharanti prasūneṣu madhu sarveṣu ṣaṭpadāḥ || 29 ||
sarvabhāvānāṃ svātmanā bhāvyatve nidarśanamāha saṃbharantīti|
saṃbharanti tathaikāṃ māṃ sarvabhāveṣu yoginaḥ|
yatra yatra mano yāti lakṣmīṃ tatraiva cintayet || 30 ||
calitvā tat kuto yāti sarvaṃ tanmanmayaṃ hi yat|
tattvānāṃ paddhatiṃ mālāṃ matsūtragrathitāṃ smaret || 31 ||
mayi vā bhittibhūtāyāṃ citravat saṃsmarejjagat|
stimitāpāragambhīre phenapiṇḍaṃ yathāmbudhau || 32 ||
smaret kramotkramābhyāṃ vā pramātrādicatuṣṭayam|
prabhavantīṃ madākārādapi yāntīṃ ca tāṃ punaḥ || 33 ||
catuṣṭayamiti| pramātā, pramākaraṇam, prameyam, pramitiriti catuṣṭayamityarthaḥ| prabhavantīmiti tattvānāṃ paddhatimityanenānveti|
pumānādyo madunmeṣaḥ sa matsaṃkalpakalpitaḥ|
antaḥkaraṇasaṃjño'nyo madunmeṣo'dharāvanau || 34 ||
bahiṣkaraṇarūpo'nyastṛtīyastadadhaḥ kṣitau|
bhāvarūpaḥ prameyātmā turyo'yamadharādharaḥ || 35 ||
matsaṃkalpavaśenaiva sadānandacidātmikām|
bhāvayannanisaṃ śaśvat kramavyutkramato dhiyā || 36 ||
imāṃ catuṣṭayīṃ hitvā madbhāvaṃ pratipadyate|
vijñānādhārabhūteṣu dvātriṃśatyambujeṣu vā || 37 ||
dhyāyeddīpaśikhābāṃ māmathavā rūpasundarīm|
ādhārapadmādārabhya nābipadmādadho bhuvi || 38 ||
ṣaṭ padmā yogināṃ cintyāsteṣāṃ nāmāni me śṛṇu|
vyucchantī vyuṣitā vyuṣṭā vyuṣuṣī vyoṣuṣī ramā || 39 ||
nābhipadmāddhṛdambhojāt pañca padmān pracakṣate|
paśyantī nābhipadmākhyā paśyā paśyetarātmikā || 40 ||
dṛśyā ca dṛśyamānā ca hṛdayaṃ paṅkajāhvayāḥ|
hṛtkaṇṭhāntaramadyācca pañcapadmāhvayāñśṛṇu || 41 ||
bodhayantī ca boddhrī ca budhyamānā tathetarā|
ghoṣonmeṣā ca saṃjñāḥ syuḥ kaṇṭhahṛnmadhyapadmagāḥ || 42 ||
kaṇṭhatālvantarāmbhojapañcakākhyā imāḥ śṛṇu|
ghoṣayantī ca ghuṣyantī ghuṣṭā ghoṣā tathetarā || 43 ||
tālubhrūmadhyadeśasthā pañcapadmāhvayā ime|
grāhayantī ca gṛhṇānā jighṛkṣā ca gṛhītikā || 44 ||
nirṇītiriti tālubhrūmadhyāmbhojagaṇābhidhāḥ|
bhrūmūrdhamadhyapadmānāṃ ṣaṇṇāmākyā imāḥ śṛṇu || 45 ||
prāṇayantī prāṇatī ca prāṇā prāṇāvabodhinī|
parā bodheti tā etā bhrūmadhyāntaḥsthapadmagāḥ || 46 ||
dvātriṃśati ambujeṣviti padacchedaḥ| dvātriṃśadambujāni nirdiśati ādhāreti| vyucchantyādīni ṣaṭ| paśyantyādīni pañca| bodhayantyādīni pañca| grāhayantyādīni pañca| praṇayantyādīni ṣaṭ|
ratnadīpaśikhābheṣu bhāvayenmāṃ kramotkramāt|
gṛṇanmāṃ tārikāṃ dīrghāṃ dīrghaghaṇṭānadopamām || 47 ||
itthaṃ māṃ cintayan yogī surūpāṃ vāpi saṃsmaran|
vihāya sakalaṃ kleśaṃ madbhāvaṃ pratipadyate || 48 ||
lakṣayedvāpi padmeṣu navasveṣu kramotkramāt|
ādhāre trīṇi padmāni hṛdayādho'mbujatrayam || 49 ||
mūrdhno'dhasrīṇi padmāni navapadmavidhikramaḥ|
dvādaśasvathavābjeṣu mūrdhādyeṣu dvike dvike || 50 ||
ṣaṭsu vā prathamābjeṣu triṣu vā mūlahṛdbhuvi|
bhrūmadhye cintayedvāpi tārikā tāranādinīm || 51 ||
tadbinduṃ cintayet pūrvaṃ mudgamātraṃ subhāsvaram|
tataḥ sarṣapamātraṃ tu tataścitramanākṛtim || 52 ||
yo yo vā gṛhyate bhāvo ghaṭakuḍyādirūpavān|
cintayettatra tattvāni yāni yāvanti śāsrataḥ || 53 ||
manmayīkṛtya tatraitānyahaṃbhāvanayā smaret|
hetumaddhetubhūtāni loke vastūni yāni vā || 54 ||
āśritāśrayarūpāṇi yāni svacchaghanāni vā|
bhāvatadvatsvarūpāṇi śubhāśubhamayāni ca || 55 ||
prasavāprasavātmāni guṇaguṇyākṛtīni vā|
ādhārādheyabhūtāni śaktitadvanmayāni vā || 56 ||
bhogyabhoktṛsvarūpāṇi nārīnaramayāni vā|
kriyākartṛsvarūpāṇi hyupāyopeyakāni vā || 57 ||
srīpuṃpratyayarūpāṇi śabdarūpāṇi yāni vā|
dvandvabhūtāni vastūni loke'smin yāni kānicit || 58 ||
tāni yogī dhiyā paśyellakṣmīnārāyaṇātmanā|
tantrasya paramaṃ guhyaṃ vrataṃ śṛṇu puraṃdara || 59 ||
yoginā yadanuṣṭheyaṃ lakṣmīyogavidhikrame|
pravartamānayā pūrvamādidevājjagadvidhau || 60 ||
āttaṃ sīmantinīrūpaṃ mayā sābhiniveśayā|
cikīrṣurmatpriyaṃ yogī lakṣmītantravicakṣaṇaḥ || 61 ||
na smaret kāminīnindāṃ karmaṇā manasā girā|
yatrāhaṃ tatra tattvāni yatrāhaṃ tatra devatāḥ || 62 ||
yatrāhaṃ tatra puṇyāni yatrāhaṃ tatra keśavaḥ|
vanitāyāmahaṃ tasmānnārī sarvajaganmayī || 63 ||
yo'bhinindati tāṃ nārīṃ sa lakṣmīmabhinindati|
yo'bhinandati tāṃ lakṣmīṃ trailokyamabhinandati || 64 ||
yo dveṣṭi vanitāṃ kāṃcit sa dveṣṭi harivallabhām|
yo harervallabhāṃ dveṣṭi sa dveṣṭi sakalaṃ jagat || 65 ||
jyotsnāmiva sriyaṃ dṛṣṭvā yasya cittaṃ prasīdati|
nāpadhyāyati yatkiṃcit sa me priyatamo mataḥ || 66 ||
yathā nārāyaṇe nāsti mayi vā śakra kilbiṣam|
yathā gavi yathā vipre yathā vedāntavedini || 67 ||
vanitāyāṃ tathā śakra duritaṃ naiva vidyate|
akalmaṣā yathā gaṅgā yathā puṇyā sarasvatī || 68 ||
aruṇā hyāpagā yadvattathā sīmantinī varā|
yadasmi jananī nāma trayāṇāṃ jagatāmaham || 69 ||
tadidaṃ nāryavaṣṭambhāt sā hi me paramaṃ balam|
trailokyajananī devī sarvakāmasamṛddhinī || 70 ||
mattanurvanitā sākṣādyogī kasmānna pūjayet|
na kuryād vṛjinanaṃ nāryāḥ kuvṛttaṃ na smaret sriyāḥ || 71 ||
ṛte pāpāt priyaṃ nāryāḥ kāryaṃ yogamabhīpsatā|
jananīmiva tāṃ paśyeddevatāmiva māmiva || 72 ||
yo dveṣṭi vanitāṃ mohāttatsāhāyyaṃ na cācaret|
idaṃ ca śṛṇu deveśa yadvakṣyāmi priyo'si me || 73 ||
śrutvā tvayāpyanuṣṭheyaṃ naiva vācyaṃ hi kasyacit|
yā rūpiṇī varārohā kācid dṛṣṭipathaṃ gatā || 74 ||
tasyāṃ māṃ bhāvayedyogī tārikāṃ manasā gṛṇan|
alolupena cittena tasyā rūpamanusmaret || 75 ||
prāṇaṃ sūryaṃ parātmānaṃ nārīhṛdayapūruṣam|
saṃsmaredanalaṃ tatra rūpalāvaṇyasaṃpadam || 76 ||
aśeṣasaṃpadopetāṃ tāṃ nārīṃ māmanusmaret|
anusmṛtya gṛṇan brahma bhāvayedeva māṃ dhiyā || 77 ||
tataḥ samādhisaṃpattau tatrāviṣṭā bhavāmyaham|
stabdhasarvāṅgavisraṃso madāveśasya lakṣaṇam || 78 ||
alolupena cittena māṃ samārādhya yatnataḥ|
viramedeva yuñjānaḥ pāpmānaṃ parivarjayan || 79 ||
etattu paradāreṣu naiva kāryaṃ vijānatā|
ayaṃ samādhiryatrāsīt sānurajyati tad dhruvam || 80 ||
svasriyāmeva kurvīta sādhāraṇyāmadhāpi vā|
viplavo'pi na doṣo'tra yato madbhāvabhāvanā || 81 ||
saṃsparśajeṣu bhogeṣu yaḥ saṃharṣamahodayaḥ|
madrūpaṃ tadanudhyāyedavikṣiptena cetasā || 82 ||
praśastaviṣayotthaṃ yat sukhaṃ lekhanamanthanāt|
smaratastatpraharṣo yastadbhāvamanuśīlayet || 83 ||
cakṣuṣā viṣaye dṛṣṭe yā prītirupajāyate|
rasite ca śrute ghrāte sā me sukhamayī tanuḥ || 84 ||
yadṛcchopanateṣvevaṃ śabdasparśarasādiṣu|
upapattiriyaṃ proktā ceto damayato yateḥ || 85 ||
ye hi saṃsparśajā bhogā duḥkhayonaya eva te|
ādimanto'ntavantaśca na teṣu ramayenmanaḥ || 86 ||
nirastarajasā dhvastatamasā sattvavartinā|
yadantaḥkaraṇenāntarvyajyate sukhamuttamam || 87 ||
ādyantavidhuraṃ tanme sukhaṃ jñānamayaṃ vapuḥ|
tattādṛgvyajyate naiva saṃsparśairviṣayāśritaiḥ || 88 ||
anubadhnanti yad duḥkhaṃ viṣayāḥ sukhavāhinaḥ|
madhvantarviṣasaṃsṛṣṭaṃ madhuraṃ yadi bhakṣaṇe || 89 ||
kiṃ tenānantaraṃ yattadvyāpādayati bhakṣiṇam|
kruddhasya phaṇinaśchāyāṃ yaḥ śrayedātapārditaḥ || 90 ||
sa seveta naro bhogān sukhāya sparśasaṃbhavān|
sādhyān duḥkhavyayāyāsaiḥ sudarśān duḥkhamiśritān || 91 ||
tanīyasaḥ kṣayiṣṇūṃśca kaḥ śrayedviṣayān sukhī|
japyapūtamitāhāradivyasattvatanūkṛtau || 92 ||
rajastamoguṇau kṣiṇvan matpriyācārakarmaṇā|
yogī samādhaye śaśvadyatnena damayenmanaḥ || 93 ||
jite manasi vai śaśvadviśvaṃ tena vijīyate|
jite manasi śuddhā me tanurunmiṣati svayam || 94 ||
śakraḥ
samastacidacidbhedatantrayantravidhāyini|
vidhātri sarvabhogānāṃ namaste paṅkajāsane || 95 ||
viṣayapravaṇaṃ śaśvaccapalaṃ balavad dṛḍham|
āśu dūragamavyaktaṃ damyate kena tanmanaḥ || 96 ||
śrīḥ
durdamaṃ durdharaṃ śasvadaṇu durbodhamutkramam|
vairāgyābhyāsanigrāhyaṃ tanmanaḥ śakra cañcalam || 97 ||
rāgastu viṣaye raktiḥ svabhāvābhyāsayogajā|
tadabhāvaśca vairāgyaṃ tattvajñānena janyate || 98 ||
daurātmyaṃ viṣayāṇāṃ yattattvajñānaṃ tu tanmatiḥ|
caturdhā lakṣayettacca daurātmyaṃ prajñayā sudhīḥ || 99 ||
yo yādṛśo yato yasmai bhāvo'yamiti cintayet|
viṣayā bandhanātmāno viṣiṇvanti svasevinaḥ || 100 ||
viṣiṇvanti; badhnantītyarthaḥ| "ṣiñ bandhane" iti dhātuḥ|
avyaktādvyaktimāpannāḥ sarve te sukhaduḥkhayoḥ|
te ca naiva svasaṃsiddhā viṣayā viṣayaiṣiṇām || 101 ||
anekāntavyayāyāsaduḥkhasādhanajā hi te|
sukhameva na te kuryurduḥkhaṃ ca suvate hi te || 102 ||
sukhaṃ ca tatkṣaṇadhvaṃsi svadaśāduḥkhasaṃmitam|
citrarūpamidaṃ cintyaṃ bhāve bhāve vipaścitā || 103 ||
tattvajñānamidaṃ proktaṃ saṃgṛhya ca vigṛhya ca|
viṣayeṣu ca rāgastu sukhahetutvaniścayāt || 104 ||
sa mithyājñānarūpatvāttattvajñānairapohyate|
tadviniścayabādhe ca hetutvaṃ naiva sidhyati || 105 ||
ahetūn viṣayān kaścinnādadīta svasiddhaye|
yadidaṃ tattvavijñānaṃ tasya yacchīlanaṃ muhuḥ || 106 ||
so'bhyāsa iti tattvajñaistattvaśāsreṣu śabdyate|
sūkṣme mahatyaṇau sthūle sthire ca calavastuni || 107 ||
yatra tiṣṭhati yaccittaṃ dhāryate'bhyāsa eṣu vai|
vairāgyābhyāsayogena yatamānena yoginā || 108 ||
damyate mana uddāmaṃ tataḥ śāmyati tad dhruvam|
śakraḥ
tattvāravindasaṃdohavihāracaturakrame || 109 ||
madhujinmānasāvāse namaste paṅkajāsane|
abhyāsena yathā cittaṃ damyate balavad dṛḍham || 110 ||
taṃ pradarśaya panthānaṃ namaste padmasaṃbhave|
śrīḥ
viyatyūrdhvaṃ tanūbhūte javāt patati patriṇi || 111 ||
arundhatyāṃ tathā sūkṣme śanaiścittaṃ nirodhayet|
śaile mahati vā cittamanante vā vibhāvayet || 112 ||
prathamaṃ vastūni yathā pratīyante tathaiva teṣu cittaṃ niveśya, krameṇa tato vairāgyamāpādya, paścāt tattvānusaṃdhāne tanniveśanīyamitīmamarthaṃ sadṛṣṭāntamāha viyatītyādinā| tanūbhūta iti| dūradeśotplavanenālpaparimāṇatayā dṛśya ityarthaḥ|
paribhramati vā cakre yat sūkṣmamaṇu vidyate|
manastatraiva saṃyojya bhrāmayedbhramatā samam || 113 ||
evaṃ cakrādi dṛṣṭānte'pi|
tathāśvatthadalāgreṇa calatā cālayenmanaḥ|
tiṣṭhati sthāpayeccittaṃ gacchatā gamayenmanaḥ || 114 ||
pradarśanārthamuktaste prakāro'yaṃ puraṃdara|
anena damayaṃścittaṃ paraṃ yogamavāpsyati || 115 ||
īśeśitavyasaṃbhedaṃ yatpuṃrūpaṃ parāvaram|
sūryasthaṃ saṃsmarettattu srīrūpaṃ pañcabindukam || 116 ||
iti te tārikārūpaṃ sarvataḥ saumya darśitam|
itthaṃ vijñātatattvasya jñeyaṃ naivānyadiṣyate || 117 ||
itīyaṃ piṇḍasiddhaste leśataḥ śakra darśitā|
saṃjñāmūrtividhānaṃ ca sādhanaṃ cātha me śṛṇu || 118 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre nānāyogaprakāśo nāma tricatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
iti tricatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

<44>
catuścatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śakraḥ
namaste cidacidvargasaṃrakṣaṇavicakṣaṇe|
jagadvidhānaśilpinyai viṣṇupatnyai namo'stu te || 1 ||
sthūlasūkṣmaparātmāno bhedāḥ pūrvaṃ pradarśitāḥ|
tārāyā bījapiṇḍādibhedaṃ darśaya me'mbuje || 2 ||
śrīḥ
eko nārāyaṇaḥ śrīmān pūrṇaṣāḍguṇyavigrahaḥ|
ekāhaṃ paramā tasya śaktiḥ ṣāḍguṇyavigrahā || 3 ||
lokānanujighṛkṣantī śaśvajjñānakriyātmanā|
sāhaṃ tārikayā tanvā poṣayāmi punāmi ca || 4 ||
tasyāḥ parādibhāvaste śakra pūrvaṃ nidarśitaḥ|
bījapiṇḍādikān bhedān gadantyā me niśāmaya || 5 ||
yā parā darśitā pūrvaṃ yādṛśī sā tridhātmanā|
sāhaṃtā tādṛśī śakra tārikābījamucyate || 6 ||
sūryānalasthitā viṣṇupiṇḍavyomeśaśekharā|
pauraścaraṇiko yogastasyāḥ siddhiśca darśitā || 7 ||
hrīṃ iti bijamantraḥ|
saṃjñākhyā tārikāyā hi yogibhiḥ śakra sevyate|
tāmadyāvadadhānastvaṃ gadantyā me niśāmaya || 8 ||
śuddhamekamupādāya svāhayā śakra yojayet|
ayaṃ saṃjñāmanuryogidevabrahmādipūjitaḥ || 9 ||
śuddhaṃ; bījamityarthaḥ|
yogibhiryatamānairvā mayā nārāyaṇena vā|
śakyaḥ prabhāva ākhyātuṃ saṃjñāyāsridaśeśvara || 10 ||
śubhānubandhamātraṃ tu saṃjñeṣṭaṃ paramakṣaram|
piṇḍitāyā ivāsyāśca siddhayaḥ sādhanāni ca || 11 ||
śubhaḥ śakāraḥ| tatsaṃbandha eva viśeṣaḥ| śrīṃ svāhā iti saṃjñāmantraḥ|
smaran satatamabhyāsāttanmayīkṛtavigrahaḥ|
yogī manmayatāṃ prāpya madbhāvaṃ pratipadyate || 12 ||
ahaṃ vā bodhitā tena sākṣātkāramupeyuṣī|
vidadhe sakalaṃ kāmaṃ sa yogī yaṃ yamicchati || 13 ||
iti saṃjñāmanuḥ śakra leśataḥ saṃpradarśitaḥ|
padamantramidānīṃ me śṛṇu tvaṃ sarvasādhakam || 14 ||
vibhajya nāyamuddhāryo na ca lekhyastathāpi ca|
aviplavāya rūpasya svarūpamupadiśyate || 15 ||
oṃ 3, hrīṃ 3, śrīṃ 3, oṃ, āṃ 3, hauṃ 3, haṃsaḥ 3, oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ namo viṣṇave| oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ namo nārāyaṇāya| oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ namo bhagavate vāsudevāya| oṃ hrīṃśrīṃ,
jitaṃ te puṇḍarīkākṣa namaste viśvabhāvana|
namaste'stu hṛṣīkeśa mahāpuruṣa pūrvaja ||
oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ bhagavan viṣṇo nārāyaṇa vāsudeva puṇḍarīkākṣa lakṣmīpate puruṣottama jagadāde jaganmadhya jagannidhana śrīnivāsa bhagavantamabhigacchāmi| bhagavantaṃ prapadye| bhagavantaṃ gato'smi| bhagavantamabhyarthaye| bhagavadanudhyāto'ham| bhagavatparikarabhūto'ham| bhagavadanujñāto'ham| bhagavati sṛṣṭo'ham| bhagavatprasādāt bhagavanmayīṃ bhagavatīṃ tārikāmayīṃ lakṣmīṃ padairāvartayiṣyāmi| tadyathā oṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ paramagurubhyaḥ paramagurupatnībhyaḥ| oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ parameṣṭhine parameṣṭhinyai| oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ pūrvasiddhebhyaḥ pūrvasiddhābhyaḥ| oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ lakṣmīyogibhyo lakṣmīyoginībhyo namo namaḥ|
oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ īṃ namaḥ saṃsiddhisamṛddhyādipradāyai paramaikarasyāyai paramahaṃsi samastajanavāṅmanasasvātmātivartinyai nirārambhastimitanirañjanaparamānandasaṃdohamahārṇavasvarūpe paraparāyai viṣṇuviṣṇupatnyai viviṣṇu īṃ svāhā|
oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ īm̐ namo nirantaraprathamānaprathamasāmarasyāyai svasvātantryasamunmiṣitanimiṣonmeṣaparaṃparārambhe svacchandaspandamānavijñānavāridhaye parasūkṣmāyai viṣṇupatnyai māyāyai īṃ svāhā|
oṃ śrīṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ īṃ namaḥ svasaṃkalpabalasamunmīlitabhagavadvyāptibhāvasvabhāve svecchāveśavi jṛmbhamāṇasattāvibhūtimūrtikāracaturaguṇagrāmayugatrikamahormijālāyai viṣṇupatnyai pañcabindave īṃ svāhā|
oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ hiṃ hiṃ hiṃ namo nityoditamahānandaparamasundarabhagavadvigrahaprakāśe vividhasiddhāñjanāspade ṣāḍguṇyaprasaramayaparamasattvarūpaparamavyomaprabhāve vicitrānandanirmalasundarabhogajālaprakārapariṇāmapravīṇasvabhāvāyai paramasūkṣmasthūlarūpasūkṣmāyai viṣṇupatnyai pañcabindave hiṃ hiṃ hiṃ svāhā|
oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ āṃ hrīṃ hrīṃ hrīṃ namaḥ svasaṃkalpasamīraṇasamīryamāṇabahuvidhajīvakośadṛṣatpuñjāyai kalitakālakālyavikalpabhedaphenapiṇḍanivahāyai vilāsanidarśitabhoktṛbhogyabhogopakaraṇabhogasaṃpadekamahāsindhave paramasūkṣmasthūlarūpasthūlāyai viṣṇupatnyai pañcabindave hrīṃ hrīṃ hrīṃ svāhā|
oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ namaḥ samastajagadupakārasvīkṛtabuddhimano'ṅgapratyaṅgasundarāyai vidhācatuṣṭayasamunmeṣitasamastajanalakṣmīkīrtijayāmāyāprabhāvātmasamastasaṃpadekanidhaye samastaśakticakrasūtradhārāyai samastajanabhogasaubhāgyadāyini vividhaviṣayopaplavapraśamani nārāyaṇāṅkasthitāyai oṃ hrīṃ śrīṃ namo nārāyaṇāya lakṣmīnārāyaṇābhyāṃ svāhā| śrīṃ hrīṃ oṃ|
iti te darśitaḥ so'yaṃ padamantro mahādbhutaḥ|
grāhyo gurumukhādeva kṛtadīkṣaṃ manīṣibhiḥ || 16 ||
asyāmadhīyamānāyāmādarśa iva nirmale|
vidyāyāṃ saṃprakāśe'haṃ yāvatī yāsmi yādṛśī || 17 ||
saṃbodhitasvatattvārthā sarvasiddhābhinanditā|
aṅgasaṃtatimasyāstu tadantyā me niśāmaya || 18 ||
prakāśānandasārāhaṃ tadante sarvadarśini|
satsattvavyañjike cātha parabrahma tadantike || 19 ||
oṃ prakāśānandasāre sarvadarśini satsattvavyañjike parabrahmarūpe bhagavati viṣṇupatni jñānāya hṛdayāya namaḥ| jātiḥ svāhāśabdaḥ|
tato bhagavatītyeva viṣṇupatni tadantike|
jñānaṃ jātiyutaṃ vācyaṃ hṛnmantro'yaṃ mahādbhutaḥ || 20 ||
avyāhatānandagate tadante parameśvari|
sarvopari sthite śeṣaṃ hṛnmantra iva jātiyuk || 21 ||
oṃ avyāhatānandagate parameśvari sarvopari sthite jñānāya śirase namaḥ|
ahaṃ ca śaktisaṃpūrṇe jagatprakṛtike tathā|
jñānavaiśvānaraśikhe śeṣaṃ pūrvavadiṣyate || 22 ||
prāṇāprāṇamayonmeṣamahāspandamayīti ca|
sarvāśramapadātīte kavacaṃ pūrvaśeṣavat || 23 ||
vikāravidhure cātha svasvabhāvastathocyate|
mahāvīryamayītyevamasraṃ pūrvoktaśeṣavat || 24 ||
parānapekṣasāmarthye sarvaprasavinīti ca|
ante bodhamayītyevaṃ netraṃ pūrvoktaśeṣavat || 25 ||
pañcake brahmaṇāṃ kūṭe śāntānalavihāriṇī|
brahmaśrīrnāma māyeyaṃ vyomeśakṛtaśekharā || 26 ||
oṃ 5 śrīṃ iti brahmaśrīmantraḥ|
catuṣke dhāraṇākūṭe sūkṣmānalavihāriṇī|
dhāraṇāśrīriyaṃ māyāvyomeśakṛtaśekharā || 27 ||
aṃ 4 yrīṃ iti dhāraṇāśrīmantraḥ|
pradhānānalapīṭhasthā vyomeśakṛtaśekharā|
puruṣaśrīriyaṃ jñeyā cetanaughaprasāriṇī || 28 ||
mrīṃ iti puruṣaśrīmantraḥ|
dhruvānalasthitā māyāvyomeśakṛtaśekharā|
pradhānaśrīriyaṃ jñeyā sarvacetanakāriṇī || 29 ||
bhrīṃ iti pradhānaśrīmantraḥ|
vāmanādipavitrānte pavitrānalagāminī|
antaḥkaraṇaśrīrmāyāvyomeśakṛtaśekharā || 30 ||
baṃ phaṃ paṃ prīṃ iti antaḥkaraṇaśrīmantraḥ|
vāmanādipavitrānte sragdharānalacāriṇī|
jñānaśrīriyamuddiṣṭā māyāvyomeśabhūṣitā || 31 ||
naṃ dhaṃ daṃ thaṃ taṃ trīṃ iti jñānaśrīmantraḥ|
kūṭe vaikuṇṭhapūrve tu viśvāpyāyānalasthitā|
karmaśrīriyamuddiṣṭā māyāvyomeśabhūṣitā || 32 ||
ṇaṃ ḍhaṃ ḍaṃ ṭhaṃ ṭaṃ ṭrīṃ iti karmaśrīmantraḥ|
uttamādikarālānte kapilānalacāriṇī|
bhūtaśrīriyamuddiṣṭā māyāvyomeśabhūṣitā || 33 ||
ñaṃ jhaṃ jaṃ chaṃ caṃ ṅaṃ ghaṃ gaṃ khaṃ kaṃ krīṃ iti bhūtaśrīmantraḥ|
brahmaśrīpūrvikā etā aṣṭau padmadalāṣṭagāḥ|
padamūrtestārikāyāḥ parivārāḥ prakīrtitāḥ || 34 ||
sarvāstāḥ padmagarbhābhāḥ prasannānanapaṅkajāḥ|
bāhudvandve dhārayantyaḥ praphullaṃ paṅkajadvayam || 35 ||
brahmaśrīḥ pūrvahastābhyāṃ brahmāñjalikarī śubhā|
pūrvābhyāṃ dhāraṇālakṣmīḥ śuddhāśuddhavibhājinī || 36 ||
pūrvābhyāṃ puruṣaśrīstu prasādāñjalikāriṇī|
pūrvābhyāṃ prakṛtiśrīstu pāśāhkuśavidhāriṇī || 37 ||
antaḥkaraṇalakṣmīstu prasādāñjalikāriṇī|
adhomukhe pāṇitale kaniṣṭhānāmikādvayam || 38 ||
kuñcayeddhārayedanyadaṅgulyordvitayaṃ saha|
viśliṣya dhārayettadvadaṅguṣṭhaṃ pronnataṃ balāt || 39 ||
unnate dakṣiṇatale bahirmukhamavasthite|
saṃniveśastathā kārya eṣā mudrā tu tārkikī || 40 ||
tathāvidhe karadvandve vyākhyāmudrā tu yā bhavet|
jñānaśriyastu sā mudrā karmalakṣmyā nibodha me || 41 ||
karaṇavyāpṛtau pāṇī sasaṃrambhodyamau sadā|
eṣā mudrā samuddiṣṭā karmalakṣmyā mahāmate || 42 ||
bhūtalakṣmyāḥ karau pūrvau nānābhogapradāyinau|
mahālakṣmīmayaṃ kūṭamekaṃ sarvasukhāvaham || 43 ||
śṛṇu me sāvadhānena cetasā balasūdana|
garuḍādikarālāntaṃ taṃ naraḥ proddharetkramāt || 44 ||
mahālakṣmīmantraḥ kṣaṃ ityārabhya kaṃ iti paryantaṃ paṭhitvā krīṃ iti paṭhet|
karālānalagāṃ māyāṃ savyomeśāmathoccaret|
mahālakṣmīmayaṃ kūṭaṃ mahāśrīrasya devatā || 45 ||
ekānekasvabhāvā sā sākārā ca nirākṛtiḥ|
anantavaktrānantapadā tathānantakarā parā || 46 ||
ādhārādheyabhāvena sarvatrāvasthitā sadā|
nānāvarṇā tathā nānāśasragrāmaughasaṃkulā || 47 ||
krūrā saumyā samāsīnā nānāsaṃpatprakarṣiṇī|
sarvatra sulabhā dhyeyā kamalodarabhāsvarā || 48 ||
iyameva viparyastā garuḍāntā mahāmate|
pūrvavadrūpato dhyeyā pūrvoktaphaladāyinī || 49 ||
svaradvisaptakaṃ devyāḥ kiraṇatvena saṃsthitam|
bindurādhārabhāvena sṛṣṭiḥ svaṃ rūpamūrjitam || 50 ||
iti te tārikāmūrtiḥ padākhyā saṃpradarśitā|
avadhānavadasyāṃ tvaṃ sadā ceto niveśaya || 51 ||
śatakoṭipravistārāllakṣmītantramahārṇavāt|
ayaṃ sāraḥ samuddhṛtya snigdhayā darśito mayā || 52 ||
śatetyādi| anena mūlabhūtaṃ lakṣmītantraṃ śatakoṭigranthaparimitamityuktaṃ bhavati|
aṅkasthāyāṃ mayi purā devadevo janārdanaḥ|
ujjahārāravindākṣo vidyāṃ matprītaye parām || 53 ||
yathā hi candrikāṃ dṛṣṭvā nṛṇāṃ cetaḥ prasīdati|
vidyāmimāṃ tathā dṛṣṭvā yogināṃ hṛtprasīdati || 54 ||
na yamādiparikleśa āsanavyasanaṃ na ca|
na prāṇāyāmataḥ kleśo naiva kiṃcidiheṣyate || 55 ||
sukhāsīnaḥ prasannātmā yatheṣṭāsanavigrahaḥ|
abhyasya manasā vidyāmimāṃ siddhiṃ niyacchati || 56 ||
yathā nadyo nadāścaiva saṃplavante mahodadhau|
evaṃ sarvāḥ parā vidyāḥ saṃplavante padātmani || 57 ||
asaṃkhyeyāni rtanāni yathā nadanadīpatau|
asaṃkhyeyāni tejāṃsi padamūrtau tathā viduḥ || 58 ||
yathānnaṃ madhusaṃsṛṣṭaṃ madhu cānnena saṃyutam|
evaṃ bījādayo bhāvāstārāyāḥ prītidāyinaḥ || 59 ||
ahaṃ nārāyaṇāṅkasthā sarvalokamaheśvarī|
sarvasaṃpanmayī dhyeyā padamūrtirvivakṣitā || 60 ||
ahameva punaḥ śakra parā karuṇayodyatā|
sādhakānāṃ hitārthāya manaso bhāvanāya ca || 61 ||
akhaṇḍā paripūrṇā hi caturdhātmānamātmanā|
imāṃ vidyāmayīṃ mūrtiṃ vibhajāmi hitaiṣaṇī || 62 ||
caturdheti| lakṣmīkīrtijayāmāyākyāścatasro vidhā anantarādhyāye vakṣyante|
vibhaktānāṃ tu mūrtīnāṃ manmayīnāṃ puraṃdara|
vidhiṃ catasṛṇāṃ tvaṃ me yathāvacchrotumarhasi || 63 ||
iti śrīpāñcarātrasāre lakṣmītantre rahasyaprakāśo nāma catuścatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
iti catuścatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ

<45>
pañcacatvāriṃśo'dhyāyaḥ
śrīḥ
ahaṃ nārāyaṇī devī pūrveṣāmapi pūrvajā|
sākṣādbhagavato viṣṇorlakṣmīḥ śrīranapāyinī || 1 ||
vibhajāmi svayā śaktyā caturdhātmānamātmanā|
lakṣmīḥ kīrtirjayā māyā catasro mūrtayaśca tāḥ || 2 ||
yā sā śaktiḥ parā lakṣmīrahaṃtāhaṃ vibhorhareḥ|
vibhajantī svamātmānaṃ caturdhā jagato hite || 3 ||
ahameva parāhaṃtā bhavāmi prathamā tanuḥ|
lakṣmīrnāma mahābhāgā sarvaiśvaryaphalapradā || 4 ||
kītirnāma dvitīyā me tanuḥ satkīrtidāyinī|
jayā nāma tṛtīyā me tanurvijayadāyinī || 5 ||
māyā nāma caturthī me sarvāścaryakarī tanuḥ|
lakṣmīḥ kītirjayā māyetyevaṃ nārāyaṇāśrayāḥ || 6 ||
nārāyaṇāśrayāyā me mūrtayaḥ paramojjvalāḥ|
svaśaktinicayopetā nirākārāstu niṣkalāḥ || 7 ||
sūryasya raśmayo yadvadūrmayaścāmbudheriva|
sarvaiśvaryaprabhāve tu lakṣmīrlakṣmīpateḥ sthitā || 8 ||
